Parsed, Plausible, Wrong: Output Format Controls Silent Errors in Small-Model Semantic Parsing
解析、合理但错误：输出格式控制小模型语义解析中的沉默错误
A small fine-tuned parser that emits a well-formed but wrong tree fails silently: nothing downstream can tell. We ask whether the output format, a free design choice, controls how often this happens. On the PIZZA ordering benchmark we fine-tune two sub-billion models (Qwen3.5-0.8B and LFM2.5-350M) with LoRA on six lossless encodings of the same target tree, from 44.3 down to 10.9 tokens per order, including two matched-length pairs that vary surface form at fixed length. Across 300 runs (5 training pool sizes, 5 seeds) with a hierarchical bootstrap and Holm correction fixed before the results were read, shortening alone never helps, but writing the target in ordinary words does. The word encoding is the most accurate format on both models (87.5% vs. 85.8% exact match for the native s-expression on Qwen) while emitting 4.1x fewer tokens, and it cuts the silent error rate by 2.3x on Qwen and 1.8x on LFM2, separating at every pool size. Error analysis locates much of the gain in negation. All runs also learn to refuse off-menu input (held out: 95.9-99.0% handled) and to apply in-utterance self-corrections. Strict refusal has a measured price: 2.1-10.1% of real, mostly polite, orders are refused.
中文翻译
一个经过微调的小型解析器若生成语法正确但错误的树结构，将无声失败：下游系统无法察觉。我们探讨输出格式——这一自由设计选择——是否会影响此类错误的发生频率。在 PIZZA 订餐基准测试中，我们对两个亚十亿参数模型（Qwen3.5-0.8B 和 LFM2.5-350M）使用 LoRA 微调，采用六种无损的目标树编码方式，将每订单的词元数量从 44.3 降至 10.9，其中包括两组长度匹配但表面形式不同的编码。在 300 次运行中（5 个训练池大小，5 个随机种子），采用分层 Bootstrap 和 Holm 校正，结果显示：单纯缩短并不奏效，但使用普通词语表达目标却显著提升性能。词语编码在两个模型上均为最准确格式（Qwen 上精确匹配率达 87.5%，高于原生 s-expression 的 85.8%），同时减少 4.1 倍词元输出，并使 Qwen 的沉默错误率降低 2.3 倍，LFM2 降低 1.8 倍，且在所有池大小下均显著分离。错误分析显示，大部分收益来自否定处理。所有实验还学会拒绝菜单外输入（外部测试集处理率达 95.9%-99.0%），并实现句内自纠正。严格拒绝策略有实际代价：约 2.1%-10.1% 的真实订单（多为礼貌请求）被错误拒绝。
👤 Umar Ahmed, Maxim Dunaev, Mohammad Nour Al Awad, Sergey Ivanov, Ivan Khodnenko 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Discovering Effective Agent Memory Designs through Program Evolution
通过程序演化发现有效的智能体记忆设计
Program evolution provides a promising approach to discover agent memory designs and study the choices underlying their effectiveness. We introduce MAPE, a framework that evolves whole memory programs from minimal seeds through a standardized lifecycle interface and an iterative diagnosis-proposal loop. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval yield an absolute accuracy gain of up to 12% over manually designed baselines at 35% of the serving cost, with further evaluations supporting transfer across serving backbones and benchmarks. Empirical analyses and ablations identify and validate two recurring design patterns: retaining raw conversations as retrievable evidence preserves details omitted from extracted memories, while composing retrieved evidence according to query intent helps the answering model use it effectively. Transplanting the composition module into five existing memory systems improves accuracy across all five. Beyond these patterns, the search objective shapes where computation goes: optimizing serving cost alongside accuracy shifts computation from LLM-based memory construction to selective processing at query time while retaining competitive accuracy. Together, these findings offer empirical lessons for agent memory design. Code is available at https://github.com/ash-ding/MAPE.
中文翻译
程序演化为发现智能体记忆设计并研究其有效性背后的决策提供了有前景的方法。我们提出MAPE框架，通过标准化生命周期接口和迭代诊断-提案循环，从极简种子演化出完整的记忆程序。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上的实验表明，相比人工设计基线，MAPE在仅35%服务成本下实现了最高达12%的绝对准确率提升，并进一步验证了其在不同服务后端和基准间的迁移能力。实证分析与消融研究识别并验证了两种反复出现的设计模式：保留原始对话作为可检索证据，可保存从提取记忆中遗漏的细节；根据查询意图组合检索到的证据，有助于回答模型有效利用信息。将该组合模块移植到五个现有记忆系统中，均提升了准确率。此外，搜索目标决定了计算分配方式：在优化服务成本的同时兼顾准确率，促使计算从基于大语言模型的记忆构建转向查询时的选择性处理，同时保持竞争力。这些发现为智能体记忆设计提供了实证启示。代码已公开于https://github.com/ash-ding/MAPE。
👤 Xinheng Ding, Seyyed Saeid Cheshmi, Ziyang Zhang, Tairan Chen, Wenya Xie et al. 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Auditing AI peer reviewers: a benchmark on real scientific papers
审计AI同行评审：基于真实科学论文的基准测试
LLM-based peer review is now used at production scale, but its evaluation remains limited. We introduce a benchmark that injects objective, localisable errors of four types (general issues, mathematics, numerics, citations) at three severity levels into real papers and measures each reviewer's detection rate by error type and severity, together with its false-positive rate on clean copies. We also present Skepthical, a multi-agent reviewer that verifies citation-backed claims by retrieving the cited papers, mathematics with computer algebra, and numerical claims with executed code. We apply the benchmark to two corpora of 15 recent arXiv preprints each, one in astrophysics and one in machine learning (ML), injecting a total of 357 errors, and evaluate twelve reviewer systems: six single-LLM reviewers, two Claude Code configurations, three publicly available reviewers, and Skepthical. The choice of model has the largest impact on detection rate: given the same prompt, three frontier LLMs differ by about 50 percentage points in total detection on both corpora. Tools and scaffolding help when they contribute what the model cannot supply on its own. In particular, on astrophysics, Claude Code improves on a single Claude call in mathematics and numerics detection, and enforced citation retrieval gives Skepthical the highest citation error detection rate of all systems by a large margin. This citation lead, however, is not observed on ML, where the models appear to know the literature better and Skepthical's citation detection rate is statistically indistinguishable from that of the best single LLM. False-positive rates are below 10% for most systems, but vary with prompt, model, and scaffolding, with mathematics flags the main source of false positives. The evaluation pipeline, the injected errors, and the adjudication logs are released as a benchmark.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型（LLM）的同行评审现已大规模应用，但其评估仍有限。我们提出一个基准测试，将四种类型（普遍性问题、数学、数值、引用）的三类严重程度的客观可定位错误注入真实论文中，并测量每个评审系统在不同错误类型和严重程度下的检测率，以及在干净样本上的误报率。我们还提出了Skepthical，一种多智能体评审系统，通过检索被引论文、使用计算机代数处理数学内容、通过执行代码验证数值声明来验证引用支持的主张。我们将该基准测试应用于两个各包含15篇近期arXiv预印本的语料库（天体物理学和机器学习），共注入357个错误，并评估了十二种评审系统：六种单LLM评审器、两种Claude Code配置、三种公开可用的评审器以及Skepthical。模型选择对检测率影响最大：在相同提示下，三种前沿LLM在两个语料库中的总检测率相差约50个百分点。当工具和框架能补充模型自身无法提供的能力时，它们才真正有效。特别是在天体物理学领域，Claude Code在数学和数值检测上优于单一Claude调用；强制引用检索使Skepthical在所有系统中取得了显著更高的引用错误检测率。然而，在机器学习领域，这一优势未显现，因为模型似乎更熟悉文献，Skepthical的引用检测率与最佳单LLM无统计差异。大多数系统的误报率低于10%，但随提示、模型和框架变化，数学标记是主要误报来源。评估流程、注入的错误及裁决日志已作为基准发布。
👤 Íñigo Zubeldia, Boris Bolliet, Francisco Villaescusa-Navarro, Pablo Villanueva-Domingo, Miles Cranmer 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16929]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
SPADE: Soft Prompts for Ai-text DEtection
SPADE：用于AI文本检测的软提示
The easy access to Large Language Models (LLMs) has made AI-generated texts on the web increasingly ubiquitous, making AI-text detection a crucial task. Prompts greatly influence the generation process, but existing detectors focus solely on the generated texts --- supervised methods learn from labeled texts only, and zero-shot methods rely on the text's probability distributions --- ignoring the underlying generation process, which limits their robustness in open-world scenarios. We introduce SPADE: Soft Prompts for Ai-text DEtection, a novel framework for detecting AI-generated text that transfers better across datasets. Specifically, we measure the prompt's influence and use it as a discriminative signal. We characterize the prompt's influence by introducing a Pointwise Mutual Information (PMI) term that captures how much a prompt explains a text. Based on this quantity, SPADE trains one soft prompt per class to amplify the separation, and predicts the class whose prompt best explains the candidate text. Extensive experiments on several benchmarks against various detectors demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of our method, achieving state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on out-of-distribution (OOD) datasets.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）的易获取性使得网络上的AI生成文本日益普遍，因此AI文本检测成为关键任务。提示对生成过程有重大影响，但现有检测器仅关注生成文本本身——监督方法仅从标注文本中学习，零样本方法依赖文本的概率分布——忽略了底层生成过程，限制了其在开放世界场景中的鲁棒性。我们提出SPADE：用于AI文本检测的软提示，一种新型框架，可在不同数据集间实现更好的迁移。具体而言，我们测量提示的影响并将其作为判别信号。通过引入点互信息（PMI）项来刻画提示对文本的解释力。基于此，SPADE为每类训练一个软提示，以增强类别间分离度，并预测哪个提示最能解释候选文本。在多个基准上对多种检测器的广泛实验表明，该方法具有出色的鲁棒性和有效性，在分布外（OOD）数据集上达到最先进的性能。
👤 Alex Jiang, Didier Schwab, Chuyuan Li 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16816]

Outcome-Mass Alignment: Controlling Length-Dependent Trajectory Influence in Multi-Turn LLM Agent Reinforcement Learning
结果质量对齐：控制多轮LLM智能体强化学习中的长度依赖轨迹影响
To keep long interaction contexts tractable, stepwise group-relative training for multi-turn LLM agents materializes each source trajectory into multiple response rows and valid tokens. This expansion creates an outer-accounting problem distinct from within-trajectory credit assignment: row-based outcome statistics can count one episode outcome multiple times, while token-level reduction can assign unequal aggregate objective mass across trajectories. We term these effects outcome-statistics multiplicity and objective-mass imbalance. We introduce Outcome-Mass Alignment (OMA), a two-component method that makes both accounting choices explicit. Outcome Alignment computes outcome statistics over unique source trajectories, while Mass Alignment controls aggregate objective mass after within-trajectory aggregation of optimizer-native token objectives. Neither redesigns the base optimizer's credit assignment, importance ratios, or clipping. Across matched ALFWorld GRPO comparisons at two model scales, where failed trajectories typically expand much more than successful ones, OMA improves matched-horizon endpoint success by 7.0-9.4 percentage points. Ordinary WebShop exhibits weak or reversed expansion-outcome coupling and correspondingly mixed effects, whereas in a success-oriented WebShop setting, WebShop-SOL, OMA improves success nAUC from 0.399 to 0.511. Component ablations and GiGPO and GSPO comparisons further support component separability and cross-optimizer compatibility. These results show that trajectory expansion always defines an implicit weighting rule, but its practical effect depends on how expansion couples to outcomes and optimizer-native updates.
中文翻译
为保持长交互上下文的可处理性，多轮LLM智能体的逐步分组相对训练将每个源轨迹展开为多个响应行和有效标记。这种扩展带来了不同于轨迹内信用分配的外部会计问题：基于行的结果统计可能多次计算同一episode的结果，而基于token的缩减可能导致不同轨迹间聚合目标质量不均。我们将这两种效应称为结果统计多重性和目标质量失衡。我们提出结果-质量对齐（OMA），一种包含两个组件的方法，使这两种会计决策显式化。结果对齐在唯一源轨迹上计算结果统计，而质量对齐在轨迹内聚合优化器原生token目标后控制总目标质量。该方法不改变基础优化器的信用分配、重要性比率或裁剪机制。在两种模型规模下对ALFWorld GRPO进行匹配比较，其中失败轨迹通常比成功轨迹扩展得更严重，OMA使匹配时间终点的成功率提升了7.0-9.4个百分点。普通WebShop任务表现出弱或反向的扩展-结果耦合，相应效果混杂；而在以成功为导向的WebShop设置（WebShop-SOL）中，OMA将成功率nAUC从0.399提升至0.511。组件消融实验及与GiGPO、GSPO的对比进一步支持各组件的可分离性与跨优化器兼容性。这些结果表明，轨迹扩展始终隐含一种加权规则，但其实际影响取决于扩展如何与结果及优化器原生更新相耦合。
👤 Dawei Zhang, Yubin Huangfu, Jessica Fan, Boyu Wei, Likang Wu et al. 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Towards Green Text Generation: Memory-Based Speculative Decoding
迈向绿色文本生成：基于记忆的推测解码
Speculative decoding accelerates text generation with large language models by letting a cheap draft model propose tokens that a large verifier checks in a single forward pass. We introduce Olifant, a draft model built on a memory-based classifier. It predicts the next token from a four-token window in under 0.3 ms on CPU, requiring no GPU, no backpropagation, and no accelerator memory. We measure throughput and energy under two verifier regimes on consumer hardware. Without a KV cache, drafting is nearly free: a domain-matched Olifant model reaches 38.1% acceptance on EU legal text, delivering 2.43x throughput and 2.51x lower energy per token than the neural verifier model alone. With a KV-cached verifier, itself a ~10x energy saver, drafting costs matter: a TinyLlama draft model halves throughput and triples energy despite high acceptance, whereas Olifant still yields 1.25x throughput and the lowest energy in the study (93 J per 100 tokens, 14x below the uncached baseline). We further show that KV caching decouples time from energy: verification of longer drafts raises power draw, shifting the energy-optimal draft length below the speed-optimal one. Speculative decoding is not dead in the age of fast verifiers, provided drafting is nearly free.
中文翻译
推测解码通过让一个低成本的草稿模型提出候选词，由大型验证模型在单次前向传播中进行校验，从而加速大语言模型的文本生成。我们提出了Olifant，一种基于记忆分类器构建的草稿模型。它能在CPU上以不到0.3毫秒的时间从四token窗口预测下一个词，无需GPU、反向传播或加速器内存。我们在消费级硬件上测量了两种验证器模式下的吞吐量和能耗。在无KV缓存的情况下，草稿几乎免费：与领域匹配的Olifant模型在欧盟法律文本上的接受率达38.1%，相比仅使用神经验证模型，吞吐量提升2.43倍，每token能耗降低2.51倍。在使用KV缓存的验证器下（本身可节省约10倍能耗），草稿成本变得重要：尽管TinyLlama草稿模型接受率高，但其吞吐量减半、能耗翻倍；而Olifant仍实现1.25倍吞吐量提升，并在研究中达到最低能耗（每100个token 93焦耳，比未缓存基线低14倍）。我们进一步表明，KV缓存将时间与能耗解耦：更长的草稿会增加功耗，使能量最优的草稿长度低于速度最优的长度。只要草稿几乎免费，推测解码在快速验证器时代仍未过时。
👤 Antal van den Bosch 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

MuSeToM: Multi-Sensory Evaluation of Belief Revision with Textual Cues Across Six Senses
MuSeToM：跨六种感官的文本线索下信念修正的多感官评估
Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer mental states in others, is pivotal for human social cognition. Existing evaluations of ToM in large language models (LLMs) mainly focus on static belief attribution and false-belief reasoning, leaving a critical aspect of social reasoning underexplored: can LLMs decide whether perceptual evidence is sufficient to justify another person's belief revision? To address this gap, we present MuSeToM, a human-validated, text-based benchmark for evaluating epistemic belief revision in ToM reasoning. MuSeToM contains 2,415 instances adapted from BigToM and OpenToM, with textual cues spanning six sensory categories, and tests whether models can determine when a protagonist should revise a belief and infer the resulting updated belief state. Using MuSeToM, we systematically evaluate state-of-the-art LLMs under direct and chain-of-thought prompting, revealing a pronounced dissonance: while models often detect that a belief may change, they struggle to identify the updated belief and frequently over-update on perceptually salient but epistemically insufficient cues.
中文翻译
心智理论（ToM）是理解他人心理状态的能力，对人类社会认知至关重要。现有针对大语言模型（LLMs）的ToM评估主要集中在静态信念归属和错误信念推理，忽视了一个关键的社会推理方面：LLMs能否判断感知证据是否足以支持他人信念的修正？为填补这一空白，我们提出了MuSeToM，一个经过人工验证的、基于文本的基准测试，用于评估ToM推理中的认识论信念修正。MuSeToM包含2,415个实例，源自BigToM和OpenToM，涵盖六种感官类别的文本线索，测试模型能否判断主角应在何时修正信念并推断出更新后的信念状态。利用MuSeToM，我们系统评估了最先进的LLMs在直接提示和思维链提示下的表现，揭示出显著的不一致：尽管模型常能察觉信念可能发生改变，但在识别更新后的信念方面表现不佳，且经常因感知显著但认识论上不足的线索而过度修正。
👤 Chunkit Chan, Kawai Chung, Sze Heng Douglas Kwok, Yuxuan Liu, Yauwai Yim et al. 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16612]
agent-benchmark
Unibuc - NLP at "Voight-Kampff" Generative AI Detection PAN 2025
Unibuc - NLP在“Voight-Kampff”生成式AI检测PAN 2025中的应用
This paper describes the approach of the Unibuc - NLP team in tackling the "Voight-Kampff" Generative AI Detection task from PAN at CLEF 2025. In the Subtask 1: Voight-Kampff AI Detection Sensitivity we have explored different approaches of leveraging embeddings extracted from LLMs, using either classical ML models or further fine-tuning LLMs with a classification head for the downstream binary classification task. Our experiments showed that using a majority vote ensemble of classical ML models (Light GBM, XGB, Logistic Regression and SVM) is comparable with fine-tuning for downstream task achieving around 99.4% F1 score. For the Subtask 2: Human-AI Collaborative Text Classification we explored a way of combining features at different layers extracted using transformer backbone with layer-wise projection and attentive pooling which proved modest results.
中文翻译
本文介绍了Unibuc-NLP团队在CLEF 2025举办的PAN“Voight-Kampff”生成式AI检测任务中的方法。在子任务1：Voight-Kampff AI检测灵敏度中，我们探索了利用大语言模型提取的嵌入向量，结合传统机器学习模型或对大语言模型进行微调以添加分类头，完成下游二分类任务。实验表明，使用Light GBM、XGB、逻辑回归和SVM的多数投票集成模型，在性能上与微调相当，F1分数约为99.4%。在子任务2：人机协作文本分类中，我们探索了使用Transformer骨干网络在不同层提取特征，并通过分层投影和注意力池化进行融合的方法，结果表现一般。
👤 Teodor Marchitan, Claudiu Creanga, Liviu P. Dinu 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16433]
agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
daVinci-Prover: Agent-Native Mid-Training for Formal Theorem Proving
daVinci-Prover：面向形式定理证明的原生智能体中期训练
Advances in large language models (LLMs) are rapidly transforming formal theorem proving. Yet further progress remains constrained by the lack of a transparent and effective data recipe for systematically improving theorem-proving models. In this work, we argue that effective agentic provers require strong mathematical skill priors to ground theorem proving and agentic trajectories to enable long-horizon, interactive proof construction. To this end, we synthesize an agent-native 33.7B-token mid-training corpus covering decomposition, search, self-verification, and revision, together with verifier-grounded agentic trajectories. We further introduce the daVinci-Prover harness, which enables models to construct proofs and interact with a Lean verifier in an isolated workspace, and use it to collect approximately 36K high-quality agent trajectories for supervised fine-tuning. Our mid-training recipe yields an average improvement of 13.7 points over the matched Qwen3-8B SFT-only baseline across MiniF2F, FATE-M, and ProofNet. When applied to the Qwen3.5 series, our approach also yields strong performance across theorem-proving benchmarks and achieves near-saturating MiniF2F performance with memory compaction. The results highlight agent-native mid-training as an effective foundation for developing stronger interactive theorem-proving agents.
中文翻译

Indic-Task Gains and Regressions after Reinforcement Learning
强化学习后印地语任务的收益与退化
We run reinforcement learning (RL) with GRPO across four environments, starting from a supervised fine-tuned 30B-A3B mixture-of-experts model covering English and eight Indic languages. We compare the model before and after RL across 34 benchmarks in one evaluation campaign. MATH improves by 36.07 points, and most Indic benchmarks also improve. Yet XQuAD-Indic F1 falls 19.32 points as English answers rise from 8.7% to 91.7%; IndicQA falls 8.64, while English XQuAD gains 11.71 F1. A second RL run from the same supervised initialization adds an Indic environment together with language and repetition rewards. Compared with the released model, it improves both passage-answering and instruction-following benchmarks, along with preserving MATH performance. But it loses ground on reasoning and coding benchmarks. Several training choices changed together, so we cannot isolate the effect of any one of the reward terms. Script compliance remains unresolved. Adding a language reward did not ensure use of the requested script. Passage answering and script compliance therefore need direct evaluation, even when other benchmark scores improve.

我们在四个环境中使用 GRPO 进行强化学习（RL），起始于一个覆盖英语和八种印地语的 30B-A3B 混合专家模型。我们在一次评估活动中，对比了 RL 前后模型在 34 个基准测试上的表现。数学（MATH）成绩提升了 36.07 分，大多数印地语基准也有所提升。然而，XQuAD-Indic 的 F1 值下降了 19.32 分，英语答案占比从 8.7% 上升至 91.7%；IndicQA 下降 8.64，而英语 XQuAD 提升 11.71 F1。第二次 RL 运行从相同的监督微调初始化出发，增加了印地语环境以及语言和重复奖励。相比发布版本，它在段落问答和指令遵循基准上均有提升，并保持了 MATH 性能。但在推理和编码基准上表现退步。由于多个训练选择同时改变，无法孤立分析任一奖励项的影响。脚本合规性问题仍未解决。增加语言奖励并未确保使用请求的脚本。因此，即使其他基准分数提升，段落问答和脚本合规性仍需直接评估。

What Roles Do System Prompts Really Assign? Validating and Steering Occupational Role Vectors in LLMs
系统提示究竟分配了什么角色？验证与引导大语言模型中的职业角色向量
Applications built on large language models assign the model a role through its system prompt, intending to shape how it responds for their use case. We introduce a validation framework for extracting and injecting occupational role vectors, activation-level directions that capture how someone in a given occupational role would distinctively respond to a task or question. We validate these vectors through two complementary lenses, on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. The interpretability lens asks whether each vector is a stable, separable, domain-consistent representation of its role: extracted vectors are highly reproducible across repeated runs and sampling temperatures, remain separable even for very similar roles, and agree more with themselves across in-domain and out-of-domain questions than with other roles' signal. The control lens asks whether injecting a role vector during generation makes the model express the role, and sustain it over a conversation, as well as a role prompt does. On Qwen, injecting the vector alone makes the model express the intended role substantially more often than unsteered, but at best matches a role prompt; on both models, adding the vector to the prompt strengthens the role in single answers but did not slow the role's decline over 30 turns. Together, the two lenses show why both are needed: out of domain the role is expressed only subtly, yet the activations still encode it; and a vector that is stable and separable can still barely steer the model's behavior.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型的应用通过系统提示赋予模型特定角色，以期望其响应符合特定使用场景。我们提出了一种验证框架，用于提取和注入职业角色向量——一种激活层级的方向，捕捉某职业角色在面对任务或问题时的独特回应方式。我们通过两个互补视角在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型上验证这些向量：解释性视角关注向量是否为稳定、可分离且领域一致的角色表征——提取的向量在重复运行和不同采样温度下高度可重现，即使在非常相似的角色之间仍保持可分离性，并且在域内和域外问题上的自一致性高于与其他角色信号的一致性；控制视角则考察在生成过程中注入角色向量是否能使模型表现出该角色并维持整个对话过程中的角色特征，如同使用角色提示一样。在Qwen模型上，仅注入向量即可使模型更频繁地表达目标角色，但效果仍不及角色提示；在两个模型上，将向量加入提示后虽增强了单次回答中的角色表现，但未能延缓角色在30轮对话后的衰退。两者结合表明：在域外情况下，角色仅被微妙地表达，但激活仍编码了该角色信息；即使向量稳定且可分离，也可能难以有效引导模型行为。

ShadowBench: Exposing Lexical Anchoring and the Illusion of Forgetting in Large Language Models
ShadowBench：揭示大语言模型中的词汇锚定现象与遗忘幻觉
Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as primary interfaces for factual retrieval, yet current evaluation paradigms rely almost exclusively on explicit entity names. In this work, we demonstrate that LLM knowledge is fundamentally lexically anchored: models possess extensive factual information but find it significantly difficult to retrieve without explicit name tokens. To quantify this limitation, we introduce ShadowBench, a rigorously hardened, shortcut-resistant benchmark designed to evaluate Latent Entity Association via a novel Dual-Trait Association (DTA) task. Our evaluation reveals a pervasive "Shadow Gap" across all model scales$\unicode{x2013}$up to the frontier models GPT-5.4 and Claude-Sonnet-4.6$\unicode{x2013}$where removing lexical anchors causes performance drops of over 20%. Furthermore, we demonstrate that this lexical dependency exposes a critical vulnerability in AI safety. Applying ShadowBench to Machine Unlearning, we find that state-of-the-art algorithms (e.g., Gradient Difference, NPO) achieve only superficial lexical erasure. While unlearned models fail on direct queries, our novel Latent Entity Leakage Rate (LELR) metric reveals that for popular entities, models explicitly reconstruct the "forgotten" entity in their internal reasoning traces in over 74% of cases, utilizing residual shadow knowledge to solve associative tasks. Conversely, for niche entities, models retain high shadow accuracy (60-90%) via implicit attribute correlation despite 0% LELR. Ultimately, ShadowBench indicates that current unlearning paradigms create an "Illusion of Forgetting," demonstrating that existing methods act as superficial output filters and are not yet reliable for achieving true parametric erasure.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）已成为事实检索的主要接口，但当前评估范式几乎完全依赖显式的实体名称。本文揭示，LLM的知识本质上是词汇锚定的：模型拥有大量事实信息，但在缺乏显式名称标记时难以有效检索。为量化这一局限，我们提出了ShadowBench，一个严格强化、抗捷径的基准测试，通过新颖的双特质关联（DTA）任务评估潜在实体关联。我们的评估发现，在所有模型规模下——包括前沿模型GPT-5.4和Claude-Sonnet-4.6——移除词汇锚定会导致性能下降超过20%，形成普遍存在的“阴影差距”。此外，我们证明这种词汇依赖暴露了AI安全中的关键漏洞。将ShadowBench应用于机器遗忘任务，发现当前最先进的算法（如梯度差、NPO）仅实现表面化的词汇擦除。虽然被遗忘模型在直接查询中失败，但我们提出的潜在实体泄漏率（LELR）指标显示，对于主流实体，模型在内部推理轨迹中重新构建“已遗忘”实体的情况超过74%，利用残留的阴影知识完成关联任务。相反，对于冷门实体，尽管LELR为0%，模型仍通过隐式属性相关性保持60%-90%的阴影准确率。最终，ShadowBench表明，当前遗忘范式制造了“遗忘幻觉”——现有方法仅作为表面输出过滤器，尚未能实现真正的参数级擦除。
👤 Sujan Maharjan, Lu Liu 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv

HeriKGQA: Grounded Knowledge-Graph Question Answering over Cultural Heritage Sensor Data
HeriKGQA：基于文化遗产传感器数据的 grounded 知识图谱问答
Cultural heritage sites are increasingly monitored by sensor networks, but querying their data requires database expertise. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a natural-language interface, yet over raw sensor records they lose track of which sensor, site, and time each value belongs to, and silently miscompute. We present HeriKGQA, a knowledge-graph question-answering method that confines the LLM to interpreting questions and phrasing answers, leaving computation to SPARQL and deterministic code. Questions are parsed into intents constrained by a heritage ontology aligning W3C SOSA/SSN with CIDOC-CRM, compiled into SPARQL, and answered from query results and temporal, spatial, and anomaly facts derived by a grounding layer, with error correction guarding each LLM step. On 500 questions over real data from five heritage sites, HeriKGQA reaches 99.0% accuracy, 18.2 points above the strongest of three baselines using the same LLM and receiving the same anomaly rules and answer conventions, with comparable answer quality while running almost four times faster. Without grounding, accuracy drops by 12.2 points and on anomaly questions from 99% to 66%. We report lessons from development and live deployment.
中文翻译
文化遗产遗址正越来越多地通过传感器网络进行监控，但查询其数据需要数据库专业知识。大型语言模型（LLMs）提供了自然语言接口，但在原始传感器记录上，它们会丢失每个数值所属的传感器、地点和时间信息，并静默地产生错误计算。我们提出 HeriKGQA，一种知识图谱问答方法，将 LLM 限制在解释问题和表述答案，而将计算任务交由 SPARQL 和确定性代码完成。问题通过遗产本体（对齐 W3C SOSA/SSN 与 CIDOC-CRM）解析为意图，编译成 SPARQL 查询，并从查询结果以及由接地层推导出的时间、空间和异常事实中获取答案，每一步都配有错误纠正机制。在五个文化遗产站点的真实数据上进行的 500 个问题测试中，HeriKGQA 达到 99.0% 的准确率，比使用相同 LLM 且接收相同异常规则和答案规范的三个基线模型高出 18.2 分，同时保持相当的答案质量，运行速度几乎快了四倍。若无接地机制，准确率下降 12.2 分，异常问题的准确率从 99% 降至 66%。我们报告了开发与实际部署中的经验教训。

SO-Arena: Benchmarking the Capability Spectrum and Reliability of System One Models
SO-Arena：系统一模型能力谱与可靠性的基准测试
System One models promise fast, general-purpose decision-making without lengthy token generation. However, understanding their capability spectrum and decision reliability requires more than aggregate performance on current benchmarks. Existing evaluations often provide limited insight into the breadth of model capabilities and the reliability of their decisions. We introduce SO-Arena, a benchmark characterizing the capability spectrum and decision reliability of System One models across 11 domains and five capability families. For the capability spectrum, SO-Arena offers evidence-grounded evaluation through reliable sources and manual verification of reference labels against model-visible inputs; fine-grained capability assessment through domain-specific difficulty criteria; and realistic interactive evaluation, where models iteratively execute actions in sandboxes. Reasoning-intensive tasks further enable system one-two comparison. Beyond accuracy, SO-Arena assesses decision reliability through two complementary measures: decision stability under controlled option-set changes and missing-answer recognition when no valid option is available. Evaluations across 21 models reveal that the benefits of scaling and additional reasoning vary substantially across domains. More importantly, strong overall performance does not necessarily imply reliable decision-making: the best-performing System One model ranks only seventh in reliability. Together, these findings highlight the need to characterize capability spectrum and decision reliability as complementary dimensions, providing a systematic view of the strengths and limitations of System One models.
中文翻译
系统一模型承诺在无需长时间生成 token 的情况下实现快速、通用的决策。然而，理解其能力谱和决策可靠性，仅靠当前基准的聚合性能是不够的。现有评估往往难以揭示模型能力的广度及其决策的可靠性。我们引入 SO-Arena，一个涵盖 11 个领域和五类能力的基准，用于刻画系统一模型的能力谱和决策可靠性。在能力谱方面，SO-Arena 通过可靠来源和人工验证参考标签与模型可见输入的一致性，提供基于证据的评估；通过领域特定的难度标准实现细粒度能力评估；并通过沙箱中模型迭代执行动作的现实交互式评估。推理密集型任务进一步支持系统一与系统二的对比。除了准确率，SO-Arena 还通过两个互补指标评估决策可靠性：在可控选项集变化下的决策稳定性，以及当无有效选项时的缺失答案识别能力。对 21 个模型的评估表明，缩放效应和额外推理的收益在不同领域间差异显著。更重要的是，整体表现优异并不意味着决策可靠：表现最佳的系统一模型在可靠性上仅排名第 7。这些发现强调，应将能力谱和决策可靠性视为互补维度，从而系统性地揭示系统一模型的优势与局限。

Outside the Purview of the Reward: Capability Drift in Multilingual Post-Training
奖励之外：多语言后训练中的能力漂移
Post-training can improve rewarded behavior while weakening capabilities users still need. We study two GRPO branches from a shared supervised checkpoint in a multilingual mixture-of-experts model. The released branch gains 36.07 points on MATH but loses 19.32 F1 points on XQuAD-Indic, while XQuAD-English improves under the same scoring rule. An independently trained sibling adds Indic instruction following and language-aware shaping. It recovers the regressing Indic task scores and preserves MATH, but does not restore script compliance and trades off competition mathematics and code. In a later round, judge training is followed by a sharp extraction decline from the Indic branch; reward-free distillation from its earlier checkpoint restores most of the lost F1. A MATH score decomposition associates most of the measured gain with increased boxed-answer emission; accuracy among boxed answers changes little. These results show why benchmark scores should be read alongside the response forms they reward.
中文翻译

ChJGBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Chinese Legal Judgment Generation with Diagnostic Evaluation
ChJGBench：面向中文法律判决生成的综合性基准及其诊断评估
With the rapid development of LLMs, increasing attention has been paid to whether they can reliably generate complete and legally valid judicial documents. This calls for a benchmark capable of evaluating whether models can accurately apply statutes and resolve outcome-critical issues, including claims, charges, penalties, and monetary obligations. However, existing benchmarks typically focus on isolated legal tasks or coarse-grained evaluation. We introduce ChJGBench, a benchmark for generating the reasoning and dispositive sections of Chinese judgments across civil, criminal, and administrative cases. Built from 3,462 real-world judgments, ChJGBench provides a case-type-aware diagnostic evaluation framework comprising 13 metric types and adopts a hybrid paradigm. Experiments reveal no uniform advantage for legal-domain models over general-purpose models, with model strengths varying across diagnostic dimensions. Performance is generally stronger on criminal cases, while civil and administrative cases continue to present substantial challenges.

Can We Make an LLM Dumber, or Is It Just Playing Dumb? An Empirical Study of Believable Performance Throttling
我们能让大模型变笨吗？还是它只是在假装笨？关于可信性能降级的实证研究
A benchmark score is commonly read as a proxy for model capability. We study the inverse problem: can a model be driven to a target score, and do its failures resemble those of a naturally weaker model? We operationalize believable performance reduction along three axes: target calibration, item-difficulty structure, and error-distribution similarity to a real weak reference. We introduce CapLadder, an IRT-calibrated suite of 1,985 items spanning mathematics, code, multiple-choice reasoning, and tool use. A simple weak-teacher LoRA dial reaches unseen target levels on reasoning with a 1.9-point held-out calibration error, whereas prompting, temperature, and weight noise cannot combine comparable range and precision. Yet on multiple choice, the dial converges to an answer-token attractor: up to 97% of its newly introduced errors land on one letter, with the attractor changing from D in Qwen to A in Llama. Logit interventions identify a dominant near-constant shift: subtracting the estimated shift on held-out items recovers 52 to 59% of lost accuracy and removes the dominant attractor, while option permutation confirms that the bias tracks token position rather than option content. With high inter-annotator agreement ($κ=0.94$), human-coded error-category distributions nonetheless match the weak reference on reasoning (JS = 0.00), showing that local plausibility and aggregate detectability differ. Structured tool calls exhibit more natural degradation in the Qwen family (JS = 0.09), while a globally calibrated dial transfers poorly across benchmarks (14.2-point error on two usable benchmarks). Precise score control, therefore, does not imply a portable or believable reduction in skill.
中文翻译
基准分数通常被视为模型能力的代理指标。我们研究其反向问题：能否将模型引导至目标分数，且其失败模式是否类似于自然较弱模型的表现？我们从三个维度操作化可信性能降低：目标校准性、题目难度结构以及错误分布与真实弱参考模型的相似性。我们引入CapLadder，一个基于项目反应理论（IRT）校准的1,985个题目的套件，涵盖数学、代码、多选推理和工具使用。一个简单的弱教师LoRA调节器可在推理任务中达到未见目标水平，持有样本校准误差仅为1.9分；而提示、温度调整和权重噪声无法实现同等范围与精度。然而在多选题中，该调节器收敛至一个答案标记吸引子：高达97%的新错误落在单一字母上，且吸引子从Qwen的D变为Llama的A。对数空间干预识别出主导性的近似恒定偏移：在持有样本上减去估计偏移可恢复52%至59%的准确率损失，并消除主要吸引子；选项排列实验确认偏差追踪的是标记位置而非选项内容。尽管人工标注的错误类别分布具有高一致性（κ=0.94），但在推理任务上，人类编码的错误分布与弱参考模型高度一致（JS=0.00），表明局部合理性与整体可检测性存在差异。结构化工具调用在Qwen系列中表现出更自然的退化（JS=0.09），而全局校准的调节器在跨基准迁移中表现差（两个可用基准上误差达14.2分）。因此，精确的分数控制并不意味着技能的可移植或可信降低。
👤 Chengguang Gan, Yunhao Liang, Hanjun Wei, QingHao Zhang, Shiwen Ni 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv

SimRec: Learning to Search, Ask, and Recommend from Simulated Interaction
SimRec：从模拟交互中学习搜索、提问与推荐
LLM-based recommendation agents can search catalogs, inspect products, ask users, and revise rejected recommendations. However, these behaviors are often governed by hand-designed workflows, while existing training methods optimize isolated components such as query rewriting or list ranking rather than the interaction policy as a whole. We present SimRec, a reinforcement learning framework that trains the full interaction policy of a tool-integrated recommendation agent. SimRec introduces two components. A task-anchored user simulator provides feedback on every recommendation: conditioned on the current task need rather than on user profile, and it can be an external LLM or the recommender itself under self-play. A task-aligned reward optimizes the whole trajectory with GRPO, crediting natural retrieval quality and valid tool use alongside the final hit, with training-only target injection to handle sparse hits over large catalogs. On ESCI and Amazon C4, SimRec improves the NDCG@100 of a Qwen3-4B agent from 32.23% to 35.42% and from 22.94% to 24.72%}, outperforming training-free agents on far larger backbones and task-aligned baselines on NDCG. Analysis shows that the trained agent verifies candidates, uses preferences, and recovers from rejected recommendations far more often than its untrained counterpart. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/SimRec .
中文翻译
基于大语言模型的推荐智能体能够搜索商品目录、检查产品、询问用户并修正被拒绝的推荐。然而，这些行为通常由人工设计的工作流程控制，而现有的训练方法仅优化查询重写或列表排序等孤立组件，而非整体的交互策略。我们提出SimRec，一种强化学习框架，用于训练工具集成推荐智能体的完整交互策略。SimRec引入两个组件：任务锚定的用户模拟器，根据当前任务需求而非用户画像提供反馈，可为外部大模型或推荐系统自身在自对弈模式下运行；任务对齐奖励机制结合GRPO优化整个轨迹，不仅奖励最终命中率，还奖励自然的检索质量和有效的工具使用，并通过仅在训练阶段注入目标来应对大规模目录中的稀疏命中问题。在ESCI和Amazon C4数据集上，SimRec将Qwen3-4B智能体的NDCG@100从32.23%提升至35.42%，从22.94%提升至24.72%，优于无需训练的大型基线模型及任务对齐基准模型。分析表明，训练后的智能体更频繁地验证候选项、利用用户偏好并从被拒绝的推荐中恢复。代码已公开于https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/SimRec。

Knowledge in the Harness, Reliability in the Weights: Teacher-Free Co-Evolution of an LLM Agent
知识在缰绳中，可靠性在权重里：无需教师的大模型智能体协同进化
Small open models suit private customer-service deployments but trail frontier models in two ways: they miss what the written policy requires in a given situation, and they act inconsistently on what they know. We study whether such a model can close both gaps from its own deployment logs and their reference outcomes, without a stronger teacher model. Our starting point is a Gemma 4 12B agent that reads its domain policy from on-demand skills rather than from its system prompt. In each round, the deployed system serves a fresh batch of tasks; the base model diagnoses failed conversations and writes rules into the skills, and a rule survives only if it passes code checks, counterexample self-review, a paired retest, and a noise-calibrated validation gate. Reinforcement learning (GRPO) then trains a LoRA adapter on complete multi-turn, tool-using conversations. On the retail and airline domains of $τ^2$-bench, without training or selecting on official tasks, the agent after two rounds improves on this starting point by 12.5 pass$^1$ points in retail and 19.5 in airline, and by 18.4 and 22.0 pass$^4$ points. It also exceeds the benchmark's reference agent, which runs the same model with the whole policy in its system prompt, by 11.8 and 8.0 pass$^1$ points and by 22.8 and 16.0 pass$^4$ points, closing most of the gap to three frontier models under the same protocol. A $2\times2$ ablation shows that the rules rescue tasks the agent never solved and carry policy knowledge that RL with the rules in context does not internalize, whereas the trained weights make solved tasks reliable.
中文翻译
小型开源模型适用于私有客户服务部署，但在两方面落后于前沿模型：一是无法理解特定情境下的书面政策要求，二是对其所知内容的行为不一致。我们研究该模型能否仅通过自身的部署日志及其参考结果，在无更强教师模型的情况下弥补这两项差距。起点是一个基于Gemma 4 12B的智能体，其领域政策通过按需调用技能获取，而非嵌入系统提示中。每轮中，部署系统处理一批新任务；基础模型诊断失败对话，并将规则写入技能库，只有通过代码检查、反例自审、成对重测和噪声校准验证门控的规则才能留存。随后，强化学习（GRPO）在完整的多轮、工具使用对话上训练LoRA适配器。在$τ^2$-bench的零售与航空领域，未在官方任务上进行训练或选择的情况下，经过两轮迭代，该智能体在零售领域提升12.5 pass$^1$分，在航空领域提升19.5分；在pass$^4$指标上分别提升18.4和22.0分。它还超越了基准参考智能体（同一模型但全政策置于系统提示中），在零售和航空领域分别领先11.8和8.0 pass$^1$分，以及22.8和16.0 pass$^4$分，基本弥合了与三个前沿模型在相同协议下的差距。2×2消融实验表明，规则挽救了智能体从未解决的任务，并承载了RL在上下文中无法内化的政策知识，而训练后的权重则使已解决问题更加可靠。

CoLFA: Collaboration-Guided Lightweight Failure Attribution for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems
CoLFA：面向基于大语言模型的多智能体系统的协作引导轻量级故障归因方法
Failure attribution in multi-agent systems requires tracing error formation to identify the decisive error step and the responsible agent. However, agents' division of work and information exchange lead to Interleaved Steps and Cluttered Content, requiring models to link executions and check details in lengthy records. This makes it difficult to balance localization accuracy and token cost. We find that multi-agent collaboration also leaves records of how executions connect and content passes between them as results are formed, providing evidence for tracing error formation. Based on this observation, we propose CoLFA (Collaboration-Guided Lightweight Failure Attribution), which uses collaboration cues to organize the attribution input according to how results are formed, determining which steps are examined and how their content is presented. Through Handoff-Guided Step Expansion and Cross-Referenced Content Organization, CoLFA helps the model focus on examining error formation and the role of each decision while reducing the processing of irrelevant and repeated records. Across five settings spanning Who&When and LongRCA and three backbone models, CoLFA achieves the highest exact step-localization accuracy with the lowest token consumption. Compared with individual baselines in each setting, CoLFA improves accuracy by up to 37.50% and reduces token consumption by up to 97.6%.
中文翻译
多智能体系统中的故障归因需要追溯错误形成过程以识别决定性错误步骤及责任智能体。然而，智能体的工作分工与信息交互导致执行步骤交错、内容杂乱，要求模型在冗长记录中建立执行关联并检查细节，难以平衡定位精度与token消耗。我们发现，多智能体协作过程中也留下了执行如何连接、内容如何传递的痕迹，这些可作为追踪错误形成过程的证据。基于此观察，我们提出CoLFA（协作引导轻量级故障归因），利用协作线索按结果形成方式组织归因输入，确定需检查的步骤及其内容呈现方式。通过“交接引导步骤扩展”与“交叉引用内容组织”，CoLFA帮助模型聚焦于错误形成过程及各决策角色，同时减少对无关重复记录的处理。在涵盖Who&When与LongRCA等五种设置及三种主干模型的实验中，CoLFA实现了最高精确的步骤定位准确率，且token消耗最低。相比各设置下的独立基线，CoLFA准确率最高提升37.50%，token消耗最多降低97.6%。
👤 Yufei Luo, Yuchen Ling, Chunrong Fang 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.14925]

ToolTurns: Benchmarking LLM Agents in Multi-Turn Dialogue
ToolTurns：面向多轮对话中大语言模型智能体的基准测试
Tool-using language models are increasingly deployed in conversational settings, yet most large-scale tool-use benchmarks focus on isolated requests. We introduce ToolTurns, a benchmark of 2,620 problems spanning 1,827 tools and 11,004 API endpoints across 47 categories. Each dialogue begins with an intentionally underspecified request and continues through two context-dependent follow-ups, testing clarification, tool use, and retention of conversational context. We also introduce an open-source user simulator that reveals withheld context through dialogue and generates follow-up requests, with a checker--fixer pipeline for improving its turns. Across nine language models, the best agent resolves 69.6% of individual requests but completes only 39.8% of full dialogues. Under this evaluation protocol, every evaluated model receives lower success scores on follow-up requests than on the initial request, and dialogue-quality rubric scores and task success can rank models very differently. Paired turn-level evaluation shows that the checker--fixer reduces detected dialogue defects, and blind human evaluation favors the revised turns. ToolTurns provides a testbed for evaluating whether agents sustain task success across multi-turn dialogue with tools.
中文翻译
使用工具的语言模型越来越多地应用于对话场景，但大多数大规模工具使用基准集中于孤立请求。我们引入ToolTurns，一个包含2,620个问题、覆盖1,827个工具和11,004个API端点、跨越47个类别的基准。每个对话以故意描述不清的请求开始，并通过两次依赖上下文的后续追问展开，测试澄清能力、工具使用及对话上下文保持能力。我们还引入一个开源用户模拟器，可通过对话揭示隐藏上下文并生成后续请求，并采用检查-修复流水线改进其对话回合。在九个语言模型中，最佳智能体解决单个请求的成功率为69.6%，但完整对话完成率仅为39.8%。在此评估协议下，所有被测模型在后续请求上的成功得分均低于初始请求，且对话质量评分与任务成功率对模型的排名可能截然不同。成对的回合级评估显示，检查-修复流程降低了检测到的对话缺陷，盲评人类更偏好修改后的对话。ToolTurns为评估智能体在带工具的多轮对话中是否能持续保持任务成功提供了测试平台。

[2611.14861]

Is Jev a Good Multilingual Judge?
Jev 是一个优秀的多语言评判者吗？
Generative large language models are widely used for multilingual text evaluation, yet whether decision models that directly output judgment probabilities can serve as multilingual judges has received limited attention. We evaluate Jev against generative LLM judges on four multilingual benchmarks with human annotations, covering up to 47 language varieties and three evaluation formats: binary decisions, pairwise comparisons, and graded ratings. At approximately $1/31$ to $1/61$ of GPT-6.1's evaluation cost, Jev is competitive on most multilingual judging tasks, but falls behind GPT-6.1 on knowledge-intensive answer verification. Further analysis shows that Jev is more likely to accept incorrect answers in low-resource languages than in English and that its probability outputs help identify errors across tasks. Based on these findings, we offer recommendations on evaluation instructions, confidence-based selection, and model routing, providing empirical guidance for using Jev as a multilingual judge.
中文翻译
生成式大语言模型广泛用于多语言文本评估，但直接输出判断概率的决策模型能否作为多语言评判者仍缺乏足够关注。我们在四个包含人类标注的多语言基准上评估了 Jev，涵盖最多47种语言变体和三种评估格式：二元决策、成对比较和分级评分。在评估成本仅为 GPT-6.1 的约 1/31 到 1/61 的情况下，Jev 在大多数多语言评判任务中表现具有竞争力，但在知识密集型答案验证任务上落后于 GPT-6.1。进一步分析显示，Jev 在低资源语言中更倾向于接受错误答案，而在英语中则相对谨慎；其概率输出有助于识别跨任务中的错误。基于这些发现，我们提出了关于评估指令设计、基于置信度的选择以及模型路由的建议，为将 Jev 用作多语言评判者提供了实证指导。
👤 Wei Liu, Yi Fan, Xiyan Fu 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.14774]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
An LLM Is All You Need: Data-Efficient Adaptation to Full-Duplex, Word-Level Spoken Dialogue
一个大语言模型就够了：面向全双工、词级语音对话的数据高效适配
Full-duplex spoken dialogue, in which a system listens while it speaks and can backchannel, yield the floor, and be interrupted, is usually obtained by training on hundreds of thousands to millions of hours of speech. This is costly, rewrites the language model that supplies the system's knowledge, and still leaves turn-taking either implicit or decided coarsely rather than word by word. We show that a frozen, general-purpose multimodal LLM can instead be adapted into a word-level full-duplex system with little data. Our system is a thinker-talker cascade on an 80 ms frame clock. The thinker keeps the LLM frozen and trains only lightweight adapters and a few new token embeddings (97M parameters); at every frame it emits one token of agent text, with pauses, yields, and onsets represented as tokens of the same vocabulary and kept legal by a finite-state machine. A four-stage curriculum, each stage anchored to the model's own behavior under the previous format, performs the conversion in about 30 GPU-hours. The talker reads only the thinker's discrete tokens, so the two halves are trained independently. On Full-Duplex-Bench and InteractionBench, the system is competitive with open-source full-duplex systems built through large-scale speech training: among them, it is the least likely to take the floor when the user merely pauses, backchannels most often, and leads in spoken-task accuracy, groundedness, and coherence over long conversations.
中文翻译
全双工语音对话系统允许系统在说话的同时监听，并能进行插话、抢话和中断，通常需要数十万至数百万小时的语音数据进行训练，成本高昂，且会重写系统知识来源的语言模型，同时对话轮次仍以粗粒度方式处理而非逐词决策。我们展示了一个冻结的通用多模态大语言模型（LLM）可通过极少数据被适配为词级全双工系统。我们的系统采用80毫秒帧时钟的思考者-说话者级联结构：思考者保持LLM冻结，仅训练轻量级适配器和少量新标记嵌入（97M参数）；每帧生成一个代理文本标记，包括停顿、让步和起始均以同一词汇表中的标记表示，并由有限状态机保证合法性。四阶段课程学习（每阶段基于前一格式下模型自身行为锚定）约耗时30 GPU小时完成转换。说话者仅读取思考者的离散标记，因此两部分可独立训练。在Full-Duplex-Bench和InteractionBench上，该系统在性能上可与通过大规模语音训练构建的开源全双工系统媲美：其中，用户仅短暂停顿时最不易抢话，回话频率最高，且在长对话中的任务准确率、上下文连贯性和真实性方面表现领先。
👤 Tairan Chen, Richard Yucheng He, Yihang Liu, Baodong Cao, Chen Xu 📅 2026-11-10 🔗 arXiv
[2611.14758]

Benchmarking Multilingual Document Understanding in Vision-Language Models
视觉-语言模型多语言文档理解基准测试
Recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) perform strongly on multimodal tasks, Document Understanding (DU) among them, but the benchmarks that measure them mostly cover English documents and narrow problems. How well current VLMs generalize across languages, tasks, styles and layouts therefore remains unclear. We introduce MDU, a multilingual DU benchmark of 23146 examples spanning 13 tasks and five languages: English, French, German, Italian, and Spanish. The tasks isolate complementary capabilities, from surface-level text and Markdown extraction through summarization, classification and named-entity recognition to layout-sensitive passage and layout extraction. We evaluate 45 state-of-the-art open-weight VLMs from 1.7B to 235B parameters, plus one commercial system under the same protocol. The benchmark is far from saturated: the best average is 69.0, only eight of the 45 evaluated systems reach 65, and the two best results come from 27B models rather than from the largest systems we evaluate. Performance is uneven in ways that scale does not fix. The gap between the strongest and weakest language does not close as models grow, and it survives controlling for the domain composition of each language. Capabilities also do not improve together: once we partial out overall model quality, per-task scores are largely uncorrelated, and transcription ability in particular trades off against semantic extraction.
中文翻译
近期的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在多模态任务中表现出色，包括文档理解（DU），但现有评估基准大多局限于英文文档和狭窄任务。当前VLMs在跨语言、跨任务、跨风格和跨布局上的泛化能力仍不明确。我们提出了MDU，一个包含23,146个样本、覆盖13项任务和5种语言（英语、法语、德语、意大利语、西班牙语）的多语言文档理解基准。任务涵盖从表面文本和Markdown提取，到摘要、分类、命名实体识别，再到布局敏感的段落和布局提取等互补能力。我们在相同协议下评估了45个最先进的开源VLMs（参数量从1.7B到235B不等）以及一个商业系统。该基准尚未饱和：最佳平均得分为69.0，仅8个系统达到65分，且表现最好的两个结果来自27B模型而非最大规模模型。性能提升并不均衡，模型规模无法弥合最强与最弱语言之间的差距，即使控制语言领域构成后该差距依然存在。能力也非同步提升：在剔除整体模型质量影响后，各项任务得分基本无相关性，尤其文本转录能力与语义提取能力之间存在明显权衡。
👤 Matteo Gabburo, Daniela Baiamonte, Stefano Simonazzi, Silvia Gardini, Sean Cesare Lazzeri et al. 📅 2026-11-10 🔗 arXiv

Probe What-If Worlds Before You Ship: Persona Simulation for Real-World Decision Making
在发布前探测‘如果世界’：用于现实世界决策的人格模拟
User research and system testing reveal whether products and services meet user needs, yet recruiting specialized participants, designing controlled what-if comparisons, and repeating studies as designs evolve often bottleneck design iteration and data-driven decision-making. We present a framework and execution infrastructure that translates real-world industry inquiries into controlled experiments with simulated users. Our methodology formally defines the target cohort, experimental stimuli, and decision-relevant metrics, while the pipeline supports large-scale concurrent runs over cohort-stimulus-environment combinations (survey, web, chatbot, and OS app), auditable trial-level logging, cohort-level outcome aggregation, and cross-cohort and cross-variant statistical reports. Across twelve industry partner cases spanning five domains, we show how constructing hard-to-reach or what-if persona cohorts supports exploratory studies and distills simulated responses into testable design hypotheses. Cross-model comparisons on selected cases largely preserve primary conclusions, whereas perturbing or ablating persona attributes alters service outcomes, effect sizes, and inter-group differences, indicating that grounding target cohorts in detailed personas materially shapes simulation results. Together, these studies show that persona-enabled agents can test specified cohorts at design speed, diagnose systems under test, and deliver directional evidence that focuses problem definition, steers design and strategic decisions, and guides further study.
中文翻译
用户研究与系统测试可揭示产品和服务是否满足用户需求，但招募特定参与者、设计受控的“如果”对比实验，并随着设计迭代重复研究常成为设计迭代与数据驱动决策的瓶颈。我们提出一个框架及执行基础设施，将真实产业问题转化为带有模拟用户的受控实验。我们的方法形式化定义目标人群、实验刺激和决策相关指标，而流水线支持在人群-刺激-环境组合（调查、网页、聊天机器人、操作系统应用）上大规模并发运行，提供可审计的试验级日志记录、人群级结果聚合，以及跨人群和跨变体的统计报告。在涵盖五个领域的十二个产业合作案例中，我们展示了构建难以触及或“如果”型人格人群如何支持探索性研究，并将模拟响应提炼为可测试的设计假设。跨模型比较在选定案例中基本保留主要结论，而扰动或删减人格属性则会改变服务结果、效应大小和组间差异，表明以详细人格为基础的目标人群显著影响模拟结果。综合来看，这些研究表明，基于人格的智能体可在设计速度下测试指定人群，诊断待测系统，并提供方向性证据，聚焦问题定义，引导设计与战略决策，并指导进一步研究。

MITRA-Swarm: Injecting Explicit Linguistic Structure into Foundation Models for Classical Machine Translation
MITRA-Swarm：在基础模型中注入显式语言结构以实现古典机器翻译
Classical Sanskrit poses three challenges unresolved by single-call monolithic models: morphophonemic fusion Sandhi, dense nominal compounding Samāsa, and discourse-level zero-anaphora forced by poetic meter. We present MITRA-Swarm, a multi-agent architecture injecting explicit linguistic structure into an otherwise monolithic pipeline: a Sandhi-splitting agent, a parallel array of generalist gemma-4-26B-A4B-it and domain-specialized gemma-2-mitra-it translators, and a discourse-aware Ellipsis Restorer that consults corpus-derived register tags to avoid inventing active-voice subjects in impersonal legal and philosophical registers. Two separated judges evaluate quality: a routing judge ($J_{\text{route}}$, same model family as the generators) driving an internal critique loop, and an independent reporting judge ($J_{\text{report}}=$gpt-4o-mini, no shared vendor lineage) scoring every output exactly once. On a stratified benchmark of 1,000 verses across five registers (23,990 scored verdicts, 12-condition pre-registered ablation), the full swarm reaches $85.90$ MQM against a monolithic baseline of $85.27$ ($Δ=+0.63$, $p_{\text{adj}}=0.0018$, 95% CI $[+0.30,+0.97]$), and isolated discourse restoration alone accounts for $+0.44$ MQM ($p_{\text{adj}}=0.0288$), outperforming a call-count-matched self-refinement baseline by $+0.51$ MQM ($p_{\text{adj}}=0.0288$). Severe per-verse failures (MQM$<80$) fall from $9.0\%$ to $7.6\%$, at roughly $9\times$ the per-verse inference cost of the baseline. Additionally, $J_{\text{route}}$ scores its own family's outputs $2$-$7$ MQM higher than $J_{\text{report}}$ on every one of the 12 conditions, empirically motivating the two-judge split; and a preflight screen of a 192,151-entry retrieval index against all 12 evaluation works verifies 0 reference leaks across 269 runtime checks. These are small gains on a metric saturating near 85-86.

古典梵语面临三个单次调用的单一模型无法解决的挑战：音位融合（Sandhi）、密集名词复合（Samāsa）以及因诗歌韵律强制导致的篇章级零回指（zero-anaphora）。我们提出MITRA-Swarm，一种多智能体架构，将显式语言结构注入原本的单体流水线：包括一个Sandhi拆分智能体、一组通用的Gemma-4-26B-A4B-it和领域专精的Gemma-2-mitra-it翻译智能体组成的并行阵列，以及一个具备篇章意识的省略恢复器，通过查阅语料库衍生的语域标签避免在非主动语态的法律与哲学语境中虚构主动语态主语。两位独立评委评估质量：路由评委（$J_{\text{route}}$，与生成器同模型家族）驱动内部批判循环，独立报告评委（$J_{\text{report}}=$gpt-4o-mini，无共享供应商血统）对每个输出仅评分一次。在包含1,000句诗、五种语域的分层基准测试中（23,990个评分判决，12个条件预注册消融），完整智能体群达到85.90 MQM，优于单体基线的85.27（Δ=+0.63，$p_{\text{adj}}=0.0018$，95% CI $[+0.30,+0.97]$），仅篇章恢复部分即贡献+0.44 MQM（$p_{\text{adj}}=0.0288$），优于调用次数匹配的自反思基线+0.51 MQM（$p_{\text{adj}}=0.0288$）。严重错误（MQM<80）比例从9.0%降至7.6%，推理成本约为基线的9倍。此外，$J_{\text{route}}$在其家族输出上始终比$J_{\text{report}}$高出2-7 MQM，验证了双评委机制的合理性；对包含192,151条目的检索索引进行预检，覆盖所有12项评估作品，在269次运行检查中确认无参考泄露。这些是在接近85-86的饱和指标上的微小提升。

Metal Sandbox: Isolated Execution and Command Scheduling for GPU Coding Agents on Apple Silicon
Metal Sandbox：适用于 Apple Silicon 上 GPU 编码智能体的隔离执行与命令调度
On-device inference requires kernels that make effective use of local accelerators. Efficient training and evaluation of models that generate Apple Metal kernels benefit from native macOS tools and concurrent agent sessions. These sessions alternate between model requests, development commands, and GPU execution, leaving GPU capacity idle for much of a rollout. We present Metal Sandbox, infrastructure that combines restricted per-instance file access, native command execution, and command-level scheduling across interconnected Macs. Each agent retains its workspace and private development environment. Parallel commands can run concurrently, while serial commands share one execution slot per node; eligible waiting commands can migrate to available Macs and return results to the original agent. Integration with Harbor supports task execution, automatic verification, and rollout collection through a common workflow. Controlled experiments on four Mac mini M4 nodes achieve $1.68\times$--$2.05\times$ gains in command throughput under concentrated demand. Sixteen static agent traces give an estimated $2.14\times$ sustained-throughput gain for four sessions per node versus one, assuming continuously replenished sessions and unchanged mean durations. Replaying these measured traces under dynamic scheduling reduces mean tool waiting by 18.8% and batch elapsed time by 22.3%, assuming unchanged phase durations. Metal Sandbox and its Harbor integration are open-source on GitHub.

设备端推理需要高效利用本地加速器的内核。高效训练和评估生成 Apple Metal 内核的模型，依赖原生 macOS 工具和并发智能体会话。这些会话在模型请求、开发命令和 GPU 执行之间交替，导致 GPU 资源在大部分时间处于空闲状态。我们提出 Metal Sandbox，一种结合实例级文件访问限制、原生命令执行和跨互联 Mac 的命令级调度的基础设施。每个智能体保留其工作区和私有开发环境。并行命令可并发运行，串行命令每节点共享一个执行槽；符合条件的等待命令可迁移到可用 Mac 并返回结果给原始智能体。与 Harbor 的集成支持任务执行、自动验证和通过统一工作流的滚动收集。在四个 Mac mini M4 节点上的受控实验显示，在集中需求下命令吞吐量提升 1.68× 至 2.05×。十六个静态智能体轨迹估算表明，每节点四会话相较于一会话，持续吞吐量提升 2.14×，假设会话持续补充且均值时长不变。在动态调度下重放测量轨迹，平均工具等待时间减少 18.8%，批处理耗时减少 22.3%，假设各阶段时长不变。Metal Sandbox 及其 Harbor 集成已在 GitHub 开源。

Actor, Judge, or Teacher? Understanding Decision Models in Long-Horizon Agents
演员、裁判还是教师？理解长周期智能体中的决策模型
Decision models answer a question with a typed output, such as a choice among options with a probability for each, so one model can choose an agent's actions, evaluate actions already taken, or score every action to teach a policy with reinforcement learning. Prior work has studied them mainly as actors, judging their usefulness by how well they act. We ask whether a decision model that acts poorly can still teach well. We compare three decision models and three general LLMs as actors and judges on ALFWorld, and use JEV as a teacher on ALFWorld and WebShop. As actors, all six models are pulled away from a subgoal that must come first by an easier one. As judges that see the whole trajectory, the same models credit the steps toward that subgoal, and asking whether each action helped, rather than whether the task will succeed, separates useful from useless actions. As a teacher, JEV raises a Qwen2.5-1.5B policy over GRPO from 72.8% to 82.9% success on ALFWorld and from 56.8% to 71.4% on WebShop. These results suggest that a poor actor can still teach well, because judging an action after the episode ends reveals the consequences that acting must anticipate. Code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/decision-agents.

CapLite: Prompt-Injection Defense for Small Language Model Agents without Model-Written Security Artifacts
CapLite：无需模型生成安全构件的小型语言模型智能体提示注入防御
Enforcement defenses against prompt injection, such as CaMeL, Progent, IPIGuard and DRIFT, ask the agent's own model to write a security artifact (a program, a privilege policy, a dependency graph or a constraint list) that code then enforces. We ask whether small language model (SLM) agents can be trusted to write these artifacts. Across 15 open-weight models, 13 of them between 1.7B and 12B parameters, they cannot: the policies, graphs and constraints that the agent's model writes admit attacks on 14 (Progent), 13 (IPIGuard) and 11 (DRIFT) of the models, and CaMeL's programs fail to run in up to 68% of episodes. We present CapLite, which derives its policy from the tool schemas, commits the write calls before any tool result is read, and admits a write only if every argument that decides whom or what it affects is stated in the user's request. This guarantees write integrity: injected text cannot add a write, and no write reaches a recipient, amount, object or destination that the user did not state. No attack succeeds in 13,935 attacked AgentDojo episodes. CapLite reaches utility under attack comparable to CaMeL's (29.0% vs. 27.2%) with fewer tokens than every other enforcement defense and than the undefended agent: 17.1k per episode, 18% of CaMeL's, and the highest utility per token of all enforcement defenses.
中文翻译
针对提示注入的强制性防御方法（如CaMeL、Progent、IPIGuard和DRIFT）要求智能体自身的模型生成安全构件（程序、权限策略、依赖图或约束列表），然后由代码执行强制。我们质疑小型语言模型（SLM）智能体是否可信生成这些构件。在15个开源权重模型中，13个参数规模在1.7B至12B之间，结果表明它们不可信：这些模型生成的策略、图谱和约束使14个（Progent）、13个（IPIGuard）和11个（DRIFT）模型易受攻击，而CaMeL的程序在高达68%的episode中无法运行。我们提出CapLite，其策略源自工具模式，承诺在读取任何工具结果前执行写入调用，并仅当决定影响对象或目标的所有参数均明确出现在用户请求中时才允许写入。这保证了写入完整性：注入文本无法添加新写入，且任何写入都不会到达用户未声明的接收者、数量、对象或目的地。在13,935次受攻击的AgentDojo episode中，无一攻击成功。CapLite在攻击下的实用性与CaMeL相当（29.0% vs. 27.2%），但使用的token更少，优于其他所有强制性防御及未受保护的智能体：每episode仅需17.1k token，仅为CaMeL的18%，且是所有强制性防御中单位token实用率最高的。

Detection Is Not Early Warning: Evaluating Token-Level Alarms on Language Model Failures
检测并非早期预警：评估语言模型失败的标记级警报
Many failures of language models begin at a specific token, the onset. A monitor that reads the model during generation can classify the finished response, detect the failure once it is under way, or warn before it begins, and only a warning leaves time to stop or steer the generation. Monitors are usually evaluated with response-level metrics such as AUROC, which ignore when the monitor fires. We introduce PREWARN, an evaluation protocol for any failure with an annotated onset. It calibrates each monitor's threshold on validation data to an error rate chosen for the application, runs the monitor, and scores its alarms relative to the onset on all three tasks. As a case study we apply it to repetitive degeneration in three open language models, with early warning as the main task, using linear probes on activations and six untrained baselines. Response-level metrics saturate on this failure, and a rule that reads only the response length scores almost perfectly, while probes that AUROC ranks as equal warn very differently under PREWARN. Probes warn before the onset on most degenerate responses of two of the three models, low-rank adapters below the probe more than double the share of tokens just before the onset that it flags, largely by flagging degenerate responses long before the onset, and no untrained baseline warns much more often than random alarms. We release the probes with calibrated thresholds at https://huggingface.co/collections/marcodene/prewarn-degeneration-probes
中文翻译
许多语言模型的失败始于某个特定标记，即“起始点”。监控器在生成过程中读取模型，可分类已完成的响应，检测失败发生后的情况，或在失败开始前发出预警；只有预警能提供时间来终止或引导生成过程。目前监控器通常用响应级指标（如AUROC）评估，忽略了监控器触发的时间点。我们引入PREWARN评估协议，适用于具有标注起始点的任何失败。该协议在验证数据上校准每个监控器的阈值至应用所需的错误率，运行监控器，并根据起始点对所有三项任务（检测、预警、分类）进行评分。作为案例研究，我们将该协议应用于三种开源语言模型中的重复退化问题，以早期预警为主要任务，采用激活值上的线性探测器和六个未经训练的基线。响应级指标在此失败上趋于饱和，仅依赖响应长度的规则几乎完美得分，而AUROC排名相等的探测器在PREWARN下预警行为差异显著。探测器在其中两个模型的多数退化响应中于起始点之前发出预警；低秩适配器在探测器之下将起始点前被标记的标记比例翻倍以上，主要通过在起始点前很久就标记退化响应实现；所有未经训练的基线预警频率均接近随机水平。我们已在https://huggingface.co/collections/marcodene/prewarn-degeneration-probes发布经校准阈值的探测器。

When Expert Disagreement Hurts: Prestige and Selectivity in LLM Decision Revision
专家分歧何时有害？大语言模型决策修订中的声望与选择性
When language models revise a decision after another agent disagrees, neither reversal counts nor final accuracy shows whether they are fixing mistakes or abandoning correct answers. We introduce a controlled two-pass audit that holds the question, the evidence, and the model's initial answer fixed and varies only the revision context: neutral reflection, anonymous disagreement, or disagreement attributed to a domain expert. For binary decisions the accounting is exact: revision raises accuracy only if it is selective enough, reversing wrong answers more than $a/(1-a)$ times as often as correct ones, where $a$ is initial accuracy (nine times at 90%). Across five models and 1,989 evidence-grounded decisions in medicine, science, and law, no model clears this bar under expert disagreement, and accuracy falls by up to 16.1 points. The expert label, a prestige cue, adds reversals for four of the five models, yet its effect on accuracy ranges from a 5.2-point loss to a 2.4-point gain, so reversal counts are a poor proxy for harm. With identical arguments from both sources, the label still raises reversal for three proprietary models and in a pooled analysis of four newer models. Models also respond to stated source reliability. Revision pipelines should therefore be evaluated with paired, conditional metrics, per model and protocol.
中文翻译
当语言模型在另一代理不同意后修正其决策时，反转次数和最终准确率无法说明它们是在纠正错误还是放弃正确答案。我们引入一种受控的双阶段审计，固定问题、证据和模型初始答案，仅改变修订上下文：中立反思、匿名分歧或归因于领域专家的分歧。对于二元决策，计算是精确的：只有当修订足够具有选择性时，准确率才会提升，即反转错误答案的频率需超过正确答案的a/(1-a)倍，其中a为初始准确率（在90%准确率时为九倍）。在医学、科学和法律领域的五种模型共1,989个基于证据的决策中，没有任何模型在专家分歧下达到此标准，准确率最高下降16.1个百分点。专家标签作为一种声望线索，使四种模型增加了反转次数，但对准确率的影响从5.2个百分点的损失到2.4个百分点的增益不等，因此反转次数是损害的不良代理。即使双方论据相同，标签仍使三种专有模型和四款较新模型的合并分析中增加反转。模型也对声明的来源可靠性作出反应。因此，修订流程应使用成对的条件指标，按模型和协议分别评估。

Lost in Delegation: Studying the Effectiveness of Task Orchestration in Generative Search
委托中的迷失：研究生成式搜索中任务编排的有效性
LLMs have quickly evolved from models used in isolation to agentic systems that delegate tasks to external tools and other LLMs to solve complex assignments. This agentic augmentation has led to impressive capability gains. However, when and how delegation improves performance remains poorly understood. For example, it is unclear whether delegation helps when tasks involve post-cutoff information that the orchestrating LLM cannot reliably resolve from its own knowledge. In this work, we use purpose-built datasets with pre- and post-cutoff splits to compare various delegation modes. We find that delegation provides no consistent accuracy improvement over non-delegated setups, with effects varying across models and tasks. When tasks involve knowledge unavailable to the orchestrator, delegation can substantially degrade accuracy, while direct tool use with the same LLM performs much better. Our analysis identifies distorted delegated queries, inefficient tool use, and failures to preserve and use information between agents as recurring failure modes. These findings suggest that effective orchestration requires both well-calibrated delegation decisions and effective exchange of information between agents.
中文翻译
大语言模型已从独立使用的模型演变为能够将任务委派给外部工具或其他大语言模型以解决复杂任务的智能体系统。这种智能体增强带来了显著的能力提升。然而，委托何时以及如何提升性能仍不明确。例如，当任务涉及委托模型无法可靠从自身知识中解决的截止后信息时，委托是否有效尚不清楚。在本研究中，我们使用专门构建的、包含截止前和截止后划分的数据集，比较了多种委托模式。结果发现，与非委托设置相比，委托并未带来一致的准确率提升，其效果在不同模型和任务间差异显著。当任务涉及委托者无法获取的知识时，委托会显著降低准确率，而使用相同大语言模型直接调用工具的表现则好得多。我们的分析识别出扭曲的委托查询、低效的工具使用以及代理间信息保存与利用失败是反复出现的失败模式。这些发现表明，有效的编排需要精确校准的委托决策以及代理间高效的信息交换。
👤 Tamara Stojanovska, Elisabeth Kirsten, Leon Swazinna, Muhammad Bilal Zafar 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

How Should Planning Agents ReSHAPE Their Search Space?
规划智能体应如何重塑其搜索空间？
Planning agents and benchmarks treat a user's initial specification as fixed. Yet the requests users write exclude plans they would gladly take, since they rarely discover the possible trade-offs they would accept until seeing the options. Agents already revise requests through interaction, but whether this helps planning is unknown. We formalize this step as the ReSHAPE task, which judges a revision by whether its search space matches the acceptable plans and a planner finds one there. Using ReSHAPE Planter, which builds instances from planning benchmarks with a human-validated simulated user, we analyze 5,800 agent-revised specifications in travel and shopping. We find that reshaping matters: one correct trade-off makes the same planner reach an acceptable plan up to 4.6x as often, and search space quality predicts what the planner finds. Current agents, however, admit at most 32% of the acceptable plans, where that trade-off alone admits over 52%, because they ask about preferences instead of showing plans; in travel, showing plans raises F1 by 9.7 points over asking, with two model families. Planning agents should thus treat a request as a hypothesis to test against the user's reactions to concrete plans to recognize user trade-offs.
中文翻译
规划智能体及其基准测试通常将用户的初始需求视为固定不变。然而，用户撰写的请求往往排除了他们实际上愿意接受的计划，因为他们在看到选项之前很少意识到自己可接受的权衡。智能体虽可通过交互修订请求，但这种修订是否有助于规划仍未知。我们将这一过程形式化为ReSHAPE任务，该任务通过判断修订后的搜索空间是否包含可接受的计划，并且规划器能否从中找到一个可接受的计划来评估修订效果。我们使用ReSHAPE Planter——一个基于人类验证的模拟用户从规划基准构建实例的工具——分析了旅行和购物场景中5800个由智能体修订的需求。研究发现，重塑搜索空间至关重要：一个正确的权衡可使同一规划器找到可接受计划的概率提高最多4.6倍，且搜索空间质量能有效预测规划器的结果。然而，当前智能体仅能接纳最多32%的可接受计划，而单一权衡本身即可容纳超过52%的可接受计划，原因是它们倾向于询问偏好而非展示计划；在旅行场景中，展示计划比提问使F1得分提高9.7分，且在两个模型族中均成立。因此，规划智能体应将请求视为待验证的假设，通过用户对具体计划的反应来识别其真实权衡。

RMIT-ADM+S at TREC RAG 2026: Research Agents, Retriever Ablations, and Open-Weight Replication
RMIT-ADM+S 参与 TREC RAG 2026：研究智能体、检索消融实验与开放权重复现
We describe the RMIT-ADM+S team's participation in the TREC 2026 Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) track using the ClimbMix corpus. We submitted seven research-agent runs using proprietary OpenAI and open-weight models, exploring different retrieval strategies, writer models, and agent workflows. Pre-submission ablations identified writer selection and adjacent-chunk expansion as the main factors affecting answer quality. In the official NIST evaluation, six of seven runs exceeded the track median across all three rubric/nugget collections. Our best-performing run, brief-hybrid, achieved the highest all-item coverage among our submissions and tied the track maximum (0.892) for Batch-1 vital coverage. Human side-by-side preferences differed across evaluation batches: the open-weight owa-glm5 led Batch 1 despite lower automated coverage, while sol-research-first led Batch 2. These findings highlight differences between automated coverage measures and human preferences, as well as the potential of lower-cost open-weight models for research-agent systems.

Eliciting and Mitigating Specification Satisficing in Coding Agents
诱发与缓解编码代理中的规范满足现象
Coding agents often fail to meet all requirements in the task description. In some of these failures, the agent considers an implementation choice that meets a requirement, but later decides to commit to another one that does not meet the requirement for a reason it finds acceptable. We call this specification satisficing. It is challenging to identify and mitigate specification satisficing because the agent accepts such choices for its own reasons, and existing self-correction methods are biased toward them. We propose SatisFix, which guides a coding agent to report these choices and their reasons, and then check each choice against the task description. We evaluate SatisFix on DeepSWE and SWE-bench Pro with five LLMs as backbones and two different agent frameworks, and compare it with three self-correction methods. SatisFix improves the pass@1 of the base agent by an average of 5.09% on DeepSWE and 7.06% on the hard subset of SWE-bench Pro. On DeepSWE, it outperforms the best baseline by 3.98%. Our ablation study shows that both steps of SatisFix contribute to the improvement.
中文翻译
编码代理常常无法满足任务描述中的所有要求。在部分失败中，代理会考虑一种满足要求的实现选择，但随后因自身认为可接受的原因而改选另一个不满足要求的方案。我们称这种现象为‘规范满足’。由于代理基于自身理由接受此类选择，且现有自我修正方法对此类情况存在偏见，因此识别和缓解规范满足极具挑战性。我们提出SatisFix，引导编码代理报告这些选择及其理由，并逐一检查每项选择是否符合任务描述。我们在DeepSWE和SWE-bench Pro上评估SatisFix，采用五种LLM作为骨干模型和两种不同的代理框架，并与三种自我修正方法进行对比。SatisFix在DeepSWE上平均提升基础代理的pass@1达5.09%，在SWE-bench Pro的困难子集上提升7.06%。在DeepSWE上，其表现优于最佳基线3.98%。消融实验证明，SatisFix的两个步骤均对性能提升有贡献。

JudgeMix: Judge-Guided Mixed-Rollout Data Synthesis for LLM Agents
JudgeMix：基于裁判指导的混合回溯数据合成用于大语言模型代理
Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on expert trajectories is a standard paradigm for training LLM agents. However, it suffers from a well-known training-inference distribution mismatch, calling for target-aware data synthesis strategies that ground the training data in the target model's native state distribution. To bridge this gap, emerging approaches incorporate the expert either to guide mixed rollouts alongside the target model, or to provide dense step-wise supervision. Nonetheless, existing methods apply expert assistance blind to the target model's inherent behavior: they either disrupt the rollout using sub-optimal random intervention heuristics, or they enforce indiscriminate uniform supervision that unnecessarily overwrites the model's already-correct actions. We introduce JudgeMix, a judge-guided data synthesis framework that dynamically interleaves the target model and expert based on step-wise semantic evaluations. JudgeMix queries expert guidance precisely when the target model struggles, ensuring high trajectory success rates while keeping the state distribution aligned with the target model. It additionally uses the judge evaluation to reweight expert supervision on different steps, preserving model's inherent valid behavior while correcting its errors. On two agentic tool-use benchmarks, AppWorld and AutomationBench, JudgeMix demonstrates consistent superior performance to existing methods, with improvements up to 10 pass@1 points.
中文翻译
在专家轨迹上的监督微调是训练大语言模型代理的标准范式。然而，该方法存在众所周知的训练-推理分布不匹配问题，亟需目标感知的数据合成策略，使训练数据扎根于目标模型的原生状态分布。为弥合这一差距，新兴方法将专家用于引导与目标模型并行的混合回溯，或提供密集的逐步监督。但现有方法在应用专家协助时忽视了目标模型的内在行为：要么使用次优的随机干预启发式方法破坏回溯过程，要么施加无差别均匀监督，不必要的覆盖模型已正确的动作。我们提出JudgeMix，一种基于裁判指导的数据合成框架，根据逐步语义评估动态交错目标模型与专家。JudgeMix仅在目标模型遇到困难时查询专家指导，确保高轨迹成功率的同时保持状态分布与目标模型一致。此外，它利用裁判评估对不同步骤的专家监督进行重加权，在保留模型固有正确行为的同时纠正错误。在两个代理工具使用基准测试AppWorld和AutomationBench上，JudgeMix展现出持续优于现有方法的表现，pass@1提升最高达10分。
👤 Cheng-Yu Hsieh, Ting-Yao Hu, Sanjoy Chowdhury, Oncel Tuzel, Raviteja Vemulapalli 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Scaling public input on AI policy with pluralistic agents
通过多元代理扩大公众对AI政策的参与度
AI is being deployed across a vast range of application domains, cultural environments, and human stakeholders. Safe and responsible AI development requires continuously accounting for diverse perspectives. We develop and test pluralistic agents, AI agent representatives that deliberate on behalf of individuals to align AI systems with diverse human perspectives. Pluralistic agents could help people contribute to many more discussions than they could directly participate in, on technical topics that would require training, and on policy choices that rely on private information that corporations and governments cannot readily share. Our experiments identify opportunities, risks, and perceptions of pluralistic agents. Agents were able to reliably shift policies in the direction of their represented persona's preferences, but the extent of each agent's influence was sensitive to the instructions from its persona, such as having more influence when instructed to be more verbose or less cooperative. This suggests that influence in AI-assisted deliberation could vary based on how participants prompt their agents and how the system is designed. In a preregistered study, 746 humans participated in the pluralistic agents process. Participants viewed pluralistic agents as useful and fair, preferring agents over the baseline of providing input to a single AI reasoning model. Most participants preferred participating themselves over either pluralistic agents or the baseline, but pluralistic agents recovered 68% of the baseline-direct gap in reported satisfaction and 82% in fairness. Because pluralistic agents can be used in many real-world contexts where direct discussion is not, they could be a promising tool to scale AI safety policy development. We encourage cautious exploration of this method, playing to its strengths as an amplifier of human input rather than a replacement.

DecArena: Evaluating Large-Scale Decentralized Multi-Agent Collaboration
DecArena：评估大规模去中心化多智能体协作
Emerging personal AI agents raise fundamental scalability and privacy concerns for centralized multi-agent systems (MASs), which rely on a global orchestrator with broad access to agents, resources, and communication. In decentralized environments, global visibility is unavailable. Agents need to proactively discover non-local resources (e.g. tools, skills), and collaborate with other agents to solve tasks. We study decentralized multi-agent collaboration under graph-constrained communication, a natural reflection of human structures. Each agent observes only its local state and can interact only with neighboring agents. We introduce a benchmark with synthetic scalable distributed tool calling with 100K+ tools. Our experiments reveal that recent LLM agents struggle to discover non-local relevant resources and collaborate in decentralized setting. Moreover, standard reinforcement learning fails to reliably overcome this limitation, suggesting that decentralized MAS needs more explicit harness mechanisms for effective collaboration, even using SOTA LLMs. We then present a strong baseline \textbf{GraphHarness}, combining topology-aware warmup with self-improving LLM-based routing. Results show that GraphHarness substantially improves task success (+48.5) and produces higher quality collaboration (+30.6), while using fewer costs (+34.0) than baselines.
中文翻译
新兴的个人AI智能体引发了对集中式多智能体系统（MAS）在可扩展性和隐私方面的根本担忧，这类系统依赖于拥有广泛访问权限的全局协调者。在去中心化环境中，全局可见性不可用。智能体需要主动发现非本地资源（如工具、技能），并与其它智能体协作解决问题。我们研究了在图结构约束通信下的去中心化多智能体协作，这自然反映了人类组织结构。每个智能体仅观察局部状态，且只能与邻近智能体交互。我们引入了一个基准测试，包含超过10万种合成可扩展分布式工具调用。实验表明，近期LLM智能体难以在去中心化环境中发现非本地相关资源并有效协作。此外，标准强化学习无法可靠克服这一局限，表明即使使用最先进的LLM，去中心化MAS也需要更明确的协同机制。随后我们提出强基线方法 extbf{GraphHarness}，结合拓扑感知预热与基于LLM的自改进路由。结果显示，GraphHarness显著提升了任务成功率（+48.5），协作质量更高（+30.6），同时成本更低（+34.0）。

One Fabrication Is Enough: A Fake News Article Changes the Forecasts of LLM Forecasting Systems
一篇伪造就足够：一则假新闻文章改变大语言模型预测系统的预测结果
LLMs are increasingly used for forecasting, and the best forecasting systems retrieve news as evidence to strengthen their predictions. This also opens them to attack. We study the weakest attacker of this kind, who knows only the forecasting question and publishes fabricated news articles, with no access to the model, the retriever or its queries. We evaluate how far this attack reaches on stock questions and real prediction-market questions. Once the article is in the model's context, it reverses 74% to 82% of the forecasts that opposed it, on every frontier model we tested. The model cannot verify the fabrication and follows it anyway. Retrieval is the attack's entry point, and the systems built to retrieve well are the most exposed: the architectures of two leading deployed forecasting systems, whose agents search for every related article, retrieve the fabricated one in about 90% of runs. Accuracy cannot reveal this harm: the attacked forecaster is right when the attacker's direction turns out right and wrong otherwise, so its average accuracy barely changes while its forecasts follow the attacker's guess. We also propose a second attacker, who publishes two articles that argue opposite directions to make the forecaster unsure. The model does not hedge; it returns a confident forecast in a direction the attacker no longer controls. We then evaluate the two strongest published retrieval-time defenses, passage filtering and partitioned retrieval. Each stops the one-article attack but is bypassed by a one-prompt change to the attack. Together they stop both bypasses at four articles, but sixteen distorted copies beat them, and they change 29% of clean decisions and discard 39% of the genuine news. Partitioned retrieval also requires control of the retrieval index. Systems that search the open web have no such control and are left with the filter alone. For them, the attack still works.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLM）越来越多地用于预测任务，最佳预测系统通过检索新闻作为证据来增强其预测。但这也使其面临攻击风险。我们研究了此类中最弱的攻击者：仅知晓预测问题，发布伪造新闻文章，但无模型、检索器或其查询的访问权限。我们评估该攻击在股票问题和真实预测市场问题上的影响范围。一旦文章进入模型上下文，它就会逆转我们测试的所有前沿模型中74%至82%的反对其立场的预测。模型无法验证伪造内容，但仍遵循其内容。检索是攻击入口，那些为良好检索而构建的系统最易受攻击：两个领先部署的预测系统，其代理会搜索每一篇相关文章，约在90%的运行中检索到伪造文章。准确性无法揭示这种损害：当攻击者的方向最终正确时，被攻击的预测者正确；否则错误，因此其平均准确率几乎不变，但预测却跟随攻击者的猜测。我们还提出第二种攻击者，发布两篇方向相反的文章，使预测者产生不确定性。模型不会采取保守策略；它仍返回一个自信的预测，方向已不再受攻击者控制。随后我们评估了两种最强的公开检索时间防御措施：段落过滤和分块检索。每种都能阻止单篇文章攻击，但可通过一次提示更改绕过。两者联合可阻止两种绕过方式，但需四篇文章才能实现；十六个扭曲副本即可突破，且会改变29%的正常决策，丢弃39%的真实新闻。分块检索还需控制检索索引。在开放网络上搜索的系统无此控制，只能依赖过滤器，因此攻击依然有效。
👤 Zeyu Zhang, Bradly C. Stadie 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15049]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Science Prediction Universe
科学预测宇宙
Humanity has long sought to explain natural phenomena. Reliable prediction of experimental outcomes is a central test of our understanding of the processes that produce them. If artificial general intelligence (AGI) is to surpass human scientific capabilities, it must also predict experimental outcomes more reliably than humans. Such capabilities could accelerate scientific discovery across disciplines, help address humanity's most pressing challenges, and improve human well-being. We introduce Science Prediction Universe (SPU), a benchmark of hidden experimental outcomes with 100 public verified tasks from chemistry, materials science, biology and medicine, and 16 private tasks from unpublished wet-lab experiments. Given an experimental setup and candidate conditions, an agent works with any resources it needs to rank the candidates by a measured outcome. We evaluate thirteen agent configurations spanning twelve frontier models across two harnesses (Codex and Claude Code). Current agents remain far from saturating wet-lab outcome prediction, with substantial performance disparities across models: on the public benchmark, configurations separate into two distinct tiers, with task-adjusted Spearman correlations ranging from 0.227 for the lowest-performing configuration to 0.478 for the best. Analysis of 1,155 scored public trajectories (227,103 tool calls) suggests that the bottleneck lies in scientific judgment rather than tool execution: agents rely on shared monotone priors, agreeing far more with one another than with experimental reality (0.52 vs. 0.33), and frequently compute trivial physical or chemical proxies that do not influence the final outcome.
中文翻译
人类长期致力于解释自然现象。对实验结果的可靠预测是我们理解其产生过程的核心检验标准。如果人工智能要超越人类的科学能力，就必须比人类更可靠地预测实验结果。这种能力可加速跨学科科学发现，帮助应对人类最紧迫的挑战，并提升人类福祉。我们提出科学预测宇宙（SPU），一个包含100个公开验证任务（来自化学、材料科学、生物学和医学）以及16个未发表湿实验任务的隐藏实验结果基准。给定实验设置和候选条件，智能体可使用任意资源对候选方案按测量结果进行排序。我们评估了十三种智能体配置，涵盖十二个前沿模型及两种工具链（Codex和Claude Code）。当前智能体在湿实验结果预测方面仍远未达到饱和，模型间性能差异显著：在公开基准上，配置分为两个明显层级，任务调整后的斯皮尔曼相关系数从最低配置的0.227到最佳配置的0.478不等。对1,155条评分公开轨迹（227,103次工具调用）的分析表明，瓶颈在于科学判断而非工具执行：智能体依赖共享的单调先验，彼此之间的一致性远高于与实验现实的一致性（0.52 vs. 0.33），且频繁计算不影响最终结果的平凡物理或化学代理。
👤 Zengji Tu, Xiang Liu, Jiarui Sun, Ruoyu Wu, Zhihui Qi et al. 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
