Aggregate Accuracy Hides Diagnosis-Specific Failure in Medical Vision-Language Models
聚合准确率掩盖了医学视觉-语言模型在诊断特定失败中的表现
Medical vision-language models (VLMs) are compared mainly by accuracy averaged across findings, a summary that can hide complete failure on a single diagnosis. We audit five open medical VLMs (MedGemma-4B, Lingshu-7B, HuatuoGPT-Vision-7B, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and MedVLM-R1) on 6,048 ChestX-ray14 questions balanced by sex, age, finding, and label. On the 432 pneumothorax-positive X-rays, HuatuoGPT-Vision and Qwen2.5-VL detect zero cases while MedGemma and Lingshu detect 38% and 67% of the same images. The two failing models answer "no" to all 864 pneumothorax cases (balanced accuracy 0.500, chance), yet produce fluent, anatomically specific explanations that name the signs of a collapsed lung and then deny them, with no hedge or abstention. This failure is not an artifact of prompting, image resolution, label ambiguity, or model scale. It persists across four prompt formats, at native resolution, and on single-label cases. A within-model control shows the same forced prompt gives HuatuoGPT 79% recall on nodule but only 6% on pneumothorax. It survives a tenfold scale increase (Qwen2.5-VL-72B detects 4%) and replicates on SIIM-ACR, an independent dataset with radiologist-drawn masks, where the failing models detect 0 of 432 confirmed pneumothoraces, including 0 of the 144 largest lesions. Reading the models' logits and internal states shows this is not blindness. The two failing models are highly confident yet no better than chance and a linear probe recovers pneumothorax from their hidden representations even where recall is zero, so the finding is represented internally but suppressed at the output. Aggregate accuracy therefore cannot establish that a medical VLM can see a given finding, because a model that misses one can still describe it convincingly. Reporting per-finding recall and specificity on balanced cases is needed to tell the difference.
中文翻译
医学视觉-语言模型（VLMs）主要通过各类发现的平均准确率进行比较，这一汇总指标可能掩盖单个诊断上的彻底失败。我们在6,048个ChestX-ray14问题上审计了五个开源医学VLM（MedGemma-4B、Lingshu-7B、HuatuoGPT-Vision-7B、Qwen2.5-VL-7B和MedVLM-R1），这些问题是按性别、年龄、发现类型和标签平衡的。在432张气胸阳性X光片中，HuatuoGPT-Vision和Qwen2.5-VL未能检测出任何病例，而MedGemma和Lingshu分别检测到38%和67%。这两个失败模型对全部864个气胸病例均回答“否”（平衡准确率为0.500，即随机水平），却生成流畅且解剖学具体的解释，提及塌陷肺的征象后又予以否认，毫无保留或回避。这种失败并非提示工程、图像分辨率、标签模糊性或模型规模所致。它在四种提示格式、原生分辨率及单标签案例中持续存在。模型内对照显示，相同的强制提示使HuatuoGPT在结节检测中召回率达79%，但在气胸检测中仅6%。即使扩大十倍规模（Qwen2.5-VL-72B检测到4%），该失败依然存在，并在SIIM-ACR独立数据集（放射科医生绘制掩码）上复现，失败模型在432例确诊气胸中检测为0，包括144个最大病灶也全漏检。分析模型logits与内部状态表明，这并非视觉盲点。两个失败模型高度自信，但表现仅相当于随机水平；线性探测器可在其隐藏表征中恢复气胸信号，即使召回率为零，说明该发现已在内部表示，但输出阶段被抑制。因此，聚合准确率无法证明医学VLM能够识别某一特定发现，因为一个漏检的模型仍可令人信服地描述该发现。报告平衡样本下的每诊断召回率与特异性才是区分关键。
👤 Shreya Ajay Kale, Angad Ripudaman Singh Bajwa 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv

Termination Lag: Coding Agents Solve Early but Stop Late
终止延迟：编码智能体提前解决但过晚停止
Coding agents can solve a task well before they stop working on it. We characterize \emph{termination lag}--the number of steps between an agent's first correct patch and the end of its run--across five models, two agent frameworks, and three SWE-bench datasets by recording and testing every distinct repository patch generated during execution. Our analysis reveals that 38--82% of successful runs achieve a correct solution on their initial source-code edit. However, agents continue to expend substantial effort after correct patches, with post-solution steps accounting for a median of 35--37% for Qwen3-Coder and 54--60% for Laguna-XS.2 in successful runs on SWE-bench Verified and Multilingual. Agents spend most of these additional steps verifying their work and exploring the repository. In a small fraction of cases (1--8%), this continued activity leads agents to undo their earlier correct patch, leaving the final solution incorrect.
中文翻译
编码智能体可在完成任务前很久就已解决该问题。我们通过记录并测试执行过程中生成的每一个不同代码仓库补丁，对五种模型、两种智能体框架及三个SWE-bench数据集中的‘终止延迟’——即智能体首次正确补丁与运行结束之间的步骤数——进行了刻画。分析显示，38–82%的成功运行在其首次源代码编辑时即已获得正确解。然而，智能体在正确补丁之后仍投入大量精力，其中后续步骤占成功运行的中位数达35–37%（Qwen3-Coder）和54–60%（Laguna-XS.2）在SWE-bench Verified和Multilingual数据集上。智能体大多将这些额外步骤用于验证工作和探索代码仓库。在极少数情况（1–8%）下，这种持续活动导致智能体撤销早期正确补丁，最终解变为错误。

Do People Trust AI Assistants Even When They Are Consistently Wrong?
当AI助手始终错误时，人们还会信任它们吗？
People are increasingly using conversational AI systems to make decisions despite concerns around their reliability. How well do people calibrate to AI systems' accuracy in multi-turn conversations? We measure how people's reliance on LLMs' advice changes across repeated interactions, varying whether the AI assistant is helpful (100% accurate), unhelpful (0% accurate), or mixed (50% accurate). Across 4 experiments, we find people learn to trust helpful models more than unhelpful ones (67% vs 21% of the time), but only when they receive external feedback on the models' accuracy. Without feedback, people continue to be persuaded 48% of the time by a consistently unhelpful model (vs 38% for helpful). Rather than gaining trust in helpful models, participant reliance on LLM advisors tended to be high in early trials and only fell with evidence of inaccuracy. Participants trusted LLM advisors more than they trusted human advisors (83% vs 65%, Experiment 4), and LLMs benefited more from having a helpful track record than humans did. In contrast with previous work, we find limited evidence of an advantage for arguing for the truth (versus a falsehood). Truth advantages emerged only when participants were already very confident in their position, and did not emerge at all for static hints. Collectively, the results suggest that people may be swayed by LLM advice, even when it is consistently false, unless they have access to timely ground-truth feedback. Unfortunately, in many domains such feedback may not be available, leading to overreliance on AI.
中文翻译
尽管对可靠性存在担忧，人们仍越来越多地使用对话式AI系统来做决策。人们在多轮对话中如何校准对LLM准确性的信任？我们测量了人们在重复交互中对LLM建议依赖程度的变化，分别考察AI助手是否有益（100%准确）、无益（0%准确）或混合（50%准确）。在四项实验中，我们发现人们能更好地信任有益模型（67% vs 21%），但前提是获得关于模型准确性的外部反馈。没有反馈时，人们仍会在48%的情况下被一个始终无益的模型说服（而有益模型为38%）。人们并非因模型表现好而建立信任，而是早期试验中对LLM顾问的依赖度较高，只有在出现不准确证据后才下降。参与者对LLM顾问的信任度高于对人类顾问（83% vs 65%，实验4），且LLM从良好记录中获益更多。与以往研究相反，我们未发现主张真相相比虚假有明显优势的证据。真理优势仅在参与者已非常自信时出现，且在静态提示条件下完全未显现。总体而言，结果表明人们可能被LLM建议影响，即使其内容一贯错误，除非他们能获得及时的真实反馈。然而，在许多领域这种反馈可能不可用，导致对AI的过度依赖。

When Is Proof-Step Verification Worth Its Cost? Operating Requirements of Certify-or-Decline Verification for Open-Weight Mathematical Reasoning
证明步骤验证是否值得其代价？开放权重数学推理中‘认证或拒绝’验证的操作要求
Proof-step verification rewrites a solver's answer into typed steps and uses LLM judges to certify or decline. We evaluate this pipeline on open-weight models across GPQA, AIME-2025, and a MATH-500 subset. Certification raises answer-key-match precision by 31 percentage points on the strong GPQA configuration and 46 on AIME, while answering only 35% and 21% of attempts. On near-ceiling MATH-500, gains are at most 5 points at a loss of 13-27 points of coverage. An output-token-matched self-consistency selector obtains 82.9% precision at 35% coverage, compared with the verifier's pooled 87.6% (paired difference: +4.8 points, 95% interval [-8.2, 18.5]). A diagnostic sample reveals a distinct failure mode: 52 of 88 completed problems end in invalid proof construction without ever reaching a judge. Finally, external-LLM acceptance of rendered certified chains is 61.0-67.6%, compared with 87.6% answer-key match. These audits lack human or formal-kernel ground truth. Open-weight auditor pairs show near-zero chance-corrected agreement, which measures consistency rather than accuracy against proof truth. These results motivate separate reporting of execution failures, selected-answer precision, and independently assessed proof validity. We provide per-configuration results and evaluation details in the supplement.

Rational Use of Time for Social Coordination in Humans and AI
人类与AI在社会协调中对时间的理性运用
All social interactions are situated in time. Previous studies have shown that humans are able to use implicit temporal cues, such as the duration of a pause, to infer others' mental states. But how do humans use temporal cues for dynamic, fine-grained social coordination? And can AI systems do the same, without ``experiencing'' continuous real-time as humans do? We study these questions using a cooperative card game task called The Mind, which requires players to achieve a shared goal across repeated interactions but forbids explicit communication: the only available signal is the passage of time. We evaluate 150 humans, and two classes of AI models which reflect different ways of operationalizing time in artificial systems: computer-use agents (CUAs), and standard text-prompted LLMs. We find that human behavior is best captured by a Bayesian strategy that infers a partner's belief from their timing---and similarly for frontier models. However, unlike humans, none of the tested models seem to efficiently adapt their behaviors to their partners' over the course of the game. From a cognitive perspective, our findings suggest that humans perform implicit probabilistic reasoning about continuous-time signals in dynamic social settings, and that such reasoning mechanisms may emerge without real-time experience. Practically, our study highlights new challenges for AI systems to coordinate efficiently with other agents by learning to interpret and adapt to their implicit signals in dynamic settings.
中文翻译
所有社会互动都发生在时间之中。先前研究表明，人类能够利用隐含的时间线索（如停顿持续时间）推断他人的心理状态。但人类如何利用时间线索实现动态、精细的社会协调？AI系统能否做到这一点，而无需像人类一样‘经历’连续实时？我们通过一种名为《心灵》的合作卡牌游戏任务来研究这些问题，该任务要求玩家在重复互动中达成共同目标，但禁止显式沟通：唯一可用信号是时间的流逝。我们评估了150名人类，以及两类反映人工智能系统中时间操作方式不同的AI模型：计算机使用代理（CUAs）和标准文本提示LLMs。我们发现人类行为最符合基于贝叶斯策略的推理——从对方的时机推断其信念——前沿模型也表现出类似行为。然而，与人类不同，所有测试模型似乎都无法在游戏过程中有效适应其伙伴的行为。从认知角度看，我们的发现表明人类在动态社会环境中对连续时间信号进行隐含的概率推理，而这种推理机制可能无需真实时间体验即可涌现。从实践角度，本研究突出了AI系统在动态环境中学习解读并适应其他智能体隐含信号以实现高效协调的新挑战。

Trait Hitchhiking in LLM Post-Training
大语言模型后训练中的特质搭便车现象
Reinforcement learning (RL) is now a standard stage of language model post-training, and the compute devoted to it is growing rapidly. While it has been shown to improve capabilities, particularly for tasks with verifiable rewards, it can also change behaviours and internal properties that the reward does not explicitly target. We present a formalism to describe the evolution of "traits" during RL post-training and, in doing so, recover the Price equation from evolutionary biology. We use this to develop a mathematical model for what we call "trait hitchhiking": the amplification or suppression of non-targeted traits as training redistributes probability across completions. We evaluate this Price update expression on language models trained with GRPO and show that the Price estimates closely track the sampled trait dynamics. These results identify trait hitchhiking as a mechanism by which RL post-training can silently amplify undesirable traits.

强化学习（RL）如今已成为语言模型后训练的标准阶段，投入的计算资源迅速增长。尽管已有研究表明其能提升模型能力，特别是在具有可验证奖励的任务中，但它也可能改变那些未被奖励明确目标所指向的行为和内部属性。我们提出了一个形式化框架来描述RL后训练过程中‘特质’的演化，并在此过程中重新推导出进化生物学中的Price方程。基于此，我们建立了一个数学模型，描述我们称之为‘特质搭便车’的现象：在训练过程中，概率在不同完成结果间重新分配时，非目标特质被放大或抑制。我们在使用GRPO训练的语言模型上评估了这一Price更新表达式，发现Price估计值与采样到的特质动态高度吻合。这些结果揭示了‘特质搭便车’是RL后训练无声放大不良特质的一种机制。

Cleaning an Agent's History Hurts When the World Still Holds the Error
清理智能体的历史记录在世界仍保留错误时反而有害
Tool-using language-model agents accumulate histories of their own mistakes, and a system that compacts, repairs or hands off such a history decides what it says about those mistakes while their effects stay in the world. Prior work cleans the history at a fixed world, where showing a corrected step and saying it is done move together. We ask whether the same correct content helps or hurts depending on how the history states it and what the world holds. We freeze an agent mid-task with its exact world state, edit the history or the world, and let a receiver model continue. On AgentCE-Bench, after the agent commits a wrong value, we hold the world fixed and add the correct value by two natural edits: a truthful note beside the preserved error or the agent's own completed action. The note raises recovery, the share of remaining targets the receiver gets right, from 51% under a matched neutral note to 66%. The completed action lowers it below the unedited history, from 50% to 44%, so the value's presence alone does not predict the effect. On AppWorld, after a database write, we vary whether its receipt reports success or failure and whether its effect is present. A success receipt lowers whole-task success from 45% to 28% while the effect is absent and raises it from 38% to 49% once present. The two effects differ by 27 points pooled over two receivers, a difference positive in six of eight receivers at 97.5% and seven at 95%. A history edit's effect thus depends on the world it describes, and a history made to look clean while the world still holds the error can leave an agent worse off than no edit at all.
中文翻译
使用工具的语言模型智能体积累自身错误的历史记录，而一个对历史进行压缩、修复或移交的系统决定了其如何表述这些错误，但这些错误的影响仍在现实中存在。以往工作在固定的世界状态下清理历史，即展示修正步骤并声称已完成同步进行。我们探讨了相同正确内容在不同历史表述方式以及世界状态不同时，是帮助还是损害恢复效果。我们在智能体任务中途冻结其精确世界状态，编辑历史或世界，然后让接收模型继续执行。在AgentCE-Bench上，智能体犯错后，我们保持世界不变，通过两种自然编辑添加正确值：在保留错误旁添加真实注释，或由智能体自身完成动作。注释使恢复率从匹配中性注释下的51%提升至66%；而完成动作则使其降至44%，低于未编辑历史的50%，说明仅存在正确值本身并不能预测其影响。在AppWorld中，数据库写入后，我们改变其接收是否报告成功或失败，以及其效果是否存在。成功报告在效果不存在时将整体任务成功率从45%降至28%，而在效果存在时则升至49%。两种效应在两个接收者中合计相差27个百分点，其中六个接收者在97.5%置信水平下为正，七个在95%水平下为正。因此，历史编辑的效果取决于其所描述的世界状态；当历史看似已清理但世界仍保留错误时，智能体可能比完全不编辑更糟糕。

Evaluating the Performance of LLMs in Drafting NIH Data Management Plans
评估大型语言模型在撰写NIH数据管理计划方面的表现
Sharing research data is essential to promote transparency, enable reproducibility, and fuel scientific discoveries. To ensure consistent sharing, the National Institutes of Health (NIH) and other funding agencies require applicants to submit data management plans (DMPs) with their proposals. They aim to encourage researchers to consider their data management and sharing strategies early on. However, researchers often face challenges when developing DMPs due to a lack of knowledge about data-sharing practices. Here, we evaluated the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) in drafting DMPs compliant with the 2023 NIH guidelines. The goal was to investigate whether LLM-supported DMP creation could reduce the burden on researchers and librarians. We compared Llama and GPT performance against NIH-provided DMP samples. We also asked domain experts to evaluate LLM-generated DMPs. Overall, we found that LLMs can accelerate the creation of initial drafts but still require expert review before submission to ensure clarity and completeness. Moreover, open-source LLMs may not be as ready off-the-shelf as commercial ones for drafting funder-compliant DMPs.

共享研究数据对于促进透明性、实现可重复性以及推动科学发现至关重要。为确保一致共享，美国国立卫生研究院（NIH）及其他资助机构要求申请人在提案中提交数据管理计划（DMP）。其目的在于鼓励研究人员尽早考虑数据管理与共享策略。然而，由于缺乏对数据共享实践的知识，研究人员在制定DMP时常面临挑战。本文评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）在撰写符合2023年NIH指南的DMP方面的表现。目标是探究LLM辅助DMP创建能否减轻研究人员和图书馆员的负担。我们将Llama和GPT的表现与NIH提供的DMP样本进行比较，并请领域专家评估LLM生成的DMP。总体而言，我们发现LLMs能够加速初始草稿的生成，但仍需专家审查后方可提交，以确保清晰性和完整性。此外，开源LLMs在起草符合资助方要求的DMP方面，可能不如商业模型即开即用。

Goal Encoding in LLMs
大语言模型中的目标编码
We investigate whether and how LLMs encode goals: internal neural representations that precede and guide action, and that are flexible enough to be realized through many different execution paths. We construct a coding setting with multiple sub-tasks that allows us to evaluate goal-seeking behavior and the selection of sub-goals. In this setting, we localize an encoding of the next sub-goal in the outputs of a sparse set of attention heads. The same heads can be used to decode the LM's future actions in natural language (e.g., "I'm checking if a word is a palindrome"). We verify that this encoding is causal for next action and is plastic: transplanting it into a different environment, e.g., another programming language, triggers the same operation realized with a different sequence of tokens. The LM also sustains the encoding throughout the action's execution, which is puzzling because a transformer LM without recurrence cannot carry a state forward for more tokens than it has layers left. We propose that it does not need to. Under a non-recurrent goal-seeking (NRGS) account, the LM frequently re-reads the goal from the task specification, compares it with the progress visible in the growing context, and pursues what remains. We test the predictions of this account with targeted causal interventions.
中文翻译
我们研究大语言模型是否以及如何编码目标：即在行动之前存在且引导行为的内部神经表征，其具备足够的灵活性以通过多种不同执行路径实现。我们构建了一个包含多个子任务的编码场景，以评估目标导向行为及子目标选择能力。在此设定下，我们定位到稀疏注意力头输出中存在下一个子目标的编码。相同注意力头可用于将模型未来动作以自然语言解码（例如，“我正在检查一个词是否是回文”）。我们验证了该编码对下一步动作具有因果作用，并具备可塑性：将其移植到不同环境（如另一种编程语言）时，仍会触发相同操作，只是通过不同的词元序列实现。此外，模型在整个动作执行过程中持续维持该编码，这令人困惑，因为无循环结构的 Transformer 模型无法将状态传递超过其层数。我们提出非递归目标寻求（NRGS）解释：模型频繁从任务说明中重新读取目标，与不断增长的上下文中的进展进行比对，并继续推进剩余任务。我们通过针对性的因果干预测试了该理论的预测。

Discovering Effective Agent Memory Designs through Program Evolution
通过程序演化发现有效的智能体记忆设计
Program evolution provides a promising approach to discover agent memory designs and study the choices underlying their effectiveness. We introduce MAPE, a framework that evolves whole memory programs from minimal seeds through a standardized lifecycle interface and an iterative diagnosis-proposal loop. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval yield an absolute accuracy gain of up to 12% over manually designed baselines at 35% of the serving cost, with further evaluations supporting transfer across serving backbones and benchmarks. Empirical analyses and ablations identify and validate two recurring design patterns: retaining raw conversations as retrievable evidence preserves details omitted from extracted memories, while composing retrieved evidence according to query intent helps the answering model use it effectively. Transplanting the composition module into five existing memory systems improves accuracy across all five. Beyond these patterns, the search objective shapes where computation goes: optimizing serving cost alongside accuracy shifts computation from LLM-based memory construction to selective processing at query time while retaining competitive accuracy. Together, these findings offer empirical lessons for agent memory design. Code is available at https://github.com/ash-ding/MAPE.
中文翻译
程序演化为发现智能体记忆设计并研究其有效性背后的决策提供了有前景的方法。我们提出MAPE框架，通过标准化生命周期接口和迭代诊断-提案循环，从极简种子演化出完整的记忆程序。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上的实验表明，相比人工设计基线，MAPE在仅35%服务成本下实现了最高达12%的绝对准确率提升，并进一步验证了其在不同服务后端和基准间的迁移能力。实证分析与消融研究识别并验证了两种反复出现的设计模式：保留原始对话作为可检索证据，可保存从提取记忆中遗漏的细节；根据查询意图组合检索到的证据，有助于回答模型有效利用信息。将该组合模块移植到五个现有记忆系统中，均提升了准确率。此外，搜索目标决定了计算分配方式：在优化服务成本的同时兼顾准确率，促使计算从基于大语言模型的记忆构建转向查询时的选择性处理，同时保持竞争力。这些发现为智能体记忆设计提供了实证启示。代码已公开于https://github.com/ash-ding/MAPE。
👤 Xinheng Ding, Seyyed Saeid Cheshmi, Ziyang Zhang, Tairan Chen, Wenya Xie et al. 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Similar but Not Redundant: Embedding-Gated Refinement for High-Granularity Topic Models
相似但不冗余：基于嵌入门控精炼的高粒度主题模型
High-granularity topic modelling extracts hundreds of niche topics from a corpus, yet this regime is rarely studied and is more commonly framed as a failure mode: "over-clustering," in which coherent themes fragment into redundant subtopics. We argue that this framing conflates genuine redundancy with topics that are semantically similar but meaningfully distinct, and that the central challenge in high-granularity modelling is separating the two. Recent post-hoc refinement methods address topic redundancy through either embedding-based similarity thresholding or LLM-guided merging. We show that neither suffices in high-granularity settings and propose embedding-gated iterative merge (EGIM), a hybrid approach that iteratively generates context-rich topic representations, uses embedding similarity to propose candidate merges across the topic space, then applies LLM judgement to accept only genuinely redundant pairs while preserving niche distinctions. Across two patent corpora and one legal corpus, LLM judgement rejects 83% of embedding-identified candidates as non-redundant. EGIM improves discriminability on merged topics by 25-55% over a prior iterative LLM baseline while better preserving the original topic distribution.
中文翻译
高粒度主题建模从语料库中提取数百个细分主题，但该范式很少被研究，常被视为一种失败模式——‘过度聚类’，即连贯的主题被拆分为冗余的子主题。我们认为这种表述混淆了真正的冗余与语义相似但意义不同的主题，而高粒度建模的核心挑战在于区分二者。现有事后精炼方法通过基于嵌入的相似性阈值或LLM引导合并来处理主题冗余。我们发现这两种方法在高粒度场景下均不充分，因此提出嵌入门控迭代合并（EGIM）——一种混合方法：迭代生成上下文丰富的主题表示，利用嵌入相似性在主题空间中提出候选合并对，再由LLM判断仅接受真正冗余的配对，同时保留细微差异。在两个专利语料库和一个法律语料库上，LLM判断拒绝了83%由嵌入识别出的候选对，认为其并非冗余。相较于先前的迭代LLM基线，EGIM在合并后主题的可区分性上提升了25%-55%，同时更好地保留了原始主题分布。
👤 Daniel Denley, Robert L. Nicol, Mike J. Chantler 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Pretraining Data Statistics Shape the Phases of Learning Entity Comparison in Language Models
预训练数据统计特征塑造语言模型学习实体比较的阶段
How does data shape language model (LM) behavior throughout pretraining? We investigate this question through a case study on entity comparison, e.g., Between France and Brazil, which country is larger?, across nine comparison tasks spanning five entity types. We begin with controlled experiments in which we train small LMs (163M parameters) on mixtures of natural text from pretraining corpora and synthetic task data. Our analysis identifies three distinct phases of learning: (1) an early phase where the LM selects entities by frequency, (2) a middle phase where the LM selects entities by position in the prompt (first vs. last mentioned entity), and (3) a late phase where the LM selects the entity that is the correct answer. We show that the emergence of these three phases is causally determined by statistical properties of the training data. With small amounts of task-specific synthetic data, we observe only the first two phases and the model fails to learn the task; with large amounts, the model jumps directly from the frequency-based heuristic to solving the task correctly. Moreover, changing the entity frequency distribution from a naturally occurring Zipfian distribution (a small number of entities are very common and the vast majority are rare) to a uniform one weakens the frequency phase and speeds up learning of the task. Lastly, we show that the three phases of training dynamics from our controlled experiments appear in the open source model OLMo-2 32B. Together, our findings demonstrate that properties of pretraining data are causal drivers of heuristic learning and show that small-scale synthetic experiments can predict training dynamics at larger scales.
中文翻译
预训练数据如何影响语言模型（LM）在整个预训练过程中的行为？我们通过一项关于实体比较的任务案例研究来探讨这一问题，例如“在法国和巴西之间，哪个国家更大？”涵盖九个跨五类实体的比较任务。我们首先在小规模语言模型（163M参数）上进行受控实验，训练其在预训练语料库的自然文本与合成任务数据混合数据集上。分析揭示出三个明显的学习阶段：（1）早期阶段，模型根据实体出现频率选择；（2）中期阶段，模型根据提示中实体的位置（首次提及 vs. 最后提及）进行选择；（3）晚期阶段，模型开始正确选择答案实体。我们证明这三个阶段的出现是由训练数据的统计特性所决定的因果关系。当使用少量特定任务的合成数据时，模型仅表现出前两个阶段，无法学会任务；而当使用大量合成数据时，模型直接从基于频率的启发式跳到正确解题。此外，将实体频率分布从自然存在的齐普夫分布（少数实体非常常见，绝大多数罕见）改为均匀分布，会削弱频率阶段并加速任务学习。最后，我们发现这些受控实验中观察到的三个训练动态阶段也出现在开源模型OLMo-2 32B中。我们的研究结果表明，预训练数据的特性是启发式学习的因果驱动因素，并证明小规模合成实验可预测大规模训练动态。
👤 Yik Siu Chan, Jing Huang, Yanai Elazar, Atticus Geiger 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

MuSeToM: Multi-Sensory Evaluation of Belief Revision with Textual Cues Across Six Senses
MuSeToM：跨六种感官的文本线索下信念修正的多感官评估
Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer mental states in others, is pivotal for human social cognition. Existing evaluations of ToM in large language models (LLMs) mainly focus on static belief attribution and false-belief reasoning, leaving a critical aspect of social reasoning underexplored: can LLMs decide whether perceptual evidence is sufficient to justify another person's belief revision? To address this gap, we present MuSeToM, a human-validated, text-based benchmark for evaluating epistemic belief revision in ToM reasoning. MuSeToM contains 2,415 instances adapted from BigToM and OpenToM, with textual cues spanning six sensory categories, and tests whether models can determine when a protagonist should revise a belief and infer the resulting updated belief state. Using MuSeToM, we systematically evaluate state-of-the-art LLMs under direct and chain-of-thought prompting, revealing a pronounced dissonance: while models often detect that a belief may change, they struggle to identify the updated belief and frequently over-update on perceptually salient but epistemically insufficient cues.

心智理论（ToM）是理解他人心理状态的能力，对人类社会认知至关重要。现有针对大语言模型（LLMs）的ToM评估主要集中在静态信念归属和错误信念推理，忽视了一个关键的社会推理方面：LLMs能否判断感知证据是否足以支持他人信念的修正？为填补这一空白，我们提出了MuSeToM，一个经过人工验证的、基于文本的基准测试，用于评估ToM推理中的认识论信念修正。MuSeToM包含2,415个实例，源自BigToM和OpenToM，涵盖六种感官类别的文本线索，测试模型能否判断主角应在何时修正信念并推断出更新后的信念状态。利用MuSeToM，我们系统评估了最先进的LLMs在直接提示和思维链提示下的表现，揭示出显著的不一致：尽管模型常能察觉信念可能发生改变，但在识别更新后的信念方面表现不佳，且经常因感知显著但认识论上不足的线索而过度修正。

Where in the Input Does a One-Pass Decision Encoder Look?
单次决策编码器在输入中的何处进行查看？
Laya is a small model that answers a multiple-choice question about a text in one pass: the question, the options (each with a name and a description) and the text form one sequence of at most 512 tokens, the window, in the English release. A longer text is silently cut at the end, and we measure what that costs. Test documents place a held-out row's text, the evidence, at a chosen token offset among distractor text; 177 items, five tasks, three checkpoints (released, short-row fine-tune, long-document fine-tune), four window sizes and 93,275 scored requests. When the evidence lies beyond the window, accuracy falls to 26.4% for the released checkpoint (27.2% for both fine-tunes), near the 27.0% of guessing and below the 35.6% of always giving the commonest label. Confidence gives no warning (AUROC 0.50 to 0.55 for telling cut from read requests; 0.5 = chance), and the runtime's truncation flag also fires on requests whose evidence was visible. The released checkpoint reads visible evidence badly once text precedes it: 38.5% pooled against 60% when the evidence comes first, so a larger window alone gains +6.2 points. Fine-tuning on long documents adds +27.6 points on visible evidence over the short-row fine-tune (95% interval +22.3 to +33.1) and nothing on cut evidence. That is an in-family result: training rows and test documents share generator and question types, and the main filler is of the training kind. With encyclopedic filler never seen in training the gain is +13.2 (+9.9 to +16.6), with rows of other tasks as filler -2.0. Among training-free rearrangements a 312-token window scan helps most (+11.9 points over the default for the released checkpoint) at six to eleven forward passes. Documents are synthetic, labels are LLM-made and one backbone is studied, so these diagnose this model, not long documents in general.
中文翻译
Laya 是一个小型模型，能够在单次遍历中回答关于文本的多选题：问题、选项（每个包含名称和描述）以及文本共同构成最多512个标记的序列窗口（英文版）。较长的文本会在末尾被无声截断，我们测量这种截断带来的损失。测试文档将待保留行的文本（证据）放置在干扰文本中的指定标记偏移位置；共177个样本，五个任务，三个检查点（发布版、短行微调、长文档微调），四种窗口大小，93,275次评分请求。当证据位于窗口之外时，发布版检查点的准确率降至26.4%（两个微调版本分别为27.2%），接近随机猜测的27.0%，低于始终选择最常见标签的35.6%。置信度无法提供预警（区分截断与完整读取请求的AUROC为0.50至0.55，0.5表示随机水平），且运行时的截断标志也对可见证据的请求触发。发布版检查点在文本前置时对可见证据的读取表现较差：整体准确率为38.5%，而当证据位于开头时为60%，因此仅增大窗口即可提升+6.2分。在长文档上进行微调，在可见证据上的性能比短行微调提升+27.6分（95%置信区间+22.3至+33.1），而在截断证据上无提升。该结果属于同家族：训练行与测试文档共享生成器和问题类型，主要干扰项为训练中出现的类型。若使用训练中从未见过的百科类干扰项，收益为+13.2分（+9.9至+16.6）；若使用其他任务的行作为干扰项，则收益为-2.0。在无需训练的重排方法中，312个标记窗口扫描效果最佳（相对于发布版检查点提升+11.9分），需六到十一轮前向传播。文档为合成数据，标签由大语言模型生成，仅研究一种主干模型，因此这些结果诊断的是该模型本身，而非一般长文档情况。

daVinci-Prover: Agent-Native Mid-Training for Formal Theorem Proving
daVinci-Prover：面向形式定理证明的原生智能体中期训练
Advances in large language models (LLMs) are rapidly transforming formal theorem proving. Yet further progress remains constrained by the lack of a transparent and effective data recipe for systematically improving theorem-proving models. In this work, we argue that effective agentic provers require strong mathematical skill priors to ground theorem proving and agentic trajectories to enable long-horizon, interactive proof construction. To this end, we synthesize an agent-native 33.7B-token mid-training corpus covering decomposition, search, self-verification, and revision, together with verifier-grounded agentic trajectories. We further introduce the daVinci-Prover harness, which enables models to construct proofs and interact with a Lean verifier in an isolated workspace, and use it to collect approximately 36K high-quality agent trajectories for supervised fine-tuning. Our mid-training recipe yields an average improvement of 13.7 points over the matched Qwen3-8B SFT-only baseline across MiniF2F, FATE-M, and ProofNet. When applied to the Qwen3.5 series, our approach also yields strong performance across theorem-proving benchmarks and achieves near-saturating MiniF2F performance with memory compaction. The results highlight agent-native mid-training as an effective foundation for developing stronger interactive theorem-proving agents.

Saidzoda-Kazakh-EmbeddingGemma-300M: How Far One Epoch of Generated Questions Takes a Small Kazakh Embedder
Saidzoda-Kazakh-EmbeddingGemma-300M：一次训练迭代如何影响小型哈萨克嵌入模型的表现
EmbeddingGemma-300M is a small open embedder, and used as released it finds the right passage first for 60.8% of the Kazakh Belebele retrieval questions. We present Saidzoda-Kazakh-EmbeddingGemma-300M, the same model fully fine-tuned for one epoch on 208,511 pairs: 166,263 Kazakh questions that our own language model wrote about source passages from encyclopedias, Wikipedia, news and books, each checked by a second model, plus English and Russian replay. On Kazakh Belebele, recall@1 rises from 60.8% to 78.0% and recall@10 from 76.3% to 91.4%. Our model scores above 161 of the 194 models with a published MTEB score on the same test, among them OpenAI text-embedding-3-large (47.1%). The other 33 score higher: the ten best are large multi-billion-parameter or closed models, reaching up to 92.2%, and at least 9 are open models of up to 0.6B parameters, the smallest with 339M. We also scored 6 Kazakh-specific embedders shared on Hugging Face with the same code in one run: our model (77.3% there) is ahead of 4 of them, including the two E5-base models of similar size (72.6% and 69.0%), and behind kaz-embedder (86.0%, three towers with 1,695M parameters in total) and a BGE-M3 fine-tune (81.0%); at recall@10 its interval overlaps those of its nearest neighbours on both sides. Our four-language model, trained with a capped share of the same Kazakh pairs, is 2.8 points lower at recall@1, with overlapping recall@10 intervals. The Kazakh model also scores higher than the released model on the Uzbek and Kyrgyz versions of Belebele, but our Tajik model, trained without any Turkic pairs, gains as much there, so these gains are not specific to Kazakh training. Our Kazakh Wikipedia test questions come from the same generator family as the training questions, so we rest the main claims on Belebele.

👤 Tohir Saidzoda, Madina Abdusalomzoda, Abubakr Abdullozoda, Amin Jalilov, Faroiz Bobokhon et al. 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16384]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
Beyond Compressing Everything: Contrastive Memory Learning for Selective Context Compression
超越全面压缩：用于选择性上下文压缩的对比记忆学习
As large language models (LLMs) advance toward increasingly long inputs, soft context compression offers a promising approach to efficient inference by converting long inputs into compact memory slots. Under limited memory budgets, effective context compression remains challenging because task-relevant evidence varies substantially across queries, ranging from localized evidence to multiple distant spans or information dispersed throughout the full document. Instead of compressing everything indiscriminately, we propose CoMem, a query-guided selective context compression framework that learns memory representations conditioned on both the document and the query. CoMem develops Contrastive Compressor Learning to achieve query-sensitive yet context-preserving memory representations. To inject a strong prior over query-dependent evidence distributions, we devise a same-context hard negative mining strategy by pairing a single document with multiple queries, requiring the compressor to selectively retain information according to each query's distinct evidence needs. To address the restricted information flow imposed by causal masking, we further enable bidirectional attention among memory slots. Experiments demonstrate that CoMem consistently outperforms existing context compression methods across compression ratios on question answering and RULER-NIAH benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/scar-on/CoMem.

随着大型语言模型（LLMs）处理越来越长的输入，软上下文压缩通过将长输入转换为紧凑的记忆槽，为高效推理提供了有前景的解决方案。在有限的内存预算下，有效的上下文压缩仍然具有挑战性，因为任务相关的证据在不同查询间差异显著，可能从局部证据到多个遥远片段，或分散在整个文档中。我们不盲目压缩所有内容，而是提出CoMem——一种查询引导的选择性上下文压缩框架，其记忆表示同时依赖于文档和查询。CoMem采用对比压缩学习，实现对查询敏感但保留上下文信息的记忆表示。为注入对查询依赖证据分布的强先验，我们设计了一种同一文档配多个查询的硬负样本挖掘策略，要求压缩器根据每个查询的独特证据需求选择性保留信息。为克服因果掩码带来的信息流限制，我们进一步在记忆槽之间引入双向注意力机制。实验表明，CoMem在问答和RULER-NIAH基准上，于不同压缩比率下均持续优于现有上下文压缩方法。代码已公开于 https://github.com/scar-on/CoMem。

We Are What We Remember: Mental-State-Driven Memory for Long-Term Character Dialogue
我们即是我们所记忆的：基于心理状态的记忆机制用于长期角色对话
Shared history in human dialogue is not merely a repository of facts, but the foundation of evolving relationships and mutual understanding. However, mainstream conversational memory systems rely on passive semantic similarity over external fact stores, frequently retrieving topically similar records while missing relationally pivotal experiences. Inspired by cognitive psychology's Self-Memory System (SMS), we propose MindMem, a mental-state-driven memory framework that asynchronously consolidates shared experiences into source-linked mental states of the character, the user, and their relationship. At each turn, a specialized mental-state reasoner (Mind) contextualizes these persistent states to anticipate latent user needs, form proactive intentions, and recover supporting episodic evidence that surface-level matching overlooks. To evaluate history-sensitive adaptation beyond factual recall, we introduce MemReal-Bench, a longitudinal benchmark grounded in month-long real-user interactions across dyadic and group scenes. By pairing Direct Question Answering (DQA) with Situated Memory Realization (SMR), it tests whether agents bridge the gap between knowing past facts and naturally using shared history, world rules, and relational trajectories. On MemReal-Bench, MindMem reaches 59.03% SMR and 82.58% DQA, exceeding the strongest memory baseline by 24.19 and 9.19 percentage points, respectively, and outperforming oracle target-memory injection on SMR. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/jiazhicheng7-ctrl/MindMem.

人类对话中的共享历史不仅是事实的存储库，更是关系演变和相互理解的基础。然而，主流对话记忆系统依赖于外部事实库中被动的语义相似性，频繁检索主题相关记录，却忽略了具有关系关键意义的经历。受认知心理学中自我记忆系统（SMS）的启发，我们提出了MindMem，一种由心理状态驱动的记忆框架，能够异步地将共享经历整合为角色、用户及其关系的源链接心理状态。在每一轮对话中，专门的心理状态推理器（Mind）会 contextualize 这些持久状态，以预测潜在的用户需求，形成主动意图，并恢复表面匹配所忽略的支持性情景证据。为了评估超越事实回忆的历史敏感适应能力，我们引入了MemReal-Bench，一个基于跨二元和群体场景长达一个月的真实用户互动的纵向基准。通过将直接问答（DQA）与情境化记忆实现（SMR）相结合，它测试智能体是否能弥合知晓过去事实与自然运用共享历史、世界规则和关系轨迹之间的差距。在MemReal-Bench上，MindMem在SMR上达到59.03%，在DQA上达到82.58%，分别比最强的记忆基线高出24.19和9.19个百分点，并在SMR上优于oracle目标记忆注入。代码和数据集可在https://github.com/jiazhicheng7-ctrl/MindMem获取。

Privileged Information Does Not Guarantee Privileged Guidance: Auditing On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Models
特权信息并不保证特权指导：对推理模型在策略内自蒸馏的审计
On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on next-token targets from a teacher, a frozen copy of the student that additionally receives privileged information, such as a worked solution, hidden from the student. Whether student gains reflect that information remains unclear. Taking the worked solution as privileged information, we audit four stages across three Qwen3 scales on mathematics: Source, whether the teacher can use the solution; Encoding, whether the resulting token targets are useful; Transmission, whether the student learns from the targets; and Expression, whether the student applies the learned behavior without the solution. With the solution, the teacher solves hard problems and recovers from student errors, yet the teacher does not reliably recognize errors, and its token targets do not favor correct trajectories. Repeated training makes successes within the student's reach more frequent rather than unlocking new ones, and corrections the student learns to predict are not reliably expressed in free generation. To isolate the solution's contribution, we vary its availability separately from teacher and student thinking modes. The largest gains occur when a teacher in thinking mode supervises a student trained and evaluated without an explicit thinking trace, and problem-only teachers often match or exceed privileged-solution teachers: the gain tracks the teacher's thinking mode rather than the solution, a pattern we call reasoning arbitrage. Accuracy gains alone therefore do not establish transfer of reasoning from privileged solutions: bottlenecks lie after Source, across Encoding, Transmission, and Expression. Future evaluations must measure what the solution adds to otherwise identical training.
中文翻译
在策略内自蒸馏（OPSD）中，学生模型在来自教师模型的下一个词目标上进行训练，该教师模型是学生模型的冻结副本，额外接收特权信息（如完整解题过程），而学生模型无法访问。学生模型获得的性能提升是否源于该信息尚不明确。我们将完整解题过程视为特权信息，对三个Qwen3规模下的数学任务进行了四个阶段的审计：来源（教师能否使用解题过程）、编码（生成的词目标是否有用）、传输（学生是否从目标中学习）和表达（学生在无解题过程情况下是否能正确应用所学行为）。结果显示，尽管教师在有解题过程时能解决难题并纠正学生错误，但教师并未可靠识别错误，其词目标也不倾向于正确路径。重复训练使学生可达的成功更频繁，但并未解锁新的可能性；学生学会预测的修正行为在自由生成中也未可靠表达。为隔离解题过程的贡献，我们分别改变其在教师和学生思考模式中的可用性。最大收益出现在教师处于思考模式时，监督一个在无显式思考痕迹下训练和评估的学生模型，且仅问题教师的表现往往与带特权解题过程的教师相当甚至更优：收益取决于教师的思考模式，而非解题过程本身，这一现象称为推理套利。因此，仅凭准确率提升无法证明从特权解题过程到推理能力的转移：瓶颈存在于来源之后的编码、传输和表达阶段。未来评估必须测量解题过程在其他条件完全相同的训练中带来的实际增益。

Better Boundary Prediction Does Not Guarantee Better Structural Representation
更好的边界预测并不保证更好的结构表示
Punctuation marks often mark phrase boundaries, carrying linguistic information. Therefore, punctuation marks' abundance and feasibility as proxies of linguistic structure has led to suggestions that using punctuation marks to optimise on phrasal structure may help language models (LMs) improve their representations of argument, semantic, and syntactic structures. In this paper, we test the above claim in English across three primary LM architectures: decoder-only, encoder-only, and encoder-decoder LMs, by complementing traditional token prediction with punctuation restoration (PR). Our evidence, collected at the <1B parameter scale, suggests that optimising for boundary prediction does not necessarily guarantee higher-quality structural representation in decoder-only, encoder-decoder, and encoder-only LMs, failing to improve performance on downstream tasks that require understanding structure in linguistic input.

ShadowBench: Exposing Lexical Anchoring and the Illusion of Forgetting in Large Language Models
ShadowBench：揭示大语言模型中的词汇锚定现象与遗忘幻觉
Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as primary interfaces for factual retrieval, yet current evaluation paradigms rely almost exclusively on explicit entity names. In this work, we demonstrate that LLM knowledge is fundamentally lexically anchored: models possess extensive factual information but find it significantly difficult to retrieve without explicit name tokens. To quantify this limitation, we introduce ShadowBench, a rigorously hardened, shortcut-resistant benchmark designed to evaluate Latent Entity Association via a novel Dual-Trait Association (DTA) task. Our evaluation reveals a pervasive "Shadow Gap" across all model scales$\unicode{x2013}$up to the frontier models GPT-5.4 and Claude-Sonnet-4.6$\unicode{x2013}$where removing lexical anchors causes performance drops of over 20%. Furthermore, we demonstrate that this lexical dependency exposes a critical vulnerability in AI safety. Applying ShadowBench to Machine Unlearning, we find that state-of-the-art algorithms (e.g., Gradient Difference, NPO) achieve only superficial lexical erasure. While unlearned models fail on direct queries, our novel Latent Entity Leakage Rate (LELR) metric reveals that for popular entities, models explicitly reconstruct the "forgotten" entity in their internal reasoning traces in over 74% of cases, utilizing residual shadow knowledge to solve associative tasks. Conversely, for niche entities, models retain high shadow accuracy (60-90%) via implicit attribute correlation despite 0% LELR. Ultimately, ShadowBench indicates that current unlearning paradigms create an "Illusion of Forgetting," demonstrating that existing methods act as superficial output filters and are not yet reliable for achieving true parametric erasure.

大语言模型（LLMs）已成为事实检索的主要接口，但当前评估范式几乎完全依赖显式的实体名称。本文揭示，LLM的知识本质上是词汇锚定的：模型拥有大量事实信息，但在缺乏显式名称标记时难以有效检索。为量化这一局限，我们提出了ShadowBench，一个严格强化、抗捷径的基准测试，通过新颖的双特质关联（DTA）任务评估潜在实体关联。我们的评估发现，在所有模型规模下——包括前沿模型GPT-5.4和Claude-Sonnet-4.6——移除词汇锚定会导致性能下降超过20%，形成普遍存在的“阴影差距”。此外，我们证明这种词汇依赖暴露了AI安全中的关键漏洞。将ShadowBench应用于机器遗忘任务，发现当前最先进的算法（如梯度差、NPO）仅实现表面化的词汇擦除。虽然被遗忘模型在直接查询中失败，但我们提出的潜在实体泄漏率（LELR）指标显示，对于主流实体，模型在内部推理轨迹中重新构建“已遗忘”实体的情况超过74%，利用残留的阴影知识完成关联任务。相反，对于冷门实体，尽管LELR为0%，模型仍通过隐式属性相关性保持60%-90%的阴影准确率。最终，ShadowBench表明，当前遗忘范式制造了“遗忘幻觉”——现有方法仅作为表面输出过滤器，尚未能实现真正的参数级擦除。

HeriKGQA: Grounded Knowledge-Graph Question Answering over Cultural Heritage Sensor Data
HeriKGQA：基于文化遗产传感器数据的 grounded 知识图谱问答
Cultural heritage sites are increasingly monitored by sensor networks, but querying their data requires database expertise. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a natural-language interface, yet over raw sensor records they lose track of which sensor, site, and time each value belongs to, and silently miscompute. We present HeriKGQA, a knowledge-graph question-answering method that confines the LLM to interpreting questions and phrasing answers, leaving computation to SPARQL and deterministic code. Questions are parsed into intents constrained by a heritage ontology aligning W3C SOSA/SSN with CIDOC-CRM, compiled into SPARQL, and answered from query results and temporal, spatial, and anomaly facts derived by a grounding layer, with error correction guarding each LLM step. On 500 questions over real data from five heritage sites, HeriKGQA reaches 99.0% accuracy, 18.2 points above the strongest of three baselines using the same LLM and receiving the same anomaly rules and answer conventions, with comparable answer quality while running almost four times faster. Without grounding, accuracy drops by 12.2 points and on anomaly questions from 99% to 66%. We report lessons from development and live deployment.

Where Is Uncertainty Observed? A Mechanistic Understanding of Uncertainty Quantification in LLMs
不确定性在何处被观测？对大语言模型中不确定性量化机制的机理理解
Knowing when a large language model's (LLM's) response can be trusted is critical for its safe use. Uncertainty quantification (UQ) can help LLMs recognize if their responses may be unreliable. Existing taxonomies organize UQ methods by uncertainty source or estimation mechanism. They leave unresolved the question of where uncertainty is observed in the model's computation. The answer matters because the observation point determines whether an estimator has access to enough information to recover a given target, and how well it can recover it. Drawing on mechanistic interpretability, we define four mechanistic observation lenses: the structural lens observes component updates, the representational lens observes hidden states, the distributional lens observes token probabilities, and the semantical lens observes distributions over meanings. We relate these lenses through the residual stream and order them by the information they carry, which gives a principle for selecting UQ methods for different targets. We survey existing methods through the four lenses. Furthermore, we distinguish evaluation targets, criteria, and metrics to clarify different UQ evaluation protocols. Finally, we identify open problems and research directions to connect uncertainty estimation to the internal computation and downstream use of LLMs.
中文翻译
了解大语言模型（LLM）的响应何时可信，对于其安全使用至关重要。不确定性量化（UQ）有助于 LLM 识别其响应可能不可靠的情况。现有的分类体系按不确定性来源或估计机制组织 UQ 方法，但未解决不确定性在模型计算中被观测的位置这一关键问题。该位置至关重要，因为它决定了估计器是否能获得足够信息以恢复目标，以及恢复效果如何。基于机理可解释性，我们定义了四种机理观测视角：结构视角观察组件更新，表征视角观察隐藏状态，分布视角观察标记概率，语义视角观察意义分布。我们通过残差流关联这些视角，并按其所携带的信息量排序，从而为针对不同目标选择 UQ 方法提供原则。我们通过这四个视角综述现有方法。此外，我们区分了评估目标、标准和度量，以明确不同的 UQ 评估协议。最后，我们指出了开放性问题和研究方向，以促进不确定性估计与 LLM 内部计算及下游应用之间的联系。

Can Agents Relive Past Experiences? Diagnosing Event-Affect Decoupling in Affective-Episodic Retrospection
智能体能否重温过往经历？情感-情景回溯中的事件-情感解耦诊断
As memory-augmented agents increasingly support long-term and personalized interactions, reliable recall of emotional experiences while preserving their associated affect becomes necessary. However, existing evaluations usually assess emotion accuracy and factual memory grounding separately, overlooking event-affect correspondence. In this work, we formulate Affective-Episodic Retrospection (AER) as a task that explicitly evaluates the recovery of past events, associated affects, and their correspondence, and define Event-Affect Decoupling as a failure mode where the correct emotion is recalled but associated with the wrong episode. To diagnose Event-Affect Decoupling, we propose EM-TRACE, a stage-wise framework that decomposes AER into retrieval, affect recovery, and realization. On a controlled GoEmotions-based benchmark, Emotion-only retrieval achieves 28.65% affect recovery but retrieves the target episode for only 4.44% of queries, yielding a UAR of 24.21%. Further analysis reveals that event retrieval and affect assignment introduce distinct binding failures. These findings show that reliable AER requires grounded recall of emotional experiences, where episode retrieval, affect recovery, event-affect binding, and generation quality should be evaluated jointly. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/A412/.

随着记忆增强型智能体越来越多地支持长期个性化交互，可靠地回忆带有相应情感体验的过去事件变得至关重要。然而，现有评估通常将情绪准确性与事实记忆基础分开评估，忽略了事件与情感之间的对应关系。本文将情感-情景回溯（AER）定义为一项任务，明确评估过去事件、相关情感及其对应关系的恢复能力，并将事件-情感解耦定义为一种失败模式：正确回忆了情绪，但关联到了错误的事件。为诊断该解耦现象，我们提出EM-TRACE框架，将AER分解为检索、情感恢复和生成三个阶段。在基于GoEmotions的受控基准上，仅情绪检索的准确率仅为28.65%，而目标事件的召回率仅为4.44%，综合UAR为24.21%。进一步分析表明，事件检索与情感分配分别引入了不同的绑定失败。这些发现表明，可靠的AER需要建立在情感体验的根基性回忆之上，必须联合评估事件检索、情感恢复、事件-情感绑定及生成质量。代码已公开于https://anonymous.4open.science/r/A412/。

Faithful in Steps: Improving Generalization and Citation in RAG via Query Decomposition
步步忠实：通过查询分解提升RAG的泛化能力与引用准确性
Retrieval-augment generation is a prevalent strategy to mitigate hallucinations of LLMs. The attributable RAG (RAGQ) generates quotes for its answers. The quotes indicate which input contexts support the RAG to derive the answers, enhancing the answer's verifiability and trustworthiness. However, existing RAGQs exhibit significant degradation when dealing with questions that require multi-hop reasoning and multi-modal understanding, suffering from over-citation, implicit entity identification failure, and poor generalization. In this paper, we propose a novel RAGQ framework, namely QDRAG. QDRAG breaks down the input question into atomic subquestions to identify the implicit entities. Then, the reranker prunes context distractors to eliminate the downstream over-citation. To facilitate query decomposition, we propose two zero-shot approaches: QD-C and QD-R, which guide the QD MLLM to decompose the question based on context knowledge and retrieval rewards, respectively. One interesting finding is that finetuning on the QD task shows better generalizability compared to directly finetuning on the downstream RAGQ task. Experiments on four multi-modal QA benchmarks demonstrate QDRAG's efficacy in grounding answers and generating faithful citations. The framework significantly outperforms all the baselines on both in-domain and out-of-domain tests, even surpassing Gemini-Pro.

检索增强生成是缓解大语言模型幻觉的常用策略。可溯源RAG（RAGQ）会生成答案对应的引文，标明支持答案的输入上下文，从而增强答案的可验证性和可信度。然而，现有RAGQ在处理需要多跳推理和多模态理解的问题时表现显著下降，存在过度引用、隐含实体识别失败和泛化能力差等问题。本文提出一种新型RAGQ框架——QDRAG。QDRAG将输入问题分解为原子子问题，以识别隐含实体；随后，重排序器剔除上下文干扰项，减少下游过度引用。为促进查询分解，我们提出两种零样本方法：QD-C和QD-R，分别基于上下文知识和检索奖励引导QD MLLM进行问题分解。一个有趣发现是：在QD任务上微调比直接在下游RAGQ任务上微调展现出更好的泛化能力。在四个多模态问答基准上的实验表明，QDRAG在答案定位和忠实引用生成方面均有效。该框架在域内和域外测试中均显著优于所有基线模型，甚至超越Gemini-Pro。

AutoOntic: Automatic Ontology Induction from Text for Knowledge Graph Construction
AutoOntic：从文本自动构建本体以支持知识图谱构建
Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation depends on the quality of the knowledge graph (KG) it retrieves over, yet schema-free LLM-based extraction pipelines typically produce flat graphs: deduplicated entities and relations with no type hierarchy and no domain/range constraints. We present AutoOntic, a pipeline that automatically constructs an ontology from text by canonicalizing open-vocabulary entity types and relations, inducing a hierarchy over the canonicalized types, and mining relation-level domain and range constraints. Its hierarchy induction procedure prioritizes types using corpus-derived relation-argument profiles and places them through local, LLM-adjudicated parent/child checks, allowing newly inserted types to trigger reassignment of existing parent-child links. The resulting hierarchy constrains entity deduplication and supports the induction of relation-level domain/range constraints. For intrinsic evaluation, we introduce a reference-based protocol that classifies induced ontology types as exact, broader, narrower, or unrelated to reference domain and range classes. On the Wikidata-TekGen and DBpedia-WebNLG subsets of Text2KGBench, over 90% of AutoOntic's induced endpoint types are semantically aligned with the reference classes across two LLM backbones: about half match exactly, while most remaining differences concern granularity. On MINE, knowledge graphs organized using an ontology built by AutoOntic achieve 88% retrieval accuracy. AutoOntic is available at https://github.com/screemix/AutoOntic.

基于图的检索增强生成依赖于其检索的知识图谱（KG）的质量，但基于无模式LLM的提取流程通常生成扁平图：去重后的实体和关系，缺乏类型层次结构和领域/范围约束。我们提出AutoOntic，一个从文本自动构建本体的流程，包括规范化开放词汇实体类型与关系、推导标准化类型的层次结构，以及挖掘关系级别的领域与范围约束。其层次结构推导过程利用语料库衍生的关系参数特征优先排序类型，并通过局部LLM仲裁的父子检查机制进行放置，允许新插入类型触发已有父子链接的重新分配。由此生成的层次结构约束实体去重，并支持关系级别领域/范围约束的推导。在内在评估中，我们引入基于参考的协议，将推导出的本体类型分类为与参考领域/范围类别的精确匹配、更广、更窄或无关。在Text2KGBench的Wikidata-TekGen和DBpedia-WebNLG子集上，超过90%的AutoOntic推导出的末端类型在语义上与参考类别对齐，其中约一半完全匹配，其余差异主要涉及粒度。在MINE数据集上，使用AutoOntic构建的本体组织的知识图谱达到88%的检索准确率。AutoOntic已开源：https://github.com/screemix/AutoOntic。

Towards Better Legal Reasoning LLMs: Signal Balancing and Reward Scheduling
迈向更优的法律推理大模型：信号平衡与奖励调度
In the legal domain, LLMs can improve reliability and interpretability by generating explicit reasoning traces. However, training LLMs to produce high-quality reasoning traces remains challenging. Existing supervised fine-tuning methods struggle when reasoning traces are significantly longer than final answers, as the learning signal for the answer becomes diluted. Meanwhile, reinforcement learning methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization also have several limitations, including costly reward design, performance plateaus, and reward hacking. We therefore propose a two-stage training framework. In Stage I, JurisCoT-SFT employs a length-normalized dual objective to balance learning signals between reasoning traces and final answers. In Stage II, Lifecycle-Aware Backtrackable Policy Optimization dynamically evaluates and retires auxiliary reward signals based on their impact on final evaluation objectives, enabling efficient reward utilization. Our framework substantially improves legal reasoning performance on relevant tasks selected from multiple legal benchmarks, including ChJGBench, LawBench, and LegalBench. Experimental results demonstrate consistent improvements in both reasoning trace quality and final answer accuracy, while our models remain competitive with larger models.

Janus-MOPD: Tracing the Student Learning Dynamics in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation
Janus-MOPD：多教师在线蒸馏中学生学习动态的追踪
Multi-domain capabilities are critical for practically applicable Large Reasoning Models (LRMs). Therefore, Multi-teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD) has become a crucial part of the post-training process, integrating the capabilities of multiple domain-specific teacher models into a single student model. However, the student usually fails to acquire multi-domain capabilities simultaneously due to undesirable domain heterogeneity. Existing works have address this problem in view of temporal uneven efficiency and spatial hard allocation in external multi-teacher supervision. While in broader multi-domain training, varying degrees of performance degradation is still largely unexplained. In this paper, we shift the focus on how the student learns and integrates multi-domain capabilities internally, and then propose \textbf{\textsc{Janus-MOPD}}, a general learning dynamics framework of geometric representations for LRMs. Specifically, our main focus is on the internal performance of multi-domain capabilities on both temporal and spatial dimensions within the student. Temporally, our study shows that the student exhibits a Expansion-Consolidation-Optimization (ECO) three-phase learning dynamics, together with inherent limitations in learning efficiency and scaling. Spatially, we summarize two types of subspace locking within and across domains by tracing the representation space of the student, while explaining its clear learning directions but inherent cross-domain conflict. Therefore, \textsc{Janus-MOPD} yields a unified learning dynamics formulation, a geometric tracing methodology, and systematic findings of how the student learns and integrates multi-domain capabilities. Overall, we provide a \textsc{Janus} perspective to internally trace the continual evolution of the multi-domain capabilities within the model itself.

多领域能力对于实际可用的大推理模型至关重要。因此，多教师在线蒸馏（MOPD）已成为后训练过程中的关键环节，旨在将多个领域专用教师模型的能力整合到单一学生模型中。然而，由于领域异质性带来的不利影响，学生模型通常无法同时获得多领域能力。现有研究主要关注外部多教师监督中的时间效率不均与空间硬分配问题。但在更广泛的多领域训练中，不同程度的性能退化仍缺乏充分解释。本文聚焦于学生模型如何在内部学习并整合多领域能力，提出 extbf{ extsc{Janus-MOPD}}——一种针对大推理模型几何表示的学习动态通用框架。具体而言，我们关注学生模型在时间和空间维度上的多领域能力内部表现。时间上，研究发现学生表现出扩张-巩固-优化（ECO）三阶段学习动态，且存在学习效率与扩展性的内在限制。空间上，通过追踪学生表示空间，我们总结出域内与跨域的两种子空间锁定现象，揭示了清晰的学习方向但存在固有的跨域冲突。因此， extsc{Janus-MOPD}提出了统一的学习动态公式、几何追踪方法以及关于学生如何学习与整合多领域能力的系统性发现。总体而言，我们提供了 extsc{Janus}视角，以内部追踪模型自身多领域能力的持续演化过程。

Knowledge in the Harness, Reliability in the Weights: Teacher-Free Co-Evolution of an LLM Agent
知识在缰绳中，可靠性在权重里：无需教师的大模型智能体协同进化
Small open models suit private customer-service deployments but trail frontier models in two ways: they miss what the written policy requires in a given situation, and they act inconsistently on what they know. We study whether such a model can close both gaps from its own deployment logs and their reference outcomes, without a stronger teacher model. Our starting point is a Gemma 4 12B agent that reads its domain policy from on-demand skills rather than from its system prompt. In each round, the deployed system serves a fresh batch of tasks; the base model diagnoses failed conversations and writes rules into the skills, and a rule survives only if it passes code checks, counterexample self-review, a paired retest, and a noise-calibrated validation gate. Reinforcement learning (GRPO) then trains a LoRA adapter on complete multi-turn, tool-using conversations. On the retail and airline domains of $τ^2$-bench, without training or selecting on official tasks, the agent after two rounds improves on this starting point by 12.5 pass$^1$ points in retail and 19.5 in airline, and by 18.4 and 22.0 pass$^4$ points. It also exceeds the benchmark's reference agent, which runs the same model with the whole policy in its system prompt, by 11.8 and 8.0 pass$^1$ points and by 22.8 and 16.0 pass$^4$ points, closing most of the gap to three frontier models under the same protocol. A $2\times2$ ablation shows that the rules rescue tasks the agent never solved and carry policy knowledge that RL with the rules in context does not internalize, whereas the trained weights make solved tasks reliable.

CoLFA: Collaboration-Guided Lightweight Failure Attribution for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems
CoLFA：面向基于大语言模型的多智能体系统的协作引导轻量级故障归因方法
Failure attribution in multi-agent systems requires tracing error formation to identify the decisive error step and the responsible agent. However, agents' division of work and information exchange lead to Interleaved Steps and Cluttered Content, requiring models to link executions and check details in lengthy records. This makes it difficult to balance localization accuracy and token cost. We find that multi-agent collaboration also leaves records of how executions connect and content passes between them as results are formed, providing evidence for tracing error formation. Based on this observation, we propose CoLFA (Collaboration-Guided Lightweight Failure Attribution), which uses collaboration cues to organize the attribution input according to how results are formed, determining which steps are examined and how their content is presented. Through Handoff-Guided Step Expansion and Cross-Referenced Content Organization, CoLFA helps the model focus on examining error formation and the role of each decision while reducing the processing of irrelevant and repeated records. Across five settings spanning Who&When and LongRCA and three backbone models, CoLFA achieves the highest exact step-localization accuracy with the lowest token consumption. Compared with individual baselines in each setting, CoLFA improves accuracy by up to 37.50% and reduces token consumption by up to 97.6%.

Probe What-If Worlds Before You Ship: Persona Simulation for Real-World Decision Making
在发布前探测‘如果世界’：用于现实世界决策的人格模拟
User research and system testing reveal whether products and services meet user needs, yet recruiting specialized participants, designing controlled what-if comparisons, and repeating studies as designs evolve often bottleneck design iteration and data-driven decision-making. We present a framework and execution infrastructure that translates real-world industry inquiries into controlled experiments with simulated users. Our methodology formally defines the target cohort, experimental stimuli, and decision-relevant metrics, while the pipeline supports large-scale concurrent runs over cohort-stimulus-environment combinations (survey, web, chatbot, and OS app), auditable trial-level logging, cohort-level outcome aggregation, and cross-cohort and cross-variant statistical reports. Across twelve industry partner cases spanning five domains, we show how constructing hard-to-reach or what-if persona cohorts supports exploratory studies and distills simulated responses into testable design hypotheses. Cross-model comparisons on selected cases largely preserve primary conclusions, whereas perturbing or ablating persona attributes alters service outcomes, effect sizes, and inter-group differences, indicating that grounding target cohorts in detailed personas materially shapes simulation results. Together, these studies show that persona-enabled agents can test specified cohorts at design speed, diagnose systems under test, and deliver directional evidence that focuses problem definition, steers design and strategic decisions, and guides further study.

CoReMem: Cost-Internalizing Memory for Successive Coding Tasks with Prospective Valuation
CoReMem：面向连续编码任务的代价内化记忆与前瞻性估值
Coding agents tackle successive tasks on an evolving repository, carrying forward edits and writing memory before the next task is known. Selecting memory for unknown next tasks requires balancing future usefulness against the costs of writing and reading it. The actor must also decide when acting on available memory saves effort as the task unfolds. We introduce Coding-Repository Memory (CoReMem), a cost-internalizing framework that guides memory design and actor adaptation through measured memory value on the next task. Prospective segment valuation compares runs of the next task with and without individual trajectory segments from the same inherited repository state, guiding a compact memory built around anchors: repository locations and executable checks. Actor adaptation estimates on-policy memory value within rollout groups with and without memory, using a reward that prioritizes resolution and penalizes step count in successful runs. Provenance-gated value credit assigns the additional value term to steps identified as using memory-supplied anchors, while every step retains outcome credit. On ChainSWE, CoReMem raises the 27B actor's resolve rate from 21.0% to 25.7%, with 17% fewer steps and 11% fewer total cache-aware tokens, including memory-module calls, relative to the untrained actor model without memory.

编码智能体需在不断演化的代码库上处理连续任务，携带前序修改并编写记忆，而下一个任务尚未可知。选择未知任务的记忆需权衡未来效用与写入和读取记忆的代价。智能体还需决定在任务进行过程中，利用现有记忆是否能节省努力。我们提出编码-仓库记忆（CoReMem），一种代价内化框架，通过衡量记忆对未来任务的价值来指导记忆设计与智能体适应。前瞻性片段估值通过对比在相同继承仓库状态下的下一任务运行，有无单个轨迹片段，从而引导围绕锚点构建紧凑记忆：仓库位置和可执行检查点。智能体适应通过在带记忆与不带记忆的滚动组内估计策略内记忆价值，使用优先解决成功任务并惩罚步数的奖励。来源门控价值信用将额外价值项分配给识别为使用记忆提供锚点的步骤，同时每一步仍保留结果信用。在 ChainSWE 上，CoReMem 将 27B 智能体的解决率从 21.0% 提升至 25.7%，步数减少 17%，总缓存感知 token 数（包括记忆模块调用）减少 11%，相比未训练的记忆模型。

CapLite: Prompt-Injection Defense for Small Language Model Agents without Model-Written Security Artifacts
CapLite：无需模型生成安全构件的小型语言模型智能体提示注入防御
Enforcement defenses against prompt injection, such as CaMeL, Progent, IPIGuard and DRIFT, ask the agent's own model to write a security artifact (a program, a privilege policy, a dependency graph or a constraint list) that code then enforces. We ask whether small language model (SLM) agents can be trusted to write these artifacts. Across 15 open-weight models, 13 of them between 1.7B and 12B parameters, they cannot: the policies, graphs and constraints that the agent's model writes admit attacks on 14 (Progent), 13 (IPIGuard) and 11 (DRIFT) of the models, and CaMeL's programs fail to run in up to 68% of episodes. We present CapLite, which derives its policy from the tool schemas, commits the write calls before any tool result is read, and admits a write only if every argument that decides whom or what it affects is stated in the user's request. This guarantees write integrity: injected text cannot add a write, and no write reaches a recipient, amount, object or destination that the user did not state. No attack succeeds in 13,935 attacked AgentDojo episodes. CapLite reaches utility under attack comparable to CaMeL's (29.0% vs. 27.2%) with fewer tokens than every other enforcement defense and than the undefended agent: 17.1k per episode, 18% of CaMeL's, and the highest utility per token of all enforcement defenses.

针对提示注入的强制性防御方法（如CaMeL、Progent、IPIGuard和DRIFT）要求智能体自身的模型生成安全构件（程序、权限策略、依赖图或约束列表），然后由代码执行强制。我们质疑小型语言模型（SLM）智能体是否可信生成这些构件。在15个开源权重模型中，13个参数规模在1.7B至12B之间，结果表明它们不可信：这些模型生成的策略、图谱和约束使14个（Progent）、13个（IPIGuard）和11个（DRIFT）模型易受攻击，而CaMeL的程序在高达68%的episode中无法运行。我们提出CapLite，其策略源自工具模式，承诺在读取任何工具结果前执行写入调用，并仅当决定影响对象或目标的所有参数均明确出现在用户请求中时才允许写入。这保证了写入完整性：注入文本无法添加新写入，且任何写入都不会到达用户未声明的接收者、数量、对象或目的地。在13,935次受攻击的AgentDojo episode中，无一攻击成功。CapLite在攻击下的实用性与CaMeL相当（29.0% vs. 27.2%），但使用的token更少，优于其他所有强制性防御及未受保护的智能体：每episode仅需17.1k token，仅为CaMeL的18%，且是所有强制性防御中单位token实用率最高的。

LazyWeights: Decode-on-Demand Edge LLMs
LazyWeights：按需解码的边缘大语言模型
Memory decides which LLMs an edge device runs, and how fast: capacity on a $4$ GB Jetson Nano, bandwidth on an $8$ GB Jetson Orin Nano Super. Deployed formats (GPTQ, AWQ, $\texttt{llama.cpp}$'s K- and I-quants) spend equal bits on every integer code of a matrix, although the codes are far from uniform. $\texttt{LazyWeights}$ keeps these formats but tunes GPTQ so the codes compress, Huffman-codes them losslessly in interleaved lanes, and decodes them on demand in a small unit between DRAM and L2, so GPU kernels run unchanged and DRAM moves only coded bytes. At $1\%$ and $3.5\%$ perplexity budgets, coded Phi-3-mini, Mistral-7B and SmolLM-1.7B are $5.7-31.4\%$ smaller than the best $\texttt{llama.cpp}$ format. A bit-exact $0.21$ mm$^2$ ASIC decoder unit ($1$ GHz, $7$ nm) cuts Orin latency and energy per token by up to $36\%$ and $38\%$ ($54\%$ and $44\%$ with prompt-lookup speculation). On the Nano, $\texttt{LazyWeights}$ needs $58-64\%$ less time and $53-57\%$ less energy per token than CPU $\texttt{llama.cpp}$, and runs a streamed Mistral-7B 6.6$\times$ faster at $3.89$ bits per weight. A Huffman decoder beats an rANS decoder in rate and area at equal throughput; the ASIC decoder unit runs bit-exact on a Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA (AMD ZCU102).
中文翻译
内存决定了边缘设备运行哪些大语言模型以及运行速度：在4GB Jetson Nano上受限于容量，在8GB Jetson Orin Nano Super上受限于带宽。现有部署格式（GPTQ、AWQ、llama.cpp的K-和I-量化）对矩阵中的每个整数编码分配相等比特，尽管这些编码分布极不均匀。LazyWeights保留这些格式，但调整GPTQ使编码可压缩，采用霍夫曼编码在交错通道中无损编码，并在DRAM与L2之间的小型单元中按需解码，使得GPU内核无需修改，且DRAM仅传输编码字节。在1%和3.5%困惑度预算下，编码后的Phi-3-mini、Mistral-7B和SmolLM-1.7B比最优的llama.cpp格式小5.7%-31.4%。一个比特精确的0.21 mm² ASIC解码单元（1 GHz，7 nm）可将Orin延迟和每token能耗降低最多36%和38%（使用提示查找推测时分别达54%和44%）。在Nano上，LazyWeights相比CPU llama.cpp，每token所需时间减少58%-64%，能耗降低53%-57%，并在3.89比特/权重下将流式Mistral-7B运行速度提升6.6倍。霍夫曼解码器在同等吞吐量下比rANS解码器具有更高的速率和更小面积；ASIC解码单元可在Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA（AMD ZCU102）上实现比特精确运行。

When Expert Disagreement Hurts: Prestige and Selectivity in LLM Decision Revision
专家分歧何时有害？大语言模型决策修订中的声望与选择性
When language models revise a decision after another agent disagrees, neither reversal counts nor final accuracy shows whether they are fixing mistakes or abandoning correct answers. We introduce a controlled two-pass audit that holds the question, the evidence, and the model's initial answer fixed and varies only the revision context: neutral reflection, anonymous disagreement, or disagreement attributed to a domain expert. For binary decisions the accounting is exact: revision raises accuracy only if it is selective enough, reversing wrong answers more than $a/(1-a)$ times as often as correct ones, where $a$ is initial accuracy (nine times at 90%). Across five models and 1,989 evidence-grounded decisions in medicine, science, and law, no model clears this bar under expert disagreement, and accuracy falls by up to 16.1 points. The expert label, a prestige cue, adds reversals for four of the five models, yet its effect on accuracy ranges from a 5.2-point loss to a 2.4-point gain, so reversal counts are a poor proxy for harm. With identical arguments from both sources, the label still raises reversal for three proprietary models and in a pooled analysis of four newer models. Models also respond to stated source reliability. Revision pipelines should therefore be evaluated with paired, conditional metrics, per model and protocol.
中文翻译
当语言模型在另一代理不同意后修正其决策时，反转次数和最终准确率无法说明它们是在纠正错误还是放弃正确答案。我们引入一种受控的双阶段审计，固定问题、证据和模型初始答案，仅改变修订上下文：中立反思、匿名分歧或归因于领域专家的分歧。对于二元决策，计算是精确的：只有当修订足够具有选择性时，准确率才会提升，即反转错误答案的频率需超过正确答案的a/(1-a)倍，其中a为初始准确率（在90%准确率时为九倍）。在医学、科学和法律领域的五种模型共1,989个基于证据的决策中，没有任何模型在专家分歧下达到此标准，准确率最高下降16.1个百分点。专家标签作为一种声望线索，使四种模型增加了反转次数，但对准确率的影响从5.2个百分点的损失到2.4个百分点的增益不等，因此反转次数是损害的不良代理。即使双方论据相同，标签仍使三种专有模型和四款较新模型的合并分析中增加反转。模型也对声明的来源可靠性作出反应。因此，修订流程应使用成对的条件指标，按模型和协议分别评估。

Do the Details Help the Whole? One-Way Transfer from Person Actions to Group Intent in VLMs on JRDB-Social
细节是否有助于整体？视觉-语言模型中从个体行为到群体意图的一向迁移（在JRDB-Social上）
Social scene datasets label people at two levels: what each person is doing and what the group is there for. Training on both levels at once is often assumed to help each of them. We test this assumption for vision-language models (VLMs) on JRDB-Social, treating person actions as the details and group intent as the whole. We fine-tune Qwen3.5 4B under eight training arms with three seeds, repeat the core arms on Qwen3.5 9B and Qwen3-VL 4B, and judge every arm with a verdict rule fixed before testing. Writing each member's actions before the intent raises intent macro-F1 from 30.1 to 31.0, and writing the intent first reaches 31.2, while action F1 stays within 0.2 points of an action-only model. Under the selected recipe, no arm trained with intent improves actions. The gain requires correct, image-grounded actions: empty action lists or actions borrowed from another group with the same intent lower intent F1. Forced-prefix tests, occlusion, member masking, linear probes and component grafting show that the intent follows the written actions, relies more on people and partly resides in the vision tower. Supplying actions as input instead of as targets hurts both levels.
中文翻译
社交场景数据集在两个层次上标注人物：每个人在做什么，以及群体存在的目的。通常假设同时训练这两个层次会对两者都有帮助。我们针对视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在JRDB-Social数据集上检验这一假设，将个体行为视为细节，群体意图视为整体。我们在Qwen3.5 4B上进行八种训练设置，每种设置使用三个种子，重复核心设置于Qwen3.5 9B和Qwen3-VL 4B，并在测试前使用固定的裁决规则评估每种设置。先写出每个成员的行为再写意图，使意图宏F1从30.1提升至31.0，而先写意图则达到31.2，同时行为F1与仅训练行为的模型相差不超过0.2分。在选定的训练配方下，任何包含意图训练的设置均未改善行为表现。意图的提升依赖于正确且图像相关的个体行为：空的行为列表或借用同意图其他群体的行为会降低意图F1。强制前缀测试、遮挡、成员掩码、线性探测和组件嫁接显示，意图跟随书写的行为，更依赖于人物信息，并部分存在于视觉塔中。将行为作为输入而非目标提供反而损害了两个层次的表现。

How Should Planning Agents ReSHAPE Their Search Space?
规划智能体应如何重塑其搜索空间？
Planning agents and benchmarks treat a user's initial specification as fixed. Yet the requests users write exclude plans they would gladly take, since they rarely discover the possible trade-offs they would accept until seeing the options. Agents already revise requests through interaction, but whether this helps planning is unknown. We formalize this step as the ReSHAPE task, which judges a revision by whether its search space matches the acceptable plans and a planner finds one there. Using ReSHAPE Planter, which builds instances from planning benchmarks with a human-validated simulated user, we analyze 5,800 agent-revised specifications in travel and shopping. We find that reshaping matters: one correct trade-off makes the same planner reach an acceptable plan up to 4.6x as often, and search space quality predicts what the planner finds. Current agents, however, admit at most 32% of the acceptable plans, where that trade-off alone admits over 52%, because they ask about preferences instead of showing plans; in travel, showing plans raises F1 by 9.7 points over asking, with two model families. Planning agents should thus treat a request as a hypothesis to test against the user's reactions to concrete plans to recognize user trade-offs.
中文翻译
规划智能体及其基准测试通常将用户的初始需求视为固定不变。然而，用户撰写的请求往往排除了他们实际上愿意接受的计划，因为他们在看到选项之前很少意识到自己可接受的权衡。智能体虽可通过交互修订请求，但这种修订是否有助于规划仍未知。我们将这一过程形式化为ReSHAPE任务，该任务通过判断修订后的搜索空间是否包含可接受的计划，并且规划器能否从中找到一个可接受的计划来评估修订效果。我们使用ReSHAPE Planter——一个基于人类验证的模拟用户从规划基准构建实例的工具——分析了旅行和购物场景中5800个由智能体修订的需求。研究发现，重塑搜索空间至关重要：一个正确的权衡可使同一规划器找到可接受计划的概率提高最多4.6倍，且搜索空间质量能有效预测规划器的结果。然而，当前智能体仅能接纳最多32%的可接受计划，而单一权衡本身即可容纳超过52%的可接受计划，原因是它们倾向于询问偏好而非展示计划；在旅行场景中，展示计划比提问使F1得分提高9.7分，且在两个模型族中均成立。因此，规划智能体应将请求视为待验证的假设，通过用户对具体计划的反应来识别其真实权衡。
👤 Yilin Zhang, Xinran Zhao, Tong Chen, Ziming Yuan, Tongshuang Wu 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Experience-Handoff Reinforcement Learning for Recursive Self-Improvement
用于递归自我改进的经验移交强化学习
It is common to train Large Language Models (LLMs) for agentic tasks with Reinforcement Learning (RL) from scalar rewards. However, this throws away the fine-grained information about the environment that the agent experienced during the trial (e.g., error logs). To utilize this rich information, recent works wrap the agent in a harness that refines its own inputs through reflection or compaction and hands past experience to future rollouts, but the construction of the handed-off artifacts (e.g., a playbook) is itself not learned. We want the best of both worlds: gradient updates from a scalar reward, while also utilizing environment information to hand off experience. As a concrete testbed, we fix the Agentic Context Engineering (\ace{}) harness and train one model to act, reflect, and curate task-local playbooks. Our method, \acerl{}, evaluates candidate experience-handoff artifacts by their downstream effect on future actor rollouts, and co-optimizes the role-specific behaviors (action generator, insight reflector, and playbook curator) under a fixed scaffold. Across agentic benchmarks, \acerl{} achieves higher held-out performance than baseline methods. The results suggest that agent training should optimize not only the actions in the current rollout, but also the model-written experience that future rollouts inherit.
中文翻译
通常使用标量奖励进行强化学习（RL）来训练大语言模型（LLM）执行智能体任务。然而，这种方法会丢弃智能体在试运行过程中所经历的环境细节信息（如错误日志）。为了利用这些丰富信息，近期工作通过封装机制让智能体通过反思或压缩来优化自身输入，并将过往经验传递给未来的运行，但移交的成果（如操作手册）本身并未被学习。我们希望兼顾两者优势：既获得标量奖励带来的梯度更新，又能利用环境信息实现经验移交。作为具体测试平台，我们固定了Agentic Context Engineering（컪c{}）封装框架，并训练一个模型同时承担行动、反思和任务本地手册编纂的角色。我们的方法췎rl{}通过评估候选经验移交成果对未来行动运行的下游影响来优化，同时在固定架构下协同优化角色特异性行为（动作生成器、洞察反思器和手册编纂者）。在多个智能体基准测试中，췎rl{}的表现优于基线方法。结果表明，智能体训练不仅应优化当前运行中的动作，还应优化未来运行所继承的模型生成经验。

Recursive State-Augmented Language Models: Persistent Latent-State Adaptation of Pretrained Language Models
递归状态增强型语言模型：预训练语言模型的持久潜在状态适应
Language models rely on finite token contexts, limiting access to earlier interactions. We introduce Recursive State-Augmented Language Models (Recursive SALM), a framework in which a persistent latent state evolves with each observation and generates adaptations of a pretrained language model. A modular read-transition-write architecture separates observation encoding, latent dynamics, and predictor adaptation. We develop deterministic and stochastic formulations. The experiments evaluate only deterministic SALM with frozen GPT-2 small and exclude earlier evidence from its decoder context. On a four-turn hypothesis-inference task, latent-gated LoRA reaches 89.1% accuracy, comparable to 88.0% for a trained full-context LoRA baseline, with earlier evidence available solely through a 64-dimensional state. Zeroing or shuffling the state substantially reduces accuracy. In a two-turn task with codewords unseen in training, several interfaces improve candidate ranking over chance, but rarely generate the exact codeword under the evaluated training schedule. These experiments show that persistent latent-conditioned adaptation can support sequential inference when earlier evidence is absent from the decoder context.

语言模型依赖有限的标记上下文，限制了对早期交互的访问。我们提出递归状态增强型语言模型（Recursive SALM），一种框架，其中持久的潜在状态随每次观察演化，并生成预训练语言模型的适应版本。采用模块化的读-转移-写架构，分离观察编码、潜在动态和预测器适应。我们开发了确定性和随机性两种形式。实验仅评估确定性SALM，使用冻结的GPT-2小模型，并排除解码器上下文中早期证据。在四轮假设推理任务中，潜在门控LoRA达到89.1%的准确率，与训练完整上下文LoRA基线（88.0%）相当，且早期证据仅通过一个64维状态提供。将状态置零或打乱会显著降低准确率。在包含训练中未见代码词的两轮任务中，多种接口提升了候选排序效果，但极少能在评估训练方案下生成确切代码词。这些实验表明，当解码器上下文中缺乏早期证据时，持久潜在条件适应仍可支持序列推理。

JudgeMix: Judge-Guided Mixed-Rollout Data Synthesis for LLM Agents
JudgeMix：基于裁判指导的混合回溯数据合成用于大语言模型代理
Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on expert trajectories is a standard paradigm for training LLM agents. However, it suffers from a well-known training-inference distribution mismatch, calling for target-aware data synthesis strategies that ground the training data in the target model's native state distribution. To bridge this gap, emerging approaches incorporate the expert either to guide mixed rollouts alongside the target model, or to provide dense step-wise supervision. Nonetheless, existing methods apply expert assistance blind to the target model's inherent behavior: they either disrupt the rollout using sub-optimal random intervention heuristics, or they enforce indiscriminate uniform supervision that unnecessarily overwrites the model's already-correct actions. We introduce JudgeMix, a judge-guided data synthesis framework that dynamically interleaves the target model and expert based on step-wise semantic evaluations. JudgeMix queries expert guidance precisely when the target model struggles, ensuring high trajectory success rates while keeping the state distribution aligned with the target model. It additionally uses the judge evaluation to reweight expert supervision on different steps, preserving model's inherent valid behavior while correcting its errors. On two agentic tool-use benchmarks, AppWorld and AutomationBench, JudgeMix demonstrates consistent superior performance to existing methods, with improvements up to 10 pass@1 points.
中文翻译
在专家轨迹上的监督微调是训练大语言模型代理的标准范式。然而，该方法存在众所周知的训练-推理分布不匹配问题，亟需目标感知的数据合成策略，使训练数据扎根于目标模型的原生状态分布。为弥合这一差距，新兴方法将专家用于引导与目标模型并行的混合回溯，或提供密集的逐步监督。但现有方法在应用专家协助时忽视了目标模型的内在行为：要么使用次优的随机干预启发式方法破坏回溯过程，要么施加无差别均匀监督，不必要的覆盖模型已正确的动作。我们提出JudgeMix，一种基于裁判指导的数据合成框架，根据逐步语义评估动态交错目标模型与专家。JudgeMix仅在目标模型遇到困难时查询专家指导，确保高轨迹成功率的同时保持状态分布与目标模型一致。此外，它利用裁判评估对不同步骤的专家监督进行重加权，在保留模型固有正确行为的同时纠正错误。在两个代理工具使用基准测试AppWorld和AutomationBench上，JudgeMix展现出持续优于现有方法的表现，pass@1提升最高达10分。
👤 Cheng-Yu Hsieh, Ting-Yao Hu, Sanjoy Chowdhury, Oncel Tuzel, Raviteja Vemulapalli 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

MuEdit: An efficient multi-task editing method towards inter-domain knowledge conflicts
MuEdit：一种针对跨域知识冲突的高效多任务编辑方法
Large language models (LLMs) require frequent knowledge updates across heterogeneous domains, but existing editing methods fail when applied sequentially to multi-task settings. We trace this collapse to inter-domain null-space misalignment: heterogeneous tasks induce distinct Jacobian geometries, causing their common null space to shrink dramatically and leaving insufficient admissible directions for conflict-free updates. We propose the Conflict Index to quantify this geometric interference, and introduce Mu-Edit, which mitigates multi-task conflicts via (1) conflict-aware ordering to minimize cumulative interference, and (2) dynamic low-rank approximation to expand the null space when ordering alone is insufficient. Experiments across five functional domains and three backbones show that Mu-Edit significantly outperforms existing baselines in multi-task editing performance while preserving general capabilities, and remains effective under incremental task arrival.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）需要在异构领域中频繁更新知识，但现有的编辑方法在多任务设置下顺序应用时会失效。我们发现这种失效源于跨域零空间错位：不同任务引发不同的雅可比几何结构，导致其公共零空间急剧缩小，从而缺乏足够的无冲突更新方向。我们提出了冲突指数来量化这种几何干扰，并引入Mu-Edit，通过（1）冲突感知的排序以最小化累积干扰，以及（2）动态低秩近似在排序不足以解决问题时扩展零空间。在五个功能领域和三种骨干模型上的实验表明，Mu-Edit在多任务编辑性能上显著优于现有基线，同时保持了通用能力，并在增量任务到达的情况下依然有效。
👤 Songshi Liang, Ting-En Lin, Zixuan Huang, Yuchuan Wu, Yongbin Li et al. 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv

Do People Trust AI Assistants Even When They Are Consistently Wrong?
当AI助手始终错误时，人们还会信任它们吗？
People are increasingly using conversational AI systems to make decisions despite concerns around their reliability. How well do people calibrate to AI systems' accuracy in multi-turn conversations? We measure how people's reliance on LLMs' advice changes across repeated interactions, varying whether the AI assistant is helpful (100% accurate), unhelpful (0% accurate), or mixed (50% accurate). Across 4 experiments, we find people learn to trust helpful models more than unhelpful ones (67% vs 21% of the time), but only when they receive external feedback on the models' accuracy. Without feedback, people continue to be persuaded 48% of the time by a consistently unhelpful model (vs 38% for helpful). Rather than gaining trust in helpful models, participant reliance on LLM advisors tended to be high in early trials and only fell with evidence of inaccuracy. Participants trusted LLM advisors more than they trusted human advisors (83% vs 65%, Experiment 4), and LLMs benefited more from having a helpful track record than humans did. In contrast with previous work, we find limited evidence of an advantage for arguing for the truth (versus a falsehood). Truth advantages emerged only when participants were already very confident in their position, and did not emerge at all for static hints. Collectively, the results suggest that people may be swayed by LLM advice, even when it is consistently false, unless they have access to timely ground-truth feedback. Unfortunately, in many domains such feedback may not be available, leading to overreliance on AI.
中文翻译
尽管对可靠性存在担忧，人们仍越来越多地使用对话式AI系统来做决策。人们在多轮对话中如何校准对LLM准确性的信任？我们测量了人们在重复交互中对LLM建议依赖程度的变化，分别考察AI助手是否有益（100%准确）、无益（0%准确）或混合（50%准确）。在四项实验中，我们发现人们能更好地信任有益模型（67% vs 21%），但前提是获得关于模型准确性的外部反馈。没有反馈时，人们仍会在48%的情况下被一个始终无益的模型说服（而有益模型为38%）。人们并非因模型表现好而建立信任，而是早期试验中对LLM顾问的依赖度较高，只有在出现不准确证据后才下降。参与者对LLM顾问的信任度高于对人类顾问（83% vs 65%，实验4），且LLM从良好记录中获益更多。与以往研究相反，我们未发现主张真相相比虚假有明显优势的证据。真理优势仅在参与者已非常自信时出现，且在静态提示条件下完全未显现。总体而言，结果表明人们可能被LLM建议影响，即使其内容一贯错误，除非他们能获得及时的真实反馈。然而，在许多领域这种反馈可能不可用，导致对AI的过度依赖。
