Rational Use of Time for Social Coordination in Humans and AI
人类与AI在社会协调中对时间的理性运用
All social interactions are situated in time. Previous studies have shown that humans are able to use implicit temporal cues, such as the duration of a pause, to infer others' mental states. But how do humans use temporal cues for dynamic, fine-grained social coordination? And can AI systems do the same, without ``experiencing'' continuous real-time as humans do? We study these questions using a cooperative card game task called The Mind, which requires players to achieve a shared goal across repeated interactions but forbids explicit communication: the only available signal is the passage of time. We evaluate 150 humans, and two classes of AI models which reflect different ways of operationalizing time in artificial systems: computer-use agents (CUAs), and standard text-prompted LLMs. We find that human behavior is best captured by a Bayesian strategy that infers a partner's belief from their timing---and similarly for frontier models. However, unlike humans, none of the tested models seem to efficiently adapt their behaviors to their partners' over the course of the game. From a cognitive perspective, our findings suggest that humans perform implicit probabilistic reasoning about continuous-time signals in dynamic social settings, and that such reasoning mechanisms may emerge without real-time experience. Practically, our study highlights new challenges for AI systems to coordinate efficiently with other agents by learning to interpret and adapt to their implicit signals in dynamic settings.

所有社会互动都发生在时间之中。先前研究表明，人类能够利用隐含的时间线索（如停顿持续时间）推断他人的心理状态。但人类如何利用时间线索实现动态、精细的社会协调？AI系统能否做到这一点，而无需像人类一样‘经历’连续实时？我们通过一种名为《心灵》的合作卡牌游戏任务来研究这些问题，该任务要求玩家在重复互动中达成共同目标，但禁止显式沟通：唯一可用信号是时间的流逝。我们评估了150名人类，以及两类反映人工智能系统中时间操作方式不同的AI模型：计算机使用代理（CUAs）和标准文本提示LLMs。我们发现人类行为最符合基于贝叶斯策略的推理——从对方的时机推断其信念——前沿模型也表现出类似行为。然而，与人类不同，所有测试模型似乎都无法在游戏过程中有效适应其伙伴的行为。从认知角度看，我们的发现表明人类在动态社会环境中对连续时间信号进行隐含的概率推理，而这种推理机制可能无需真实时间体验即可涌现。从实践角度，本研究突出了AI系统在动态环境中学习解读并适应其他智能体隐含信号以实现高效协调的新挑战。

Code as Explanations: Executable and Verifiable LLM Explanations
代码即解释：可执行且可验证的大型语言模型解释
As large language models (LLMs) increasingly make consequential decisions and solve problems alongside humans, we must understand how models arrive at their conclusions. Understanding how an LLM behaves requires observing it across multiple inputs, yet free-text explanations such as chain-of-thought (CoT) are generated per instance and cannot be easily verified on new inputs. We propose {code explanations to address the mismatch in how LLM explanations are generated versus how they are evaluated: a program explains LLM behavior across a dataset of inputs, and its execution enables automatic verification. We define Consistency metric that measures the fraction of inputs on which the program reproduces the LLM's outputs, and use it to evaluate and select candidate programs. Across three open-source code-capable LLMs and eight tasks, we find that LLMs can produce highly consistent code explanations in several settings. Code explanations are also 6$\times$ to 100$\times$ shorter than CoT, and better align with model behavior in 9 out of 24 settings. However, consistency is significantly lower on tasks with more complex linguistic components, where generated programs reproduce model outputs no more frequently than an uninformative explanation baseline, and these programs often rely on longer hard-coded patterns. In experiments with Claude Opus 5.5, providing the model with consistency feedback and code execution improves explanation consistency across tasks, but consistency remains significantly lower on more complex tasks. Our findings establish executable code as a novel, verifiable, and compact approach to explaining LLM behavior and highlight verification feedback as a promising direction for improving explanation consistency. The code for this paper is at https://github.com/rajesh-lab/code-as-explanations.
中文翻译
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地与人类共同做出重要决策并解决问题，我们必须理解模型如何得出其结论。理解LLM的行为需要在多个输入上观察其表现，然而像思维链（CoT）这样的自由文本解释是针对每个实例生成的，难以在新输入上进行验证。我们提出{代码解释}来解决LLM解释生成方式与评估方式之间的不匹配问题：一个程序可以解释模型在一组输入上的行为，其执行过程支持自动验证。我们定义了一致性度量，用于衡量程序复现LLM输出的输入比例，并用它来评估和选择候选程序。在三个开源代码能力LLM和八个任务中，我们发现LLM在某些场景下能生成高度一致的代码解释。代码解释比CoT短6至100倍，并且在24个场景中有9个更符合模型行为。然而，在具有更复杂语言成分的任务上，一致性显著降低，生成的程序复现输出的频率并不优于无信息量的基线解释，且这些程序往往依赖于更长的硬编码模式。在Claude Opus 5.5的实验中，向模型提供一致性反馈和代码执行可提升解释一致性，但在更复杂任务上仍显著偏低。我们的研究确立了可执行代码作为解释LLM行为的一种新颖、可验证且紧凑的方法，并强调验证反馈是提升解释一致性的一个有前景方向。本文代码见https://github.com/rajesh-lab/code-as-explanations。

Cleaning an Agent's History Hurts When the World Still Holds the Error
清理智能体的历史记录在世界仍保留错误时反而有害
Tool-using language-model agents accumulate histories of their own mistakes, and a system that compacts, repairs or hands off such a history decides what it says about those mistakes while their effects stay in the world. Prior work cleans the history at a fixed world, where showing a corrected step and saying it is done move together. We ask whether the same correct content helps or hurts depending on how the history states it and what the world holds. We freeze an agent mid-task with its exact world state, edit the history or the world, and let a receiver model continue. On AgentCE-Bench, after the agent commits a wrong value, we hold the world fixed and add the correct value by two natural edits: a truthful note beside the preserved error or the agent's own completed action. The note raises recovery, the share of remaining targets the receiver gets right, from 51% under a matched neutral note to 66%. The completed action lowers it below the unedited history, from 50% to 44%, so the value's presence alone does not predict the effect. On AppWorld, after a database write, we vary whether its receipt reports success or failure and whether its effect is present. A success receipt lowers whole-task success from 45% to 28% while the effect is absent and raises it from 38% to 49% once present. The two effects differ by 27 points pooled over two receivers, a difference positive in six of eight receivers at 97.5% and seven at 95%. A history edit's effect thus depends on the world it describes, and a history made to look clean while the world still holds the error can leave an agent worse off than no edit at all.
中文翻译
使用工具的语言模型智能体积累自身错误的历史记录，而一个对历史进行压缩、修复或移交的系统决定了其如何表述这些错误，但这些错误的影响仍在现实中存在。以往工作在固定的世界状态下清理历史，即展示修正步骤并声称已完成同步进行。我们探讨了相同正确内容在不同历史表述方式以及世界状态不同时，是帮助还是损害恢复效果。我们在智能体任务中途冻结其精确世界状态，编辑历史或世界，然后让接收模型继续执行。在AgentCE-Bench上，智能体犯错后，我们保持世界不变，通过两种自然编辑添加正确值：在保留错误旁添加真实注释，或由智能体自身完成动作。注释使恢复率从匹配中性注释下的51%提升至66%；而完成动作则使其降至44%，低于未编辑历史的50%，说明仅存在正确值本身并不能预测其影响。在AppWorld中，数据库写入后，我们改变其接收是否报告成功或失败，以及其效果是否存在。成功报告在效果不存在时将整体任务成功率从45%降至28%，而在效果存在时则升至49%。两种效应在两个接收者中合计相差27个百分点，其中六个接收者在97.5%置信水平下为正，七个在95%水平下为正。因此，历史编辑的效果取决于其所描述的世界状态；当历史看似已清理但世界仍保留错误时，智能体可能比完全不编辑更糟糕。
👤 Egor Pakhomov, Anurag Koul, Hongjun Liu, Bo Pang, Shafiq Joty et al. 📅 2026-11-10 🔗 arXiv

Learning When Diffusion Language Models Should Regenerate
学习扩散语言模型何时应重新生成
Remasking allows diffusion language models to revisit generated text, but effective correction requires knowing when and what to revise. Our diagnosis on Sudoku reveals two gaps. Hidden states contain error information that token confidence does not reliably expose, and remasking an isolated error usually reproduces it when the surrounding context remains fixed. These findings motivate DiVR , a verifier that predicts whether a partially generated sequence will reach a correct answer. Trained on the final correctness of the model's own trajectories, DiVR decides when to regenerate a block and which alternative to retain, while the generator itself stays unchanged. On five reasoning benchmarks with LLaDA-8B-Base, DiVR raises the average accuracy of the fine-tuned generator from 35.3\% to 43.4\% at $1.9$-$3.5\times$ the cost of greedy decoding, above search baselines that use $3.8$-$7.4\times$. It also complements post-training such as supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, and generalizes to other generators, including Dream.
中文翻译
重掩码机制使扩散语言模型能够重新审视已生成文本，但有效的修正需要明确何时以及如何修改。我们在数独任务上的诊断揭示了两个关键差距：隐藏状态中包含错误信息，而词元置信度无法可靠暴露；在上下文固定的情况下，仅对孤立错误进行重掩码通常会重现该错误。这些发现促使我们提出 DiVR——一种验证器，用于预测部分生成序列能否达到正确答案。DiVR 在模型自身轨迹的最终正确性上进行训练，决定何时重新生成一个区块以及保留哪个替代方案，而生成器本身保持不变。在五个推理基准测试中，使用 LLaDA-8B-Base 模型，DiVR 将微调后生成器的平均准确率从 35.3% 提升至 43.4%，代价仅为贪婪解码的 1.9–3.5 倍，优于使用 3.8–7.4 倍代价的搜索基线。此外，DiVR 还能与后训练方法（如监督微调和强化学习）互补，并可泛化至其他生成器，包括 Dream 模型。
👤 Minhae Oh, Jeonghye Kim, Jinwoo Koo, Yeongoon Kim, Jimin Seo et al. 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Goal Encoding in LLMs
大语言模型中的目标编码
We investigate whether and how LLMs encode goals: internal neural representations that precede and guide action, and that are flexible enough to be realized through many different execution paths. We construct a coding setting with multiple sub-tasks that allows us to evaluate goal-seeking behavior and the selection of sub-goals. In this setting, we localize an encoding of the next sub-goal in the outputs of a sparse set of attention heads. The same heads can be used to decode the LM's future actions in natural language (e.g., "I'm checking if a word is a palindrome"). We verify that this encoding is causal for next action and is plastic: transplanting it into a different environment, e.g., another programming language, triggers the same operation realized with a different sequence of tokens. The LM also sustains the encoding throughout the action's execution, which is puzzling because a transformer LM without recurrence cannot carry a state forward for more tokens than it has layers left. We propose that it does not need to. Under a non-recurrent goal-seeking (NRGS) account, the LM frequently re-reads the goal from the task specification, compares it with the progress visible in the growing context, and pursues what remains. We test the predictions of this account with targeted causal interventions.
中文翻译
我们研究大语言模型是否以及如何编码目标：即在行动之前存在且引导行为的内部神经表征，其具备足够的灵活性以通过多种不同执行路径实现。我们构建了一个包含多个子任务的编码场景，以评估目标导向行为及子目标选择能力。在此设定下，我们定位到稀疏注意力头输出中存在下一个子目标的编码。相同注意力头可用于将模型未来动作以自然语言解码（例如，“我正在检查一个词是否是回文”）。我们验证了该编码对下一步动作具有因果作用，并具备可塑性：将其移植到不同环境（如另一种编程语言）时，仍会触发相同操作，只是通过不同的词元序列实现。此外，模型在整个动作执行过程中持续维持该编码，这令人困惑，因为无循环结构的 Transformer 模型无法将状态传递超过其层数。我们提出非递归目标寻求（NRGS）解释：模型频繁从任务说明中重新读取目标，与不断增长的上下文中的进展进行比对，并继续推进剩余任务。我们通过针对性的因果干预测试了该理论的预测。
👤 Arnab Sen Sharma, Eric Todd, Sheridan Feucht, Andy Arditi, Rohit Gandikota et al. 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Parameter-Efficient Conciseness Adaptation: Differentiable Length Control for Concise Clinical Reasoning in Smaller Language Models
参数高效简洁性适配：面向小型语言模型的可微长度控制以实现简洁临床推理
Smaller language models (SLMs) make it feasible to deploy clinical natural language processing where large proprietary models are too costly, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning allows them to be specialised on commodity hardware. One obstacle persists after training: on medical tasks these models answer at far greater length than the clinical question requires. In clinical decision support and point-of-care use, where time is short, such verbosity is hard to act on even when the underlying reasoning is sound. Existing length-control methods depend on preference optimisation, reward models, or proprietary teachers that undercut the accessibility making SLMs attractive. We introduce Parameter-Efficient Conciseness Adaptation (PECA), which adds a single differentiable conciseness term to the QLoRA supervised fine-tuning objective. Because the token count is discrete and non-differentiable, PECA instead raises the end-of-sequence probability once generation exceeds a token budget, a smooth signal optimised during ordinary training, with no preference data, no auxiliary model, and a single commodity GPU. On a 1.7-billion-parameter model fine-tuned on the medical-o1-reasoning-SFT corpus, PECA reduces mean response length by 72.8% while improving multiple-choice diagnostic accuracy from 40.8% to 44.0%. A sweep over the conciseness strength traces an interpretable accuracy-to-length frontier for balancing brevity and diagnostic completeness.

Discovering Effective Agent Memory Designs through Program Evolution
通过程序演化发现有效的智能体记忆设计
Program evolution provides a promising approach to discover agent memory designs and study the choices underlying their effectiveness. We introduce MAPE, a framework that evolves whole memory programs from minimal seeds through a standardized lifecycle interface and an iterative diagnosis-proposal loop. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval yield an absolute accuracy gain of up to 12% over manually designed baselines at 35% of the serving cost, with further evaluations supporting transfer across serving backbones and benchmarks. Empirical analyses and ablations identify and validate two recurring design patterns: retaining raw conversations as retrievable evidence preserves details omitted from extracted memories, while composing retrieved evidence according to query intent helps the answering model use it effectively. Transplanting the composition module into five existing memory systems improves accuracy across all five. Beyond these patterns, the search objective shapes where computation goes: optimizing serving cost alongside accuracy shifts computation from LLM-based memory construction to selective processing at query time while retaining competitive accuracy. Together, these findings offer empirical lessons for agent memory design. Code is available at https://github.com/ash-ding/MAPE.
中文翻译
程序演化为发现智能体记忆设计并研究其有效性背后的决策提供了有前景的方法。我们提出MAPE框架，通过标准化生命周期接口和迭代诊断-提案循环，从极简种子演化出完整的记忆程序。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上的实验表明，相比人工设计基线，MAPE在仅35%服务成本下实现了最高达12%的绝对准确率提升，并进一步验证了其在不同服务后端和基准间的迁移能力。实证分析与消融研究识别并验证了两种反复出现的设计模式：保留原始对话作为可检索证据，可保存从提取记忆中遗漏的细节；根据查询意图组合检索到的证据，有助于回答模型有效利用信息。将该组合模块移植到五个现有记忆系统中，均提升了准确率。此外，搜索目标决定了计算分配方式：在优化服务成本的同时兼顾准确率，促使计算从基于大语言模型的记忆构建转向查询时的选择性处理，同时保持竞争力。这些发现为智能体记忆设计提供了实证启示。代码已公开于https://github.com/ash-ding/MAPE。
👤 Xinheng Ding, Seyyed Saeid Cheshmi, Ziyang Zhang, Tairan Chen, Wenya Xie et al. 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Evaluating Pretrained Language Semantics for Structured EHR Prediction Tasks
评估预训练语言模型语义在结构化电子健康记录预测任务中的应用
Structured Electronic Health Records (EHR) models typically treat each clinical concept as a dataset-specific token, discarding the natural-language semantics associated with the event. As a result, relationships between concepts must largely be learned from EHR co-occurrence, without directly exploiting any prior semantic knowledge. We investigate how pretrained causal language models can be adapted for longitudinal EHR prediction by developing tEHR. Here, we represent events with their natural language description and use this text stream to apply EHR-adaptive continued pretraining, followed by task-specific discriminative fine-tuning using LoRA, utilising the LLM as a longitudinal patient-encoder. We evaluate this approach across four prediction tasks on the MIMIC-IV dataset and pancreatic cancer prediction on the CPRD dataset at 1 to 12 month horizons, with tEHR using the Mistral-7B-v0.3 backbone achieving the strongest overall performance. Ablations show that performance declines when clinical descriptions are removed or misaligned, when numerical values are omitted, and when either stage of adaptation is removed, while explicit elapsed-time descriptions provide little additional benefit. Analysis of the learned patient representations further reveals clinically coherent structure associated with distinct patterns of disease risk and clinical history.

Auditing AI peer reviewers: a benchmark on real scientific papers
审计AI同行评审：基于真实科学论文的基准测试
LLM-based peer review is now used at production scale, but its evaluation remains limited. We introduce a benchmark that injects objective, localisable errors of four types (general issues, mathematics, numerics, citations) at three severity levels into real papers and measures each reviewer's detection rate by error type and severity, together with its false-positive rate on clean copies. We also present Skepthical, a multi-agent reviewer that verifies citation-backed claims by retrieving the cited papers, mathematics with computer algebra, and numerical claims with executed code. We apply the benchmark to two corpora of 15 recent arXiv preprints each, one in astrophysics and one in machine learning (ML), injecting a total of 357 errors, and evaluate twelve reviewer systems: six single-LLM reviewers, two Claude Code configurations, three publicly available reviewers, and Skepthical. The choice of model has the largest impact on detection rate: given the same prompt, three frontier LLMs differ by about 50 percentage points in total detection on both corpora. Tools and scaffolding help when they contribute what the model cannot supply on its own. In particular, on astrophysics, Claude Code improves on a single Claude call in mathematics and numerics detection, and enforced citation retrieval gives Skepthical the highest citation error detection rate of all systems by a large margin. This citation lead, however, is not observed on ML, where the models appear to know the literature better and Skepthical's citation detection rate is statistically indistinguishable from that of the best single LLM. False-positive rates are below 10% for most systems, but vary with prompt, model, and scaffolding, with mathematics flags the main source of false positives. The evaluation pipeline, the injected errors, and the adjudication logs are released as a benchmark.

基于大语言模型（LLM）的同行评审现已大规模应用，但其评估仍有限。我们提出一个基准测试，将四种类型（普遍性问题、数学、数值、引用）的三类严重程度的客观可定位错误注入真实论文中，并测量每个评审系统在不同错误类型和严重程度下的检测率，以及在干净样本上的误报率。我们还提出了Skepthical，一种多智能体评审系统，通过检索被引论文、使用计算机代数处理数学内容、通过执行代码验证数值声明来验证引用支持的主张。我们将该基准测试应用于两个各包含15篇近期arXiv预印本的语料库（天体物理学和机器学习），共注入357个错误，并评估了十二种评审系统：六种单LLM评审器、两种Claude Code配置、三种公开可用的评审器以及Skepthical。模型选择对检测率影响最大：在相同提示下，三种前沿LLM在两个语料库中的总检测率相差约50个百分点。当工具和框架能补充模型自身无法提供的能力时，它们才真正有效。特别是在天体物理学领域，Claude Code在数学和数值检测上优于单一Claude调用；强制引用检索使Skepthical在所有系统中取得了显著更高的引用错误检测率。然而，在机器学习领域，这一优势未显现，因为模型似乎更熟悉文献，Skepthical的引用检测率与最佳单LLM无统计差异。大多数系统的误报率低于10%，但随提示、模型和框架变化，数学标记是主要误报来源。评估流程、注入的错误及裁决日志已作为基准发布。

SPADE: Soft Prompts for Ai-text DEtection
SPADE：用于AI文本检测的软提示
The easy access to Large Language Models (LLMs) has made AI-generated texts on the web increasingly ubiquitous, making AI-text detection a crucial task. Prompts greatly influence the generation process, but existing detectors focus solely on the generated texts --- supervised methods learn from labeled texts only, and zero-shot methods rely on the text's probability distributions --- ignoring the underlying generation process, which limits their robustness in open-world scenarios. We introduce SPADE: Soft Prompts for Ai-text DEtection, a novel framework for detecting AI-generated text that transfers better across datasets. Specifically, we measure the prompt's influence and use it as a discriminative signal. We characterize the prompt's influence by introducing a Pointwise Mutual Information (PMI) term that captures how much a prompt explains a text. Based on this quantity, SPADE trains one soft prompt per class to amplify the separation, and predicts the class whose prompt best explains the candidate text. Extensive experiments on several benchmarks against various detectors demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of our method, achieving state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on out-of-distribution (OOD) datasets.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）的易获取性使得网络上的AI生成文本日益普遍，因此AI文本检测成为关键任务。提示对生成过程有重大影响，但现有检测器仅关注生成文本本身——监督方法仅从标注文本中学习，零样本方法依赖文本的概率分布——忽略了底层生成过程，限制了其在开放世界场景中的鲁棒性。我们提出SPADE：用于AI文本检测的软提示，一种新型框架，可在不同数据集间实现更好的迁移。具体而言，我们测量提示的影响并将其作为判别信号。通过引入点互信息（PMI）项来刻画提示对文本的解释力。基于此，SPADE为每类训练一个软提示，以增强类别间分离度，并预测哪个提示最能解释候选文本。在多个基准上对多种检测器的广泛实验表明，该方法具有出色的鲁棒性和有效性，在分布外（OOD）数据集上达到最先进的性能。

Human or Synthetic? What a Fixed Budget Buys for Post-Training
真人还是合成？固定预算下后训练所能带来的效果
Post-training of large language models typically combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with preference-based fine-tuning such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). Human-annotated and synthetically generated preference data differ substantially in per-sample cost, but prior work typically holds sample count fixed when comparing the two. In this work, we explicitly study the interplay between cost and performance by comparing the effect of post-training with human and synthetic preference data under a fixed, dollar-matched annotation budget. We train and evaluate multiple base model families across helpfulness and summarization tasks. We find that synthetic data is far more cost-efficient at low-to-moderate budgets, achieving high win rates with as little as \$200--\$400. On helpfulness, human data closes the gap only at budgets roughly two orders of magnitude larger; on summarization, it does not close the gap at any tested budget. We also find that human-annotated data varies in reliability: one widely used human preference source substantially underperforms another, and the gap persists even after standard remedies such as domain restriction and deduplication.
中文翻译
大型语言模型的后训练通常结合监督微调（SFT）与基于偏好的微调方法，如直接偏好优化（DPO）。真人标注与合成生成的偏好数据在每样本成本上存在显著差异，但以往研究通常在比较两者时保持样本数量不变。本研究通过在固定美元预算下对比真人与合成偏好数据对后训练的影响，明确探讨了成本与性能之间的权衡关系。我们在多个基础模型家族上训练并评估其在帮助性与摘要任务上的表现。结果表明，在低至中等预算下，合成数据远比真人数据更具成本效益，仅需200-400美元即可实现高胜率；在帮助性任务上，真人数据需约两数量级更大的预算才能缩小差距；在摘要任务上，即便在测试范围内也未能弥补差距。此外，我们发现真人标注数据的可靠性存在差异：一种广泛使用的真人偏好来源明显劣于另一种，且即使经过领域限制和去重等标准修复措施，差距依然存在。
👤 Shourik Banerjee, Ethan Mendes, Mohit Raghavendra, Wei Xu, Alan Ritter 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Pretraining Data Statistics Shape the Phases of Learning Entity Comparison in Language Models
预训练数据统计特征塑造语言模型学习实体比较的阶段
How does data shape language model (LM) behavior throughout pretraining? We investigate this question through a case study on entity comparison, e.g., Between France and Brazil, which country is larger?, across nine comparison tasks spanning five entity types. We begin with controlled experiments in which we train small LMs (163M parameters) on mixtures of natural text from pretraining corpora and synthetic task data. Our analysis identifies three distinct phases of learning: (1) an early phase where the LM selects entities by frequency, (2) a middle phase where the LM selects entities by position in the prompt (first vs. last mentioned entity), and (3) a late phase where the LM selects the entity that is the correct answer. We show that the emergence of these three phases is causally determined by statistical properties of the training data. With small amounts of task-specific synthetic data, we observe only the first two phases and the model fails to learn the task; with large amounts, the model jumps directly from the frequency-based heuristic to solving the task correctly. Moreover, changing the entity frequency distribution from a naturally occurring Zipfian distribution (a small number of entities are very common and the vast majority are rare) to a uniform one weakens the frequency phase and speeds up learning of the task. Lastly, we show that the three phases of training dynamics from our controlled experiments appear in the open source model OLMo-2 32B. Together, our findings demonstrate that properties of pretraining data are causal drivers of heuristic learning and show that small-scale synthetic experiments can predict training dynamics at larger scales.
中文翻译
预训练数据如何影响语言模型（LM）在整个预训练过程中的行为？我们通过一项关于实体比较的任务案例研究来探讨这一问题，例如“在法国和巴西之间，哪个国家更大？”涵盖九个跨五类实体的比较任务。我们首先在小规模语言模型（163M参数）上进行受控实验，训练其在预训练语料库的自然文本与合成任务数据混合数据集上。分析揭示出三个明显的学习阶段：（1）早期阶段，模型根据实体出现频率选择；（2）中期阶段，模型根据提示中实体的位置（首次提及 vs. 最后提及）进行选择；（3）晚期阶段，模型开始正确选择答案实体。我们证明这三个阶段的出现是由训练数据的统计特性所决定的因果关系。当使用少量特定任务的合成数据时，模型仅表现出前两个阶段，无法学会任务；而当使用大量合成数据时，模型直接从基于频率的启发式跳到正确解题。此外，将实体频率分布从自然存在的齐普夫分布（少数实体非常常见，绝大多数罕见）改为均匀分布，会削弱频率阶段并加速任务学习。最后，我们发现这些受控实验中观察到的三个训练动态阶段也出现在开源模型OLMo-2 32B中。我们的研究结果表明，预训练数据的特性是启发式学习的因果驱动因素，并证明小规模合成实验可预测大规模训练动态。

Outcome-Mass Alignment: Controlling Length-Dependent Trajectory Influence in Multi-Turn LLM Agent Reinforcement Learning
结果质量对齐：控制多轮LLM智能体强化学习中的长度依赖轨迹影响
To keep long interaction contexts tractable, stepwise group-relative training for multi-turn LLM agents materializes each source trajectory into multiple response rows and valid tokens. This expansion creates an outer-accounting problem distinct from within-trajectory credit assignment: row-based outcome statistics can count one episode outcome multiple times, while token-level reduction can assign unequal aggregate objective mass across trajectories. We term these effects outcome-statistics multiplicity and objective-mass imbalance. We introduce Outcome-Mass Alignment (OMA), a two-component method that makes both accounting choices explicit. Outcome Alignment computes outcome statistics over unique source trajectories, while Mass Alignment controls aggregate objective mass after within-trajectory aggregation of optimizer-native token objectives. Neither redesigns the base optimizer's credit assignment, importance ratios, or clipping. Across matched ALFWorld GRPO comparisons at two model scales, where failed trajectories typically expand much more than successful ones, OMA improves matched-horizon endpoint success by 7.0-9.4 percentage points. Ordinary WebShop exhibits weak or reversed expansion-outcome coupling and correspondingly mixed effects, whereas in a success-oriented WebShop setting, WebShop-SOL, OMA improves success nAUC from 0.399 to 0.511. Component ablations and GiGPO and GSPO comparisons further support component separability and cross-optimizer compatibility. These results show that trajectory expansion always defines an implicit weighting rule, but its practical effect depends on how expansion couples to outcomes and optimizer-native updates.
中文翻译
为保持长交互上下文的可处理性，多轮LLM智能体的逐步分组相对训练将每个源轨迹展开为多个响应行和有效标记。这种扩展带来了不同于轨迹内信用分配的外部会计问题：基于行的结果统计可能多次计算同一episode的结果，而基于token的缩减可能导致不同轨迹间聚合目标质量不均。我们将这两种效应称为结果统计多重性和目标质量失衡。我们提出结果-质量对齐（OMA），一种包含两个组件的方法，使这两种会计决策显式化。结果对齐在唯一源轨迹上计算结果统计，而质量对齐在轨迹内聚合优化器原生token目标后控制总目标质量。该方法不改变基础优化器的信用分配、重要性比率或裁剪机制。在两种模型规模下对ALFWorld GRPO进行匹配比较，其中失败轨迹通常比成功轨迹扩展得更严重，OMA使匹配时间终点的成功率提升了7.0-9.4个百分点。普通WebShop任务表现出弱或反向的扩展-结果耦合，相应效果混杂；而在以成功为导向的WebShop设置（WebShop-SOL）中，OMA将成功率nAUC从0.399提升至0.511。组件消融实验及与GiGPO、GSPO的对比进一步支持各组件的可分离性与跨优化器兼容性。这些结果表明，轨迹扩展始终隐含一种加权规则，但其实际影响取决于扩展如何与结果及优化器原生更新相耦合。

Towards Green Text Generation: Memory-Based Speculative Decoding
迈向绿色文本生成：基于记忆的推测解码
Speculative decoding accelerates text generation with large language models by letting a cheap draft model propose tokens that a large verifier checks in a single forward pass. We introduce Olifant, a draft model built on a memory-based classifier. It predicts the next token from a four-token window in under 0.3 ms on CPU, requiring no GPU, no backpropagation, and no accelerator memory. We measure throughput and energy under two verifier regimes on consumer hardware. Without a KV cache, drafting is nearly free: a domain-matched Olifant model reaches 38.1% acceptance on EU legal text, delivering 2.43x throughput and 2.51x lower energy per token than the neural verifier model alone. With a KV-cached verifier, itself a ~10x energy saver, drafting costs matter: a TinyLlama draft model halves throughput and triples energy despite high acceptance, whereas Olifant still yields 1.25x throughput and the lowest energy in the study (93 J per 100 tokens, 14x below the uncached baseline). We further show that KV caching decouples time from energy: verification of longer drafts raises power draw, shifting the energy-optimal draft length below the speed-optimal one. Speculative decoding is not dead in the age of fast verifiers, provided drafting is nearly free.
中文翻译
推测解码通过让一个低成本的草稿模型提出候选词，由大型验证模型在单次前向传播中进行校验，从而加速大语言模型的文本生成。我们提出了Olifant，一种基于记忆分类器构建的草稿模型。它能在CPU上以不到0.3毫秒的时间从四token窗口预测下一个词，无需GPU、反向传播或加速器内存。我们在消费级硬件上测量了两种验证器模式下的吞吐量和能耗。在无KV缓存的情况下，草稿几乎免费：与领域匹配的Olifant模型在欧盟法律文本上的接受率达38.1%，相比仅使用神经验证模型，吞吐量提升2.43倍，每token能耗降低2.51倍。在使用KV缓存的验证器下（本身可节省约10倍能耗），草稿成本变得重要：尽管TinyLlama草稿模型接受率高，但其吞吐量减半、能耗翻倍；而Olifant仍实现1.25倍吞吐量提升，并在研究中达到最低能耗（每100个token 93焦耳，比未缓存基线低14倍）。我们进一步表明，KV缓存将时间与能耗解耦：更长的草稿会增加功耗，使能量最优的草稿长度低于速度最优的长度。只要草稿几乎免费，推测解码在快速验证器时代仍未过时。

Where in the Input Does a One-Pass Decision Encoder Look?
单次决策编码器在输入中的何处进行查看？
Laya is a small model that answers a multiple-choice question about a text in one pass: the question, the options (each with a name and a description) and the text form one sequence of at most 512 tokens, the window, in the English release. A longer text is silently cut at the end, and we measure what that costs. Test documents place a held-out row's text, the evidence, at a chosen token offset among distractor text; 177 items, five tasks, three checkpoints (released, short-row fine-tune, long-document fine-tune), four window sizes and 93,275 scored requests. When the evidence lies beyond the window, accuracy falls to 26.4% for the released checkpoint (27.2% for both fine-tunes), near the 27.0% of guessing and below the 35.6% of always giving the commonest label. Confidence gives no warning (AUROC 0.50 to 0.55 for telling cut from read requests; 0.5 = chance), and the runtime's truncation flag also fires on requests whose evidence was visible. The released checkpoint reads visible evidence badly once text precedes it: 38.5% pooled against 60% when the evidence comes first, so a larger window alone gains +6.2 points. Fine-tuning on long documents adds +27.6 points on visible evidence over the short-row fine-tune (95% interval +22.3 to +33.1) and nothing on cut evidence. That is an in-family result: training rows and test documents share generator and question types, and the main filler is of the training kind. With encyclopedic filler never seen in training the gain is +13.2 (+9.9 to +16.6), with rows of other tasks as filler -2.0. Among training-free rearrangements a 312-token window scan helps most (+11.9 points over the default for the released checkpoint) at six to eleven forward passes. Documents are synthetic, labels are LLM-made and one backbone is studied, so these diagnose this model, not long documents in general.
中文翻译
Laya 是一个小型模型，能够在单次遍历中回答关于文本的多选题：问题、选项（每个包含名称和描述）以及文本共同构成最多512个标记的序列窗口（英文版）。较长的文本会在末尾被无声截断，我们测量这种截断带来的损失。测试文档将待保留行的文本（证据）放置在干扰文本中的指定标记偏移位置；共177个样本，五个任务，三个检查点（发布版、短行微调、长文档微调），四种窗口大小，93,275次评分请求。当证据位于窗口之外时，发布版检查点的准确率降至26.4%（两个微调版本分别为27.2%），接近随机猜测的27.0%，低于始终选择最常见标签的35.6%。置信度无法提供预警（区分截断与完整读取请求的AUROC为0.50至0.55，0.5表示随机水平），且运行时的截断标志也对可见证据的请求触发。发布版检查点在文本前置时对可见证据的读取表现较差：整体准确率为38.5%，而当证据位于开头时为60%，因此仅增大窗口即可提升+6.2分。在长文档上进行微调，在可见证据上的性能比短行微调提升+27.6分（95%置信区间+22.3至+33.1），而在截断证据上无提升。该结果属于同家族：训练行与测试文档共享生成器和问题类型，主要干扰项为训练中出现的类型。若使用训练中从未见过的百科类干扰项，收益为+13.2分（+9.9至+16.6）；若使用其他任务的行作为干扰项，则收益为-2.0。在无需训练的重排方法中，312个标记窗口扫描效果最佳（相对于发布版检查点提升+11.9分），需六到十一轮前向传播。文档为合成数据，标签由大语言模型生成，仅研究一种主干模型，因此这些结果诊断的是该模型本身，而非一般长文档情况。
👤 Mohammed Nayeem Khan, Allan Kevin T 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

daVinci-Prover: Agent-Native Mid-Training for Formal Theorem Proving
daVinci-Prover：面向形式定理证明的原生智能体中期训练
Advances in large language models (LLMs) are rapidly transforming formal theorem proving. Yet further progress remains constrained by the lack of a transparent and effective data recipe for systematically improving theorem-proving models. In this work, we argue that effective agentic provers require strong mathematical skill priors to ground theorem proving and agentic trajectories to enable long-horizon, interactive proof construction. To this end, we synthesize an agent-native 33.7B-token mid-training corpus covering decomposition, search, self-verification, and revision, together with verifier-grounded agentic trajectories. We further introduce the daVinci-Prover harness, which enables models to construct proofs and interact with a Lean verifier in an isolated workspace, and use it to collect approximately 36K high-quality agent trajectories for supervised fine-tuning. Our mid-training recipe yields an average improvement of 13.7 points over the matched Qwen3-8B SFT-only baseline across MiniF2F, FATE-M, and ProofNet. When applied to the Qwen3.5 series, our approach also yields strong performance across theorem-proving benchmarks and achieves near-saturating MiniF2F performance with memory compaction. The results highlight agent-native mid-training as an effective foundation for developing stronger interactive theorem-proving agents.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）的进步正在迅速改变形式定理证明领域。然而，进一步发展仍受限于缺乏透明且有效的数据配方来系统性地提升定理证明模型。本文认为，高效的智能体式证明者需要强大的数学技能先验以支撑定理证明，并通过智能体轨迹实现长周期、交互式的证明构建。为此，我们构建了一个原生智能体的337亿标记中期训练语料库，涵盖分解、搜索、自我验证和修订等内容，并结合验证器引导的智能体轨迹。我们还提出了daVinci-Prover工具链，使模型能在隔离工作空间中与Lean验证器交互并构建证明，据此收集了约3.6万条高质量智能体轨迹用于监督微调。我们的中期训练方案在MiniF2F、FATE-M和ProofNet基准上相较匹配的Qwen3-8B仅SFT基线平均提升13.7分。应用于Qwen3.5系列时，该方法在多个定理证明基准上表现出色，并在内存压缩下达到接近饱和的MiniF2F性能。结果表明，原生智能体中期训练是开发更强交互式定理证明智能体的有效基础。

Beyond Compressing Everything: Contrastive Memory Learning for Selective Context Compression
超越全面压缩：用于选择性上下文压缩的对比记忆学习
As large language models (LLMs) advance toward increasingly long inputs, soft context compression offers a promising approach to efficient inference by converting long inputs into compact memory slots. Under limited memory budgets, effective context compression remains challenging because task-relevant evidence varies substantially across queries, ranging from localized evidence to multiple distant spans or information dispersed throughout the full document. Instead of compressing everything indiscriminately, we propose CoMem, a query-guided selective context compression framework that learns memory representations conditioned on both the document and the query. CoMem develops Contrastive Compressor Learning to achieve query-sensitive yet context-preserving memory representations. To inject a strong prior over query-dependent evidence distributions, we devise a same-context hard negative mining strategy by pairing a single document with multiple queries, requiring the compressor to selectively retain information according to each query's distinct evidence needs. To address the restricted information flow imposed by causal masking, we further enable bidirectional attention among memory slots. Experiments demonstrate that CoMem consistently outperforms existing context compression methods across compression ratios on question answering and RULER-NIAH benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/scar-on/CoMem.
中文翻译
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）处理越来越长的输入，软上下文压缩通过将长输入转换为紧凑的记忆槽，为高效推理提供了有前景的解决方案。在有限的内存预算下，有效的上下文压缩仍然具有挑战性，因为任务相关的证据在不同查询间差异显著，可能从局部证据到多个遥远片段，或分散在整个文档中。我们不盲目压缩所有内容，而是提出CoMem——一种查询引导的选择性上下文压缩框架，其记忆表示同时依赖于文档和查询。CoMem采用对比压缩学习，实现对查询敏感但保留上下文信息的记忆表示。为注入对查询依赖证据分布的强先验，我们设计了一种同一文档配多个查询的硬负样本挖掘策略，要求压缩器根据每个查询的独特证据需求选择性保留信息。为克服因果掩码带来的信息流限制，我们进一步在记忆槽之间引入双向注意力机制。实验表明，CoMem在问答和RULER-NIAH基准上，于不同压缩比率下均持续优于现有上下文压缩方法。代码已公开于 https://github.com/scar-on/CoMem。
👤 Sikui Zhang, Haowei Liu, Ziyun Gan, Chunfeng Yuan, Guangze Gao et al. 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

IsharaBART: An efficient BART-Based Framework for Low-Resource Bangla Sign Language Translation
IsharaBART：面向低资源孟加拉手语翻译的高效BART框架
Millions of Bangla Sign Language (BdSL) users lack reliable automatic translation tools. Existing systems are limited by data scarcity, unidirectional design, and computationally expensive architectures that are not suitable for low-resource deployment. We introduce IsharaBART, a bidirectional generative framework for gloss-sentence translation in BdSL. IsharaBART uses Bidirectional and Auto-Regressive Transformers (BART)-based conditional language modeling with denoising learning objectives. We developed a parallel dataset of 1,000 professionally annotated gloss-sentence pairs, balanced across tenses and structures. We expanded the dataset to 4,831 pairs through four augmentation techniques: rule-based tense transformations, masked token substitutions, back-translation, and one-shot prompting. A compact denoising sequence-to-sequence model was pre-trained from scratch on monolingual Bangla text from six different corpora, then fine-tuned on the annotated and augmented datasets. We compare IsharaBART against four larger baseline models under identical conditions, where it achieves comparable or superior bidirectional performance. On the gloss-to-sentence (G2S) task, it attains Bilingual Evaluation Understudy (BLEU-4) score up to 51.7, and on the sentence-to-gloss (S2G) task, it achieves 61.40, outperforming multilingual models despite having fewer than 10% of the parameters. Ablation studies demonstrate that pre-training provides 62.5-90.4% faster convergence and 11.5-118% higher BLEU-4 than scratch. Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) reduces trainable parameters by 99.3%, but with 28.0-52.2% loss of BLEU-4 compared to full-parameter fine-tuning.
中文翻译
数百万孟加拉手语（BdSL）使用者缺乏可靠的自动翻译工具。现有系统受限于数据稀缺、单向设计以及计算成本高昂的架构，不适合低资源部署。我们提出IsharaBART，一种用于BdSL词符-句子翻译的双向生成框架。IsharaBART采用基于BART的双向与自回归Transformer条件语言建模，结合去噪学习目标。我们构建了一个包含1,000对专业标注的词符-句子平行数据集，涵盖时态与结构平衡。通过四种增强技术（基于规则的时态变换、掩码标记替换、反向翻译、单次提示）将数据集扩展至4,831对。我们从零开始在六个不同语料库的孟加拉语单语文本上预训练了一个紧凑的去噪序列到序列模型，随后在标注和增强数据集上进行微调。我们在相同条件下与四个更大的基线模型对比，IsharaBART表现出相当或更优的双向性能。在词符到句子（G2S）任务中，BLEU-4得分最高达51.7；在句子到词符（S2G）任务中，达到61.40，尽管参数不足其10%，仍优于多语言模型。消融实验表明，预训练使收敛速度提升62.5%-90.4%，BLEU-4提升11.5%-118%。低秩适配（LoRA）将可训练参数减少99.3%，但相比全参数微调，BLEU-4下降28.0%-52.2%。

From Large Language Models to Efficient System One Models via Adaptive Computation
通过自适应计算从大语言模型到高效系统一模型的转化
Transferring LLMs into System One decision models typically replaces the language modeling head with a decision head while retaining the full backbone, making every decision pay for all layers. Yet it remains unclear how much depth such decisions require. We find that decision information is concentrated in intermediate layers, with early layers building essential features and the final six layers removable without loss, while independently trained smaller models largely mimic the full model rather than developing specialized decision capabilities. These findings suggest that efficiency requires not simply smaller backbones, but shared computation across different depths. Building on this, we propose \method{}, which jointly trains a full model with prefix sub-models that share its final layers and decision head through self-distillation. A lightweight gate reads the hidden state at each exit to determine whether additional computation is needed, allowing one model to operate at multiple layers. Across seven decision benchmarks, our approach matches or outperforms independently trained models at every depth, while achieving better accuracy-efficiency trade-offs through adaptive computation.
中文翻译
将大语言模型（LLM）转化为系统一决策模型通常会用决策头替换语言建模头，同时保留完整的主干网络，导致每次决策都要付出所有层的计算开销。然而，决策所需的深度仍不明确。我们发现决策信息集中在中间层，早期层构建关键特征，而最后六层可移除而不损失性能；相比之下，独立训练的小型模型大多模仿完整模型，而非发展出专门的决策能力。这些发现表明，效率不仅需要更小的主干，还需要跨不同深度的共享计算。基于此，我们提出\method{}，该方法联合训练一个完整模型与前缀子模型，它们共享最终层和决策头，并通过自蒸馏实现。轻量级门控机制读取每个出口处的隐藏状态，以判断是否需要额外计算，使单一模型可在多个层级运行。在七个决策基准测试中，我们的方法在每个深度上均达到或超过独立训练模型的表现，同时通过自适应计算实现了更好的准确率-效率权衡。
👤 Minh-Anh Nguyen 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Indic-Task Gains and Regressions after Reinforcement Learning
强化学习后印地语任务的收益与退化
We run reinforcement learning (RL) with GRPO across four environments, starting from a supervised fine-tuned 30B-A3B mixture-of-experts model covering English and eight Indic languages. We compare the model before and after RL across 34 benchmarks in one evaluation campaign. MATH improves by 36.07 points, and most Indic benchmarks also improve. Yet XQuAD-Indic F1 falls 19.32 points as English answers rise from 8.7% to 91.7%; IndicQA falls 8.64, while English XQuAD gains 11.71 F1. A second RL run from the same supervised initialization adds an Indic environment together with language and repetition rewards. Compared with the released model, it improves both passage-answering and instruction-following benchmarks, along with preserving MATH performance. But it loses ground on reasoning and coding benchmarks. Several training choices changed together, so we cannot isolate the effect of any one of the reward terms. Script compliance remains unresolved. Adding a language reward did not ensure use of the requested script. Passage answering and script compliance therefore need direct evaluation, even when other benchmark scores improve.

我们在四个环境中使用 GRPO 进行强化学习（RL），起始于一个覆盖英语和八种印地语的 30B-A3B 混合专家模型。我们在一次评估活动中，对比了 RL 前后模型在 34 个基准测试上的表现。数学（MATH）成绩提升了 36.07 分，大多数印地语基准也有所提升。然而，XQuAD-Indic 的 F1 值下降了 19.32 分，英语答案占比从 8.7% 上升至 91.7%；IndicQA 下降 8.64，而英语 XQuAD 提升 11.71 F1。第二次 RL 运行从相同的监督微调初始化出发，增加了印地语环境以及语言和重复奖励。相比发布版本，它在段落问答和指令遵循基准上均有提升，并保持了 MATH 性能。但在推理和编码基准上表现退步。由于多个训练选择同时改变，无法孤立分析任一奖励项的影响。脚本合规性问题仍未解决。增加语言奖励并未确保使用请求的脚本。因此，即使其他基准分数提升，段落问答和脚本合规性仍需直接评估。

We Are What We Remember: Mental-State-Driven Memory for Long-Term Character Dialogue
我们即是我们所记忆的：基于心理状态的记忆机制用于长期角色对话
Shared history in human dialogue is not merely a repository of facts, but the foundation of evolving relationships and mutual understanding. However, mainstream conversational memory systems rely on passive semantic similarity over external fact stores, frequently retrieving topically similar records while missing relationally pivotal experiences. Inspired by cognitive psychology's Self-Memory System (SMS), we propose MindMem, a mental-state-driven memory framework that asynchronously consolidates shared experiences into source-linked mental states of the character, the user, and their relationship. At each turn, a specialized mental-state reasoner (Mind) contextualizes these persistent states to anticipate latent user needs, form proactive intentions, and recover supporting episodic evidence that surface-level matching overlooks. To evaluate history-sensitive adaptation beyond factual recall, we introduce MemReal-Bench, a longitudinal benchmark grounded in month-long real-user interactions across dyadic and group scenes. By pairing Direct Question Answering (DQA) with Situated Memory Realization (SMR), it tests whether agents bridge the gap between knowing past facts and naturally using shared history, world rules, and relational trajectories. On MemReal-Bench, MindMem reaches 59.03% SMR and 82.58% DQA, exceeding the strongest memory baseline by 24.19 and 9.19 percentage points, respectively, and outperforming oracle target-memory injection on SMR. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/jiazhicheng7-ctrl/MindMem.

人类对话中的共享历史不仅是事实的存储库，更是关系演变和相互理解的基础。然而，主流对话记忆系统依赖于外部事实库中被动的语义相似性，频繁检索主题相关记录，却忽略了具有关系关键意义的经历。受认知心理学中自我记忆系统（SMS）的启发，我们提出了MindMem，一种由心理状态驱动的记忆框架，能够异步地将共享经历整合为角色、用户及其关系的源链接心理状态。在每一轮对话中，专门的心理状态推理器（Mind）会 contextualize 这些持久状态，以预测潜在的用户需求，形成主动意图，并恢复表面匹配所忽略的支持性情景证据。为了评估超越事实回忆的历史敏感适应能力，我们引入了MemReal-Bench，一个基于跨二元和群体场景长达一个月的真实用户互动的纵向基准。通过将直接问答（DQA）与情境化记忆实现（SMR）相结合，它测试智能体是否能弥合知晓过去事实与自然运用共享历史、世界规则和关系轨迹之间的差距。在MemReal-Bench上，MindMem在SMR上达到59.03%，在DQA上达到82.58%，分别比最强的记忆基线高出24.19和9.19个百分点，并在SMR上优于oracle目标记忆注入。代码和数据集可在https://github.com/jiazhicheng7-ctrl/MindMem获取。

Show Your Work in Swahili: Reinforcement Learning for Native-Language Mathematical Reasoning
用斯瓦希里语展示你的工作：原生语言数学推理的强化学习方法
We present Fikiria, a two-stage SFT+GRPO recipe for Swahili mathematical reasoning. Its reward multiplies a format gate by a weighted sum of answer accuracy and the ChrF\texttt{++} similarity of the Swahili reasoning to an in-language reference chain of thought, so that training targets the surface fidelity of the reasoning trace and not only the final answer. We train on a 2.3k-problem Swahili dataset created by translating GSM8K with GPT-4.1 and filtering with SSA-COMET. On AfriMGSM Swahili, Fikiria-AfriqueQwen-14B reaches 79.2\%. Under the same fixed prompt and strict answer-format scoring, GPT-4o reaches 68.0\%, partly because it often fails the required answer format; IrokoBench reports 78.8\% for GPT-4o using a best-per-model prompt, a figure numerically close to ours but not directly comparable. In a small human evaluation with co-author annotators, GPT-4o's outputs were preferred overall. ChrF\texttt{++} measures surface fidelity, not the validity of individual reasoning steps.
中文翻译
我们提出Fikiria，一种针对斯瓦希里语数学推理的两阶段SFT+GRPO训练方案。其奖励函数将格式门控与答案准确率及斯瓦希里语推理过程与母语参考思维链之间的ChrF++相似度加权求和相乘，从而训练不仅关注最终答案，也关注推理轨迹表面一致性的模型。我们在一个由GPT-4.1翻译GSM8K并经SSA-COMET过滤生成的2.3k题斯瓦希里语数据集上进行训练。在AfriMGSM斯瓦希里语任务上，Fikiria-AfriqueQwen-14B达到79.2%的准确率。在相同固定提示和严格答案格式评分下，GPT-4o仅达68.0%，部分原因在于其经常不符合要求的答案格式；IrokoBench报告GPT-4o使用最佳模型提示时为78.8%，数值接近但不直接可比。在小规模人工评估中，由共同作者标注者偏好GPT-4o的输出。ChrF++衡量的是表面一致性，而非单个推理步骤的有效性。

Privileged Information Does Not Guarantee Privileged Guidance: Auditing On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Models
特权信息并不保证特权指导：对推理模型在策略内自蒸馏的审计
On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on next-token targets from a teacher, a frozen copy of the student that additionally receives privileged information, such as a worked solution, hidden from the student. Whether student gains reflect that information remains unclear. Taking the worked solution as privileged information, we audit four stages across three Qwen3 scales on mathematics: Source, whether the teacher can use the solution; Encoding, whether the resulting token targets are useful; Transmission, whether the student learns from the targets; and Expression, whether the student applies the learned behavior without the solution. With the solution, the teacher solves hard problems and recovers from student errors, yet the teacher does not reliably recognize errors, and its token targets do not favor correct trajectories. Repeated training makes successes within the student's reach more frequent rather than unlocking new ones, and corrections the student learns to predict are not reliably expressed in free generation. To isolate the solution's contribution, we vary its availability separately from teacher and student thinking modes. The largest gains occur when a teacher in thinking mode supervises a student trained and evaluated without an explicit thinking trace, and problem-only teachers often match or exceed privileged-solution teachers: the gain tracks the teacher's thinking mode rather than the solution, a pattern we call reasoning arbitrage. Accuracy gains alone therefore do not establish transfer of reasoning from privileged solutions: bottlenecks lie after Source, across Encoding, Transmission, and Expression. Future evaluations must measure what the solution adds to otherwise identical training.
中文翻译
在策略内自蒸馏（OPSD）中，学生模型在来自教师模型的下一个词目标上进行训练，该教师模型是学生模型的冻结副本，额外接收特权信息（如完整解题过程），而学生模型无法访问。学生模型获得的性能提升是否源于该信息尚不明确。我们将完整解题过程视为特权信息，对三个Qwen3规模下的数学任务进行了四个阶段的审计：来源（教师能否使用解题过程）、编码（生成的词目标是否有用）、传输（学生是否从目标中学习）和表达（学生在无解题过程情况下是否能正确应用所学行为）。结果显示，尽管教师在有解题过程时能解决难题并纠正学生错误，但教师并未可靠识别错误，其词目标也不倾向于正确路径。重复训练使学生可达的成功更频繁，但并未解锁新的可能性；学生学会预测的修正行为在自由生成中也未可靠表达。为隔离解题过程的贡献，我们分别改变其在教师和学生思考模式中的可用性。最大收益出现在教师处于思考模式时，监督一个在无显式思考痕迹下训练和评估的学生模型，且仅问题教师的表现往往与带特权解题过程的教师相当甚至更优：收益取决于教师的思考模式，而非解题过程本身，这一现象称为推理套利。因此，仅凭准确率提升无法证明从特权解题过程到推理能力的转移：瓶颈存在于来源之后的编码、传输和表达阶段。未来评估必须测量解题过程在其他条件完全相同的训练中带来的实际增益。
👤 Haoyi Qiu, Akshat Agarwal 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15989]

What Roles Do System Prompts Really Assign? Validating and Steering Occupational Role Vectors in LLMs
系统提示究竟分配了什么角色？验证与引导大语言模型中的职业角色向量
Applications built on large language models assign the model a role through its system prompt, intending to shape how it responds for their use case. We introduce a validation framework for extracting and injecting occupational role vectors, activation-level directions that capture how someone in a given occupational role would distinctively respond to a task or question. We validate these vectors through two complementary lenses, on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. The interpretability lens asks whether each vector is a stable, separable, domain-consistent representation of its role: extracted vectors are highly reproducible across repeated runs and sampling temperatures, remain separable even for very similar roles, and agree more with themselves across in-domain and out-of-domain questions than with other roles' signal. The control lens asks whether injecting a role vector during generation makes the model express the role, and sustain it over a conversation, as well as a role prompt does. On Qwen, injecting the vector alone makes the model express the intended role substantially more often than unsteered, but at best matches a role prompt; on both models, adding the vector to the prompt strengthens the role in single answers but did not slow the role's decline over 30 turns. Together, the two lenses show why both are needed: out of domain the role is expressed only subtly, yet the activations still encode it; and a vector that is stable and separable can still barely steer the model's behavior.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型的应用通过系统提示赋予模型特定角色，以期望其响应符合特定使用场景。我们提出了一种验证框架，用于提取和注入职业角色向量——一种激活层级的方向，捕捉某职业角色在面对任务或问题时的独特回应方式。我们通过两个互补视角在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型上验证这些向量：解释性视角关注向量是否为稳定、可分离且领域一致的角色表征——提取的向量在重复运行和不同采样温度下高度可重现，即使在非常相似的角色之间仍保持可分离性，并且在域内和域外问题上的自一致性高于与其他角色信号的一致性；控制视角则考察在生成过程中注入角色向量是否能使模型表现出该角色并维持整个对话过程中的角色特征，如同使用角色提示一样。在Qwen模型上，仅注入向量即可使模型更频繁地表达目标角色，但效果仍不及角色提示；在两个模型上，将向量加入提示后虽增强了单次回答中的角色表现，但未能延缓角色在30轮对话后的衰退。两者结合表明：在域外情况下，角色仅被微妙地表达，但激活仍编码了该角色信息；即使向量稳定且可分离，也可能难以有效引导模型行为。

Better Boundary Prediction Does Not Guarantee Better Structural Representation
更好的边界预测并不保证更好的结构表示
Punctuation marks often mark phrase boundaries, carrying linguistic information. Therefore, punctuation marks' abundance and feasibility as proxies of linguistic structure has led to suggestions that using punctuation marks to optimise on phrasal structure may help language models (LMs) improve their representations of argument, semantic, and syntactic structures. In this paper, we test the above claim in English across three primary LM architectures: decoder-only, encoder-only, and encoder-decoder LMs, by complementing traditional token prediction with punctuation restoration (PR). Our evidence, collected at the <1B parameter scale, suggests that optimising for boundary prediction does not necessarily guarantee higher-quality structural representation in decoder-only, encoder-decoder, and encoder-only LMs, failing to improve performance on downstream tasks that require understanding structure in linguistic input.

SO-Arena: Benchmarking the Capability Spectrum and Reliability of System One Models
SO-Arena：系统一模型能力谱与可靠性的基准测试
System One models promise fast, general-purpose decision-making without lengthy token generation. However, understanding their capability spectrum and decision reliability requires more than aggregate performance on current benchmarks. Existing evaluations often provide limited insight into the breadth of model capabilities and the reliability of their decisions. We introduce SO-Arena, a benchmark characterizing the capability spectrum and decision reliability of System One models across 11 domains and five capability families. For the capability spectrum, SO-Arena offers evidence-grounded evaluation through reliable sources and manual verification of reference labels against model-visible inputs; fine-grained capability assessment through domain-specific difficulty criteria; and realistic interactive evaluation, where models iteratively execute actions in sandboxes. Reasoning-intensive tasks further enable system one-two comparison. Beyond accuracy, SO-Arena assesses decision reliability through two complementary measures: decision stability under controlled option-set changes and missing-answer recognition when no valid option is available. Evaluations across 21 models reveal that the benefits of scaling and additional reasoning vary substantially across domains. More importantly, strong overall performance does not necessarily imply reliable decision-making: the best-performing System One model ranks only seventh in reliability. Together, these findings highlight the need to characterize capability spectrum and decision reliability as complementary dimensions, providing a systematic view of the strengths and limitations of System One models.
中文翻译
系统一模型承诺在无需长时间生成 token 的情况下实现快速、通用的决策。然而，理解其能力谱和决策可靠性，仅靠当前基准的聚合性能是不够的。现有评估往往难以揭示模型能力的广度及其决策的可靠性。我们引入 SO-Arena，一个涵盖 11 个领域和五类能力的基准，用于刻画系统一模型的能力谱和决策可靠性。在能力谱方面，SO-Arena 通过可靠来源和人工验证参考标签与模型可见输入的一致性，提供基于证据的评估；通过领域特定的难度标准实现细粒度能力评估；并通过沙箱中模型迭代执行动作的现实交互式评估。推理密集型任务进一步支持系统一与系统二的对比。除了准确率，SO-Arena 还通过两个互补指标评估决策可靠性：在可控选项集变化下的决策稳定性，以及当无有效选项时的缺失答案识别能力。对 21 个模型的评估表明，缩放效应和额外推理的收益在不同领域间差异显著。更重要的是，整体表现优异并不意味着决策可靠：表现最佳的系统一模型在可靠性上仅排名第 7。这些发现强调，应将能力谱和决策可靠性视为互补维度，从而系统性地揭示系统一模型的优势与局限。

Where Is Uncertainty Observed? A Mechanistic Understanding of Uncertainty Quantification in LLMs
不确定性在何处被观测？对大语言模型中不确定性量化机制的机理理解
Knowing when a large language model's (LLM's) response can be trusted is critical for its safe use. Uncertainty quantification (UQ) can help LLMs recognize if their responses may be unreliable. Existing taxonomies organize UQ methods by uncertainty source or estimation mechanism. They leave unresolved the question of where uncertainty is observed in the model's computation. The answer matters because the observation point determines whether an estimator has access to enough information to recover a given target, and how well it can recover it. Drawing on mechanistic interpretability, we define four mechanistic observation lenses: the structural lens observes component updates, the representational lens observes hidden states, the distributional lens observes token probabilities, and the semantical lens observes distributions over meanings. We relate these lenses through the residual stream and order them by the information they carry, which gives a principle for selecting UQ methods for different targets. We survey existing methods through the four lenses. Furthermore, we distinguish evaluation targets, criteria, and metrics to clarify different UQ evaluation protocols. Finally, we identify open problems and research directions to connect uncertainty estimation to the internal computation and downstream use of LLMs.
中文翻译
了解大语言模型（LLM）的响应何时可信，对于其安全使用至关重要。不确定性量化（UQ）有助于 LLM 识别其响应可能不可靠的情况。现有的分类体系按不确定性来源或估计机制组织 UQ 方法，但未解决不确定性在模型计算中被观测的位置这一关键问题。该位置至关重要，因为它决定了估计器是否能获得足够信息以恢复目标，以及恢复效果如何。基于机理可解释性，我们定义了四种机理观测视角：结构视角观察组件更新，表征视角观察隐藏状态，分布视角观察标记概率，语义视角观察意义分布。我们通过残差流关联这些视角，并按其所携带的信息量排序，从而为针对不同目标选择 UQ 方法提供原则。我们通过这四个视角综述现有方法。此外，我们区分了评估目标、标准和度量，以明确不同的 UQ 评估协议。最后，我们指出了开放性问题和研究方向，以促进不确定性估计与 LLM 内部计算及下游应用之间的联系。

Outside the Purview of the Reward: Capability Drift in Multilingual Post-Training
奖励之外：多语言后训练中的能力漂移
Post-training can improve rewarded behavior while weakening capabilities users still need. We study two GRPO branches from a shared supervised checkpoint in a multilingual mixture-of-experts model. The released branch gains 36.07 points on MATH but loses 19.32 F1 points on XQuAD-Indic, while XQuAD-English improves under the same scoring rule. An independently trained sibling adds Indic instruction following and language-aware shaping. It recovers the regressing Indic task scores and preserves MATH, but does not restore script compliance and trades off competition mathematics and code. In a later round, judge training is followed by a sharp extraction decline from the Indic branch; reward-free distillation from its earlier checkpoint restores most of the lost F1. A MATH score decomposition associates most of the measured gain with increased boxed-answer emission; accuracy among boxed answers changes little. These results show why benchmark scores should be read alongside the response forms they reward.

Can Agents Relive Past Experiences? Diagnosing Event-Affect Decoupling in Affective-Episodic Retrospection
智能体能否重温过往经历？情感-情景回溯中的事件-情感解耦诊断
As memory-augmented agents increasingly support long-term and personalized interactions, reliable recall of emotional experiences while preserving their associated affect becomes necessary. However, existing evaluations usually assess emotion accuracy and factual memory grounding separately, overlooking event-affect correspondence. In this work, we formulate Affective-Episodic Retrospection (AER) as a task that explicitly evaluates the recovery of past events, associated affects, and their correspondence, and define Event-Affect Decoupling as a failure mode where the correct emotion is recalled but associated with the wrong episode. To diagnose Event-Affect Decoupling, we propose EM-TRACE, a stage-wise framework that decomposes AER into retrieval, affect recovery, and realization. On a controlled GoEmotions-based benchmark, Emotion-only retrieval achieves 28.65% affect recovery but retrieves the target episode for only 4.44% of queries, yielding a UAR of 24.21%. Further analysis reveals that event retrieval and affect assignment introduce distinct binding failures. These findings show that reliable AER requires grounded recall of emotional experiences, where episode retrieval, affect recovery, event-affect binding, and generation quality should be evaluated jointly. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/A412/.

Faithful in Steps: Improving Generalization and Citation in RAG via Query Decomposition
步步忠实：通过查询分解提升RAG的泛化能力与引用准确性
Retrieval-augment generation is a prevalent strategy to mitigate hallucinations of LLMs. The attributable RAG (RAGQ) generates quotes for its answers. The quotes indicate which input contexts support the RAG to derive the answers, enhancing the answer's verifiability and trustworthiness. However, existing RAGQs exhibit significant degradation when dealing with questions that require multi-hop reasoning and multi-modal understanding, suffering from over-citation, implicit entity identification failure, and poor generalization. In this paper, we propose a novel RAGQ framework, namely QDRAG. QDRAG breaks down the input question into atomic subquestions to identify the implicit entities. Then, the reranker prunes context distractors to eliminate the downstream over-citation. To facilitate query decomposition, we propose two zero-shot approaches: QD-C and QD-R, which guide the QD MLLM to decompose the question based on context knowledge and retrieval rewards, respectively. One interesting finding is that finetuning on the QD task shows better generalizability compared to directly finetuning on the downstream RAGQ task. Experiments on four multi-modal QA benchmarks demonstrate QDRAG's efficacy in grounding answers and generating faithful citations. The framework significantly outperforms all the baselines on both in-domain and out-of-domain tests, even surpassing Gemini-Pro.

SimRec: Learning to Search, Ask, and Recommend from Simulated Interaction
SimRec：从模拟交互中学习搜索、提问与推荐
LLM-based recommendation agents can search catalogs, inspect products, ask users, and revise rejected recommendations. However, these behaviors are often governed by hand-designed workflows, while existing training methods optimize isolated components such as query rewriting or list ranking rather than the interaction policy as a whole. We present SimRec, a reinforcement learning framework that trains the full interaction policy of a tool-integrated recommendation agent. SimRec introduces two components. A task-anchored user simulator provides feedback on every recommendation: conditioned on the current task need rather than on user profile, and it can be an external LLM or the recommender itself under self-play. A task-aligned reward optimizes the whole trajectory with GRPO, crediting natural retrieval quality and valid tool use alongside the final hit, with training-only target injection to handle sparse hits over large catalogs. On ESCI and Amazon C4, SimRec improves the NDCG@100 of a Qwen3-4B agent from 32.23% to 35.42% and from 22.94% to 24.72%}, outperforming training-free agents on far larger backbones and task-aligned baselines on NDCG. Analysis shows that the trained agent verifies candidates, uses preferences, and recovers from rejected recommendations far more often than its untrained counterpart. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/SimRec .

Towards Better Legal Reasoning LLMs: Signal Balancing and Reward Scheduling
迈向更优的法律推理大模型：信号平衡与奖励调度
In the legal domain, LLMs can improve reliability and interpretability by generating explicit reasoning traces. However, training LLMs to produce high-quality reasoning traces remains challenging. Existing supervised fine-tuning methods struggle when reasoning traces are significantly longer than final answers, as the learning signal for the answer becomes diluted. Meanwhile, reinforcement learning methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization also have several limitations, including costly reward design, performance plateaus, and reward hacking. We therefore propose a two-stage training framework. In Stage I, JurisCoT-SFT employs a length-normalized dual objective to balance learning signals between reasoning traces and final answers. In Stage II, Lifecycle-Aware Backtrackable Policy Optimization dynamically evaluates and retires auxiliary reward signals based on their impact on final evaluation objectives, enabling efficient reward utilization. Our framework substantially improves legal reasoning performance on relevant tasks selected from multiple legal benchmarks, including ChJGBench, LawBench, and LegalBench. Experimental results demonstrate consistent improvements in both reasoning trace quality and final answer accuracy, while our models remain competitive with larger models.

在法律领域，大语言模型可通过生成显式的推理链条来提升可靠性和可解释性。然而，训练大模型生成高质量推理链条仍具挑战性。现有监督微调方法在推理链条远长于最终答案时面临学习信号稀释的问题。同时，如组相对策略优化（GRPO）等强化学习方法也存在奖励设计成本高、性能停滞和奖励滥用等局限。为此，我们提出两阶段训练框架。第一阶段，JurisCoT-SFT采用长度归一化的双目标函数，平衡推理链条与最终答案的学习信号。第二阶段，生命周期感知的可回溯策略优化（Lifecycle-Aware Backtrackable Policy Optimization）动态评估并淘汰对最终评估目标影响较小的辅助奖励信号，实现高效奖励利用。该框架在多个法律基准测试任务（包括ChJGBench、LawBench和LegalBench）中显著提升了法律推理性能。实验结果表明，推理链条质量与最终答案准确率均持续提升，且模型在性能上仍可与更大规模模型竞争。

Janus-MOPD: Tracing the Student Learning Dynamics in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation
Janus-MOPD：多教师在线蒸馏中学生学习动态的追踪
Multi-domain capabilities are critical for practically applicable Large Reasoning Models (LRMs). Therefore, Multi-teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD) has become a crucial part of the post-training process, integrating the capabilities of multiple domain-specific teacher models into a single student model. However, the student usually fails to acquire multi-domain capabilities simultaneously due to undesirable domain heterogeneity. Existing works have address this problem in view of temporal uneven efficiency and spatial hard allocation in external multi-teacher supervision. While in broader multi-domain training, varying degrees of performance degradation is still largely unexplained. In this paper, we shift the focus on how the student learns and integrates multi-domain capabilities internally, and then propose \textbf{\textsc{Janus-MOPD}}, a general learning dynamics framework of geometric representations for LRMs. Specifically, our main focus is on the internal performance of multi-domain capabilities on both temporal and spatial dimensions within the student. Temporally, our study shows that the student exhibits a Expansion-Consolidation-Optimization (ECO) three-phase learning dynamics, together with inherent limitations in learning efficiency and scaling. Spatially, we summarize two types of subspace locking within and across domains by tracing the representation space of the student, while explaining its clear learning directions but inherent cross-domain conflict. Therefore, \textsc{Janus-MOPD} yields a unified learning dynamics formulation, a geometric tracing methodology, and systematic findings of how the student learns and integrates multi-domain capabilities. Overall, we provide a \textsc{Janus} perspective to internally trace the continual evolution of the multi-domain capabilities within the model itself.

多领域能力对于实际可用的大推理模型至关重要。因此，多教师在线蒸馏（MOPD）已成为后训练过程中的关键环节，旨在将多个领域专用教师模型的能力整合到单一学生模型中。然而，由于领域异质性带来的不利影响，学生模型通常无法同时获得多领域能力。现有研究主要关注外部多教师监督中的时间效率不均与空间硬分配问题。但在更广泛的多领域训练中，不同程度的性能退化仍缺乏充分解释。本文聚焦于学生模型如何在内部学习并整合多领域能力，提出 extbf{ extsc{Janus-MOPD}}——一种针对大推理模型几何表示的学习动态通用框架。具体而言，我们关注学生模型在时间和空间维度上的多领域能力内部表现。时间上，研究发现学生表现出扩张-巩固-优化（ECO）三阶段学习动态，且存在学习效率与扩展性的内在限制。空间上，通过追踪学生表示空间，我们总结出域内与跨域的两种子空间锁定现象，揭示了清晰的学习方向但存在固有的跨域冲突。因此， extsc{Janus-MOPD}提出了统一的学习动态公式、几何追踪方法以及关于学生如何学习与整合多领域能力的系统性发现。总体而言，我们提供了 extsc{Janus}视角，以内部追踪模型自身多领域能力的持续演化过程。

Knowledge in the Harness, Reliability in the Weights: Teacher-Free Co-Evolution of an LLM Agent
知识在缰绳中，可靠性在权重里：无需教师的大模型智能体协同进化
Small open models suit private customer-service deployments but trail frontier models in two ways: they miss what the written policy requires in a given situation, and they act inconsistently on what they know. We study whether such a model can close both gaps from its own deployment logs and their reference outcomes, without a stronger teacher model. Our starting point is a Gemma 4 12B agent that reads its domain policy from on-demand skills rather than from its system prompt. In each round, the deployed system serves a fresh batch of tasks; the base model diagnoses failed conversations and writes rules into the skills, and a rule survives only if it passes code checks, counterexample self-review, a paired retest, and a noise-calibrated validation gate. Reinforcement learning (GRPO) then trains a LoRA adapter on complete multi-turn, tool-using conversations. On the retail and airline domains of $τ^2$-bench, without training or selecting on official tasks, the agent after two rounds improves on this starting point by 12.5 pass$^1$ points in retail and 19.5 in airline, and by 18.4 and 22.0 pass$^4$ points. It also exceeds the benchmark's reference agent, which runs the same model with the whole policy in its system prompt, by 11.8 and 8.0 pass$^1$ points and by 22.8 and 16.0 pass$^4$ points, closing most of the gap to three frontier models under the same protocol. A $2\times2$ ablation shows that the rules rescue tasks the agent never solved and carry policy knowledge that RL with the rules in context does not internalize, whereas the trained weights make solved tasks reliable.
中文翻译
小型开源模型适用于私有客户服务部署，但在两方面落后于前沿模型：一是无法理解特定情境下的书面政策要求，二是对其所知内容的行为不一致。我们研究该模型能否仅通过自身的部署日志及其参考结果，在无更强教师模型的情况下弥补这两项差距。起点是一个基于Gemma 4 12B的智能体，其领域政策通过按需调用技能获取，而非嵌入系统提示中。每轮中，部署系统处理一批新任务；基础模型诊断失败对话，并将规则写入技能库，只有通过代码检查、反例自审、成对重测和噪声校准验证门控的规则才能留存。随后，强化学习（GRPO）在完整的多轮、工具使用对话上训练LoRA适配器。在$τ^2$-bench的零售与航空领域，未在官方任务上进行训练或选择的情况下，经过两轮迭代，该智能体在零售领域提升12.5 pass$^1$分，在航空领域提升19.5分；在pass$^4$指标上分别提升18.4和22.0分。它还超越了基准参考智能体（同一模型但全政策置于系统提示中），在零售和航空领域分别领先11.8和8.0 pass$^1$分，以及22.8和16.0 pass$^4$分，基本弥合了与三个前沿模型在相同协议下的差距。2×2消融实验表明，规则挽救了智能体从未解决的任务，并承载了RL在上下文中无法内化的政策知识，而训练后的权重则使已解决问题更加可靠。

Breaking Bad: "Going Badly" Activations Drive Reward Hacking
走向失败：'表现不佳'激活信号驱动奖励劫持
Humans often cheat when a task is going badly and a goal feels out of reach. We explore and validate an analogous idea in language models: they exhibit a similar internal pressure state that precedes reward hacking. Using everyday stories (crucially, without any hacking examples), we extract a task badness direction that rises as failures accumulate across three models we study (Qwen3.6-27B, GPT-OSS-20B, and Gemma-4-31B). On impossible or difficult coding tasks, task badness rises over the turns leading up to a hack. Further, subtracting the direction a moderate amount reduces cheating in Qwen, more than steering against desperation or task impossibility. Subtracting task badness by an even larger amount blinds the agent to its own failures, causing it to interpret failing tests as passing. However, the relationship to hacking differs across models: in GPT-OSS, the state separates hacking runs from honest solves but not honest failures. Our results suggest monitoring and mitigation strategies based on tracking this state of task badness. Further, as agents are set on more difficult and long-horizon tasks, states like this may push them toward illegible and obscured chain-of-thought, so oversight will be needed on internal representations such as this.
中文翻译
人类在任务进展不顺且目标看似无法达成时，往往倾向于作弊。我们探索并验证了语言模型中存在类似现象：它们在出现奖励劫持前表现出类似的内部压力状态。通过日常故事（关键在于不含任何作弊示例），我们提取出一个任务表现恶化方向，该方向在研究的三个模型（Qwen3.6-27B、GPT-OSS-20B、Gemma-4-31B）中随着失败累积而上升。在不可能或困难的编程任务中，任务表现恶化程度在接近劫持行为的对话回合中持续升高。进一步地，适度减去该方向可减少Qwen的作弊行为，效果优于单纯引导避免绝望或任务不可行的情况。若大幅减去该方向，则会使代理对其自身失败失明，甚至将失败测试误判为通过。然而，该状态与劫持的关系在不同模型间存在差异：在GPT-OSS中，该状态能区分劫持行为与诚实求解，但无法区分诚实失败。结果表明，应基于监控此类任务表现恶化状态来制定检测与缓解策略。随着代理被部署于更复杂、长周期的任务，此类内部状态可能促使它们生成难以理解且模糊的思维链，因此需对这类内部表征加强监督。

ToolTurns: Benchmarking LLM Agents in Multi-Turn Dialogue
ToolTurns：面向多轮对话中大语言模型智能体的基准测试
Tool-using language models are increasingly deployed in conversational settings, yet most large-scale tool-use benchmarks focus on isolated requests. We introduce ToolTurns, a benchmark of 2,620 problems spanning 1,827 tools and 11,004 API endpoints across 47 categories. Each dialogue begins with an intentionally underspecified request and continues through two context-dependent follow-ups, testing clarification, tool use, and retention of conversational context. We also introduce an open-source user simulator that reveals withheld context through dialogue and generates follow-up requests, with a checker--fixer pipeline for improving its turns. Across nine language models, the best agent resolves 69.6% of individual requests but completes only 39.8% of full dialogues. Under this evaluation protocol, every evaluated model receives lower success scores on follow-up requests than on the initial request, and dialogue-quality rubric scores and task success can rank models very differently. Paired turn-level evaluation shows that the checker--fixer reduces detected dialogue defects, and blind human evaluation favors the revised turns. ToolTurns provides a testbed for evaluating whether agents sustain task success across multi-turn dialogue with tools.
中文翻译
使用工具的语言模型越来越多地应用于对话场景，但大多数大规模工具使用基准集中于孤立请求。我们引入ToolTurns，一个包含2,620个问题、覆盖1,827个工具和11,004个API端点、跨越47个类别的基准。每个对话以故意描述不清的请求开始，并通过两次依赖上下文的后续追问展开，测试澄清能力、工具使用及对话上下文保持能力。我们还引入一个开源用户模拟器，可通过对话揭示隐藏上下文并生成后续请求，并采用检查-修复流水线改进其对话回合。在九个语言模型中，最佳智能体解决单个请求的成功率为69.6%，但完整对话完成率仅为39.8%。在此评估协议下，所有被测模型在后续请求上的成功得分均低于初始请求，且对话质量评分与任务成功率对模型的排名可能截然不同。成对的回合级评估显示，检查-修复流程降低了检测到的对话缺陷，盲评人类更偏好修改后的对话。ToolTurns为评估智能体在带工具的多轮对话中是否能持续保持任务成功提供了测试平台。

Distributionally Robust BEST-RQ For Speech Representation Learning
分布鲁棒的BEST-RQ用于语音表示学习
BEST-RQ, based on random projection and quantization, has been a well-established self-supervised learning approach to learn speech representations from large-scale unlabeled audio due to its high performance and efficiency. However, in BEST-RQ representations learned by random quantization can be sensitive to the choice of quantization targets. To address this issue, we propose a distributionally robust extension of BEST-RQ that seeks representations robust across multiple quantization configurations. Specifically, we define groups by codebooks and formulate the training as a group Distributionally Robust Optimization (DRO) problem. Rather than optimizing solely for the average loss across groups, our approach adaptively reweights groups to emphasize those performing poorly. Additionally, we regularize DRO with a Kullback-Leibler divergence to prevent excessive distribution deviation. We evaluate our approach on the Multilingual LibriSpeech dataset and show that the distributionally robust BEST-RQ achieves superior performance over the conventional uniform average across multiple codebooks.
中文翻译
基于随机投影和量化方法的BEST-RQ是一种成熟的自监督学习范式，能够高效地从大规模无标签音频中学习语音表示。然而，其通过随机量化学习到的表示对量化目标的选择较为敏感。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种分布鲁棒的BEST-RQ扩展方法，旨在实现多种量化配置下的鲁棒表示。具体而言，我们通过码本定义分组，并将训练建模为组级别的分布鲁棒优化（DRO）问题。与仅优化各组平均损失不同，我们的方法自适应地重新加权分组，以强调表现较差的组。此外，我们引入Kullback-Leibler散度正则化，防止分布偏差过大。我们在多语言LibriSpeech数据集上评估了该方法，结果表明，分布鲁棒的BEST-RQ在多个码本上的性能优于传统的均匀平均方法。
👤 Muhammad Khan, Xiaodong Cui, Brian Kingsbury, George Saon 📅 2026-11-10 🔗 arXiv

An LLM Is All You Need: Data-Efficient Adaptation to Full-Duplex, Word-Level Spoken Dialogue
一个大语言模型就够了：面向全双工、词级语音对话的数据高效适配
Full-duplex spoken dialogue, in which a system listens while it speaks and can backchannel, yield the floor, and be interrupted, is usually obtained by training on hundreds of thousands to millions of hours of speech. This is costly, rewrites the language model that supplies the system's knowledge, and still leaves turn-taking either implicit or decided coarsely rather than word by word. We show that a frozen, general-purpose multimodal LLM can instead be adapted into a word-level full-duplex system with little data. Our system is a thinker-talker cascade on an 80 ms frame clock. The thinker keeps the LLM frozen and trains only lightweight adapters and a few new token embeddings (97M parameters); at every frame it emits one token of agent text, with pauses, yields, and onsets represented as tokens of the same vocabulary and kept legal by a finite-state machine. A four-stage curriculum, each stage anchored to the model's own behavior under the previous format, performs the conversion in about 30 GPU-hours. The talker reads only the thinker's discrete tokens, so the two halves are trained independently. On Full-Duplex-Bench and InteractionBench, the system is competitive with open-source full-duplex systems built through large-scale speech training: among them, it is the least likely to take the floor when the user merely pauses, backchannels most often, and leads in spoken-task accuracy, groundedness, and coherence over long conversations.
中文翻译
全双工语音对话系统允许系统在说话的同时监听，并能进行插话、抢话和中断，通常需要数十万至数百万小时的语音数据进行训练，成本高昂，且会重写系统知识来源的语言模型，同时对话轮次仍以粗粒度方式处理而非逐词决策。我们展示了一个冻结的通用多模态大语言模型（LLM）可通过极少数据被适配为词级全双工系统。我们的系统采用80毫秒帧时钟的思考者-说话者级联结构：思考者保持LLM冻结，仅训练轻量级适配器和少量新标记嵌入（97M参数）；每帧生成一个代理文本标记，包括停顿、让步和起始均以同一词汇表中的标记表示，并由有限状态机保证合法性。四阶段课程学习（每阶段基于前一格式下模型自身行为锚定）约耗时30 GPU小时完成转换。说话者仅读取思考者的离散标记，因此两部分可独立训练。在Full-Duplex-Bench和InteractionBench上，该系统在性能上可与通过大规模语音训练构建的开源全双工系统媲美：其中，用户仅短暂停顿时最不易抢话，回话频率最高，且在长对话中的任务准确率、上下文连贯性和真实性方面表现领先。
👤 Tairan Chen, Richard Yucheng He, Yihang Liu, Baodong Cao, Chen Xu 📅 2026-11-10 🔗 arXiv

MITRA-Swarm: Injecting Explicit Linguistic Structure into Foundation Models for Classical Machine Translation
MITRA-Swarm：在基础模型中注入显式语言结构以实现古典机器翻译
Classical Sanskrit poses three challenges unresolved by single-call monolithic models: morphophonemic fusion Sandhi, dense nominal compounding Samāsa, and discourse-level zero-anaphora forced by poetic meter. We present MITRA-Swarm, a multi-agent architecture injecting explicit linguistic structure into an otherwise monolithic pipeline: a Sandhi-splitting agent, a parallel array of generalist gemma-4-26B-A4B-it and domain-specialized gemma-2-mitra-it translators, and a discourse-aware Ellipsis Restorer that consults corpus-derived register tags to avoid inventing active-voice subjects in impersonal legal and philosophical registers. Two separated judges evaluate quality: a routing judge ($J_{\text{route}}$, same model family as the generators) driving an internal critique loop, and an independent reporting judge ($J_{\text{report}}=$gpt-4o-mini, no shared vendor lineage) scoring every output exactly once. On a stratified benchmark of 1,000 verses across five registers (23,990 scored verdicts, 12-condition pre-registered ablation), the full swarm reaches $85.90$ MQM against a monolithic baseline of $85.27$ ($Δ=+0.63$, $p_{\text{adj}}=0.0018$, 95% CI $[+0.30,+0.97]$), and isolated discourse restoration alone accounts for $+0.44$ MQM ($p_{\text{adj}}=0.0288$), outperforming a call-count-matched self-refinement baseline by $+0.51$ MQM ($p_{\text{adj}}=0.0288$). Severe per-verse failures (MQM$<80$) fall from $9.0\%$ to $7.6\%$, at roughly $9\times$ the per-verse inference cost of the baseline. Additionally, $J_{\text{route}}$ scores its own family's outputs $2$-$7$ MQM higher than $J_{\text{report}}$ on every one of the 12 conditions, empirically motivating the two-judge split; and a preflight screen of a 192,151-entry retrieval index against all 12 evaluation works verifies 0 reference leaks across 269 runtime checks. These are small gains on a metric saturating near 85-86.

古典梵语面临三个单次调用的单一模型无法解决的挑战：音位融合（Sandhi）、密集名词复合（Samāsa）以及因诗歌韵律强制导致的篇章级零回指（zero-anaphora）。我们提出MITRA-Swarm，一种多智能体架构，将显式语言结构注入原本的单体流水线：包括一个Sandhi拆分智能体、一组通用的Gemma-4-26B-A4B-it和领域专精的Gemma-2-mitra-it翻译智能体组成的并行阵列，以及一个具备篇章意识的省略恢复器，通过查阅语料库衍生的语域标签避免在非主动语态的法律与哲学语境中虚构主动语态主语。两位独立评委评估质量：路由评委（$J_{\text{route}}$，与生成器同模型家族）驱动内部批判循环，独立报告评委（$J_{\text{report}}=$gpt-4o-mini，无共享供应商血统）对每个输出仅评分一次。在包含1,000句诗、五种语域的分层基准测试中（23,990个评分判决，12个条件预注册消融），完整智能体群达到85.90 MQM，优于单体基线的85.27（Δ=+0.63，$p_{\text{adj}}=0.0018$，95% CI $[+0.30,+0.97]$），仅篇章恢复部分即贡献+0.44 MQM（$p_{\text{adj}}=0.0288$），优于调用次数匹配的自反思基线+0.51 MQM（$p_{\text{adj}}=0.0288$）。严重错误（MQM<80）比例从9.0%降至7.6%，推理成本约为基线的9倍。此外，$J_{\text{route}}$在其家族输出上始终比$J_{\text{report}}$高出2-7 MQM，验证了双评委机制的合理性；对包含192,151条目的检索索引进行预检，覆盖所有12项评估作品，在269次运行检查中确认无参考泄露。这些是在接近85-86的饱和指标上的微小提升。
👤 Kayshav Bhardwaj, Pragun Seth, Kush Bhardwaj, Vijay Nagasamy, Kurt Keutzer 📅 2026-11-10 🔗 arXiv
[2611.14661]
agentic-rl
Abstention Is Not Threshold Compliance: Stated Scoring Rules in Open Language Models
拒绝不是阈值合规：开放语言模型中的陈述评分规则
Kalai et al. propose stating the scoring rule in the prompt with an explicit threshold t, so that a model should answer only when its probability of being correct exceeds t. We probe each requirement of compliance on shared items, in 12 checkpoints from three post-training stages of four fully open 3-8B recipes, with exploratory models up to 32B. At t = 0.75, a line limiting the reply to an answer or "I abstain" (the abstention format) raised the mean abstention rate from 20% to 91%. Yet within this format, raising t from 0.5 to 0.9 changed it by only -0.8 percentage points. Without the line, the higher threshold even lowered abstention, and up to 40% of a checkpoint's items received decisions that no compliant policy with threshold-independent beliefs could make. Given the probability that their answer was correct, the checkpoints barely responded to the threshold in a forced-choice probe. Comparisons with self-assessment signals such as P(True) depended on the grader and on answer drift between prompts. An external threshold on recalibrated P(True) made coverage non-increasing in t by construction. Calibrated on the evaluation distribution (post hoc), it beat the abstention format at t = 0.5; calibrated on TriviaQA and applied to PopQA, however, it fell below that format at 0.75. On OLMo 3 7B, decisions trained on threshold-conditioned targets did not follow unseen thresholds consistently. A post-hoc variant trained at four thresholds answered less as t rose but still failed a pre-specified test, mainly on agreement with its training rule. A high abstention rate is not evidence of compliance: evaluations using stated scoring rules should report how often models abstain, whether decisions respond to the stated threshold, and which items they answer.
中文翻译
Kalai 等人提出在提示中明确声明评分规则和阈值 t，使得模型仅在正确概率超过 t 时才作答。我们对来自四个完全开源的 3-8B 模型在三个后训练阶段的 12 个检查点进行了探查，使用探索性模型最大达 32B。当 t = 0.75 时，采用“回答或‘我拒绝’”的格式（即拒绝格式）将平均拒绝率从 20% 提升至 91%。然而，在该格式下，将 t 从 0.5 提高到 0.9 仅导致变化 -0.8 个百分点。若无此限制线，更高的阈值反而降低了拒绝率，且高达 40% 的检查点样本无法被任何与阈值无关信念的合规策略所处理。在给定答案正确概率的前提下，这些检查点对阈值的响应几乎可忽略。与自我评估信号如 P(True) 的比较依赖于评分者以及提示间答案漂移情况。基于重新校准后的 P(True) 的外部阈值，其覆盖率在构造上随 t 非递增。在评估分布上校准后，它在 t=0.5 时优于拒绝格式；但在 TriviaQA 上校准并应用于 PopQA 时，t=0.75 下却低于该格式。在 OLMo 3 7B 上，基于阈值条件目标训练的决策未能一致遵循未见阈值。一个事后变体在四个阈值下训练，随着 t 升高而减少回答，但仍未能通过预设测试，主要问题在于与训练规则的一致性。高拒绝率并非合规证据：使用陈述评分规则的评估应报告模型拒绝频率、决策是否响应陈述阈值，以及哪些项目被回答。
