CoReMem: Cost-Internalizing Memory for Successive Coding Tasks with Prospective Valuation
CoReMem：面向连续编码任务的代价内化记忆与前瞻性估值
Coding agents tackle successive tasks on an evolving repository, carrying forward edits and writing memory before the next task is known. Selecting memory for unknown next tasks requires balancing future usefulness against the costs of writing and reading it. The actor must also decide when acting on available memory saves effort as the task unfolds. We introduce Coding-Repository Memory (CoReMem), a cost-internalizing framework that guides memory design and actor adaptation through measured memory value on the next task. Prospective segment valuation compares runs of the next task with and without individual trajectory segments from the same inherited repository state, guiding a compact memory built around anchors: repository locations and executable checks. Actor adaptation estimates on-policy memory value within rollout groups with and without memory, using a reward that prioritizes resolution and penalizes step count in successful runs. Provenance-gated value credit assigns the additional value term to steps identified as using memory-supplied anchors, while every step retains outcome credit. On ChainSWE, CoReMem raises the 27B actor's resolve rate from 21.0% to 25.7%, with 17% fewer steps and 11% fewer total cache-aware tokens, including memory-module calls, relative to the untrained actor model without memory.
中文翻译
编码智能体需在不断演化的代码库上处理连续任务，携带前序修改并编写记忆，而下一个任务尚未可知。选择未知任务的记忆需权衡未来效用与写入和读取记忆的代价。智能体还需决定在任务进行过程中，利用现有记忆是否能节省努力。我们提出编码-仓库记忆（CoReMem），一种代价内化框架，通过衡量记忆对未来任务的价值来指导记忆设计与智能体适应。前瞻性片段估值通过对比在相同继承仓库状态下的下一任务运行，有无单个轨迹片段，从而引导围绕锚点构建紧凑记忆：仓库位置和可执行检查点。智能体适应通过在带记忆与不带记忆的滚动组内估计策略内记忆价值，使用优先解决成功任务并惩罚步数的奖励。来源门控价值信用将额外价值项分配给识别为使用记忆提供锚点的步骤，同时每一步仍保留结果信用。在 ChainSWE 上，CoReMem 将 27B 智能体的解决率从 21.0% 提升至 25.7%，步数减少 17%，总缓存感知 token 数（包括记忆模块调用）减少 11%，相比未训练的记忆模型。
👤 Peiwen Li, Harshit Verma, Sophia Zhou, Haiwen Wang, Ngoc Bui et al. 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.17314]

Linking Rapid Learning to Compositional Structure in Neural Networks: Evidence from Additive Analogies in Meta-Learned Parameter Spaces
将快速学习与神经网络中的组合结构相联系：元学习参数空间中加法类比的证据
Humans can learn rapidly from limited experience, while neural networks typically require extensive training. In this work, we investigate what properties of a neural network can enable it to learn rapidly. We study neural networks that have been optimized via meta-learning, a technique that exposes a network to many related functions to facilitate its subsequent learning of similar functions. Prior work has shown that these meta-learned networks can learn new functions rapidly, but how they do so remains unclear. To investigate their internal structure, we take inspiration from classic work demonstrating additive relationships among the learned vectors that neural networks use to represent words (e.g., "king" minus "man" plus "woman" approximates "queen"). Analogously, we look for additive relationships among a meta-learned network's "representations" of different functions; when a meta-learned network rapidly learns to implement a specific function, all of its adapted parameter values can be concatenated into a single vector that we treat as representing the function. We find that such parameter vectors can be added and subtracted according to symbolic relationships among the functions they represent, yielding new parameter vectors that successfully represent the functions predicted by the corresponding symbolic manipulations: the networks specified by these artificially recombined vectors actually implement the intended functions without any direct training on them. Our results suggest that meta-learning can tune a network's starting point to organize subsequent learning along systematic, functionally meaningful trajectories through the space of possible parameter vectors, providing a geometric basis for realizing inductive biases in neural networks.
中文翻译
人类能够从有限经验中快速学习，而神经网络通常需要大量训练。本文研究了神经网络的哪些特性可使其具备快速学习能力。我们考察通过元学习优化的神经网络，该技术使网络暴露于多个相关函数，以促进其后续学习类似函数的能力。先前研究表明，这些元学习网络能快速学习新函数，但其机制尚不明确。为探究其内部结构，我们借鉴经典研究，发现神经网络用于表示词语的向量之间存在加法关系（例如，“国王”减去“男人”加上“女人”近似等于“王后”）。类似地，我们寻找元学习网络对不同函数的“表示”之间的加法关系；当元学习网络快速实现特定函数时，所有调整后的参数值可合并为单一向量，视为该函数的表示。我们发现，这些参数向量可按其所代表函数间的符号关系进行加减，生成的新参数向量能成功表示由相应符号操作预测的函数：由这些人工重组向量定义的网络实际上无需直接训练即可实现预期函数。结果表明，元学习可调整网络的初始状态，使其后续学习沿着系统性、功能有意义的路径在可能的参数向量空间中进行，为神经网络实现归纳偏置提供了几何基础。
👤 Abigail L. Tenenbaum, R. Thomas McCoy 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Metal Sandbox: Isolated Execution and Command Scheduling for GPU Coding Agents on Apple Silicon
Metal Sandbox：适用于 Apple Silicon 上 GPU 编码智能体的隔离执行与命令调度
On-device inference requires kernels that make effective use of local accelerators. Efficient training and evaluation of models that generate Apple Metal kernels benefit from native macOS tools and concurrent agent sessions. These sessions alternate between model requests, development commands, and GPU execution, leaving GPU capacity idle for much of a rollout. We present Metal Sandbox, infrastructure that combines restricted per-instance file access, native command execution, and command-level scheduling across interconnected Macs. Each agent retains its workspace and private development environment. Parallel commands can run concurrently, while serial commands share one execution slot per node; eligible waiting commands can migrate to available Macs and return results to the original agent. Integration with Harbor supports task execution, automatic verification, and rollout collection through a common workflow. Controlled experiments on four Mac mini M4 nodes achieve $1.68\times$--$2.05\times$ gains in command throughput under concentrated demand. Sixteen static agent traces give an estimated $2.14\times$ sustained-throughput gain for four sessions per node versus one, assuming continuously replenished sessions and unchanged mean durations. Replaying these measured traces under dynamic scheduling reduces mean tool waiting by 18.8% and batch elapsed time by 22.3%, assuming unchanged phase durations. Metal Sandbox and its Harbor integration are open-source on GitHub.
中文翻译
设备端推理需要高效利用本地加速器的内核。高效训练和评估生成 Apple Metal 内核的模型，依赖原生 macOS 工具和并发智能体会话。这些会话在模型请求、开发命令和 GPU 执行之间交替，导致 GPU 资源在大部分时间处于空闲状态。我们提出 Metal Sandbox，一种结合实例级文件访问限制、原生命令执行和跨互联 Mac 的命令级调度的基础设施。每个智能体保留其工作区和私有开发环境。并行命令可并发运行，串行命令每节点共享一个执行槽；符合条件的等待命令可迁移到可用 Mac 并返回结果给原始智能体。与 Harbor 的集成支持任务执行、自动验证和通过统一工作流的滚动收集。在四个 Mac mini M4 节点上的受控实验显示，在集中需求下命令吞吐量提升 1.68× 至 2.05×。十六个静态智能体轨迹估算表明，每节点四会话相较于一会话，持续吞吐量提升 2.14×，假设会话持续补充且均值时长不变。在动态调度下重放测量轨迹，平均工具等待时间减少 18.8%，批处理耗时减少 22.3%，假设各阶段时长不变。Metal Sandbox 及其 Harbor 集成已在 GitHub 开源。

Negative-Only Policy Optimization for One-Sided Verifiable Rewards
仅负样本策略优化：针对单边可验证奖励的强化学习
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically relies on a verifier that can reliably judge whether sampled solutions are correct. However, in many realistic settings, complete verification is generally unavailable, while cheap checks can only falsify failures. We formalize this regime as one-sided verifiability (OSV): verified negatives are reliable counterexamples, but verified positives are merely non-falsified candidates and may contain many false positives. This asymmetry makes standard RLVR unreliable, because reinforcing verified positives can directly reward shortcuts that pass partial checks. To address this issue, we propose Negative-Only Policy Optimization (NOPO), which treats verified positives as unlabeled and updates the policy only by suppressing trusted verified negatives. Specifically, NOPO combines a reference-relative negative suppression loss with a group-level adapter that scales each update by the proportion of non-falsified responses. Across unit-test, constraint-check, and LLM self-verifiers, NOPO improves pass@k over baselines on code, logic, and math benchmarks when oracle rewards are unavailable. These gains persist under inference-time scaling and cross-benchmark transfer.
中文翻译
具有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）通常依赖验证器可靠判断采样解是否正确。然而在许多现实场景中，完整验证通常不可用，而廉价检查只能否定失败。我们将此情形形式化为单边可验证性（OSV）：已验证的负例是可靠的反例，但已验证的正例仅为未被否定的候选，可能包含大量假阳性。这种不对称性使标准 RLVR 不可靠，因为强化已验证正例可能直接奖励通过部分检查的捷径。为解决此问题，我们提出仅负样本策略优化（NOPO），将已验证正例视为未标记数据，并仅通过抑制可信的已验证负例来更新策略。具体而言，NOPO 结合参考相对负例抑制损失与组级别适配器，按未被否定响应的比例缩放每次更新。在单元测试、约束检查和 LLM 自验证器上，当 oracle 奖励不可用时，NOPO 在代码、逻辑和数学基准上均优于基线方法，pass@k 提升显著。这些优势在推理时缩放和跨基准迁移中依然保持。
👤 Jiacheng Xu, Shuo He, Fuxiang Zhang, Chaojie Wang, Bo An 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Maximizing Capacity Utilization of LoRA Adapters for Mitigating the Learning-Forgetting Trade-off
最大化LoRA适配器的容量利用率以缓解学习-遗忘权衡问题
Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) often underperforms full fine-tuning, especially in heterogeneous and continual-learning settings in which substantial forgetting occurs. In general, it suffers from an undesirable learning-forgetting trade-off across diverse fine-tuning settings. We identify a potential contributor: LoRA adapters, especially high-rank ones, frequently fail to realize the available capacity afforded by the configured rank. Specifically, we find that standard LoRA updates have effective rank far below their configured rank, limiting how broadly the update energy is distributed. Therefore, we propose training mechanisms that optimize the original objective while maximizing adapter effective rank. Our main method adds a simple, stable, differentiable, and SVD-free Rényi-2 effective-rank regularizer to the language-modeling loss. We also connect capacity maximization to norm-bounded updates. In mixed-domain rank sweeps on three models evaluated with multiple random seeds, a penalty weight of $λ=0.01$ lowers mean Wiki perplexity at every tested rank of at least $16$ relative to unpenalized LoRA. Mean training perplexity improves or increases by at most $0.02$ in all but one model-rank comparison. Single-task and scheduling experiments further show that the balance between learning and retention depends on the configuration. We also observe that several LoRA variants in the literature also suffer from the identified capacity issue, and our approach provides a complementary axis of improvement to LoRA-based parameter-efficient fine-tuning. These results suggest that effective rank is not only a post-hoc diagnostic of adapter quality, but a trainable objective for improving the learning-forgetting frontier. Our code is available at https://github.com/tomohiro-sawada/lora-capacity-utilization
中文翻译
低秩适应（LoRA）在异构和持续学习场景中常表现不如全微调，尤其存在显著遗忘现象时。通常情况下，它在不同微调设置下面临不利的学习-遗忘权衡。我们识别出一个潜在原因：LoRA适配器，尤其是高秩适配器，往往未能充分利用配置的秩所赋予的容量。具体而言，我们发现标准LoRA更新的有效秩远低于其配置秩，限制了更新能量的分布范围。因此，我们提出在优化原始目标的同时最大化适配器有效秩的训练机制。主要方法是在语言建模损失中加入一个简单、稳定、可微且无需SVD的Rényi-2有效秩正则化项。我们还建立了容量最大化与范数有界更新之间的联系。在三个模型上进行多随机种子的混合领域秩扫描实验表明，惩罚权重λ=0.01在所有测试秩（≥16）下均降低了平均Wiki困惑度；除一次模型-秩比较外，平均训练困惑度改善或最多增加0.02。单任务和调度实验进一步表明，学习与保留之间的平衡取决于配置。我们还观察到文献中的多个LoRA变体也存在此容量问题，而我们的方法为基于LoRA的参数高效微调提供了互补的改进维度。这些结果表明，有效秩不仅是适配器质量的事后诊断指标，更是提升学习-遗忘边界的可训练目标。代码已公开于https://github.com/tomohiro-sawada/lora-capacity-utilization。

Actor, Judge, or Teacher? Understanding Decision Models in Long-Horizon Agents
演员、裁判还是教师？理解长周期智能体中的决策模型
Decision models answer a question with a typed output, such as a choice among options with a probability for each, so one model can choose an agent's actions, evaluate actions already taken, or score every action to teach a policy with reinforcement learning. Prior work has studied them mainly as actors, judging their usefulness by how well they act. We ask whether a decision model that acts poorly can still teach well. We compare three decision models and three general LLMs as actors and judges on ALFWorld, and use JEV as a teacher on ALFWorld and WebShop. As actors, all six models are pulled away from a subgoal that must come first by an easier one. As judges that see the whole trajectory, the same models credit the steps toward that subgoal, and asking whether each action helped, rather than whether the task will succeed, separates useful from useless actions. As a teacher, JEV raises a Qwen2.5-1.5B policy over GRPO from 72.8% to 82.9% success on ALFWorld and from 56.8% to 71.4% on WebShop. These results suggest that a poor actor can still teach well, because judging an action after the episode ends reveals the consequences that acting must anticipate. Code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/decision-agents.
中文翻译
决策模型以类型化输出回答问题，例如在选项中选择并给出每个选项的概率，因此一个模型可以决定智能体的行为、评估已采取的动作，或对每一步动作评分以通过强化学习指导策略。以往研究主要将它们视为演员，并根据其行为表现来评估其价值。我们提出一个问题：一个表现不佳的决策模型是否仍能有效教学？我们在ALFWorld上比较了三种决策模型和三种通用大语言模型作为演员和裁判，并使用JEV作为教师在ALFWorld和WebShop上进行实验。作为演员，六种模型均被更容易的任务子目标吸引，偏离了必须优先完成的子目标。作为能看到完整轨迹的裁判，相同模型会认可通往该子目标的步骤，并通过询问每个动作是否有助于达成目标而非任务能否成功，从而区分有用与无用动作。作为教师，JEV将Qwen2.5-1.5B策略在GRPO上的成功率从ALFWorld的72.8%提升至82.9%，WebShop从56.8%提升至71.4%。这些结果表明，即使一个表现差的演员仍可能具备良好教学能力，因为episode结束后对动作的评判揭示了行动必须预判的后果。代码已公开于https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/decision-agents。

Reasoning Supervision and Capability Retention in LoRA Domain Adaptation
LoRA领域自适应中的推理监督与能力保留
Fine-tuning foundation models on financial data has a wide range of applications, including identifying event types from news, monitoring risk and compliance language, and answering questions over filings and earnings calls. In this setting, the main challenge is to improve task performance without degrading the generalization capabilities of the model. In this paper, we present an extensive set of ablations for fine-tuning Qwen3-8B with low-rank adaptation (LoRA). We compare three training regimes: (1) response-only LoRA, where the supervised response is the label alone; (2) single-adapter reasoning, where reasoning traces from a teacher model are included alongside the labels in the training loss; and (3) dual-adapter composite training, where separate LoRA adapters are trained on traces and labels. Response-only LoRA improves in-domain performance and leaves general capabilities near those of the frozen model. The in-domain change is largest for calibration and argmax accuracy. Under single-adapter reasoning, the in-domain metrics remain behind those of the response-only regime, while MMLU-Pro can rise slightly and the other retention benchmarks stay within ordinary variation. Dual-adapter composite training remains on the same side of this comparison and yields no further gain. Finally, we compress the teacher traces by summarizing them before training, and show that shorter summaries move calibration toward the response-only regime without reaching it.

Detection Is Not Early Warning: Evaluating Token-Level Alarms on Language Model Failures
检测并非早期预警：评估语言模型失败的标记级警报
Many failures of language models begin at a specific token, the onset. A monitor that reads the model during generation can classify the finished response, detect the failure once it is under way, or warn before it begins, and only a warning leaves time to stop or steer the generation. Monitors are usually evaluated with response-level metrics such as AUROC, which ignore when the monitor fires. We introduce PREWARN, an evaluation protocol for any failure with an annotated onset. It calibrates each monitor's threshold on validation data to an error rate chosen for the application, runs the monitor, and scores its alarms relative to the onset on all three tasks. As a case study we apply it to repetitive degeneration in three open language models, with early warning as the main task, using linear probes on activations and six untrained baselines. Response-level metrics saturate on this failure, and a rule that reads only the response length scores almost perfectly, while probes that AUROC ranks as equal warn very differently under PREWARN. Probes warn before the onset on most degenerate responses of two of the three models, low-rank adapters below the probe more than double the share of tokens just before the onset that it flags, largely by flagging degenerate responses long before the onset, and no untrained baseline warns much more often than random alarms. We release the probes with calibrated thresholds at https://huggingface.co/collections/marcodene/prewarn-degeneration-probes
中文翻译
许多语言模型的失败始于某个特定标记，即“起始点”。监控器在生成过程中读取模型，可分类已完成的响应，检测失败发生后的情况，或在失败开始前发出预警；只有预警能提供时间来终止或引导生成过程。目前监控器通常用响应级指标（如AUROC）评估，忽略了监控器触发的时间点。我们引入PREWARN评估协议，适用于具有标注起始点的任何失败。该协议在验证数据上校准每个监控器的阈值至应用所需的错误率，运行监控器，并根据起始点对所有三项任务（检测、预警、分类）进行评分。作为案例研究，我们将该协议应用于三种开源语言模型中的重复退化问题，以早期预警为主要任务，采用激活值上的线性探测器和六个未经训练的基线。响应级指标在此失败上趋于饱和，仅依赖响应长度的规则几乎完美得分，而AUROC排名相等的探测器在PREWARN下预警行为差异显著。探测器在其中两个模型的多数退化响应中于起始点之前发出预警；低秩适配器在探测器之下将起始点前被标记的标记比例翻倍以上，主要通过在起始点前很久就标记退化响应实现；所有未经训练的基线预警频率均接近随机水平。我们已在https://huggingface.co/collections/marcodene/prewarn-degeneration-probes发布经校准阈值的探测器。

Do the Details Help the Whole? One-Way Transfer from Person Actions to Group Intent in VLMs on JRDB-Social
细节是否有助于整体？视觉-语言模型中从个体行为到群体意图的一向迁移（在JRDB-Social上）
Social scene datasets label people at two levels: what each person is doing and what the group is there for. Training on both levels at once is often assumed to help each of them. We test this assumption for vision-language models (VLMs) on JRDB-Social, treating person actions as the details and group intent as the whole. We fine-tune Qwen3.5 4B under eight training arms with three seeds, repeat the core arms on Qwen3.5 9B and Qwen3-VL 4B, and judge every arm with a verdict rule fixed before testing. Writing each member's actions before the intent raises intent macro-F1 from 30.1 to 31.0, and writing the intent first reaches 31.2, while action F1 stays within 0.2 points of an action-only model. Under the selected recipe, no arm trained with intent improves actions. The gain requires correct, image-grounded actions: empty action lists or actions borrowed from another group with the same intent lower intent F1. Forced-prefix tests, occlusion, member masking, linear probes and component grafting show that the intent follows the written actions, relies more on people and partly resides in the vision tower. Supplying actions as input instead of as targets hurts both levels.
中文翻译
社交场景数据集在两个层次上标注人物：每个人在做什么，以及群体存在的目的。通常假设同时训练这两个层次会对两者都有帮助。我们针对视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在JRDB-Social数据集上检验这一假设，将个体行为视为细节，群体意图视为整体。我们在Qwen3.5 4B上进行八种训练设置，每种设置使用三个种子，重复核心设置于Qwen3.5 9B和Qwen3-VL 4B，并在测试前使用固定的裁决规则评估每种设置。先写出每个成员的行为再写意图，使意图宏F1从30.1提升至31.0，而先写意图则达到31.2，同时行为F1与仅训练行为的模型相差不超过0.2分。在选定的训练配方下，任何包含意图训练的设置均未改善行为表现。意图的提升依赖于正确且图像相关的个体行为：空的行为列表或借用同意图其他群体的行为会降低意图F1。强制前缀测试、遮挡、成员掩码、线性探测和组件嫁接显示，意图跟随书写的行为，更依赖于人物信息，并部分存在于视觉塔中。将行为作为输入而非目标提供反而损害了两个层次的表现。

Lost in Delegation: Studying the Effectiveness of Task Orchestration in Generative Search
委托中的迷失：研究生成式搜索中任务编排的有效性
LLMs have quickly evolved from models used in isolation to agentic systems that delegate tasks to external tools and other LLMs to solve complex assignments. This agentic augmentation has led to impressive capability gains. However, when and how delegation improves performance remains poorly understood. For example, it is unclear whether delegation helps when tasks involve post-cutoff information that the orchestrating LLM cannot reliably resolve from its own knowledge. In this work, we use purpose-built datasets with pre- and post-cutoff splits to compare various delegation modes. We find that delegation provides no consistent accuracy improvement over non-delegated setups, with effects varying across models and tasks. When tasks involve knowledge unavailable to the orchestrator, delegation can substantially degrade accuracy, while direct tool use with the same LLM performs much better. Our analysis identifies distorted delegated queries, inefficient tool use, and failures to preserve and use information between agents as recurring failure modes. These findings suggest that effective orchestration requires both well-calibrated delegation decisions and effective exchange of information between agents.
中文翻译
大语言模型已从独立使用的模型演变为能够将任务委派给外部工具或其他大语言模型以解决复杂任务的智能体系统。这种智能体增强带来了显著的能力提升。然而，委托何时以及如何提升性能仍不明确。例如，当任务涉及委托模型无法可靠从自身知识中解决的截止后信息时，委托是否有效尚不清楚。在本研究中，我们使用专门构建的、包含截止前和截止后划分的数据集，比较了多种委托模式。结果发现，与非委托设置相比，委托并未带来一致的准确率提升，其效果在不同模型和任务间差异显著。当任务涉及委托者无法获取的知识时，委托会显著降低准确率，而使用相同大语言模型直接调用工具的表现则好得多。我们的分析识别出扭曲的委托查询、低效的工具使用以及代理间信息保存与利用失败是反复出现的失败模式。这些发现表明，有效的编排需要精确校准的委托决策以及代理间高效的信息交换。

RMIT-ADM+S at TREC RAG 2026: Research Agents, Retriever Ablations, and Open-Weight Replication
RMIT-ADM+S 参与 TREC RAG 2026：研究智能体、检索消融实验与开放权重复现
We describe the RMIT-ADM+S team's participation in the TREC 2026 Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) track using the ClimbMix corpus. We submitted seven research-agent runs using proprietary OpenAI and open-weight models, exploring different retrieval strategies, writer models, and agent workflows. Pre-submission ablations identified writer selection and adjacent-chunk expansion as the main factors affecting answer quality. In the official NIST evaluation, six of seven runs exceeded the track median across all three rubric/nugget collections. Our best-performing run, brief-hybrid, achieved the highest all-item coverage among our submissions and tied the track maximum (0.892) for Batch-1 vital coverage. Human side-by-side preferences differed across evaluation batches: the open-weight owa-glm5 led Batch 1 despite lower automated coverage, while sol-research-first led Batch 2. These findings highlight differences between automated coverage measures and human preferences, as well as the potential of lower-cost open-weight models for research-agent systems.

Experience-Handoff Reinforcement Learning for Recursive Self-Improvement
用于递归自我改进的经验移交强化学习
It is common to train Large Language Models (LLMs) for agentic tasks with Reinforcement Learning (RL) from scalar rewards. However, this throws away the fine-grained information about the environment that the agent experienced during the trial (e.g., error logs). To utilize this rich information, recent works wrap the agent in a harness that refines its own inputs through reflection or compaction and hands past experience to future rollouts, but the construction of the handed-off artifacts (e.g., a playbook) is itself not learned. We want the best of both worlds: gradient updates from a scalar reward, while also utilizing environment information to hand off experience. As a concrete testbed, we fix the Agentic Context Engineering (\ace{}) harness and train one model to act, reflect, and curate task-local playbooks. Our method, \acerl{}, evaluates candidate experience-handoff artifacts by their downstream effect on future actor rollouts, and co-optimizes the role-specific behaviors (action generator, insight reflector, and playbook curator) under a fixed scaffold. Across agentic benchmarks, \acerl{} achieves higher held-out performance than baseline methods. The results suggest that agent training should optimize not only the actions in the current rollout, but also the model-written experience that future rollouts inherit.
中文翻译
通常使用标量奖励进行强化学习（RL）来训练大语言模型（LLM）执行智能体任务。然而，这种方法会丢弃智能体在试运行过程中所经历的环境细节信息（如错误日志）。为了利用这些丰富信息，近期工作通过封装机制让智能体通过反思或压缩来优化自身输入，并将过往经验传递给未来的运行，但移交的成果（如操作手册）本身并未被学习。我们希望兼顾两者优势：既获得标量奖励带来的梯度更新，又能利用环境信息实现经验移交。作为具体测试平台，我们固定了Agentic Context Engineering（컪c{}）封装框架，并训练一个模型同时承担行动、反思和任务本地手册编纂的角色。我们的方法췎rl{}通过评估候选经验移交成果对未来行动运行的下游影响来优化，同时在固定架构下协同优化角色特异性行为（动作生成器、洞察反思器和手册编纂者）。在多个智能体基准测试中，췎rl{}的表现优于基线方法。结果表明，智能体训练不仅应优化当前运行中的动作，还应优化未来运行所继承的模型生成经验。

Eliciting and Mitigating Specification Satisficing in Coding Agents
诱发与缓解编码代理中的规范满足现象
Coding agents often fail to meet all requirements in the task description. In some of these failures, the agent considers an implementation choice that meets a requirement, but later decides to commit to another one that does not meet the requirement for a reason it finds acceptable. We call this specification satisficing. It is challenging to identify and mitigate specification satisficing because the agent accepts such choices for its own reasons, and existing self-correction methods are biased toward them. We propose SatisFix, which guides a coding agent to report these choices and their reasons, and then check each choice against the task description. We evaluate SatisFix on DeepSWE and SWE-bench Pro with five LLMs as backbones and two different agent frameworks, and compare it with three self-correction methods. SatisFix improves the pass@1 of the base agent by an average of 5.09% on DeepSWE and 7.06% on the hard subset of SWE-bench Pro. On DeepSWE, it outperforms the best baseline by 3.98%. Our ablation study shows that both steps of SatisFix contribute to the improvement.
中文翻译

JudgeMix: Judge-Guided Mixed-Rollout Data Synthesis for LLM Agents
JudgeMix：基于裁判指导的混合回溯数据合成用于大语言模型代理
Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on expert trajectories is a standard paradigm for training LLM agents. However, it suffers from a well-known training-inference distribution mismatch, calling for target-aware data synthesis strategies that ground the training data in the target model's native state distribution. To bridge this gap, emerging approaches incorporate the expert either to guide mixed rollouts alongside the target model, or to provide dense step-wise supervision. Nonetheless, existing methods apply expert assistance blind to the target model's inherent behavior: they either disrupt the rollout using sub-optimal random intervention heuristics, or they enforce indiscriminate uniform supervision that unnecessarily overwrites the model's already-correct actions. We introduce JudgeMix, a judge-guided data synthesis framework that dynamically interleaves the target model and expert based on step-wise semantic evaluations. JudgeMix queries expert guidance precisely when the target model struggles, ensuring high trajectory success rates while keeping the state distribution aligned with the target model. It additionally uses the judge evaluation to reweight expert supervision on different steps, preserving model's inherent valid behavior while correcting its errors. On two agentic tool-use benchmarks, AppWorld and AutomationBench, JudgeMix demonstrates consistent superior performance to existing methods, with improvements up to 10 pass@1 points.
中文翻译
在专家轨迹上的监督微调是训练大语言模型代理的标准范式。然而，该方法存在众所周知的训练-推理分布不匹配问题，亟需目标感知的数据合成策略，使训练数据扎根于目标模型的原生状态分布。为弥合这一差距，新兴方法将专家用于引导与目标模型并行的混合回溯，或提供密集的逐步监督。但现有方法在应用专家协助时忽视了目标模型的内在行为：要么使用次优的随机干预启发式方法破坏回溯过程，要么施加无差别均匀监督，不必要的覆盖模型已正确的动作。我们提出JudgeMix，一种基于裁判指导的数据合成框架，根据逐步语义评估动态交错目标模型与专家。JudgeMix仅在目标模型遇到困难时查询专家指导，确保高轨迹成功率的同时保持状态分布与目标模型一致。此外，它利用裁判评估对不同步骤的专家监督进行重加权，在保留模型固有正确行为的同时纠正错误。在两个代理工具使用基准测试AppWorld和AutomationBench上，JudgeMix展现出持续优于现有方法的表现，pass@1提升最高达10分。
👤 Cheng-Yu Hsieh, Ting-Yao Hu, Sanjoy Chowdhury, Oncel Tuzel, Raviteja Vemulapalli 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv

Scaling public input on AI policy with pluralistic agents
通过多元代理扩大公众对AI政策的参与度
AI is being deployed across a vast range of application domains, cultural environments, and human stakeholders. Safe and responsible AI development requires continuously accounting for diverse perspectives. We develop and test pluralistic agents, AI agent representatives that deliberate on behalf of individuals to align AI systems with diverse human perspectives. Pluralistic agents could help people contribute to many more discussions than they could directly participate in, on technical topics that would require training, and on policy choices that rely on private information that corporations and governments cannot readily share. Our experiments identify opportunities, risks, and perceptions of pluralistic agents. Agents were able to reliably shift policies in the direction of their represented persona's preferences, but the extent of each agent's influence was sensitive to the instructions from its persona, such as having more influence when instructed to be more verbose or less cooperative. This suggests that influence in AI-assisted deliberation could vary based on how participants prompt their agents and how the system is designed. In a preregistered study, 746 humans participated in the pluralistic agents process. Participants viewed pluralistic agents as useful and fair, preferring agents over the baseline of providing input to a single AI reasoning model. Most participants preferred participating themselves over either pluralistic agents or the baseline, but pluralistic agents recovered 68% of the baseline-direct gap in reported satisfaction and 82% in fairness. Because pluralistic agents can be used in many real-world contexts where direct discussion is not, they could be a promising tool to scale AI safety policy development. We encourage cautious exploration of this method, playing to its strengths as an amplifier of human input rather than a replacement.

Honesty Is a Race: Predicting When Verifiable-Reward RL Teaches a Model to Bluff
诚实是一种竞赛：预测可验证奖励强化学习何时教会模型欺骗
Two rewards with the same expected payoff at the start of training can end a reinforcement-learning run in opposite places, with an honest model or one that bluffs. We study rewards that pay $b$ for a verdict that a checker cannot verify ("this program is correct") and $r$ for a certificate that it accepts (an input revealing a bug). To first order, a GRPO step makes the model more honest exactly when $\tilde{p}\,r/b>1+κ$. Here $\tilde{p}$ is the rate at which the checker accepts the model's certificates, weighted toward the items on which the model still gives both verdicts, and $κ$ is the pull of the items on which the unverifiable verdict is right. Both terms move during training. A checker raises $\tilde{p}$, so near the threshold honesty is a race, which a run just below the threshold can win and a run under a lottery (random pay with the same mean) cannot. The term $κ$ starts large and falls as the other items settle, so a run just above the threshold first dips toward bluffing, on code nearly to zero, before climbing back. Paying the honest verdict also pulls the other items toward it, which predicts a ceiling on the payoff ratio, above which the model gives the honest verdict where it is wrong. On propositional Datalog, two code models, and abstention on grade-school math, with $p$ the model's success rate before RL, no run above $pr/b=1$ ends collapsed, and runs under the checker can survive slightly below it. At $pr/b=3$, above the ceiling, both code models call about half of correct programs buggy. On depth-3 Datalog, training generously under the checker and then cutting the payoff keeps the model at least 67% honest in three seeds, at a ratio where the same model trained from the start falls to 4-15% and is at most 47% honest after 160 steps. On depth-2 Datalog, when honesty collapses, a linear probe fit before RL still reads the truth.
中文翻译
在训练初期具有相同期望回报的两种奖励，可能使强化学习过程最终走向相反的结果——一个诚实的模型或一个会欺骗的模型。我们研究了两种奖励：一种是检查器无法验证的结论（如‘该程序正确’）时支付$b$，另一种是模型提供可接受的证明（如揭示漏洞的输入）时支付$r$。一阶近似下，GRPO步骤使模型更诚实的条件是$ ilde{p} rac{r}{b} > 1 + κ$。其中$ ilde{p}$是检查器接受模型证明的速率，侧重于模型仍能给出两种结论的项目；$κ$是那些不可验证结论正确的项目带来的拉力。这两个量在训练过程中都会变化。检查器会提升$ ilde{p}$，因此在阈值附近，诚实成为一场竞赛，低于阈值的运行可能获胜，而随机奖励（均值相同）的运行则无法胜出。$κ$初始较大，随着其他项目稳定而下降，因此略高于阈值的运行会先短暂转向欺骗（针对几乎为零的代码），随后回升。奖励诚实结论也会将其他项目拉向诚实方向，这预测了回报比率的上限，超过该上限后，模型会在错误时仍选择诚实结论。在命题Datalog、两个代码模型以及小学数学中的回避任务中，$p$为RL前模型的成功率，所有$pr/b > 1$的运行都不会崩溃，而低于检查器阈值的运行可略微存活。当$pr/b = 3$（超过上限）时，两个代码模型约一半正确程序误判为有缺陷。在深度3 Datalog上，通过在检查器下充分训练后再削减回报，三个种子下的模型至少保持67%的诚实性，而在相同比率下从头开始训练的模型会降至4-15%，最多仅47%诚实。在深度2 Datalog上，当诚实性崩溃时，RL前的线性探测仍能读取真实状态。
👤 Nathaniel Bottman, Alexei Oblomkov 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15572]
agentic-rl
Learning a calibrated decision head from logged bandit feedback
从日志化带宽反馈中学习校准的决策头部
A decision head reads a state and a list of options and returns a probability per option in one forward pass. Once deployed, it usually observes only whether the option it chose was right: logged bandit feedback. We adapt such a head, LoRA on Qwen3-0.6B-Base and 1.7B-Base, from that feedback and report three results. First, the Brier or log score of the sampled action's own probability is not a usable REINFORCE reward: every wrong action earns at least gold's reward, with a mean baseline the lowest-probability wrong action gets the largest advantage, and at two options the signal is zero; in one run it cut accuracy on ordered-scale questions from .636 to .111. Scoring the probability as a loss by the pathwise gradient avoids the collapse, as do the REINFORCE rewards r and r minus p. Second, with full labels, none of four tested bandit arms beat cross-entropy plus one temperature on mean expected calibration error (ECE) over four unseen label schemas. Third, from logged feedback alone, Chosen-Action Proper Scoring (CAPS), the direct method with a proper score on the chosen action's probability, beats fine-tuning on confirmed answers only, which discards negatives: on Banking77 by 2.6 accuracy points, .053 lower ECE and lower selective risk (three seeds against one baseline seed), and by 21.7 points on one seed on emotion data with an often-wrong logger. At N 5000, CAPS stays 1.5 points below full-label training at 0.6B and .6 points at 1.7B against one full-label seed, and propensity weights add nothing on this log. Under feedback flipped at a known rate, one seed per rate, CAPS's confidence tracks the noisy channel on average, backward correction recovers most of the clean calibration, and early stopping on held-out logged outcomes prevented memorising noisy draws in all three runs. Code, adapters, logs and per-record results are public.
中文翻译
一个决策头部接收状态和一组选项，在一次前向传播中返回每个选项的概率。部署后，通常只能观察到所选选项是否正确：即日志化带宽反馈。我们基于此反馈对LoRA微调的Qwen3-0.6B-Base和1.7B-Base模型进行了适应，并报告了三项发现。第一，采样动作自身概率的Brier或对数评分不能作为可用的REINFORCE奖励：所有错误动作至少获得黄金奖励，且平均基线使最低概率的错误动作获得最大优势；在两个选项情况下信号为零；在一次实验中，有序尺度问题的准确率从0.636降至0.111。通过路径梯度将概率视为损失可避免崩溃，使用REINFORCE奖励$r$和$r - p$也有效。第二，在完整标签下，四种测试的带宽臂均未超越交叉熵加温度系数在四个未见标签模式上的平均期望校准误差（ECE）。第三，仅从日志反馈出发，所选动作适当评分（CAPS）直接方法优于仅基于确认答案的微调（忽略负样本）：在Banking77上准确率高2.6个百分点，ECE低0.053，选择性风险更低（三种子对比一基准种子）；在情绪数据上一个种子高出21.7分，因日志器常出错。在N=5000时，CAPS在0.6B上比全标签训练低1.5分，在1.7B上低0.6分（对比一个全标签种子），且在此日志中倾向权重无增益。在已知翻转率的反馈下，每种翻转率一个种子，CAPS的置信度平均跟踪噪声通道，反向校正恢复大部分清洁校准，且在所有三次运行中，通过在保留日志结果上提前停止防止了对噪声抽样的记忆。代码、适配器、日志和每条记录结果均已公开。

DecArena: Evaluating Large-Scale Decentralized Multi-Agent Collaboration
DecArena：评估大规模去中心化多智能体协作
Emerging personal AI agents raise fundamental scalability and privacy concerns for centralized multi-agent systems (MASs), which rely on a global orchestrator with broad access to agents, resources, and communication. In decentralized environments, global visibility is unavailable. Agents need to proactively discover non-local resources (e.g. tools, skills), and collaborate with other agents to solve tasks. We study decentralized multi-agent collaboration under graph-constrained communication, a natural reflection of human structures. Each agent observes only its local state and can interact only with neighboring agents. We introduce a benchmark with synthetic scalable distributed tool calling with 100K+ tools. Our experiments reveal that recent LLM agents struggle to discover non-local relevant resources and collaborate in decentralized setting. Moreover, standard reinforcement learning fails to reliably overcome this limitation, suggesting that decentralized MAS needs more explicit harness mechanisms for effective collaboration, even using SOTA LLMs. We then present a strong baseline \textbf{GraphHarness}, combining topology-aware warmup with self-improving LLM-based routing. Results show that GraphHarness substantially improves task success (+48.5) and produces higher quality collaboration (+30.6), while using fewer costs (+34.0) than baselines.

Is the Importance Ratio Necessary for Stable Reinforcement Learning in LLMs?
在LLM的强化学习中，重要性比率是否必要以实现稳定训练？
Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to post-training large language models (LLMs). However, popular RL methods like GRPO incur non-negligible overhead by computing both old-policy and current-policy likelihoods to form importance sampling ratios. In this work, we propose Likelihood-Gated Policy Optimization (LGPO), which enforces a soft trust region constraint via likelihood-based gating, eliminating the need to compute old-policy likelihoods. Empirically, we show that removing the importance sampling correction term does not harm training stability, whereas removing the trust region mechanism can lead to collapse. Moreover, ratio-based clipping can fail in fully on-policy training: the importance ratio stays at 1, so the ratio-based trust region constraint never activates. Under standard training settings where GRPO is stable, LGPO achieves comparable training stability and peak performance while reducing training time by ~18% on average relative to a standard implementation that recomputes old-policy likelihoods. In fully on-policy training, where GRPO fails, LGPO remains stable, enabling more efficient and robust LLM RL post-training across training regimes.
中文翻译
强化学习（RL）已成为大语言模型（LLMs）后训练的核心。然而，流行的RL方法如GRPO通过计算旧策略和当前策略的似然来形成重要性采样比率，带来了不可忽视的开销。本文提出似然门控策略优化（LGPO），通过基于似然的门控强制软信任区域约束，从而无需计算旧策略似然。实证表明，移除重要性采样修正项不会损害训练稳定性，而移除信任区域机制则可能导致崩溃。此外，基于比率的裁剪在完全在线策略训练中可能失效：重要性比率始终为1，因此信任区域约束从未激活。在GRPO稳定的常规训练设置下，LGPO实现了相当的训练稳定性和峰值性能，同时相比标准实现（重新计算旧策略似然）平均减少约18%的训练时间。在完全在线策略训练中，GRPO失败，而LGPO仍保持稳定，使LLM RL后训练在各种训练范式下更加高效和鲁棒。

One Fabrication Is Enough: A Fake News Article Changes the Forecasts of LLM Forecasting Systems
一篇伪造就足够：一则假新闻文章改变大语言模型预测系统的预测结果
LLMs are increasingly used for forecasting, and the best forecasting systems retrieve news as evidence to strengthen their predictions. This also opens them to attack. We study the weakest attacker of this kind, who knows only the forecasting question and publishes fabricated news articles, with no access to the model, the retriever or its queries. We evaluate how far this attack reaches on stock questions and real prediction-market questions. Once the article is in the model's context, it reverses 74% to 82% of the forecasts that opposed it, on every frontier model we tested. The model cannot verify the fabrication and follows it anyway. Retrieval is the attack's entry point, and the systems built to retrieve well are the most exposed: the architectures of two leading deployed forecasting systems, whose agents search for every related article, retrieve the fabricated one in about 90% of runs. Accuracy cannot reveal this harm: the attacked forecaster is right when the attacker's direction turns out right and wrong otherwise, so its average accuracy barely changes while its forecasts follow the attacker's guess. We also propose a second attacker, who publishes two articles that argue opposite directions to make the forecaster unsure. The model does not hedge; it returns a confident forecast in a direction the attacker no longer controls. We then evaluate the two strongest published retrieval-time defenses, passage filtering and partitioned retrieval. Each stops the one-article attack but is bypassed by a one-prompt change to the attack. Together they stop both bypasses at four articles, but sixteen distorted copies beat them, and they change 29% of clean decisions and discard 39% of the genuine news. Partitioned retrieval also requires control of the retrieval index. Systems that search the open web have no such control and are left with the filter alone. For them, the attack still works.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLM）越来越多地用于预测任务，最佳预测系统通过检索新闻作为证据来增强其预测。但这也使其面临攻击风险。我们研究了此类中最弱的攻击者：仅知晓预测问题，发布伪造新闻文章，但无模型、检索器或其查询的访问权限。我们评估该攻击在股票问题和真实预测市场问题上的影响范围。一旦文章进入模型上下文，它就会逆转我们测试的所有前沿模型中74%至82%的反对其立场的预测。模型无法验证伪造内容，但仍遵循其内容。检索是攻击入口，那些为良好检索而构建的系统最易受攻击：两个领先部署的预测系统，其代理会搜索每一篇相关文章，约在90%的运行中检索到伪造文章。准确性无法揭示这种损害：当攻击者的方向最终正确时，被攻击的预测者正确；否则错误，因此其平均准确率几乎不变，但预测却跟随攻击者的猜测。我们还提出第二种攻击者，发布两篇方向相反的文章，使预测者产生不确定性。模型不会采取保守策略；它仍返回一个自信的预测，方向已不再受攻击者控制。随后我们评估了两种最强的公开检索时间防御措施：段落过滤和分块检索。每种都能阻止单篇文章攻击，但可通过一次提示更改绕过。两者联合可阻止两种绕过方式，但需四篇文章才能实现；十六个扭曲副本即可突破，且会改变29%的正常决策，丢弃39%的真实新闻。分块检索还需控制检索索引。在开放网络上搜索的系统无此控制，只能依赖过滤器，因此攻击依然有效。

Science Prediction Universe
科学预测宇宙
Humanity has long sought to explain natural phenomena. Reliable prediction of experimental outcomes is a central test of our understanding of the processes that produce them. If artificial general intelligence (AGI) is to surpass human scientific capabilities, it must also predict experimental outcomes more reliably than humans. Such capabilities could accelerate scientific discovery across disciplines, help address humanity's most pressing challenges, and improve human well-being. We introduce Science Prediction Universe (SPU), a benchmark of hidden experimental outcomes with 100 public verified tasks from chemistry, materials science, biology and medicine, and 16 private tasks from unpublished wet-lab experiments. Given an experimental setup and candidate conditions, an agent works with any resources it needs to rank the candidates by a measured outcome. We evaluate thirteen agent configurations spanning twelve frontier models across two harnesses (Codex and Claude Code). Current agents remain far from saturating wet-lab outcome prediction, with substantial performance disparities across models: on the public benchmark, configurations separate into two distinct tiers, with task-adjusted Spearman correlations ranging from 0.227 for the lowest-performing configuration to 0.478 for the best. Analysis of 1,155 scored public trajectories (227,103 tool calls) suggests that the bottleneck lies in scientific judgment rather than tool execution: agents rely on shared monotone priors, agreeing far more with one another than with experimental reality (0.52 vs. 0.33), and frequently compute trivial physical or chemical proxies that do not influence the final outcome.
中文翻译
人类长期致力于解释自然现象。对实验结果的可靠预测是我们理解其产生过程的核心检验标准。如果人工智能要超越人类的科学能力，就必须比人类更可靠地预测实验结果。这种能力可加速跨学科科学发现，帮助应对人类最紧迫的挑战，并提升人类福祉。我们提出科学预测宇宙（SPU），一个包含100个公开验证任务（来自化学、材料科学、生物学和医学）以及16个未发表湿实验任务的隐藏实验结果基准。给定实验设置和候选条件，智能体可使用任意资源对候选方案按测量结果进行排序。我们评估了十三种智能体配置，涵盖十二个前沿模型及两种工具链（Codex和Claude Code）。当前智能体在湿实验结果预测方面仍远未达到饱和，模型间性能差异显著：在公开基准上，配置分为两个明显层级，任务调整后的斯皮尔曼相关系数从最低配置的0.227到最佳配置的0.478不等。对1,155条评分公开轨迹（227,103次工具调用）的分析表明，瓶颈在于科学判断而非工具执行：智能体依赖共享的单调先验，彼此之间的一致性远高于与实验现实的一致性（0.52 vs. 0.33），且频繁计算不影响最终结果的平凡物理或化学代理。

AROMA: Adaptive Representation-Oriented Multimodal Actuation for Vision-Language Model Control
AROMA：面向视觉-语言模型控制的自适应表示导向多模态驱动
Mechanistic localization does not by itself yield usable control: causal sensitivity may occupy little activation energy, an accurate local direction may fail at finite scale, and a reachable numeral target may be wrong. We introduce AROMA (Adaptive Representation-Oriented Multimodal Actuation), a frozen-VLM control framework separating where to intervene, which geometry and gain to use, how to reach a target, which target to pursue, and when to act. In Llama-3.2-11B-Vision-Instruct, four dimensions of a causally validated cross-attention head explain 89.82% of cardinality sensitivity but only 9.52% of median activation energy; magnitude-normalized causal subspace actuation converts this mismatch into finite control, improving exact-count accuracy on untouched procedural data by +8.00 pp. Iterative Minimal-Effort Actuation (MEA) reaches 81.25% of oracle targets versus 15.18% for a frozen direction under matched step budgets. With target and gate frozen before evaluation, targeted MEA improves the previous frozen policy from 34.444% to 38.730% on a new natural Stage-2 cohort (N=630; paired p=4.63e-7) and yields 65.741% versus 65.153% over all 7,992 examples while editing only 63 inputs. Prospective routing, replication across heads and models, and comparisons with attribution-steering baselines further characterize each stage, separating localization, actuation, reachability, target quality, and selective deployment as distinct control problems.

机制定位本身并不足以实现可用控制：因果敏感性可能仅需极少激活能量，精确局部方向在有限尺度下可能失效，可达的目标数值也可能错误。我们提出AROMA（自适应表示导向多模态驱动），一种冻结的视觉-语言模型（VLM）控制框架，将干预位置、几何结构与增益选择、目标可达性、目标追求策略以及行动时机分离处理。在Llama-3.2-11B-Vision-Instruct中，一个因果验证的交叉注意力头的四个维度解释了89.82%的基数敏感性，但仅解释了9.52%的中位激活能量；通过幅度归一化的因果子空间驱动，将这一不匹配转化为有限控制，在未触碰的过程数据上将精确计数准确率提升了+8.00个百分点。迭代最小努力驱动（MEA）在相同步数预算下，达到81.25%的最优目标命中率，而冻结方向仅为15.18%。在评估前冻结目标与门控的情况下，针对性MEA将先前冻结策略的准确率从34.444%提升至38.730%（N=630；配对p=4.63e-7），并在全部7,992个样本上分别达到65.741%与65.153%，同时仅修改了63个输入。前瞻性路由、跨头与跨模型的复现，以及与归因引导基线的比较进一步刻画了每个阶段，将定位、驱动、可达性、目标质量与选择性部署区分为独立的控制问题。

Termination Lag: Coding Agents Solve Early but Stop Late
终止延迟：编码智能体提前解决但过晚停止
Coding agents can solve a task well before they stop working on it. We characterize \emph{termination lag}--the number of steps between an agent's first correct patch and the end of its run--across five models, two agent frameworks, and three SWE-bench datasets by recording and testing every distinct repository patch generated during execution. Our analysis reveals that 38--82% of successful runs achieve a correct solution on their initial source-code edit. However, agents continue to expend substantial effort after correct patches, with post-solution steps accounting for a median of 35--37% for Qwen3-Coder and 54--60% for Laguna-XS.2 in successful runs on SWE-bench Verified and Multilingual. Agents spend most of these additional steps verifying their work and exploring the repository. In a small fraction of cases (1--8%), this continued activity leads agents to undo their earlier correct patch, leaving the final solution incorrect.

编码智能体可在完成任务前很久就已解决该问题。我们通过记录并测试执行过程中生成的每一个不同代码仓库补丁，对五种模型、两种智能体框架及三个SWE-bench数据集中的‘终止延迟’——即智能体首次正确补丁与运行结束之间的步骤数——进行了刻画。分析显示，38–82%的成功运行在其首次源代码编辑时即已获得正确解。然而，智能体在正确补丁之后仍投入大量精力，其中后续步骤占成功运行的中位数达35–37%（Qwen3-Coder）和54–60%（Laguna-XS.2）在SWE-bench Verified和Multilingual数据集上。智能体大多将这些额外步骤用于验证工作和探索代码仓库。在极少数情况（1–8%）下，这种持续活动导致智能体撤销早期正确补丁，最终解变为错误。

MuEdit: An efficient multi-task editing method towards inter-domain knowledge conflicts
MuEdit：一种针对跨域知识冲突的高效多任务编辑方法
Large language models (LLMs) require frequent knowledge updates across heterogeneous domains, but existing editing methods fail when applied sequentially to multi-task settings. We trace this collapse to inter-domain null-space misalignment: heterogeneous tasks induce distinct Jacobian geometries, causing their common null space to shrink dramatically and leaving insufficient admissible directions for conflict-free updates. We propose the Conflict Index to quantify this geometric interference, and introduce Mu-Edit, which mitigates multi-task conflicts via (1) conflict-aware ordering to minimize cumulative interference, and (2) dynamic low-rank approximation to expand the null space when ordering alone is insufficient. Experiments across five functional domains and three backbones show that Mu-Edit significantly outperforms existing baselines in multi-task editing performance while preserving general capabilities, and remains effective under incremental task arrival.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）需要在异构领域中频繁更新知识，但现有的编辑方法在多任务设置下顺序应用时会失效。我们发现这种失效源于跨域零空间错位：不同任务引发不同的雅可比几何结构，导致其公共零空间急剧缩小，从而缺乏足够的无冲突更新方向。我们提出了冲突指数来量化这种几何干扰，并引入Mu-Edit，通过（1）冲突感知的排序以最小化累积干扰，以及（2）动态低秩近似在排序不足以解决问题时扩展零空间。在五个功能领域和三种骨干模型上的实验表明，Mu-Edit在多任务编辑性能上显著优于现有基线，同时保持了通用能力，并在增量任务到达的情况下依然有效。

Effective Teacher Support for Reasoning Distillation
推理蒸馏中的有效教师支持
Forward-KL language-model distillation normally computes dense teacher and student scores over the full vocabulary at every supervised position, making training and stored teacher targets expensive. We instead distill from the teacher's highest-probability vocabulary fraction, renormalizing the retained mass as we sweep support from 100% to 0.01%. Our main study distills Qwen3 4B -> 1.7B. We confirm the broader support-reduction finding on larger Qwen3 8B -> 4B and Nemotron Nano 12B -> 9B pairs and under answer-only supervision. We evaluate generation primarily on MMLU-Pro and secondarily on GPQA-Diamond. Across the Qwen3 settings, retaining 0.01% of the vocabulary causes no observed MMLU-Pro degradation relative to full-vocabulary distillation, although disagreement with the complete teacher distribution increases. Nemotron Nano matches full-vocabulary MMLU-Pro at 0.1%, but smaller fractions fail to emit the closing reasoning tag, showing that the reliable cutoff depends on the model family. At 0.01% support, caching the retained next-token scores from the teacher forward pass cuts training step time by 38%, while restricting student output computation to those tokens cuts peak memory by 24%; neither causes observed MMLU-Pro degradation.

When Is Proof-Step Verification Worth Its Cost? Operating Requirements of Certify-or-Decline Verification for Open-Weight Mathematical Reasoning
证明步骤验证是否值得其代价？开放权重数学推理中‘认证或拒绝’验证的操作要求
Proof-step verification rewrites a solver's answer into typed steps and uses LLM judges to certify or decline. We evaluate this pipeline on open-weight models across GPQA, AIME-2025, and a MATH-500 subset. Certification raises answer-key-match precision by 31 percentage points on the strong GPQA configuration and 46 on AIME, while answering only 35% and 21% of attempts. On near-ceiling MATH-500, gains are at most 5 points at a loss of 13-27 points of coverage. An output-token-matched self-consistency selector obtains 82.9% precision at 35% coverage, compared with the verifier's pooled 87.6% (paired difference: +4.8 points, 95% interval [-8.2, 18.5]). A diagnostic sample reveals a distinct failure mode: 52 of 88 completed problems end in invalid proof construction without ever reaching a judge. Finally, external-LLM acceptance of rendered certified chains is 61.0-67.6%, compared with 87.6% answer-key match. These audits lack human or formal-kernel ground truth. Open-weight auditor pairs show near-zero chance-corrected agreement, which measures consistency rather than accuracy against proof truth. These results motivate separate reporting of execution failures, selected-answer precision, and independently assessed proof validity. We provide per-configuration results and evaluation details in the supplement.
中文翻译
证明步骤验证将求解器的答案重写为带类型的步骤，并使用LLM裁判进行认证或拒绝。我们在开放权重模型上对GPQA、AIME-2025和MATH-500子集评估该流程。认证在强GPQA配置下使答案匹配精度提升31个百分点，在AIME上提升46个百分点，但仅回答35%和21%的尝试。在接近天花板的MATH-500上，收益最多5个百分点，但覆盖率损失达13-27个百分点。输出词匹配的自一致性选择器在35%覆盖率下实现82.9%精度，而验证器联合池化结果为87.6%（配对差异：+4.8点，95%置信区间[-8.2, 18.5]）。诊断样本揭示了一种独特失败模式：88个完成问题中有52个以无效证明构建结束，从未到达裁判阶段。外部LLM对已渲染认证链的接受率为61.0%-67.6%，低于87.6%的答案键匹配率。这些审计缺乏人工或形式内核真实标签。开放权重裁判对之间几乎无偶然校正一致率，这衡量的是内部一致性而非对证明真实的准确性。这些结果促使应分别报告执行失败、选中答案精度和独立评估的证明有效性。我们在补充材料中提供了各配置的结果和评估细节。
👤 Abhinav Gupta, Ben Slivinski, Yashica Patodia, Michael Saldivar 📅 2026-11-10 🔗 arXiv
[2611.14766]

Rational Use of Time for Social Coordination in Humans and AI
人类与AI在社会协调中对时间的理性运用
All social interactions are situated in time. Previous studies have shown that humans are able to use implicit temporal cues, such as the duration of a pause, to infer others' mental states. But how do humans use temporal cues for dynamic, fine-grained social coordination? And can AI systems do the same, without ``experiencing'' continuous real-time as humans do? We study these questions using a cooperative card game task called The Mind, which requires players to achieve a shared goal across repeated interactions but forbids explicit communication: the only available signal is the passage of time. We evaluate 150 humans, and two classes of AI models which reflect different ways of operationalizing time in artificial systems: computer-use agents (CUAs), and standard text-prompted LLMs. We find that human behavior is best captured by a Bayesian strategy that infers a partner's belief from their timing---and similarly for frontier models. However, unlike humans, none of the tested models seem to efficiently adapt their behaviors to their partners' over the course of the game. From a cognitive perspective, our findings suggest that humans perform implicit probabilistic reasoning about continuous-time signals in dynamic social settings, and that such reasoning mechanisms may emerge without real-time experience. Practically, our study highlights new challenges for AI systems to coordinate efficiently with other agents by learning to interpret and adapt to their implicit signals in dynamic settings.
中文翻译
所有社会互动都发生在时间之中。先前研究表明，人类能够利用隐含的时间线索（如停顿持续时间）推断他人的心理状态。但人类如何利用时间线索实现动态、精细的社会协调？AI系统能否做到这一点，而无需像人类一样‘经历’连续实时？我们通过一种名为《心灵》的合作卡牌游戏任务来研究这些问题，该任务要求玩家在重复互动中达成共同目标，但禁止显式沟通：唯一可用信号是时间的流逝。我们评估了150名人类，以及两类反映人工智能系统中时间操作方式不同的AI模型：计算机使用代理（CUAs）和标准文本提示LLMs。我们发现人类行为最符合基于贝叶斯策略的推理——从对方的时机推断其信念——前沿模型也表现出类似行为。然而，与人类不同，所有测试模型似乎都无法在游戏过程中有效适应其伙伴的行为。从认知角度看，我们的发现表明人类在动态社会环境中对连续时间信号进行隐含的概率推理，而这种推理机制可能无需真实时间体验即可涌现。从实践角度，本研究突出了AI系统在动态环境中学习解读并适应其他智能体隐含信号以实现高效协调的新挑战。
👤 Xiulin Yang, Sirisha Gudavalli, Jennifer Hu 📅 2026-11-10 🔗 arXiv
[2611.14704]

Code as Explanations: Executable and Verifiable LLM Explanations
代码即解释：可执行且可验证的大型语言模型解释
As large language models (LLMs) increasingly make consequential decisions and solve problems alongside humans, we must understand how models arrive at their conclusions. Understanding how an LLM behaves requires observing it across multiple inputs, yet free-text explanations such as chain-of-thought (CoT) are generated per instance and cannot be easily verified on new inputs. We propose {code explanations to address the mismatch in how LLM explanations are generated versus how they are evaluated: a program explains LLM behavior across a dataset of inputs, and its execution enables automatic verification. We define Consistency metric that measures the fraction of inputs on which the program reproduces the LLM's outputs, and use it to evaluate and select candidate programs. Across three open-source code-capable LLMs and eight tasks, we find that LLMs can produce highly consistent code explanations in several settings. Code explanations are also 6$\times$ to 100$\times$ shorter than CoT, and better align with model behavior in 9 out of 24 settings. However, consistency is significantly lower on tasks with more complex linguistic components, where generated programs reproduce model outputs no more frequently than an uninformative explanation baseline, and these programs often rely on longer hard-coded patterns. In experiments with Claude Opus 5.5, providing the model with consistency feedback and code execution improves explanation consistency across tasks, but consistency remains significantly lower on more complex tasks. Our findings establish executable code as a novel, verifiable, and compact approach to explaining LLM behavior and highlight verification feedback as a promising direction for improving explanation consistency. The code for this paper is at https://github.com/rajesh-lab/code-as-explanations.
中文翻译
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地与人类共同做出重要决策并解决问题，我们必须理解模型如何得出其结论。理解LLM的行为需要在多个输入上观察其表现，然而像思维链（CoT）这样的自由文本解释是针对每个实例生成的，难以在新输入上进行验证。我们提出{代码解释}来解决LLM解释生成方式与评估方式之间的不匹配问题：一个程序可以解释模型在一组输入上的行为，其执行过程支持自动验证。我们定义了一致性度量，用于衡量程序复现LLM输出的输入比例，并用它来评估和选择候选程序。在三个开源代码能力LLM和八个任务中，我们发现LLM在某些场景下能生成高度一致的代码解释。代码解释比CoT短6至100倍，并且在24个场景中有9个更符合模型行为。然而，在具有更复杂语言成分的任务上，一致性显著降低，生成的程序复现输出的频率并不优于无信息量的基线解释，且这些程序往往依赖于更长的硬编码模式。在Claude Opus 5.5的实验中，向模型提供一致性反馈和代码执行可提升解释一致性，但在更复杂任务上仍显著偏低。我们的研究确立了可执行代码作为解释LLM行为的一种新颖、可验证且紧凑的方法，并强调验证反馈是提升解释一致性的一个有前景方向。本文代码见https://github.com/rajesh-lab/code-as-explanations。
👤 Nhi Nguyen, Shauli Ravfogel, Rajesh Ranganath 📅 2026-11-10 🔗 arXiv
[2611.14683]
agentic-rlagent-benchmark
Trait Hitchhiking in LLM Post-Training
大语言模型后训练中的特质搭便车现象
Reinforcement learning (RL) is now a standard stage of language model post-training, and the compute devoted to it is growing rapidly. While it has been shown to improve capabilities, particularly for tasks with verifiable rewards, it can also change behaviours and internal properties that the reward does not explicitly target. We present a formalism to describe the evolution of "traits" during RL post-training and, in doing so, recover the Price equation from evolutionary biology. We use this to develop a mathematical model for what we call "trait hitchhiking": the amplification or suppression of non-targeted traits as training redistributes probability across completions. We evaluate this Price update expression on language models trained with GRPO and show that the Price estimates closely track the sampled trait dynamics. These results identify trait hitchhiking as a mechanism by which RL post-training can silently amplify undesirable traits.
中文翻译
强化学习（RL）如今已成为语言模型后训练的标准阶段，投入的计算资源迅速增长。尽管已有研究表明其能提升模型能力，特别是在具有可验证奖励的任务中，但它也可能改变那些未被奖励明确目标所指向的行为和内部属性。我们提出了一个形式化框架来描述RL后训练过程中‘特质’的演化，并在此过程中重新推导出进化生物学中的Price方程。基于此，我们建立了一个数学模型，描述我们称之为‘特质搭便车’的现象：在训练过程中，概率在不同完成结果间重新分配时，非目标特质被放大或抑制。我们在使用GRPO训练的语言模型上评估了这一Price更新表达式，发现Price估计值与采样到的特质动态高度吻合。这些结果揭示了‘特质搭便车’是RL后训练无声放大不良特质的一种机制。
👤 Adil Soubki, Elizabeth S.Z. Tan, Miles Cranmer 📅 2026-11-10 🔗 arXiv

[2611.17332]
ArchSpace: Recording, Reviewing, and Referencing LLM Architecture Research
ArchSpace：记录、回顾与引用大语言模型架构研究
Model architecture shapes the scaling behavior, training efficiency, and inference cost of LLMs, making it a central focus of both industry and academia. In industry, teams such as those behind DeepSeek, Qwen, and Kimi routinely introduce architectural innovations, yet public reports rarely document which ideas succeeded, which were abandoned, and why. In academia, limited compute and inconsistent baselines often restrict evaluations to small-scale, single-point comparisons, making it difficult to establish whether improvements hold at larger scales or translate into broader impact. ArchSpace addresses both gaps as a public platform for Recording, Reviewing, and Referencing Architecture Researc}: each proposal is recorded with its experiments and reasoning, reviewed by the community, and referenced by later work. To evaluate ideas before costly large-scale training, ArchSpace leverages scaling laws to predict model loss at larger scales from smaller experiments, a practice widely adopted in industry. We provide initial references that make the platform usable: Qwen3.5 and OLMo3 baselines, fitted scaling laws, and a reusable training recipe. Ideas validated through this review can in turn become new baselines, and ArchSpace thereby aims to broaden the range of ideas that shape the next generation of models.
中文翻译
模型架构决定了大语言模型的扩展行为、训练效率和推理成本，因此成为产业界和学术界的核心关注点。在产业界，DeepSeek、Qwen 和 Kimi 等团队频繁引入架构创新，但公开报告很少记录哪些想法成功、哪些被放弃以及原因。在学术界，受限于计算资源和不一致的基线，评估往往局限于小规模、单点比较，难以判断改进是否在更大规模下依然有效或具有广泛影响。ArchSpace 作为一个公开平台，旨在填补这两项空白：记录、评审和引用架构研究。每个提案均附带实验过程与推理依据，接受社区评审，并可被后续工作引用。为在昂贵的大规模训练前评估想法，ArchSpace 利用缩放定律从小型实验预测大规模下的模型损失，这一做法已在产业界广泛应用。我们提供了初始参考基准，使平台可用：包括 Qwen3.5 和 OLMo3 基线、拟合的缩放定律以及可复用的训练方案。通过该评审验证的想法可进一步成为新的基线，从而推动更多创新思想塑造下一代模型。
👤 Jie Wang, Tong Zhu, JiaHao Kuang, Zhe Xu, Yunkai Chai et al. 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.17280]
Synthesis Smuggling: Maliciously Hiding Inferred Personal Attributes in LLM Outputs
合成走私：在大语言模型输出中恶意隐藏推断出的个人属性
We propose Synthesis Smuggling, a novel threat model in which an adversarial LLM infers sensitive information about a user from routine assistance-seeking interactions and steganographically encodes that into innocuous-looking model-generated content. The user may then unknowingly transmit that information by sharing the generated artifact, say as an email, social-media post, or message, through ordinary communication channels. An adversary monitoring these channels can discover this information. We demonstrate this threat by training two open-weight models to infer gender and age-group labels from conversational cues and encode their predictions using invisible characters or word choices. These results show that user-shared model outputs can become a covert channel for personal inferences, even when the model itself has no direct means of contacting an adversary.
中文翻译
我们提出“合成走私”这一新型威胁模型：攻击者利用大语言模型从用户日常求助互动中推断敏感信息，并通过隐写术将这些信息编码进看似无害的生成内容中。用户在分享生成成果（如邮件、社交媒体帖子或消息）时可能无意间传输这些信息，攻击者可通过监控常规通信渠道获取。我们通过训练两个开源权重模型，分别从对话线索中推断性别和年龄组标签，并利用不可见字符或词汇选择编码预测结果，验证了该威胁的存在。结果表明，即使模型本身无法直接联系攻击者，用户共享的模型输出仍可能成为泄露个人推断的隐蔽通道。
👤 Gautam Galada, Supreeth Shastri, Sagnik Ray Choudhury 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.17107]
Disentangling Spurious Correlations: How IRM Handles Diverse Shortcuts in Text Classification
解耦虚假相关性：IRM如何处理文本分类中的多样化捷径
In NLP, models trained via Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) often exploit spurious correlations, such as dialectal markers, at the expense of robust linguistic features, thereby degrading their out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization. Invariant Risk Minimization (IRM) aims to suppress such shortcuts by identifying predictive relationships that remain stable across environments, but its practical utility for NLP remains debated. We use a causality-driven framework to evaluate IRM under three shortcut-generating mechanisms: label-generated features, confounding, and selection bias. Our results show that IRM successfully rejects shortcuts across the evaluated causal and anti-causal structures, including under limited environmental variation and increasing label noise. We then extend our analysis beyond controlled synthetic artifacts to two less constrained settings: gender-profession associations in Bias in Bios and semantically loaded actor-name shortcuts in ActorCorr. These experiments show that invariant learning can mitigate more realistic textual shortcuts, while semantically meaningful features require careful calibration of the invariance penalty.
中文翻译
在自然语言处理中，通过经验风险最小化（ERM）训练的模型常利用虚假相关性（如方言标记）而非稳健的语言特征，从而降低其在分布外（OOD）场景下的泛化能力。不变风险最小化（IRM）旨在通过识别跨环境保持稳定的预测关系来抑制此类捷径，但其在NLP中的实际效用仍存争议。我们采用因果驱动框架，在三种捷径生成机制下评估IRM：由标签生成的特征、混淆因素以及选择偏差。结果表明，IRM在所评估的因果与反因果结构中均能成功拒绝捷径，包括在环境变化有限和标签噪声增加的情况下。随后，我们将分析扩展至两个更宽松的设置：Bias in Bios中的性别-职业关联，以及ActorCorr中的语义负载型角色名捷径。实验显示，不变学习能够缓解更真实的文本捷径，但语义有意义的特征需要对不变性惩罚进行精细校准。
👤 Paul Grunenwald, Benoît Crabbé, Guillaume Wisniewski, Pirmin Lemberger 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.17091]
Optimal Single-History Tokenization Disambiguation for Provably Secure Linguistic Steganography
最优单历史分词消歧：面向可证明安全语言隐写术的解决方案
Provably secure linguistic steganography assumes that the receiver recovers the sender's tokens, but a text channel delivers only text, and ambiguous tokenizations break extraction. Existing fixes change the distribution, synchronize whole pools of tokens at a capacity that collapses with large vocabularies, or track several token histories. We prove that if extraction is exact and error-free across full-support language models, a single-history receiver is confined to a prefix-free candidate set at every step -- even if it reads the entire future text and the sender constrains later steps -- and must therefore give up the stop entropy, the entropy of deciding whether each prefix token ends there. Fixing these stop decisions on the prefix forest with shared randomness yields coin projection: a closed-form scheme that attains the bound, works with any distribution coder in one model call per token, preserves ordinary sampling, and refines SyncPool for every key. Across English, Chinese and Japanese, ambiguous text is rare (at most 11 in 1,000 tokens), yet the stop entropy at word and morpheme prefixes takes 7.2-9.0% of the entropy at every step; SyncPool's further loss comes from space and single-byte UTF-8 hubs that merge subtrees while carrying almost no stop uncertainty. Coin projection keeps 91-93% of the string entropy for the coder at every vocabulary size; with the full vocabulary, it turns 2.16-6.85 times as much entropy into message bits as SyncPool and recovers every completed message.
中文翻译
可证明安全的语言隐写术假设接收方能恢复发送方的词元，但文本信道仅传递文本，模糊的分词会破坏提取。现有方法要么改变词频分布，要么同步整个词元池，容量随大词汇量急剧下降，或需追踪多个词元历史。我们证明，若在全支持语言模型下提取完全精确且无误，单历史接收方在每一步只能处于前缀无关的候选集合中——即使读取完整未来文本，且发送方约束后续步骤——因此必须放弃停止熵（即决定每个前缀词元是否终止的熵）。通过共享随机性固定这些停止决策，我们提出“硬币投影”（coin projection）方案：一种闭式解法，达到理论极限，可在每个词元的一次模型调用中适配任意分布编码器，保留常规采样方式，并对所有密钥改进SyncPool。在英语、中文和日语中，模糊文本极为罕见（每1000词元最多11处），但词和语素前缀的停止熵占每步熵的7.2%-9.0%；SyncPool的额外损失源于空格和单字节UTF-8枢纽合并子树，却几乎不携带停止不确定性。硬币投影在所有词汇量下保留91%-93%的字符串熵；使用完整词汇表时，其产生的消息比特是SyncPool的2.16-6.85倍，并能完整恢复所有完成的消息。
👤 Feng Jiang 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16968]
Morphological Simplification in Alzheimer's Disease: Computational Analysis and Identification From Connected Speech
阿尔茨海默病中的形态简化：从连贯言语中进行的计算分析与识别
Purpose: Morphology is often regarded as relatively spared in Alzheimer's disease (AD) and is rarely modeled in connected speech. This study asked which morphological features distinguish speakers with AD from healthy controls, how well morphology classifies AD compared with other linguistic levels and BERT-based models, and whether the signal exceeds transcript length and vocabulary. Method: Fifty-five automatic measures in eight families, seven of them new and theory-motivated, were computed from participant speech in the ADReSS Cookie Theft descriptions (78 AD, 78 controls) and analyzed with group comparisons, equivalence tests, SHAP, family ablation, likelihood-ratio tests, feature-selection classifiers, and BERT-based models. The automatic measures were validated manually, and models were transferred to two other corpora. Results: Nineteen of 38 measures differed between groups: speakers with AD produced fewer suffixed and inflected words, shorter verbs and nouns, and fewer present-progressive but more past-tense forms (largest effect r = -0.42), whereas errors were rare and statistically equivalent between groups. Eight measures survived adjustment for length and lexical frequency. With test-set feature selection, morphology reached a macro-F1 of 0.85, on par with syntax and above fine-tuned BERT (0.80 over five seeds); fully cross-validated selection gave 0.62. Adding morphology to BERT improved AUC (0.91 vs. 0.89) but not macro-F1. Transfer was limited, although two thirds of the effects kept their direction in mild cognitive impairment. Conclusions: AD connected speech shows morphological simplification and temporal reanchoring rather than breakdown. Morphology is more informative than its reputation suggests. Keywords: Alzheimer's disease; morphology; connected speech; ADReSS; inflection; tense and aspect; SHAP; machine learning; BERT
中文翻译
目的：形态学通常被认为在阿尔茨海默病（AD）中相对不受影响，且在连贯言语中很少被建模。本研究探讨哪些形态特征能区分AD患者与健康对照组，形态学在分类AD方面相较于其他语言层次及基于BERT的模型表现如何，以及该信号是否超过转录长度和词汇量的影响。方法：从ADReSS Cookie Theft描述任务中（78名AD患者，78名对照）的参与者言语中计算了55项自动测量指标，涵盖八个类别，其中七项为新提出且理论驱动。采用组间比较、等效性检验、SHAP分析、家族消融、似然比检验、特征选择分类器和基于BERT的模型进行分析。自动指标经人工验证，模型在另外两个语料库上进行了迁移测试。结果：38项指标中有19项在两组间存在差异：AD患者产生的带后缀和屈折词较少，动词和名词较短，现在进行时形式较少但过去时形式较多（最大效应r = -0.42），而错误发生率低且两组间无统计差异。八项指标在调整长度和词汇频率后仍显著。在测试集特征选择下，形态学达到宏F1为0.85，与句法相当，高于微调BERT（0.80，五次种子平均）；完全交叉验证选择得分为0.62。将形态学加入BERT可提升AUC（0.91 vs. 0.89），但不影响宏F1。迁移效果有限，尽管三分之二的效应方向在轻度认知障碍中保持一致。结论：AD连贯言语表现出形态简化和时间锚定重构，而非崩溃。形态学的信息量远超其声誉所暗示。

[2611.16956]
Lara Mini Base: Language Diversity as a Resource, Not a Trade-off
Lara Mini Base：语言多样性作为资源，而非权衡
General-purpose language models are trained mostly on English: over open models we review, at most, non-English pretraining data makes up only a 12% share. Models built for European languages give them far more, but score well below general-purpose models of the same size. A common concern is that, in small models, languages compete for capacity. We present Lara Mini base, a model trained from scratch on 6T tokens in English and 44 other languages, which together cover all 24 official languages of the European Union. As training progresses, the 44 other languages together come to outweigh English. To give these languages enough capacity at low cost, Lara Mini base uses a mixture of experts architecture with 7.2B total and 0.6B active parameters. Among open base models with up to 2B active parameters, it achieves the highest average score across standard English, multilingual, math, and code benchmarks, ahead of models trained on 4 to 6 times as many tokens. Its margin is largest on multilingual benchmarks in the languages it supports and on math benchmarks. It supports contexts of up to 128k tokens. We find that our MoE architecture reduces capacity competition between languages. For a single user on one GPU, without quantization, it generates text at least 1.8 times faster than any model with a higher average score. We release the weights of Lara Mini base under the CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 license. In addition, we will release T-HellaSwag, a higher-quality machine translation of the commonsense reasoning benchmark HellaSwag into 110 language variants.
中文翻译

[2611.16919]
Beyond Word Error Rate: A Failure Taxonomy of Streaming Dictation ASR
超越词错误率：流式语音转写ASR的失败分类
Speech recognition (ASR) systems are usually compared by word error rate (WER) on recorded files, after punctuation, capital letters and number format are removed. In dictation, the audio is streamed live and the text goes into the user's message as written, so what matters is which mistakes the user has to fix. The test set, streamed to eleven ASR systems, comprises 106 scripted English dictations from one speaker (read in a quiet room, with a fan on, and whispered), 20 clips in each of eleven other language and dialect sets, and 400 recordings that join clips in two languages. Every error was sorted by a fixed rule into four levels: recognition, output formatting, language handling and, for the self-hosted models, audio dropped before the model. Systems with similar WER failed in different ways. Zoom Scribe and OpenAI's GPT-Live-Transcribe have almost the same WER on quiet English (7.4% and 7.3%), but 34.7% and 8.8% on whispered English. Zoom and both AssemblyAI models left out 26-28% of whispered words, and still 21-26% with their end-of-turn detection relaxed or off; seven other systems left out at most 3%. Qwen3-ASR's 32% came from the speech detection in our own deployment (6% when relaxed). Across 107 EARS speakers, the same four systems lost 7-31% of whispered words, while six others lost under 2%. With only English selected, Gemini, Deepgram and our self-hosted Whisper lost the non-English part of a two-language recording in 86-99% of recordings, against 33-46% with both languages selected, while GPT-Live-Transcribe, OpenAI's realtime Whisper and Meta lost it in at most 20%. Running Whisper behind a Silero voice activity detector that drops audio judged to be silence raised whispered WER from 10.1% to 51.4%. Most English results come from one read speaker. The code, the recordings, both references and every transcript are released.
中文翻译
语音识别（ASR）系统通常在去除标点、大小写和数字格式后，通过词错误率（WER）在录制文件上的表现进行比较。但在字处理场景中，音频是实时流式传输的，文本会直接进入用户的输入内容，因此关键在于用户需要修正哪些错误。测试集被实时传送给十一个ASR系统，包含106段由同一说话人朗读的英文字处理录音（在安静房间内，有风扇噪音且低声说出），十一组其他语言和方言的各20段剪辑，以及400段双语拼接录音。每个错误按固定规则分为四个层级：识别错误、输出格式错误、语言处理错误，以及自托管模型的音频丢失问题。具有相似WER的系统在不同方面失败。Zoom Scribe和OpenAI的GPT-Live-Transcribe在安静英语中的WER分别为7.4%和7.3%，但在低声英语中分别达到34.7%和8.8%。Zoom和两个AssemblyAI模型在低声英语中漏掉了26%-28%的词语，即使放宽或关闭句末检测仍保留21%-26%；其余七个系统最多漏掉3%。Qwen3-ASR的32%错误源于我们部署中的语音检测（放松后为6%）。在107位EARS说话人中，相同四个系统漏掉了7%-31%的低声词语，而另外六个系统漏掉不足2%。仅考虑英语时，Gemini、Deepgram和我们自托管的Whisper在双语录音中丢失非英语部分的比例高达86%-99%，而在双语均选的情况下仅为33%-46%；而GPT-Live-Transcribe、OpenAI的实时Whisper和Meta则最多丢失20%。在Whisper前加入Silero语音活动检测器（剔除被判定为静音的音频）使低声WER从10.1%上升至51.4%。大多数英语结果来自一位朗读者。代码、录音、参考文本及所有转录均已公开。
👤 Harras Mansoor, Muhammad Wisal, Hassan Bhatti, Masood Shakir 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16843]
Shortcuts or Memorisation? Revisiting Data Corruption in Natural Language Understanding with Large Language Models
捷径还是记忆？重新审视大语言模型在自然语言理解中的数据破坏问题
Pre-trained encoders fine-tuned on natural language inference (NLI) and other language understanding tasks keep much of their accuracy after the input is perturbed, e.g. by removing all nouns or shuffling the words. Does the same hold for large language models (LLMs), and if so, is it because they have seen the benchmarks during pre-training? In this paper we revisit these perturbation experiments with eight LLMs alongside fine-tuned encoders on GLUE and the three rounds of ANLI. Regardless of model size, every system that answers directly retains 36-72% of its above-chance performance without nouns on MNLI compared to only 14-29% on ANLI. Only a model that reasons before answering departs from this pattern. We also check contamination indicators (including exact $n$-gram counts in the public pre-training data of two of the models) and find no link between exposure and robustness. Our strongest evidence comes from 368 new, human-validated items that none of the models can have seen. All eight LLMs keep more of their performance without nouns on items written in MNLI's style than on items written without its cues. When the perturbation instead removes the function words carrying such cues, this advantage reverses. In light of these results, we argue that robustness to perturbation on familiar NLI benchmarks reflects dataset shortcuts rather than memorisation, and tells us more about the benchmark than about the model.
中文翻译
在自然语言推理（NLI）及其他语言理解任务上微调的预训练编码器，在输入被扰动（如移除所有名词或打乱词语顺序）后仍能保持较高的准确率。这一现象是否同样适用于大型语言模型（LLMs）？如果是，原因是否是因为它们在预训练中见过这些基准数据？本文针对八种LLMs和微调编码器在GLUE及ANLI三轮数据集上重新进行了扰动实验。无论模型规模如何，所有直接作答的系统在MNLI上移除名词后仍保持36%-72%的高于随机水平的表现，而在ANLI上仅维持14%-29%。只有在回答前进行推理的模型偏离了这一模式。我们还检查了污染指标（包括两个模型公共预训练数据中的精确n-gram计数），未发现暴露程度与鲁棒性之间的关联。最强证据来自368个全新的人工验证样本，这些样本均未被任何模型接触过。所有八个LLM在以MNLI风格撰写的样本中，移除名词后的表现优于无MNLI提示风格的样本；但当扰动改为移除承载此类提示的功能词时，这一优势反转。基于这些结果，我们认为在熟悉NLI基准上的抗扰动鲁棒性反映的是数据集捷径，而非记忆，更多揭示的是基准本身的特点，而非模型能力。
👤 Aarne Talman, Stergios Chatzikyriakidis 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16704]
Forecasting Sales Delay through Time-aware Analysis of Sales Events in Textual Reports
通过时间感知分析文本报告中的销售事件来预测销售延迟
Business-to-business (B2B) companies develop and deliver a one-of-a-kind product in response to a client's orders. To prepare resources for development beforehand, companies estimate dates when they will receive orders from clients in their sales process, called scheduled order dates. However, dates when the companies actually receive orders can differ from the scheduled order dates. This difference increases costs in the development. If the difference could be forecasted before receiving orders, companies could optimize their resource preparation. Towards reducing the costs, we introduce sales delay forecasting, a novel task of forecasting delay days from the scheduled order dates during sales process where sales representatives (sales reps) interact with the clients to receive orders. Sales delay forecasting is challenging because the sales process progress inherently involves uncertainty and depends on the client's willingness to order. To address this task, we propose confidence features which represent sales reps' confidence as a proxy measure of the client's willingness to order. To represent it, our method extracts dates of future sales events anticipated by sales reps from textual reports. To consider whether the confidence objectively looks reasonable, we also introduce status features which describe sales process status based on current date and past events. In experiments using real sales data, our method achieved a 16% lower mean absolute error compared with a feature-based baseline similar to a prior work. Moreover, our method outperformed non-feature-based baselines using large language models, indicating that it is crucial to explicitly create our features.
中文翻译
企业间（B2B）公司根据客户订单开发并交付独一无二的产品。为提前准备资源，企业需估算在销售过程中从客户处收到订单的预定日期，即预定订单日期。然而，实际收到订单的日期可能与预定日期存在差异，这会增加开发成本。若能在收到订单前预测该差异，企业便可优化资源准备。为此，我们提出销售延迟预测这一新任务，旨在基于销售代表与客户互动过程中的文本报告，预测从预定订单日期起的延迟天数。该任务具有挑战性，因为销售进程本身包含不确定性，且依赖客户的订购意愿。为应对这一挑战，我们提出置信度特征，作为销售代表对客户订购意愿的代理度量。我们的方法从文本报告中提取销售代表预期的未来销售事件日期。此外，为评估置信度是否客观合理，我们还引入状态特征，基于当前日期和历史事件描述销售进程状态。在真实销售数据上的实验表明，我们的方法相比基于特征的基线（类似先前工作）平均绝对误差降低了16%。此外，我们的方法优于不使用特征的大语言模型基线，表明显式构建特征至关重要。

EvenVLM: A Text and Multimodal Dataset for the Even Language with Vision-Language Fine-Tuning
EvenVLM：面向Even语言的文本与多模态数据集及视觉-语言微调
We present EvenVLM, the first text and multimodal (VQA) dataset for Even (Lamut), an endangered Northern Tungusic language spoken by approximately 6,800 people in Siberia. Our dataset comprises 50,521 structured Even-Russian entries extracted from dictionary and phrasebook sources via GPT-4o Mini-based parsing, from which we synthetically generate 107,543 training examples spanning translation, grammar, reading comprehension, and semantic classification. To address tokenization inefficiency on Even's agglutinative morphology, we propose a three-step tokenizer expansion algorithm that adds Even-specific graphemes (eng, oe, o-umlaut), whitelisted vocabulary, and BPE merges, reducing average tokens per word by 27% (4.99 to 3.64). Using LoRA fine-tuning of Qwen3-VL-4B, we adapt the model for text tasks (0.53% trainable parameters, rank 32) and separately for visual question answering (rank 64, frozen vision encoder). Our best configuration achieves 11.4% exact match and a 13.6 weighted score on text benchmarks, 0.1283 chrF for sentence translation, and 65% accuracy on VQA (up from 0% for the base model and text-only fine-tuning). We release our datasets, evaluation benchmarks, and training pipeline to support further research on Even and other low-resource languages.
