[2611.16270]
SMIC: Shared Multiscale Input-Reference Conditioning across Transformer Layers for Natural Language Inference
Transformer 层间共享多尺度输入-参考条件化用于自然语言推理
Natural Language Inference models can be sensitive to localized lexical changes. We introduce Shared Multiscale Input-Reference Conditioning (SMIC), which constructs a single input-dependent multiscale reference and reuses it across Transformer layers. Each layer applies its own multiscale transformation to the shared reference and injects the resulting conditioning feature before the attention update. We instantiate SMIC in a token--context Transformer (TCT) and conduct from-scratch comparisons against decoder-based and Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) baselines across all three ANLI rounds. TCT-SMIC attains the highest observed ten-seed mean accuracy on all three ANLI rounds. The ablations further support the contribution of SMIC. We evaluate a real-valued Llama-style plug-in and extend deletion/insertion diagnostics to pretrained ModernBERT encoder and Llama decoder models with and without SMIC. Across the evaluated perturbation protocols, TCT-SMIC exhibits comparatively low pooled-attention redistribution and smaller signed accuracy changes. We further establish a finite-sample bound on average subset-mass redistribution in terms of the mean Jensen--Shannon divergence between the restricted and renormalized attention distributions.
中文翻译
自然语言推理模型对局部词汇变化可能十分敏感。我们引入了共享多尺度输入-参考条件化（SMIC），构建一个依赖于输入的多尺度参考，并在整个 Transformer 层间复用。每一层对其共享参考应用自身的多尺度变换，并在注意力更新前注入生成的条件特征。我们在一个 token--context Transformer（TCT）中实例化 SMIC，并在 ANLI 的全部三个回合中与基于解码器和多层感知机（MLP）的基线进行从零开始的对比。TCT-SMIC 在所有三个 ANLI 回合中达到了最高的十次种子平均准确率。消融实验进一步支持了 SMIC 的贡献。我们评估了一个类似 Llama 的实值插件，并将删除/插入诊断扩展至预训练的 ModernBERT 编码器和 Llama 解码器模型（含与不含 SMIC）。在所有评估的扰动协议中，TCT-SMIC 表现出较低的注意力重分配和较小的符号准确率变化。我们还建立了关于平均子集质量重分配的有限样本界，其以受限与重归一化注意力分布之间的平均 Jensen--Shannon 散度为表达。
👤 Xin Zhang, Victor S. Sheng 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16264]
Label-Efficient Document-Grounded Factuality Detection via Cross-Paraphrastic Invariance Learning
基于跨改写不变性学习的标签高效文档支撑事实性检测
Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent claims that are unsupported by a given source document, motivating accurate and efficient document-grounded factuality detection. Existing LLM-based evaluators often require repeated inference and incur high computational cost, whereas compact factuality detectors are efficient at inference time but commonly rely on large labeled or synthetically generated corpora. We propose CPIL (Cross-Paraphrastic Invariance Learning), a two-stage Siamese framework that converts a small labeled set into relational supervision without acquiring additional factuality labels. CPIL constructs positive pairs from distinct label-preserving paraphrastic views of the same document-claim instance and negative pairs from opposite-label examples associated with the same source document. It further introduces quantile-aware semi-hard negative mining, which derives a boundary from the positive-pair similarity distribution and balances informative negatives with broader coverage. In the two-stage training procedure, Stage 1 learns an encoder from these task-aware pairs, and Stage 2 jointly fine-tunes the encoder and a linear classification head for binary factuality prediction. At inference time, CPIL requires no paraphrase generation, pair construction, or Siamese comparison and uses only one encoder-classifier forward pass. On the 11-task LLM-AggreFact benchmark, CPIL-2% and CPIL-3% achieve macro-F1 scores of 81.04 and 81.60 while using only 2-3% of the FactCG-released labeled pool. Both settings outperform all compared compact detectors and two vanilla LLM references. Ablations and sensitivity analyses support the contributions of task-aware pair construction, dynamic negative mining, and multi-pivot paraphrasing.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLM）能够生成流畅但缺乏源文档支持的陈述，这促使人们需要准确且高效的文档支撑事实性检测方法。现有的基于 LLM 的评估器通常需要多次推理，计算成本高；而紧凑的事实性检测器虽然推理效率高，但通常依赖大规模标注或合成语料库。我们提出 CPIL（跨改写不变性学习），一种两阶段孪生框架，无需获取额外的事实性标签即可将少量标注数据转化为关系监督。CPIL 从同一文档-陈述实例的不同保标签改写视角构造正样本对，从同一源文档关联的相反标签示例中构造负样本对。此外，引入分位数感知的半困难负样本挖掘，从正样本对相似度分布中推导边界，平衡有信息量的负样本与更广覆盖范围。在两阶段训练过程中，第一阶段从这些任务感知样本对中学习编码器，第二阶段联合微调编码器和线性分类头以实现二元事实性预测。推理时，CPIL 无需生成改写、构造样本对或进行孪生比较，仅需一次编码器-分类器前向传播。在 11 个任务的 LLM-AggreFact 基准测试中，CPIL-2% 和 CPIL-3% 分别达到 81.04 和 81.60 的宏 F1 分数，仅使用 FactCG 发布标注池的 2-3%。两种设置均优于所有对比的紧凑检测器及两个原始 LLM 参考模型。消融实验和敏感性分析支持任务感知样本构造、动态负样本挖掘和多枢轴改写的贡献。
👤 Chao Chen, Shanshan Lin, Dongsheng Hong, Sibo Ju, Wenjie Yang et al. 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16260]
Low Language Transfer Speech-to-Text
低语言迁移语音转文本
A multilingual speech recogniser has heard much speech in its own languages. We ask how much of that it brings to a language it was never trained on, when it is fine-tuned on the little that language has. We score 28 open multilingual models untouched on Low-Language-TTS, 50 low-resource languages in 11 writing systems, and fine-tune 24 of them on at most 1,000 clips a language, 82 hours in all, with a dev set choosing every epoch. Fine-tuning lowers every model's macro character error rate (CER), by 16.6 to 53.1 points, to 9.5 at best, and a word language model takes three CTC models 1.2 to 1.4 points lower; WER is still 34.4 at best. On the 12 languages that no model lists, every model starts at 46 or worse and the best ends at 11.7. The same architectures from random weights stay 60 points or more behind, so the gain is transfer, and an encoder left untouched already gives a Whisper or Qwen decoder most of what it needs. It is the language, not the corpus: on FLEURS recordings that ours do not share, the median model keeps 79% of its gain. It costs the languages the models knew, 5.7 points of CER on English, Spanish, French and German in the median model. Low-rank adapters on 4% of the weights come within 1.1 points of tuning everything, and on 2 of 3 models forget less. Seq2seq and language-model decoders are done within three to five epochs, while a new CTC head is still improving at twenty or thirty. Data beats epochs: 25 clips a language take Qwen3-ASR 0.6B from 60.9 to 39.3, and at equal clips presented, ten times the data is worth 9.7 points. A model that first learned forty of the languages learns the other ten no faster. On a public Malay benchmark, untouched Whisper large-v3 scores 8.9 when it detects the language and 14.9 when told the clip is Malay, because it then translates the English that speakers mix in; trained on the Scicom telephony dataset it scores 6.0.
中文翻译
一个多语言语音识别器在其自身语言中已听过大量语音。我们探讨当它在从未训练过的语言上进行微调时，能带来多少已有知识。我们对 28 个未在低语言语音转文本（Low-Language-TTS）上接触过的开放多语言模型进行了评分，涵盖 50 种低资源语言，分布在 11 种书写系统中，并对其中 24 个模型在每种语言最多 1,000 段语音（总计 82 小时）上进行微调，使用开发集每轮选择最优模型。微调使所有模型的宏观字符错误率（CER）下降 16.6 至 53.1 点，最低达 9.5；而词语言模型使三个 CTC 模型的 CER 再降低 1.2 至 1.4 点，但词错误率（WER）仍为 34.4 最佳。在 12 种无任何模型列出的语言上，所有模型初始 CER 均在 46 或以上，最佳结果为 11.7。从随机权重初始化的相同架构始终落后 60 点以上，因此增益来自知识迁移；一个未被触碰的编码器已为 Whisper 或 Qwen 解码器提供了大部分所需内容。关键是语言本身，而非语料库：在我们未共享的 FLEURS 录音上，中位模型保留了 79% 的增益。这种迁移代价是模型已知语言的性能损失，中位模型在英语、西班牙语、法语和德语上的 CER 上升了 5.7 点。仅对 4% 权重使用低秩适配器，性能仅比全量微调低 1.1 点，且在 3 个模型中的 2 个中遗忘更少。Seq2seq 和语言模型解码器在三到五个周期内完成，而新 CTC 头在二十至三十个周期仍在提升。数据胜过周期：每语言 25 段语音使 Qwen3-ASR 0.6B 的 CER 从 60.9 降至 39.3；在相同段数下，十倍数据带来 9.7 点提升。一个先学习了四十种语言的模型，并未更快学习其余十种语言。在公开的马来语基准测试中，未经微调的 Whisper large-v3 在检测到语言时得分为 8.9，在被告知片段为马来语时得分为 14.9，因为此时它会翻译说话者混入的英语；而在 Scicom 电话数据集上微调后，得分降至 6.0。
👤 Husein Zolkepli 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16183]
Data-Efficient LLM Safety Alignment Guided by Interpretable Features
基于可解释特征的数据高效大语言模型安全对齐
Existing large language models typically rely on massive fine-tuning datasets for safety alignment, which results in higher costs for safety alignment and often leads to the problem of over-refusal. We propose Less is Safer (LIS), a compact yet highly effective dataset for safety alignment, constructed through interpretable feature-guided selection and adversarial synthesis. Specifically, we leverage Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) activations to efficiently select prompts that cover diverse and interpretable safety features. Then, we employ a multi-agent adversarial synthesis framework to generate harmless prompts that nevertheless elicit unnecessary refusals. LIS contains only 2.4K samples, yet outperforms much larger datasets, e.g., TRIDENT-Core (26K), TRIDENT-Edge (19K) and WildJailbreak (262K), in overall performance on four safety and five over-refusal benchmarks. Through ablation experiments, we demonstrate that neither safety data nor over-refusal data alone can achieve optimal results. We also used safety features from SAE, annotated by an LLM, for data filtering, but the results were inferior to those obtained using the pipeline proposed in this paper.
中文翻译
现有的大型语言模型通常依赖大规模微调数据集进行安全对齐，导致安全对齐成本较高，并常引发过度拒绝问题。我们提出Less is Safer（LIS），一种紧凑但高度有效的安全对齐数据集，通过可解释特征引导选择和对抗合成构建而成。具体而言，我们利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）激活来高效筛选涵盖多样且可解释安全特征的提示。随后，我们采用多智能体对抗合成框架生成无害但能引发不必要的拒绝行为的提示。LIS仅包含2.4K样本，但在四个安全基准和五个过度拒绝基准上的整体表现优于更大规模的数据集，如TRIDENT-Core（26K）、TRIDENT-Edge（19K）和WildJailbreak（262K）。通过消融实验，我们证明仅使用安全数据或仅使用过度拒绝数据均无法取得最优效果。我们还尝试使用SAE提取的安全特征并由大语言模型标注后进行数据过滤，但结果仍劣于本文提出的流水线方法。
👤 Hongyu Li, Zhan Yu, Zhe Zhao, Kimmo Yan, Xiong Kuang et al. 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16139]
Temporal Context-Aware Spam Detection Using Transformer-Based NLP Models
基于Transformer的NLP模型的时序上下文感知垃圾信息检测
Spam detection is usually treated as single-message classification, ignoring behavioral patterns that emerge across sequences of messages from the same sender. We study outbound SMS spam detection at the carrier level, where the goal is to predict at send time whether a carrier will flag a message as spam. Using a proprietary production database of 43,741 outbound SMS messages with UTC transmission timestamps and carrier delivery statuses, we report three sets of results. On an 8,749-message held-out test set, a TF-IDF + logistic regression baseline reaches AUC-ROC 0.8721, and a text-only DeBERTa-v3-base classifier reaches AUC-ROC 0.9011 and F1 0.8412. A deployed DistilBERT model augmented with inter-message temporal features reaches accuracy 0.8238, spam-class F1 0.8382, and recall 0.9128 at threshold 0.5 on a separate, class-balanced 20,000-message production evaluation set; these results are not directly comparable with the held-out test results. We also describe TemporalBertSpam, an architecture that encodes each sender's message history with a causal Transformer, and outline the same-split evaluation needed to measure its contribution.
中文翻译
垃圾信息检测通常被视为单条消息分类任务，忽视了同一发送者消息序列中浮现的行为模式。我们研究运营商层面的出站短信垃圾信息检测，目标是在发送时预测运营商是否会将某条消息标记为垃圾信息。基于一个包含43,741条出站短信的专有生产数据库，包含UTC传输时间戳和运营商投递状态，我们报告了三组结果。在8,749条独立测试集上，TF-IDF + 逻辑回归基线的AUC-ROC为0.8721，纯文本DeBERTa-v3-base分类器的AUC-ROC为0.9011，F1为0.8412。一个部署的DistilBERT模型结合消息间时序特征，在独立的、类别平衡的20,000条生产评估集上，阈值为0.5时准确率为0.8238，垃圾类F1为0.8382，召回率为0.9128；这些结果与独立测试结果不可直接比较。我们还介绍了TemporalBertSpam架构，该架构使用因果Transformer编码每个发送者的消息历史，并概述了需保持相同划分的评估方式以衡量其贡献。
👤 Fariz Zeynalov, Razim Mammadli 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16051]
Human-Likeness and Conversational Preference Diverge for Synthesized Agent Speech in Customer-Service Calls
合成代理语音在客户服务通话中的人类相似性与对话偏好存在分歧
Listeners can judge an agent's speech on how human it sounds (human-likeness) and on how much they would want to talk to it (conversational preference). Listening tests rarely ask about conversational preference, so the relation between the two judgments is unclear. We compared them in paired-choice tests on 120 turns that actors improvised as customer-service representatives. On each trial, two agents spoke the same turn. In Study 1, both agents came from six commercial text-to-speech systems, each with eight voices. In Study 2, one was the actor's original recording and the other a system. Some systems ranked higher on human-likeness, and others on conversational preference. Listeners chose systems over actors in 19% of human-likeness trials but 36% of conversational preference trials (and 49% for the most preferred system). On both judgments, variation among a system's voices was about three-quarters of that between systems. Of three automatic naturalness scores, two predicted lower human-likeness, and none predicted conversational preference. In this setting, listeners tended to favor faster speech and fewer pauses between clauses on both judgments, and more pitch variation for conversational preference. Thus, measuring only human-likeness or testing one voice per system can misjudge which agents listeners would rather talk to.
中文翻译
听众可以判断代理语音的自然程度（人类相似性）以及他们是否愿意与其交谈（对话偏好）。由于听觉测试很少涉及对话偏好，因此这两种判断之间的关系尚不明确。我们在120个由演员即兴扮演客服代表的对话回合中进行了配对选择测试。每个试验中，两个代理说出相同的语句。在研究1中，两个代理均来自六个商用文本转语音系统，每个系统有八个声音；在研究2中，一个是演员的原始录音，另一个是系统生成的语音。某些系统在人类相似性上排名较高，而另一些则在对话偏好上表现更优。在人类相似性测试中，听众选择了系统而非演员的比例为19%，而在对话偏好测试中这一比例上升至36%（对于最受欢迎的系统甚至达到49%）。在两种判断中，同一系统内不同声音之间的差异约为系统间差异的四分之三。三种自动自然度评分中，有两个预测了较低的人类相似性，但没有一个能预测对话偏好。在此情境下，听众在两种判断中都倾向于更快的语速和更少的句间停顿，而在对话偏好方面则更偏好更大的音高变化。因此，仅测量人类相似性或每系统仅测试一个声音可能会错误判断听众更愿意与哪个代理交谈。
👤 Jeffrey M. Girard, Antony D'Avirro, Jason Z. Zheng, Jacqueline R. Vertino, Benjamin Peloquin 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15961]
Is Spoken Hungarian Low-resource?: A Quantitative Survey of Hungarian Speech Data Sets
匈牙利语口语是否属于低资源语言？一项关于匈牙利语语音数据集的定量调查
Even though various speech data sets are available in Hungarian, there is a lack of a general overview about their types and sizes. To fill in this gap, we provide a survey of available data sets in spoken Hungarian in five categories (e.g., monolingual, Hungarian part of multilingual, pathological, child-related and dialectal collections). In total, the estimated size of available data is about 2800 hours (across 7500 speakers) and it represents a rich spoken language diversity. However, the distribution of the data and its alignment to real-life (e.g. speech recognition) tasks is far from optimal indicating the need for additional larger-scale natural language speech data sets. Our survey presents an overview of available data sets for Hungarian explaining their strengths and weaknesses which is useful for researchers working on Hungarian across disciplines. In addition, our survey serves as a starting point towards a unified foundational speech model specific to Hungarian.
中文翻译
尽管已有多种匈牙利语语音数据集可用，但缺乏对其类型和规模的总体概述。为填补这一空白，我们对五类口语匈牙利语数据集（如单语、多语中匈牙利语部分、病理型、儿童相关及方言类）进行了全面调查。总计估算可用数据量约为2800小时（涵盖约7500名说话者），体现了丰富的口语语言多样性。然而，数据分布及其与真实应用场景（如语音识别）的匹配度远未理想，表明亟需更大规模的自然语言语音数据集。本调查为匈牙利语研究者提供了现有数据集的概览，分析其优缺点，对跨学科研究具有参考价值。此外，本调查也为构建专属于匈牙利语的统一基础语音模型提供了起点。
👤 Péter Mihajlik, Katalin Mády, Anna Kohári, Fruzsina Sára Vargha, Gábor Kiss et al. 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15804]
Computational Explanations of Semantic Universals
语义普遍性的计算解释
Despite their differences, the languages of the world share many deep features when it comes to how they express meaning. These semantic universals are likely to reflect shared aspects of human cognitive, communicative, and social structure. Recent advances in cross-linguistic semantics and computational linguistics allow us to develop explanations of the sources of such properties. This Element presents a body of work doing just that: we explicate a framework for providing computational explanations of semantic universals and then describe several such explanations using principles of ease of learning and efficient communication. Empirical domains include quantifiers, color terms, modals, and indefinites. After providing these explanations, we provide a discussion of future directions for advancing the explanatory power of computational methods in semantic typology.
中文翻译
尽管世界上的语言存在差异，但在表达意义方面却共享许多深层特征。这些语义普遍性可能反映了人类认知、交流和社会结构的共同方面。近年来跨语言语义学和计算语言学的进步使我们能够探索这些特性的来源。本文呈现了一项工作，旨在提供语义普遍性的计算解释框架，并利用学习简便性和高效沟通原则描述若干具体解释。实证领域包括量词、颜色术语、情态动词和不定词。在提供这些解释后，本文还讨论了未来推进计算方法在语义类型学中解释力的方向。
👤 Shane Steinert-Threlkeld, Jakub Szymanik 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15691]
Dynamic Block-Online Streaming ASR for Low-Resource Agglutinative Code-Switching Speech with Morphology-Aware Evaluation
面向低资源黏着语代码混用语音的动态块-在线流式ASR及其形态学感知评估
Low-resource, agglutinative languages challenge low-latency ASR, as fixed lookahead windows fail when grapheme order diverges, suffixes cross code-switch boundaries, or code-switching data is imbalanced. We present the first comprehensive study of streaming ASR for intra-sentential Bangla-English code-switching, identifying a mismatch between strict causal streaming and long-range morphological dependencies. We introduce a Dynamic Block-Online framework with VAD-aligned inference that balances semantics, latency, and context, enabling global bidirectional attention to preserve morphological integrity. To mitigate data scarcity, we propose Script-Anchored Loanword Injection, augmenting training text with English loanwords to model phonotactic transitions. We introduce CS-WER to diagnose switch, root, and suffix errors, and show that flexible latency budgets enhance recognition of agglutinative and mixed-language speech, with successful transfer to menstrual-medical domains.
中文翻译
低资源黏着语在低延迟自动语音识别中面临挑战，因为固定前瞻窗口在字符顺序偏离、词尾跨越代码切换边界或代码切换数据不平衡时会失效。我们首次系统研究了句内孟加拉语-英语代码混用语音的流式ASR，发现严格因果流与长程形态依赖之间存在不匹配。我们提出一种动态块-在线框架，结合语音活动检测对齐推理，在语义、延迟和上下文之间取得平衡，支持全局双向注意力以保持形态完整性。为缓解数据稀缺问题，我们提出脚本锚定借词注入方法，通过在训练文本中加入英语借词来建模音位过渡。我们引入CS-WER用于诊断切换、词根和词尾错误，并表明灵活的延迟预算能提升对黏着语和混合语言语音的识别效果，且成功迁移至月经-医疗领域。
👤 Kazi Rafat, Afifa Imran, Md. Ismail Hossain, Md. Romzan Ali, Fuad Rahman et al. 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15661]
ALMeD: Medieval French Gold-Standard
ALMeD：中世纪法语金标准语料库
Medieval French is one of the Romance vernacular languages with a significant history of transmission and an enormous influence on other vernacular languages of the time. The sources convey valuable knowledge about all aspects of culture. The study of Medieval French has, over the last few years, increasingly benefited from NLP-based methods. There have been numerous efforts to parse medieval French; however, these efforts use corpora comprising legal texts and -- primarily -- literary texts as their foundation and training data. This means that, except for juridical resources, scientific literature is overlooked. We present ALMeD, an annotated, semantically rich corpus (work-in-progress) of medical and surgical treatises produced in medieval French, i.e., Old and Middle French, including Anglo-Norman. Our semantically disambiguated gold standard goes beyond existing resources, focusing on medical terminology, and accounts for morphological development, evolving syntactical structures and the lack of spelling normalisation in these languages.
中文翻译
中世纪法语是罗曼语族中具有重要传播历史并深刻影响其他同时期方言的语言之一。其文献资料蕴含了关于文化各个方面的宝贵知识。近年来，中世纪法语的研究越来越多地受益于基于自然语言处理的方法。尽管已有大量针对中世纪法语的解析工作，但这些研究主要基于法律文本和文学文本构建语料库和训练数据，导致科学文献被忽视。我们提出了ALMeD，一个正在进行中的标注丰富、语义丰富的医学与外科 treatises 语料库，涵盖中世纪法语（即古法语和中古法语）以及英诺曼语。该语义消歧的金标准超越现有资源，聚焦于医学术语，并考虑了这些语言的形态演变、语法结构变化以及拼写未标准化的问题。
👤 Ragini Menon, Sabine Tittel 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15642]
Differential Backpack Language Models: Sense Inhibition via Signed Contextualization Weights
差异背包语言模型：通过带符号上下文权重实现意义抑制
Backpack language models predict with a strictly linear combination of non-contextual per-word sense vectors, weighted by non-negative contextualization scores. We show that non-negativity conflates two properties: the linear readout, which carries the interpretability, and the conic combination, which merely restricts it. We relax the latter by computing the sense weights with differential attention, a difference of two softmax maps with a learned per-sense gain, so context can actively inhibit a sense; we call the result a differential Backpack. At equal parameters on WikiText-103 this cuts the Backpack's perplexity gap to a Transformer by 31%, improves over the softmax Backpack at every sense count, persists with a shrinking margin up to 350M parameters on OpenWebText, and is localized at the sense layer by ablations and controls. The learned inhibition is directly interpretable (a financial context drives the river sense of bank to -0.50), and the preserved per-sense attribution predicts counterfactual removals exactly. We tested a number of combination rules, showing the linear interface is nearly free. Making the readout nonlinear gains only 0.43 perplexity points while invalidating two thirds of the decomposition. The differential Backpack's senses are also better representations, improving zero-shot sentence similarity by up to 33%.
中文翻译
背包语言模型通过非上下文感知的词义向量的严格线性组合进行预测，权重为非负的上下文化分数。我们指出，非负性混淆了两个属性：线性读出（具有可解释性）和锥组合（仅施加限制）。我们通过差分注意力放松后者——即使用两个softmax映射的差值并引入每个意义的可学习增益，使上下文能够主动抑制某一意义；由此产生的模型称为差分背包模型。在WikiText-103上参数相当的情况下，该模型将背包模型与Transformer之间的困惑度差距降低了31%，在所有词义数量下均优于原始softmax背包模型，并在OpenWebText上持续表现出优势，直至3.5亿参数规模，且通过消融实验和控制验证其作用集中在词义层。所学习的抑制机制具有直接可解释性（如金融语境使bank的river义项权重降至-0.50），且保留的词义归因能精确预测反事实移除效果。我们测试了多种组合规则，表明线性接口几乎无代价。使读出非线性仅带来0.43个困惑度点的提升，却破坏了三分之二的可分解性。此外，差分背包模型的意义表示也更优，零样本句子相似度最高提升达33%。
👤 Konstantinos Skianis, Giannis Nikolentzos, Polykarpos Meladianos, Michalis Vazirgiannis 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15597]
When Local Definitions Are Missing: Stable Errors in Language Models
当局部定义缺失时：语言模型中的稳定错误
Contracts, technical manuals, and online communities often give familiar words special meanings and define them in one place. A language model that reads a passage without that definition may use the usual meaning and give an answer that sounds right but is wrong. We study this with multiple-choice questions whose correct answers depend on a local definition. The questions come from a synthetic vocabulary, commercial contracts, Reddit community glossaries, and the documentation of a decentralized exchange. We ask three language models each question with and without the definition. Without using the answer key, we then test whether their repeated answers and stated confidence reveal their own errors. Separately, we give a small open model the answer key and test whether it can predict which questions larger models get wrong. We report three findings. (1) Familiar words that a source redefines cause the most errors. Models often repeat the same wrong answer, and neither repeated answers nor stated confidence reliably flag it. (2) The source definition fixes most errors on synthetic questions, but a definition drawn from the model's own prior knowledge does not. In real contracts, the source definition fixes only about half of the errors. (3) Given an answer key, a small model can rank questions by how likely larger models are to get them wrong. These results call for tests that check answers against the full source, including its local definitions.
中文翻译
合同、技术手册和在线社区经常为常见词汇赋予特殊含义并在某处进行定义。若语言模型在未读到该定义的文本中阅读，可能会使用常规含义，给出听起来正确但实际错误的答案。我们通过多选题研究这一现象，其正确答案依赖于局部定义。题目来源包括合成词汇、商业合同、Reddit社区术语表以及去中心化交易所的文档。我们让三个语言模型在有和无定义的情况下分别回答每个问题，并在不使用答案键的情况下测试它们重复答案和声明信心是否能揭示自身错误。此外，我们给一个小的开源模型提供答案键，测试其能否预测大模型在哪些问题上出错。我们报告了三个发现：(1) 源头重新定义的熟悉词汇导致最多错误。模型常重复同一错误答案，而重复答案和信心声明无法可靠识别错误。(2) 源定义可解决大部分合成问题的错误，但模型自身先验知识中的定义则无效。在真实合同中，源定义仅解决约一半错误。(3) 给定答案键后，小模型能够按大模型出错可能性对问题进行排序。这些结果呼吁设计更全面的测试，需将答案与完整来源（包括局部定义）进行比对。
👤 Zengqing Wu, Chuan Xiao 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15587]
VWEN: A Curated Small Language Model for Vietnamese Paraphrasing
VWEN：面向越南语改写任务的精选小型语言模型
We present VWEN, a 0.8B-parameter model for Vietnamese paraphrasing at a requested level of change and for five related tasks: generating several paraphrases, simplification, gist, register transfer and paraphrase judgement. VWEN is Qwen3.5-0.8B fine-tuned on about 7k samples chosen by a four-stage pipeline: rules and deduplication, a bidirectional entailment filter, a divergence score that assigns low and high paraphrase levels, and selection for coverage and instruction-following difficulty. We compare it with paraphrase-only recipes from the same pipeline and with training on the ViSP corpus under matched and larger step budgets. On high-divergence paraphrasing, VWEN reaches a success rate (requested level reached and meaning preserved) of 0.61 against 0.46 for the ViSP model at matched compute, a difference that holds on inputs resembling ViSP. Given four times more training examples, the ViSP model closes the gap on such inputs but not on questions, which ViSP lacks and which it often turns into statements. Across all comparisons the entailment filter is the most effective stage. Training on paraphrases alone erases abilities of the base model that the multi-task data preserves.
中文翻译
我们提出VWEN，一个0.8B参数的小型语言模型，用于越南语改写任务，支持不同层次的语义变化，并涵盖五个相关任务：生成多个改写句、简化、摘要、语体转换和改写判断。VWEN基于Qwen3.5-0.8B，在约7000个样本上进行微调，这些样本通过四阶段筛选流程选出：规则与去重、双向蕴含过滤器、用于分配低/高改写程度的分歧得分，以及覆盖度与指令遵循难度的选择。我们将其与同一流程生成的仅改写训练方案，以及在ViSP语料库上以匹配和更大训练预算进行训练的结果进行比较。在高分歧改写任务中，VWEN的成功率（达到目标层次且语义保留）为0.61，而ViSP模型在相同计算资源下为0.46，该差距在类似ViSP输入上依然存在。当训练样本增加四倍时，ViSP模型在类似输入上缩小了差距，但在问题类输入上仍表现不佳，因为ViSP缺乏问题形式，常将其转化为陈述句。所有对比中，蕴含过滤器是最有效的阶段。仅用改写数据训练会抹除基础模型的能力，而多任务数据能保留这些能力。

AraCrypto-ML: A Multi-Label Corpus of Communicative Functions in Arabic Facebook Cryptocurrency Discourse
AraCrypto-ML：阿拉伯语Facebook加密货币话语中的多标签语用功能语料库
Arabic cryptocurrency discourse on social media is communicatively heterogeneous: a single post may report prices, explain protocols, promote investment, warn of scams, or invoke Islamic jurisprudence. Yet no annotated resource captures this functional diversity. We introduce AraCrypto-ML, a 10,000-post multi-label corpus of Arabic Facebook cryptocurrency discourse (2014-2024) from 2,898 public pages using a set of related keywords. Collected posts are annotated into six categories: Market Commentary, Educational, Promotional, Skeptical-Warning, Religious Framing, and Other/Unclear, using Fanar-2-27B under a prompt-only multi-label protocol. We validate 1,000 posts against adjudicated gold labels (macro kappa = 0.74) and apply Rogan-Gladen calibration to correct labelling bias. The two annotators agree with each other at macro kappa = 0.62 before adjudication, below the model's agreement with either of them (0.70 and 0.69). Reaction profiles differ strongly by function: Love is about 70% of reactions per post on Educational and Promotional posts but about 33% on Skeptical-Warning, which draws the largest Haha share (about 39%).

[2611.15267]
A Closer Look at Language Model Introspection: Probing Information and Reporting
对语言模型内省的深入考察：探测信息与报告
One way to study language-model introspection is to test whether models can identify where their activations have been perturbed. We train a simple linear classifier to identify the perturbed sentence from the model's activations, then compare its predictions with the model's own answers across pretraining and injection strengths. This tests whether the model is being asked to report information that is already easy to recover. If so, successful localization may be less demanding than it first appears and may not extend to the broader uses that motivate introspection research, such as faithfully explaining a model's reasoning or helping researchers assess the reliability of its answers. With an injection four times the typical activation norm at layer 0 and probe readout at layer 24 in OLMo~3 32B, the supervised probe reaches 98.3\% accuracy after 8.4 billion training tokens, while the model's zero-shot answer remains at chance (11.1\%). At the released base checkpoint, these accuracies reach 99.1\% and 89.6\%, respectively. Across 7B and 32B, location is already decodable in randomly initialized networks, showing that the architecture can support these traces without pretraining. In these experiments, location information lies close enough to the surface for a simple linear probe to recover it, so successful reporting need not imply broader access to the model's internal computation.
中文翻译
研究语言模型内省的一种方法是测试模型能否识别其激活值被扰动的位置。我们训练了一个简单的线性分类器，从模型的激活中识别出被扰动的句子，并将其预测结果与模型自身的回答进行比较，涵盖预训练和注入强度的变化。这检验了模型是否被要求报告那些已易于恢复的信息。如果属实，则成功定位可能比最初看起来更简单，且未必能扩展到内省研究所驱动的更广泛用途，例如忠实解释模型推理过程或帮助研究人员评估答案的可靠性。在OLMo~3 32B模型中，第0层注入强度为典型激活范数四倍、第24层进行探测读出的情况下，监督探测器在84亿训练标记后达到98.3%准确率，而模型的零样本回答仍处于随机水平（11.1%）。在发布的基础检查点上，准确率分别达到99.1%和89.6%。在7B和32B模型中，位置信息在随机初始化网络中即可解码，表明架构本身就能支持这些痕迹而无需预训练。实验表明，位置信息足够接近表面，使得简单的线性探测器即可恢复，因此成功的报告并不一定意味着模型具备对其内部计算的广泛访问能力。
👤 Dustin Rubin, Vincenzo Timmel, Xufeng He, Aritra Saha, JooMan Kim et al. 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15172]
Where Language-Model Vocabulary Rose on Stack Exchange
语言模型词汇在Stack Exchange上的兴起
Stack Overflow banned answers generated by ChatGPT five days after the model's release, and many other Stack Exchange sites announced similar rules. I measure the vocabulary of the answers posted afterwards, 3.8 million answers on 170 sites from 2019 to 2024. A fixed list of 56 words that language models overuse rose about 14 percent above its pre-2023 trend, while the highest of 1,000 frequency-matched lists of ordinary words rose about 7 percent. In a screen of 15 site traits measured before the release, the strongest predictor of where the rise happened was the share of answers that received a body edit within 30 days of posting. On the least edited third of sites the list rose about 29 percent relative to matched controls, and on the most edited third it did not change. The gradient is also present among answers that were never edited. It follows the share of answers edited by their own authors, and the share edited by others adds little once that share is held fixed. Use of the model is unobserved, so these results cannot separate lower use on sites with a high self-edit share from any effect of editing on what the vocabulary records. Any vocabulary-based estimate for the network as a whole therefore pools sites where the marker vocabulary rose sharply with sites where it did not change.
中文翻译
Stack Overflow在模型发布五天后禁止使用ChatGPT生成的答案，许多其他Stack Exchange站点也宣布了类似规则。我测量了此后发布的答案词汇量，涵盖2019至2024年间170个站点的380万条答案。一个固定列表中的56个语言模型过度使用的词语，其使用频率相比2023年前的趋势上升约14%，而1000个频率匹配的普通词语最高上升约7%。在对15个网站特征的前期筛查中，最能预测词汇上升位置的因素是发布后30天内收到正文编辑的答案占比。在编辑最少的三分之一站点中，该列表上升约29%，而在编辑最多的三分之一站点中则无变化。这一趋势在从未被编辑的答案中同样存在，它与作者自编辑的比例相关，而他人编辑的贡献在控制作者自编辑后几乎不再显著。由于模型使用情况未被观测，这些结果无法区分高自编辑比例站点中模型使用较少的影响，或编辑行为对词汇记录的影响。因此，任何基于词汇的整体网络估计都会混合那些标记词汇急剧上升的站点与未发生变化的站点。

[2611.15130]
Read Once, Answer Many: Parallel Prompting for Shared-Context LLM Inference
读取一次，回答多次：面向共享上下文LLM推理的并行提示技术
LLM workloads sometimes ask many independent questions about the same long document, as in document QA, data labeling and LLM-based evaluation. Prefix caching removes the redundant prefill shared by these queries, but each decode step still reads the document's key-value (KV) cache once per question, so decoding is bound by memory bandwidth and scales with the number of questions. We present Parallel Prompting, which decodes P questions as P independent streams of a single request all at once, fixing this issue. In our method, every step reads the shared document once for all P streams, and a stream-id attention mask, evaluated inside the attention kernel, keeps each answer conditioned only on the document and its own question. We implement Parallel Prompting in SGLang by adding about 80 lines to three existing files and a small engine package; the mask needs no dense tensor, and every decode step has a fixed shape that is captured as a CUDA graph. With CodeLlama-7B, 8k-16k-token documents and 64-256 questions per document, Parallel Prompting is 3.7x faster than stock SGLang with its FlashInfer backend on an A100 and 5.9-6.0x faster on an RTX A6000, on two SGLang releases, including the most recent release. Timed on identical inputs, it is faster than stock SGLang in all 144 runs (2 GPUs x 2 releases x 6 workloads x 6 input sets). Code: https://github.com/jlab-nlp/parallel-prompting
中文翻译
大型语言模型工作负载有时需要针对同一长文档提出多个独立问题，如文档问答、数据标注和基于LLM的评估。前缀缓存可消除这些查询之间的冗余预填充，但每个解码步骤仍需为每个问题单独读取文档的键值（KV）缓存，导致解码受内存带宽限制，并随问题数量增加而线性扩展。我们提出并行提示（Parallel Prompting），将P个问题作为单个请求的P个独立流同时解码，解决了此问题。在我们的方法中，每一步仅读取一次共享文档，通过流ID注意力掩码（在注意力核内计算）确保每个答案仅依赖于文档及其自身问题。我们在SGLang中通过修改三个现有文件约80行代码及添加一个小引擎包实现该方法；掩码无需密集张量，且每个解码步骤具有固定形状，可作为CUDA图捕获。在CodeLlama-7B、8k-16k令牌文档和每文档64-256个问题的设置下，相较于使用FlashInfer后端的标准SGLang，在A100上提速3.7倍，在RTX A6000上提速5.9-6.0倍，涵盖两个SGLang版本，包括最新版本。在相同输入条件下，所有144次运行（2块GPU × 2版本 × 6工作负载 × 6输入集）均快于标准SGLang。代码开源：https://github.com/jlab-nlp/parallel-prompting

[2611.15114]
What Does Each Part of Speech Need? Contexts, Subspaces and Dimensions in Static Word Embeddings
每种词性需要什么？静态词嵌入中的上下文、子空间与维度
Static word embeddings give a word one vector, ignoring its different functions as noun, verb or adjective. Training on part-of-speech-tagged text gives each function its own vector, at the cost of a larger table. Using nine controlled corpora, we ask which context words each part of speech needs, whether it needs its own subspace, and whether the number of dimensions a word keeps should follow its part of speech or its frequency. The tags raise verb similarity on SimVerb-3500 at every dimension from 50 to 300 but help nouns much less. Added to verb-only text, adverbs help verbs more than as many random words; adjectives help nouns only slightly. At a fixed dimension, per-part-of-speech PCA bases preserve verb and adjective similarity better than a global basis or frequency-band bases. Nouns need more dimensions than verbs only among frequent words. We therefore allocate dimensions by frequency with these bases: at a third of the original size, only rare words lose dimensions, SimVerb-3500 and SimLex-999 nouns match the uncompressed table, and verb-to-noun retrieval stays within 0.02. Part of speech decides what a word's vector should be; frequency decides how much of it to keep.
中文翻译
静态词嵌入为每个词分配单一向量，忽略了其作为名词、动词或形容词的不同功能。通过对词性标注文本进行训练，可为每种功能分配独立向量，但代价是更大的参数表。利用九个受控语料库，我们探究了每种词性所需的关键上下文词、是否需要专属子空间，以及词向量保留的维度数应依据词性还是频率。词性标签在50至300维范围内均提升了SimVerb-3500上的动词相似度，但对名词帮助较小。在仅含动词的文本中加入副词，对动词的帮助超过同等数量的随机词；形容词对名词的帮助则微弱。在固定维度下，按词性进行主成分分析（PCA）的基础能更好地保持动词和形容词的相似度，优于全局基或按频率分段的基。仅在高频词中，名词需要比动词更多的维度。因此，我们采用基于频率的维度分配策略：在原始大小的三分之一时，仅稀有词损失维度，SimVerb-3500和SimLex-999的名词表现与未压缩表相当，动词到名词的检索性能保持在0.02以内。词性决定词向量应为何种形式，频率决定保留多少内容。
👤 Jintang Xue, Chengwei Wei, Yun-Cheng Wang, C.-C. Jay Kuo 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15058]
FIBER: Bayesian Fusion of Fast-DetectGPT and IRT-Informed Detector Evidence for LLM-Generated Text Detection
FIBER：基于IRT启发的贝叶斯融合快速DetectGPT与检测证据以识别LLM生成文本
Detecting large language model (LLM)-generated text remains challenging under domain and generator shifts. Supervised detectors may generalize poorly, while zero-shot methods often rely on individual statistical signals without leveraging historical detector behavior. We propose FIBER (Fast-DetectGPT with IRT-informed Bayesian Evidence Recalibration), which integrates continuous Fast-DetectGPT scores with auxiliary detector outputs through Bayesian evidence fusion. Item response theory (IRT) models historical detector-correctness patterns, providing class-conditional detector-output likelihoods through marginalization over latent text representations. These likelihoods are combined with Fast-DetectGPT score densities and a historical class prior for prediction. When historical Fast-DetectGPT scores are unavailable, a variant called FIBER-Stream estimates class-specific score distributions from an unlabeled batch. On a 32,000-text multi-domain benchmark, FIBER achieves the highest accuracy (0.620) and improves mean AUC over Fast-DetectGPT (0.664 versus 0.600) under five-fold cross-validation. FIBER also consistently improves AUC across four individual domains. Under out-of-distribution transfer, FIBER-Stream achieved an AUC of 0.911, compared with 0.889 for FIBER and 0.922 for Fast-DetectGPT alone. These results demonstrate the potential of integrating complementary detector evidence and historical information to improve detection performance across heterogeneous settings.
中文翻译
在领域和生成器变化下，检测大型语言模型（LLM）生成文本仍具挑战性。监督检测器泛化能力差，而零样本方法常依赖单一统计信号，未能利用历史检测器行为。我们提出FIBER（Fast-DetectGPT结合IRT启发的贝叶斯证据再校准），通过贝叶斯证据融合将连续的Fast-DetectGPT分数与辅助检测器输出相结合。项目反应理论（IRT）模型刻画历史检测器正确性模式，通过边际化潜在文本表示，提供类别条件的检测器输出似然。这些似然与Fast-DetectGPT得分密度及历史类别先验结合用于预测。当缺乏历史Fast-DetectGPT得分时，FIBER-Stream变体通过无标签批次估计类别特定得分分布。在一个包含32,000条文本的多领域基准测试中，FIBER达到最高准确率（0.620），在五折交叉验证下平均AUC优于Fast-DetectGPT（0.664 vs 0.600）。FIBER在四个独立领域中也持续提升AUC。在分布外迁移场景下，FIBER-Stream的AUC达0.911，高于FIBER的0.889和Fast-DetectGPT单独使用的0.922。这些结果表明，整合互补检测证据与历史信息，可在异构环境中有效提升检测性能。

[2611.15051]
A Time-Frequency-Condition Multimodal Fusion Foundation Model for Universal Rotating-Machinery Fault Diagnosis under Multi-Element Heterogeneous Scenarios
一种面向多元素异构场景下通用旋转机械故障诊断的时间-频率-条件多模态融合基础模型
Intelligent fault diagnosis models are typically developed for a single machine under a specific set of operating conditions, such that each new machine or condition requires retraining from scratch. This one-machine-one-model paradigm scales poorly. Although transfer learning and domain generalization can reduce the labeling burden, they primarily seek domain-invariant commonality, potentially discarding machine-specific characteristics that are critical for diagnosis. This paper proposes TFC-MMFD, a time-frequency-condition multimodal fusion foundation model for rotating-machinery fault diagnosis under multi-element heterogeneous scenarios. Statistical indicators, time-frequency images, vibration sequences, and textual machine and condition descriptions are tokenized into a unified language-like space, allowing inputs of different physical origin to be represented as a single sequence. A LLaMA backbone, together with cross-modal bidirectional attention and a Q-Former, then distills this sequence into fault-mode queries. Diagnosis is performed by matching these queries against natural-language fault-mode anchors rather than fixed class indices, while regression-adversarial condition disentanglement and condition-conditioned masked pre-training improve robustness to changing conditions. Across four progressive tasks on ten public bearing datasets spanning diverse bearing types, speeds, and loads, TFC-MMFD attains 99.40% accuracy and 99.16% macro-F1, maintains over 80% accuracy on most interpolated unseen conditions without adaptation, and improves from 20.26% to 95.63% accuracy on an unseen machine with only five labeled samples per class. It outperforms the strongest of eight domain-generalization baselines by 10.57 percentage points and compares favorably with a recent bearing foundation model while using less than half of its fine-tuning data.
中文翻译
智能故障诊断模型通常针对特定工况下的单一机器进行开发，导致每新增一台机器或工况都需要从头训练。这种‘一机一模型’的范式扩展性差。尽管迁移学习和领域泛化可减轻标注负担，但主要寻求领域不变的共性，可能忽略对诊断至关重要的机器特异性特征。本文提出TFC-MMFD，一种用于多元素异构场景下旋转机械故障诊断的时间-频率-条件多模态融合基础模型。统计指标、时频图像、振动序列及文本形式的机器与工况描述被统一编码为类语言空间，使不同物理来源的输入可表示为单一序列。基于LLaMA主干网络，结合跨模态双向注意力与Q-Former，将该序列提炼为故障模式查询。诊断通过将这些查询与自然语言故障模式锚点匹配而非固定类别索引实现；同时，回归对抗性条件解耦与条件条件化的掩码预训练提升了对工况变化的鲁棒性。在涵盖多种轴承类型、转速和负载的十个公开轴承数据集上，TFC-MMFD达到99.40%准确率和99.16%宏F1值，在大多数未见插值工况下无需适应仍保持超80%准确率，并在仅每个类别5个标注样本的情况下，将未见机器上的准确率从20.26%提升至95.63%。其性能优于八个领域泛化基线中的最强者10.57个百分点，且在使用不到一半微调数据的情况下，表现媲美近期轴承基础模型。

[2611.14935]
Do Large Language Models Show Racial and Sex Bias in Pain Management? A Counterfactual Audit of GPT, Claude, Gemini, Llama, DeepSeek and Qwen
大型语言模型在疼痛管理中是否存在种族与性别偏见？对GPT、Claude、Gemini、Llama、DeepSeek和Qwen的反事实审计
Racial and ethnic minority patients receive less pain treatment than non-Hispanic White patients, and large language models (LLMs) used as medical AI could reproduce that bias. Prior audits tested a few proprietary models, stated race explicitly and rarely measured the models' own response instability. In exploratory, non-preregistered analyses, Study 1 had four open-weight LLMs assess six acute-pain vignettes in every combination of four races and two sexes, with identity stated or cued only by a name (two names per group), comparing each identity with a White male reference on the same case and sampling seed. In Study 2, 24 configurations of 17 models, including OpenAI's GPT-4.1 mini and gpt-oss, Anthropic's Claude Haiku 4.5, Google's Gemini 2.5 Flash, Meta's Llama, DeepSeek and Qwen, with reasoning on and off and at two precisions, answered the same vignettes and the 50 clinician-written Q-Pain cases. In this single-prompt, JSON-constrained task, no difference survived correction in Study 1, none of 1,386 comparisons survived the false discovery rate in Study 2, and every pain-severity difference was equivalent to zero within one point on a 0-10 scale (within a quarter point for 237 of 238 Q-Pain comparisons). Opioid decisions were bounded less precisely (Study 1 could not exclude differences of about 20 percentage points), so they show no detected difference, not proof of none. The few differences surviving correction within a model favoured Black patients. Models differed far more from one another and across their own settings than between patients (the reference patient received the high opioid dose in 6% to 93% of responses), although serving software, provider and prompt format also differed. Fairness audits of clinical AI should use several names per group, pair comparisons on the seed, report the noise floor and test each deployed configuration.
中文翻译
少数族裔患者获得的疼痛治疗少于非西班牙裔白人患者，而用于医疗AI的大型语言模型（LLMs）可能复制这一偏见。以往审计仅测试少数专有模型，明确声明种族身份，且极少测量模型自身响应的不稳定性。在探索性、非预注册分析中，研究1让四个开源权重LLM评估六个急性疼痛案例，涵盖四种种族与两种性别组合，身份仅通过姓名暗示（每组两个名字），将每种身份与同一案例中的白人男性参考对象比较，并采样种子。研究2中，24种配置的17个模型（包括OpenAI的GPT-4.1 mini和gpt-oss，Anthropic的Claude Haiku 4.5，Google的Gemini 2.5 Flash，Meta的Llama、DeepSeek和Qwen）在开启/关闭推理与两种精度下回答相同案例及50个临床医生撰写的Q-Pain案例。在此单提示、JSON约束任务中，研究1无差异通过校正，研究2中1,386次比较无一通过错误发现率校正，所有疼痛严重度差异在0-10分量表上等效于零（237/238 Q-Pain比较中误差小于四分之一分）。阿片类药物决策的界定较宽松（研究1无法排除约20个百分点的差异），故未检测到差异，但不能证明无差异。少数通过校正的差异在模型内部偏向黑人患者。模型间差异远大于患者间差异（参考患者在6%至93%响应中获得高剂量阿片类药物），且受软件、提供方和提示格式影响。临床AI公平性审计应采用每组多个姓名、配对比较种子、报告噪声水平并测试每个部署配置。
👤 Richard J. Young, Alice M. Matthews 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.14908]
Hy ASR 3.0 Technical Report
Hy ASR 3.0 技术报告
We present Hy ASR 3.0, a large language model (LLM)-based automatic speech recognition (ASR) system that delivers strong recognition accuracy, supports flexible contextual conditioning, and maintains robust recognition performance across diverse speech conditions. Hy ASR 3.0 supports Mandarin Chinese, English and 13 Chinese dialect groups, including Cantonese. Among the evaluated systems, it achieves the lowest aggregate error rates on public Mandarin Chinese, English, and Cantonese benchmarks and the lowest error rates on the corresponding internal test sets, alongside first-tier dialect recognition performance. Beyond audio-only transcription, Hy ASR 3.0 supports multiple forms of external context, including hotword lists, question--answer dialogues, and summaries, enabling the use of task-relevant information during recognition. The model also leverages intra-utterance context to disambiguate homophonic and phonetically similar keywords using information from other parts of the same utterance, without requiring a separate contextual input. Evaluations demonstrate strong contextual recognition in both external-context and intra-utterance settings, as well as robust recognition of long-form speech, children's and older adults' speech, low-volume speech, and speech in noisy environments. This report describes the model architecture and training methodology and presents evaluations across recognition accuracy, context utilization, and robustness.
中文翻译
我们介绍了 Hy ASR 3.0，这是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的自动语音识别（ASR）系统，具备出色的识别准确率，支持灵活的上下文条件输入，并在多种语音条件下保持稳健的识别性能。Hy ASR 3.0 支持普通话、英语以及13种汉语方言（包括粤语）。在所评估的系统中，它在公开的普通话、英语和粤语基准测试上取得了最低的综合错误率，并在相应的内部测试集上也达到了最低错误率，同时在方言识别方面表现处于第一梯队。除了仅音频转录外，Hy ASR 3.0 还支持多种外部上下文形式，包括关键词列表、问答对话和摘要，从而在识别过程中利用任务相关的信息。该模型还利用句内上下文，通过同一话语中其他部分的信息来消除同音词和发音相似词的歧义，而无需额外的上下文输入。评估结果表明，该系统在外部上下文和句内上下文设置下均表现出强大的上下文识别能力，且对长篇语音、儿童及老年人语音、低音量语音以及嘈杂环境中的语音具有鲁棒性。本报告描述了模型架构与训练方法，并展示了在识别准确率、上下文利用和鲁棒性方面的评估结果。
👤 Aoxiong Yin, Auden Li, Chao Huang, Chuanyang Hu, Eitan Wang et al. 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.14828]
LLMs and the Homogenization of Teaching
大语言模型与教学的同质化
Large language models (LLMs) are known to lack diversity in open-ended text generation, a limitation that is especially consequential in education, where homogeneity can narrow the kinds of thinking and disciplinary work students are invited to do. We study diversity in two educational generation tasks: math items and essay feedback. We compare frontier and open-weight models with human-authored reference distributions using lexical, semantic, and domain-grounded measures. For math, we examine the mathematical activities students are asked to perform; for feedback, we examine the revision work requested and the rhetorical strategies used to communicate it. We evaluate direct prompting, two diversity prompting methods, and post-training. Across both tasks, directly prompted model outputs are markedly less diverse than human-authored content--78% higher on lexical (Self-BLEU) and 51% higher on semantic (embedding) similarity--and represent a narrower range of instructional forms. Diversity prompting and post-training increase variation largely through surface features without broadening the instructional repertoire. Models inherit and amplify the most normative routines of human instruction. Overcoming mode collapse in education will require richer instructional data and investment in teachers' creative capacity and professional judgment.
中文翻译
大语言模型在开放式文本生成中缺乏多样性，这一局限在教育领域尤为严重，因为同质化会限制学生被邀请参与的思维方式和学科工作类型。我们研究了两个教育生成任务中的多样性：数学题目生成和作文反馈生成。通过词汇、语义和领域相关指标，将前沿模型与开源权重模型与人工创作的参考分布进行对比。针对数学题，我们考察学生被要求执行的数学活动；针对反馈，我们分析所请求的修改工作及传达这些内容所采用的修辞策略。我们评估了直接提示、两种多样性提示方法以及后训练策略。在两项任务中，直接提示的模型输出明显比人工内容缺乏多样性——词汇层面（Self-BLEU）高出78%，语义层面（嵌入相似度）高出51%，且代表的教学形式范围更窄。多样性提示和后训练主要通过表层特征增加变化，但并未显著拓宽教学范式。模型继承并放大了人类教学中最规范化的常规做法。要克服教育领域的模式崩溃，需要更丰富的教学数据，以及对教师创造力和专业判断力的投资。
👤 Mei Tan, Hyunji Nam, Hari Subramonyam, Dorottya Demszky 📅 2026-11-10 🔗 arXiv
[2611.14823]
Discharge Summary Generation Is Not Just Summarization: A Reality Check for Evaluation
出院摘要生成不仅仅是摘要：对评估的一次现实检验
Discharge summaries combine a retrospective account of hospitalization with discharge-time decisions. Physician-written references therefore contain both facts supported by prior clinical notes and reference-only content, potentially reflecting clinical synthesis, handoffs, or discharge planning. Reference-only evaluation can conflate failure to recover available information with failure to reproduce content absent from the model input. We introduce FairSumEval, an evidence-aware, fact-level framework that assesses whether each reference and generated fact is supported by the model's evidence and captured by the other summary. The resulting outcome map separates matches, omissions, and additions. Two complementary metrics, Soft IoU-S and Soft IoU-NS, measure reference-generation agreement for facts with and without identified evidence support. On 200 MIMIC-III admissions, 18.8% of physician reference facts have identified support in the prior evidence, compared with 65.4% and 63.5% of facts generated by a general-purpose and a medically specialized model. Overlap is substantially higher for supported content (0.255 and 0.236) than for content without identified support (0.030 and 0.026). The two scores are weakly correlated across admissions (rho = 0.22 and 0.17), and conventional metrics do not consistently capture both dimensions. FairSumEval distinguishes evidence-grounded summarization from agreement on content without identified support, revealing what models omit and add in each category.
中文翻译
出院摘要结合了住院经历的回顾性描述与出院时的决策内容。因此，医生撰写的参考文本既包含由先前临床记录支持的事实，也包含仅具参考意义的内容，可能反映临床综合、交接班或出院计划。仅基于参考内容的评估可能混淆未能恢复可用信息与未能复现模型输入中不存在的内容之间的区别。我们提出了 FairSumEval，一种基于证据、事实级别的评估框架，用于判断每个参考事实和生成事实是否得到模型证据的支持，并被另一份摘要所涵盖。由此产生的结果图谱可分离出匹配、遗漏和新增内容。两个互补指标 Soft IoU-S 和 Soft IoU-NS 分别衡量有证据支持和无证据支持的事实上的参考与生成内容的一致性。在200例 MIMIC-III 入院病例中，18.8% 的医生参考事实在先前证据中找到了支持，而通用模型和医学专用模型生成的事实中分别有65.4%和63.5%存在支持。对于有支持的内容，重叠度显著更高（0.255 和 0.236），而对于无支持内容则极低（0.030 和 0.026）。这两个得分在病例间相关性较弱（rho = 0.22 和 0.17），传统指标无法一致捕捉这两个维度。FairSumEval 区分了基于证据的摘要与无明确证据支持的内容一致性，揭示了模型在各类别中遗漏和添加的内容。

What Do Tokenizer Comparisons Measure? Disentangling Granularity, Root Assignment, and Word-Level Readout across Amharic and Hausa
分词器比较究竟测量了什么？解耦阿姆哈拉语和豪萨语中的粒度、根分配与词级读取
Morphology-aware tokenizers are expected to help morphologically rich languages, and their reported gains over byte-pair encoding (BPE) are usually credited to their linguistic units. A comparison with BPE cannot support this attribution, since such a tokenizer also segments text more finely (granularity) and, on token-level tasks, changes how much the sub-token readout matters. We separate both factors from the effect of grouping words by their roots (root assignment) in Amharic, a Semitic language whose Ge'ez abugida hides consonantal roots inside syllabic glyphs, with Latin-script Hausa as an orthographic contrast. Our design combines a lossless root-and-pattern tokenizer with controls that change one factor at a time, in two independent implementations. In both, real root assignment improves Amharic named-entity recognition over scrambled roots (+4.9 and +4.7 F1), and root-frequency structure explains little of this gain. In small from-scratch models, the readout choice moves F1 by up to 13.4 points and reverses the sign of every comparison of the root-and-pattern arms with BPE, whereas in two pretrained encoders it moves F1 by less than one point. The granularity effect changes sign across languages and implementations. We recommend decomposing tokenizer effects, and reporting the readout, before a gain is attributed to linguistic structure.
中文翻译
具有形态意识的分词器预期能帮助形态丰富的语言，其相对于字节对编码（BPE）的性能提升通常归因于其语言学单位。然而，与 BPE 的比较无法支持这一归因，因为此类分词器还具有更细粒度的文本分割（粒度），并且在词级任务中改变了子词读取的重要性。我们通过阿姆哈拉语（一种塞姆语，其吉兹字母表隐藏了辅音根于音节符号中）与拉丁字母书写的豪萨语作为正交对照，在阿姆哈拉语中分离出根分配（root assignment）的影响，同时控制其他因素。我们的设计结合了无损的根与模式分词器，并在两个独立实现中逐项控制单一变量。在两种实现中，真实的根分配相较于随机打乱的根，显著提升了阿姆哈拉语命名实体识别性能（F1 提升 +4.9 和 +4.7），而根频率结构对此提升贡献甚微。在小型自建模型中，读取方式的选择可使 F1 变化高达13.4分，甚至逆转所有根与模式分支与 BPE 比较的结果；而在两个预训练编码器中，读取选择的影响小于1分。粒度效应在不同语言和实现中甚至出现符号反转。我们建议在将性能提升归因于语言结构前，应先分解分词器效应并报告读取方式。

[2611.17035]
Prevalence Bounds for Edited Watermarked Text: Power Envelopes and Calibration Sensitivity
编辑水印文本的普遍性界限：功率包络与校准敏感性
Watermark detectors decide whether one document came from a watermarked language model. Auditors often need a different number: the share of a corpus that did. We study this share for Gumbel-max and green-list (KGW) watermarks when every document may have been edited by an unknown amount. Without restrictions on the edits, the upper end of the identified set is 1. Combining a known least-favourable next-token distribution with a sequential coupling, we derive a power envelope indexed by three budgets: a retention floor, an entropy budget, and a budget for in-place substitutions, which for Gumbel-max make edited positions look less watermarked than human text. The envelope gives an identified set that is sharp for the alternatives it dominates, finite-sample bounds on the realized share of a fixed corpus with either the exact null or a null calibrated on an independent human sample, and confidence sequences for streams. In Gumbel-max simulations where the envelope binds, the bounds cover, and the threshold that maximizes the gap between envelope and null is generally as tight as twelve thresholds. On text from OPT-1.3B and Qwen3-1.7B, 12,000 fresh human documents scored under three keys show no robust departure from the exact null, and all bounds covered in resampling trials from finite pools of documents with random edits, substitutions and paraphrases; at a share of 0.2 the exact-null bound is about [0.14,0.38]. These results are fragile in two ways we make precise. LLM-paraphrased KGW pools fall below the envelope, yet at 2000 documents the bounds still covered in these trials. A simulated null error of 0.01, comparable to the largest deviation we observed at our thresholds, is likewise harmless at 2000 documents but breaks exact-null coverage at 100,000; a null calibrated on an independent human sample keeps coverage there, but needs a large reference sample to stay narrow.
中文翻译
水印检测器判断某文档是否来自带有水印的语言模型。审计人员通常需要的是：语料库中有多大比例的文档来自水印模型。我们研究了在每篇文档可能被未知程度编辑的情况下，Gumbel-max和绿色列表（KGW）水印的这一比例。在无编辑限制时，识别集合的上限为1。结合已知的最不利下一个词分布与序列耦合，我们推导出一个由三个预算参数索引的功率包络：保留下限、熵预算和原位替换预算，该包络使Gumbel-max中编辑过的部分看起来比人类文本更少水印特征。该包络给出了对所主导的替代方案具有紧致性的识别集合，对固定语料库实际比例的有限样本边界（基于精确零假设或基于独立人类样本校准的零假设），以及对数据流的置信序列表。在Gumbel-max模拟中，当包络起作用时，边界覆盖良好；最大化包络与零假设之间差距的阈值通常与十二个阈值相当。在OPT-1.3B和Qwen3-1.7B生成的文本上，使用三个密钥对12,000条新的人类文档评分，未发现显著偏离精确零假设的情况；在包含随机编辑、替换和改写的小规模文档池重采样试验中，所有边界均保持覆盖；在0.2的比例下，精确零假设的边界约为[0.14, 0.38]。这些结果在两种方式上是脆弱的，我们进行了精确分析。LLM改写后的KGW语料库低于包络线，但在2000条文档时边界仍能覆盖；模拟零假设误差为0.01（与我们在阈值观察到的最大偏差相当）在2000条文档时仍无害，但在10万条文档时会破坏精确零假设的覆盖性；而基于独立人类样本校准的零假设在此情况下仍能保持覆盖，但需要大量参考样本以维持边界狭窄。
👤 Kuancheng Ye 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16890]
Look It Up: Multi-bit LLM Watermarking via Codebook Search
查找它：通过码本搜索实现多比特大语言模型水印
Attributing short LLM-generated texts to a specific source remains challenging for existing multi-bit watermarking methods, which often require long generations or costly decoding to recover payloads reliably. We propose a codebook-based watermarking scheme that maps an $n$-bit payload to a longer $u$-bit codeword and embeds it during generation through token-level logit biasing. During extraction, the received bit sequence is matched to the nearest codeword in a precomputed secret codebook using Hamming or Levenshtein distance. Experiments show that our method achieves near-perfect recovery for 16-24 bit payloads on 200-token generations and strong performance on 127-token generations for base models. Results are weaker on some instruction-tuned models with very short texts, where low-entropy output distributions reduce embedding reliability. We also study a BCH-based variant that avoids codebook storage but generally reduces robustness. The Levenshtein variant improves resilience to deletions and light paraphrasing, while insertions and stronger rewrites remain challenging. We analyze collision probability, codeword separation, extraction time, tokenization consistency, and text quality, highlighting both the practicality and limitations of codebook-based watermarking for short-form attribution.
中文翻译
现有多种比特水印方法在将短文本归因于特定来源方面仍具挑战性，通常需要长生成序列或高成本解码才能可靠恢复载荷。我们提出一种基于码本的水印方案，将n比特载荷映射为更长的u比特码字，并通过逐标记logit偏置在生成过程中嵌入。提取阶段，接收的比特序列通过汉明距离或莱文施泰因距离与预计算的秘密码本中的最近码字匹配。实验表明，我们的方法在200个标记的生成中对16-24比特载荷实现了近乎完美的恢复，在基础模型上对127个标记的生成也表现出强劲性能。但在某些指令微调模型的极短文本上表现较弱，因为低熵输出分布降低了嵌入可靠性。我们还研究了一种基于BCH的变体，避免了码本存储但通常降低了鲁棒性。莱文施泰因变体增强了对删除和轻度改写的抗性，但插入和强重写仍具挑战。我们分析了碰撞概率、码字分离度、提取时间、分词一致性及文本质量，突出了基于码本水印在短文本溯源中的实用性和局限性。
👤 Jarosław Janas, Paweł Morawiecki, Josef Pieprzyk 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.16323]
Does Deep Knowledge Tracing Learn Semantic Relations Between Knowledge Components?
深度知识追踪是否学习了知识组件之间的语义关系？
Deep Knowledge Tracing (DKT) is a knowledge tracing (KT) model that updates a shared latent state from sequences of interactions, each pairing a knowledge component (KC) with a response. An interaction with one KC can therefore affect predictions for other KCs. We ask: Are the KC relations learned by DKT semantic? We construct text-derived semantic KC representations using nine pipelines to test whether the conclusions hold across alternative ways of constructing those representations. The first test compares DKT-derived KC relation graphs with semantic relation structures induced by pairwise cosine similarities among the KC representations. Across the evaluated datasets, embedding models, and pipelines, the two structures align only weakly and inconsistently. The second test introduces semantic information into DKT; the pretrained text-derived representations do not consistently outperform the standard DKT baseline. In the reduced-data experiments, semantic initialization yields a higher test area under the ROC curve (AUC) than the baseline at eight of nine nested sample sizes for ASSISTments 2017. KC-supervised fine-tuned BERT produces small positive AUC differences in some matched comparisons, but the study does not establish their stability across runs. These effects remain limited and inconsistent. The findings suggest that behavioral, temporal, structural, or psychometric signals may also shape DKT's learned relations. DKT-derived relations are not primarily semantic: the tested text-derived semantic structures do not strongly recover the DKT-derived relation structure.
中文翻译
深度知识追踪（DKT）是一种知识追踪（KT）模型，通过序列化的交互更新共享的潜在状态，每次交互将一个知识组件（KC）与一个回答配对。因此，对一个KC的交互可能影响其他KC的预测。我们提出问题：DKT所学习的KC关系是否具有语义性？我们使用九种不同的文本衍生语义KC表示方法构建测试用例，以检验这些结论在不同表示构造方式下是否成立。第一个测试将DKT生成的KC关系图与基于KC表示之间成对余弦相似性的语义关系结构进行比较。在评估的数据集、嵌入模型和管道中，两者仅表现出微弱且不一致的对齐。第二个测试将语义信息引入DKT；预训练的文本衍生表示并未持续优于标准DKT基线。在数据量减少的实验中，语义初始化在ASSISTments 2017的九个嵌套样本大小中的八个上取得了比基线更高的ROC曲线下面积（AUC）。KC监督微调的BERT在某些匹配比较中产生微小的正向AUC差异，但研究未证明其跨运行的稳定性。这些效应仍然有限且不一致。研究结果表明，行为、时间、结构或心理测量信号也可能塑造DKT所学习的关系。DKT生成的关系并非主要为语义性：所测试的文本衍生语义结构未能有效恢复DKT生成的关系结构。
👤 Kia Karbasi, Mahdi Saiedi, Kevin Hong, Gregory Pottie 📅 2026-11-12 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15809]
Fine-Grained Generative Hallucination Detection and Correction for Aerial Image Captioning
面向航拍图像描述的细粒度生成幻觉检测与修正
Aerial imagery captured by unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) plays a vital role in applications such as surveillance, disaster response, and large-scale environmental monitoring. Automatic generation of textual descriptions for such imagery is essential for reducing manual inspection overhead and enabling better downstream decision-making. However, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), despite their impressive captioning capabilities, are prone to hallucination, producing descriptions that are inconsistent with the visual input. This problem is significantly amplified in aerial imagery due to its unique challenges such as extreme viewpoint variation, small objects, class imbalance, and ambiguous visual entities. Existing hallucination detection approaches either rely on computationally expensive large external models such as GPT-4, which perform poorly on aerial domains, or treat the problem as a rigid binary classification task, ignoring the inherent uncertainty in ambiguous cases. To address these limitations, we propose a lightweight, reference-free framework for hallucination detection and correction in free-form aerial image captioning. Our method leverages state-of-the-art MLLMs, specifically InternVL3 and Qwen2.5-VL, to detect hallucinated content in generated captions with minimal computational overhead. Detected hallucinations are corrected through a dedicated refinement module that improves the factual consistency of the original captions. Extensive experiments on the Labelled Illusion Dataset (LID) demonstrate that our framework significantly reduces hallucination rates while remaining practical for real-time deployment in resource-constrained environments. Code is available at: https://github.com/Debolena7/Hal_Detect.
中文翻译
由无人飞行器（UAV）捕获的航拍影像在监控、灾害响应和大规模环境监测等应用中起着关键作用。自动生成此类影像的文本描述对于降低人工检查负担并支持下游决策至关重要。然而，尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLM）具备出色的描述能力，却容易产生幻觉，生成与视觉输入不一致的描述。这一问题在航拍影像中尤为严重，因其面临极端视角变化、小物体、类别不平衡和视觉实体模糊等独特挑战。现有幻觉检测方法要么依赖计算成本高昂的大型外部模型（如GPT-4），在航拍领域表现不佳；要么将问题视为严格的二分类任务，忽略了模糊情况中的固有不确定性。为解决这些局限，我们提出一种轻量级、无需参考的航拍图像自由描述幻觉检测与修正框架。我们的方法利用最先进的MLLM（如InternVL3和Qwen2.5-VL），以极低计算开销检测生成描述中的幻觉内容。检测到的幻觉通过专用精炼模块进行修正，提升原始描述的事实一致性。在Labelled Illusion Dataset（LID）上的大量实验表明，该框架显著降低了幻觉率，同时在资源受限环境中仍具备实时部署的可行性。代码已公开：https://github.com/Debolena7/Hal_Detect。
👤 Debolena Basak, Somsubhra De, Maunendra Sankar Desarkar 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15710]
Curiosity-0826-4B: Generative Medical Event Sequence Modeling Across Pediatric and Adult Populations
Curiosity-0826-4B：跨儿科与成人人群的生成式医疗事件序列建模
We introduce Curiosity-0826-4B, an updated version of the generative medical event foundation model trained on Epic Cosmos that simulates patient timelines to produce zero-shot clinical predictions. Curiosity-0826-4B incorporates a broader patient population and richer clinical event representations, while generally matching or exceeding Curiosity-0226-1B, the largest model in the previous Curiosity-0226 series, on evaluated tasks supported by both models. Laboratory value prediction improved, with MAE 18--46% lower than that of Curiosity-0226-1B. We evaluated prediction of disease events across pediatric cohorts aged 2--17 years. AUROCs for new asthma diagnosis (0.742--0.785) and asthma exacerbation (0.829--0.852) were comparable to those in the adult reference cohort, while AUROCs for sickle cell crisis (0.820--0.847) were lower than in adults (0.915). Explicit representation of documented death enabled new mortality prediction tasks among adults across hospice, inpatient, and ambulatory settings (AUROC 0.821--0.942). Scaling-law analysis guided compute-optimal initial pretraining followed by continued pretraining with context extension. The resulting Curiosity-0826-4B model has more parameters (4.3B versus 1B) and a longer context window (16K versus 8K). It processes 56% fewer training tokens and requires 2.2 times the training floating point operations (FLOPs) compared with its predecessor. By extending a common zero-shot prediction strategy to pediatric patients and additional outcomes, Curiosity-0826-4B broadens opportunities to develop clinical and operational applications without training separate task-specific models. Further validation should assess local calibration, performance across patient subgroups and event frequencies, timeline completion rates, and generalization to new settings, alongside prospective evaluation of clinical and operational utility.
中文翻译
我们介绍了Curiosity-0826-4B，这是基于Epic Cosmos数据集训练的生成式医疗事件基础模型的更新版本，能够模拟患者时间线以实现零样本临床预测。Curiosity-0826-4B纳入了更广泛的患者群体和更丰富的临床事件表示，整体表现与前代最大模型Curiosity-0226-1B相当或更优。实验室值预测性能提升，MAE较Curiosity-0226-1B降低18%-46%。我们在2至17岁儿科队列中评估了疾病事件预测，新哮喘诊断（AUROC 0.742–0.785）和哮喘急性加重（AUROC 0.829–0.852）的表现与成人参考队列相当，而镰状细胞危象（AUROC 0.820–0.847）的表现低于成人（0.915）。明确表示记录死亡使得在临终关怀、住院及门诊环境中实现了新的死亡率预测任务（AUROC 0.821–0.942）。通过尺度定律分析指导计算最优的初始预训练，并结合上下文扩展进行持续预训练。结果模型参数量增至43亿（对比10亿），上下文窗口延长至16K（对比8K）。其训练token减少56%，但所需浮点运算量（FLOPs）为前代的2.2倍。通过将通用零样本预测策略扩展至儿科患者和更多结局，Curiosity-0826-4B拓宽了无需训练专用模型即可开发临床与运营应用的机会。进一步验证应评估本地校准性、不同患者亚组与事件频率下的表现、时间线完成率以及在新环境中的泛化能力，同时需前瞻性评估其临床与运营实用性。
👤 Mani Nagarathnam, Luke Benham, Paul Blazek, John Cashy, Kevin Chung et al. 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15328]
Vision-Grounded Semantic Learning and Alignment for Scalable Multilingual Text-Based Person Retrieval
面向可扩展多语言文本驱动人员检索的视觉基础语义学习与对齐
Multilingual Text-Based Person Retrieval (TBPR) aims to retrieve individuals with texts in multiple languages. Despite its practical importance, it has been overlooked by existing research, which mainly focuses on English-only retrieval. The only prior attempt at multilingual TBPR suffers from (1) the absence of a large-scale, multilingual pretraining dataset and (2) a suboptimal model architecture for multilingual text encoding and cross-modal alignment, which employs a shared encoder that overlooks linguistic heterogeneity and relies on English as a pivot for cross-modal alignment, introducing English-centric bias. For this, we contribute MultiPedes, the first large-scale, pedestrian-centric multilingual pretraining dataset. It contains 10 million image-text pairs across four languages, constructed by a multilingual quality-aware data construction pipeline. We also propose ViSeLA (Vision-grounded Semantic Learning and Alignment), a novel framework featuring: (1) a semantic-aware multilingual learning module that extends the mixture-of-experts architecture by routing language-invariant semantics to specialized experts for improved multilingual feature learning; and (2) a vision-grounded cross-modal alignment module that employs a semantic probe to capture fine-grained semantics across modalities, enabling direct cross-modal alignment without relying on a pivot language. Extensive experiments on both multilingual and English-only TBPR datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of ViSeLA. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/Flame-Chasers/ViSeLA
中文翻译
多语言文本驱动人员检索（TBPR）旨在通过多种语言的文本检索特定个体。尽管其具有实际重要性，但现有研究主要集中在仅英语的检索上，忽视了多语言TBPR。此前唯一尝试存在两个问题：(1) 缺乏大规模多语言预训练数据集；(2) 模型架构在多语言文本编码和跨模态对齐方面表现不佳，采用共享编码器忽略了语言异质性，并依赖英语作为跨模态对齐的枢纽，引入了以英语为中心的偏差。为此，我们提出了MultiPedes，首个大规模、以行人为核心的多语言预训练数据集，包含四种语言共一千万张图像-文本对，由一个多语言质量感知的数据构建流程生成。我们还提出了ViSeLA（视觉基础语义学习与对齐），一种新框架，包含：(1) 语义感知的多语言学习模块，通过路由语言无关语义至专用专家，扩展了专家混合架构，提升多语言特征学习能力；(2) 视觉基础的跨模态对齐模块，采用语义探测器捕捉跨模态的细粒度语义，实现无需依赖枢纽语言的直接跨模态对齐。在多语言及仅英语TBPR数据集上的大量实验验证了ViSeLA的有效性。数据集与代码已公开于 https://github.com/Flame-Chasers/ViSeLA
👤 Xinyu Zhou, Fan Li, Shasha Chen, Min Cao, Ahtamjan Ahmat et al. 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15234]
Schedule-Calibrated Commitment Order in Blockwise Masked Diffusion Language Models
块级掩码扩散语言模型中的时序校准承诺顺序
Sequential block schedules can make token commitment in masked diffusion language models appear strongly left-to-right even when within-block order is random or reversed. For equal contiguous blocks processed without interleaving in positional order, with untied irreversible commitments, we use a Spearman-rank identity to express global position-commitment correlation as a deterministic schedule reference plus a weighted mean within-block correlation. In a reported paired study of LLaDA-8B-Instruct on 500 math and code prompts with 256 generated positions and updates, increasing block size from 64 to 128 lowers mean global correlation from 0.9845 to 0.9149. Uniform-random within-block references are already 0.9375 and 0.7500. After calibration, mean within-block correlations are 0.751 and 0.660, so the larger raw residual at block size 128 does not indicate stronger local positional ordering. We give sharp bounds and derive calibrated intervals from reported summaries, while documenting the limitations of the aggregate-only empirical release. The analysis supports schedule-aware interpretation of commitment order; its empirical scope is one checkpoint and two schedules, without evaluation of output quality or inference speed.
中文翻译
序列化块调度会使掩码扩散语言模型中的标记承诺表现出强烈的从左到右特性，即使块内顺序是随机或逆序的。对于未交错处理的等长连续块，在位置顺序中无绑定不可逆承诺的情况下，我们使用Spearman等级恒等式将全局位置-承诺相关性表示为确定性调度参考加上块内相关性的加权均值。在对LLaDA-8B-Instruct模型在500个数学与代码提示（每条生成256个位置与更新）进行的配对研究中，块大小从64增加到128时，平均全局相关性从0.9845降至0.9149。块内均匀随机参考的相关性分别为0.9375和0.7500。校准后，块内平均相关性为0.751和0.660，因此块大小为128时更大的原始残差并不表明更强的局部位置排序。我们给出了严格的界限并推导出校准区间，同时记录了仅聚合经验发布存在的局限性。分析支持对承诺顺序进行时序感知解释；其经验范围仅限于一个检查点和两种调度，未评估输出质量或推理速度。
👤 Gaurav Sarkar, Umang Pandey, Pratik Prabhanjan Brahma, Zicheng Liu, Emad Barsoum 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15194]
The Neighbour's Label: Opposite Subgroup Effects of Same-Dialogue Exposure in Next-Act Evaluation
邻居的标签：同一对话暴露对下一行为评估中相反子组效应的影响
Window-based next-act evaluation often aims to measure generalisation across dialogues, yet overlapping windows and similarity retrieval can expose labelled examples from the test dialogue. The nearest such example is the target's labelled predecessor, whose act is correct when the target continues it and wrong when the target changes act; a model that copies it can gain and lose in two subgroups that one aggregate averages. We induce this exposure under controlled designs on SGD, STAR and AMI, whose official splits are clean. Randomly including the predecessor's row in training raises a lexical classifier's continuation scores and lowers its transition scores in all three corpora, and purging the adjacent rows removes most of the gain. With TF-IDF-retrieved demonstrations, two 7B instruction-tuned models show the same opposite pattern in all eight model-corpus-sample arms (some single-cell intervals include zero; one replication is partial), while natural-weight accuracy changes by only -0.05 to +0.04. Exposure frequency depends on the retrieval pipeline. We evaluate two diagnostic comparisons without model retraining and find that excluding the test dialogue does not detectably reduce accuracy on SGD. All exposure is induced; no published system was re-evaluated.
中文翻译
基于窗口的下一行为评估通常旨在衡量跨对话的泛化能力，但重叠窗口和相似性检索可能导致测试对话中的标注示例被暴露。最近的此类示例是目标的标注前驱，当目标延续该行为时正确，改变行为时错误；一个复制前驱的行为模型在两个子组中可能获得收益和损失，而整体平均值会掩盖这种差异。我们在SGD、STAR和AMI数据集上通过受控设计诱导了这种暴露，这些数据集的官方划分是干净的。在训练中随机包含前驱行会提高所有三个语料库中词汇分类器的延续得分，降低其转换得分，而清除相邻行则消除了大部分增益。使用TF-IDF检索的示范，两个7B指令微调模型在全部八个模型-语料库-样本组合中表现出相同的相反模式（部分单细胞区间包含零；一次复现不完整），而自然权重准确率仅变化-0.05至+0.04。暴露频率取决于检索流程。我们进行了两次无需重新训练模型的诊断比较，发现排除测试对话并未显著降低SGD上的准确率。所有暴露均为人为诱导；无已发表系统被重新评估。
👤 Ali Amini, Masoumeh Chapariniya, Rytis Maskeliunas, Robertas Damaševičius 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15108]
Typed Knowledge over Constructive Institutions: A Proof-Relevant Type Theory for Verified Science
构造性制度中的类型化知识：用于验证科学的证明相关类型论
Mathematical proof assistants such as Coq, Agda, and Lean 4 build proof irrelevance into definitional conversion ($p \equiv q$ for $p, q : P$ with $P : Prop$), erasing which derivation established a theorem. While natural for mathematics over fixed axioms, judgmental proof irrelevance is incompatible with verified scientific stores, which must inspect and compare independent derivations of the same claim to assess consilience and compute counterfactual retractions when an assay, reagent, or model is invalidated. Moreover, combining definitional proof irrelevance with subsingleton elimination breaks strong normalization and renders algorithmic conversion non-transitive. We present the type-theoretic and institutional foundations of Eigenius, a verified knowledge substrate for science. First, we formulate a dependent kernel with cumulative universes ($Prop, Set, Type_k$), impredicative $Prop$, inductive families with index-forced subsingleton elimination, and intensional identity types, where definitional equality $\equiv$ is untyped joinability under an orthogonal CRS and proof irrelevance is a witnessed propositional axiom ($irrel : ΠP{:}Prop.\,Πp,q{:}P.\,Id\,P\,p\,q$). Second, we unify universe cumulativity, codomain-covariant $Π$-subtyping, and ontology class inclusions in a coercion-free subsumption judgment whose bidirectional fragments ($\mathcal{K}_0, \mathcal{K}_1$) satisfy subject reduction and are sound and complete for declarative typing. Third, we organize external verifiers into a three-level institutional hierarchy with explicit axiom policies (Adopt, Discharge, Relativize), internalize a justification logic with Why-provenance semiring support $supp(j,P)$, and embed Cooper's Type Theory with Records (TTR) to type open-world knowledge graphs. Finally, we prove consistency in Grothendieck universes and mechanize the metatheory in Lean 4 with zero sorry axioms.
中文翻译
数学证明助手如Coq、Agda和Lean 4将证明无关性嵌入定义转换中（对于P: Prop，有p ≡ q，其中p, q : P），从而擦除建立定理的推导过程。这在固定公理体系下的数学中是自然的，但判断性证明无关性与验证科学存储不兼容，后者必须检查并比较同一主张的独立推导以评估一致性，并在检测到实验、试剂或模型失效时计算反事实撤回。此外，将定义性证明无关性与亚单体消除结合会破坏强正规化性，并使算法转换失去传递性。我们提出Eigenius的类型论与制度基础，这是一个用于科学的验证知识底座。首先，我们构建了一个带有累积类型层级（Prop, Set, Type_k）、非命题式Prop、索引强制亚单体消除的归纳族以及外延等价类型的依赖核，其中定义等价≡是在正交CRS下的无类型可合并性，而证明无关性是一个有见证的命题公理（irrel : ΠP{:}Prop. Πp,q{:}P. Id P p q）。其次，我们将类型层级累积、余域协变Π-子类型化与本体类包含统一在一个无强制的包含判断中，其双向片段（K₀, K₁）满足主题还原性，且对声明式类型系统是可靠且完备的。第三，我们将外部验证者组织成三级制度层次，包含显式的公理策略（采纳、释放、相对化），内化一个理由逻辑并支持Why-溯源半环supp(j,P)，并嵌入Cooper的带记录类型论（TTR）以对开放世界知识图谱进行类型化。最后，我们在格罗滕迪克宇宙中证明了一致性，并在Lean 4中以零sorry公理机制化元理论。
👤 Hans-Martin Will 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.15010]
Calibrate, Don't Retrain: Decomposing the Value of Annotator Disagreement in Cyberbullying Severity
校准而非重训练：分解标注者分歧在网络欺凌严重性中的价值
Machine learning models are often trained on labeled data produced by multiple human annotators. When those annotators disagree, their labels are commonly collapsed into a single majority vote, which discards information about the underlying disagreement. We examine the cost of this practice for cyberbullying severity, a subjective judgment on which annotators disagree heavily. In the corpus we study, all 5 annotators assigned the same severity label to only 4.1% of comments. Turning 5 judgments into one label hides this disagreement and obscures how much the annotators differed in their assessments. In this paper, we ask whether a model can preserve this information by predicting the distribution of annotator judgments instead of a single majority label. We find 3 main results. First, accuracy against the majority label cannot tell competent models apart: 2 models with similar accuracy can differ greatly in how well they match the distribution of annotator judgments. Second, predicting the full distribution requires little extra effort. Calibrating an existing model, which adjusts its output probabilities after training, captures most of the improvement without retraining, across 3 datasets covering severity, abuse, and irony, while training on label distributions adds a small further gain. Third, we examine a dataset that includes annotator demographics, where a single shared model has one advantage and one limit. The advantage is that a model predicting the distribution of judgments matches each demographic group's judgments significantly better than a model trained on majority labels, so both groups gain. The limit is that it does not narrow the gap in performance between the groups, because one shared model cannot encode which group is right on a given comment. Preserving annotator disagreement is therefore a low-cost step toward fairer moderation, though not a complete one.
中文翻译
机器学习模型通常基于多个标注者提供的标注数据进行训练。当标注者存在分歧时，其标签常被合并为单一多数投票，从而丢弃了潜在分歧信息。我们研究了这种做法在网络欺凌严重性这一高度主观判断上的代价。在所研究语料库中，所有5名标注者对同一评论赋予相同严重性标签的比例仅为4.1%。将5个判断简化为一个标签会掩盖这种分歧，并模糊标注者评估之间的差异程度。本文探讨模型是否可通过预测标注者判断分布而非单一多数标签来保留该信息。我们发现三个主要结果：第一，对多数标签的准确性无法区分优秀模型——两个准确率相近的模型在匹配标注者判断分布方面可能差异巨大；第二，预测完整分布所需额外成本极低：通过校准现有模型（训练后调整输出概率）即可捕获大部分改进，无需重新训练，覆盖严重性、攻击性和讽刺三个数据集，而基于标签分布的训练仅带来小幅提升；第三，我们考察了一个包含标注者人口统计信息的数据集，单一共享模型具有优势与局限。优势在于，预测判断分布的模型能显著优于多数标签训练模型，更好地匹配各群体的判断，使双方均受益；局限在于，它无法缩小群体间的性能差距，因为单一模型无法编码某条评论中哪个群体是正确的。因此，保留标注者分歧是一种低成本实现更公平内容审核的途径，但并非完全解决方案。
👤 Arslan Bisharat, Katelyn Skees, Manuel Sandoval Madrigal, Mujtaba Nazari, Ayaan Khan et al. 📅 2026-11-11 🔗 arXiv
[2611.14526]
Partial pooling beats synthetic respondents as estimators, not as covariates: a preregistered small-sample tournament
部分池化优于合成受访者作为估计器，而非协变量：一项预注册的小样本竞赛
Survey estimates for population segments, such as media use by sex and age, rest on few respondents per cell, and a growing literature offers synthetic respondents generated by large language models to fill the gap. That literature has not tested them against the small-area estimators a methodologist would otherwise use. We ran ten estimators, preregistered, on identical household-clustered splits of a Korean media panel across five calibration budgets and three holdout regimes. A multilevel partial-pooling model of the MRP family led every calibration-only estimator at every budget, matching at one hundred respondents what direct estimation reaches with roughly four times as many. On unobserved segments, information fixed as a per-segment prior was inert for synthetic panel and previous wave alike; attached as a bias correction it transferred, at the price of estimation noise. No registered synthetic advantage over MRP survived the paired decision rule. Entered instead as a covariate inside the multilevel model, an exploratory hybrid, the same panel improved on MRP on unobserved segments: the information survives where the registered constructions did not. Interval coverage stayed below nominal throughout and worsened with budget for partial pooling, contrary to prediction.
中文翻译
针对人口子群体（如按性别和年龄划分的媒体使用情况）的调查估计通常因每单元样本量过少而受限，现有文献提出使用大型语言模型生成的合成受访者来填补空白。然而，该文献尚未将其与方法学家通常使用的小区估计器进行对比测试。我们预先注册了十种估计器，在韩国媒体面板数据的相同家庭集群分割上，分别在五个校准预算和三种保留方案下进行测试。属于MRP家族的多层次部分池化模型在所有预算下均优于仅校准的估计器，仅用一百名受访者即可达到直接估计约四倍样本量的效果。在未观测子群体上，作为各子群体先验信息固定的合成面板和前一周期数据均无效；若作为偏差校正附加，则可传递信息，但代价是增加估计噪声。在配对决策规则下，合成方法的优势未通过检验。然而，当作为多层次模型中的协变量引入一个探索性混合模型时，同一面板在未观测子群体上表现优于MRP：信息得以留存，而预注册构造未能做到这一点。区间覆盖率在整个过程中均低于名义水平，且随预算增加而恶化，与预期相反。
👤 Howard Kim 📅 2026-09-30 🔗 arXiv
